### [1] [Locality of KMS states for infinite fermionic lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31858)
*Lennart Becker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究无限费米子格点热平衡态在哈密顿量微扰下的响应。主要结果：对选定KMS态的广延微扰，若原态具指数衰减关联与指数局部不可区分性，则微扰态关联呈拉伸指数衰减；不要求态唯一，可覆盖相共存。方法基于在C*-代数上直接建立的量子信念传播（QBP）结合Lieb-Robinson界，建立关联衰减、局域微扰局域影响与局部不可区分性之间的循环等价（针对迹态微扰）。在均匀聚集条件下，将广延微扰下局域平衡期望的变化线性地界定于相互作用范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限费米子格点热平衡态对哈密顿量微扰的稳定性，特别是允许相共存（态非唯一）情形下的稳定性，这在既有唯象唯一性假设的工作之外具有重要性。

Method: 在底层C*-代数上直接建立量子信念传播（QBP），并将其与Lieb-Robinson界结合；用以分析关联衰减、局域微扰局域影响（LPL）与局部不可区分性之间的关系，并构造迹态微扰下的循环论证。

Result: 证明广延微扰下，指数关联衰减与指数局部不可区分性推出微扰态的拉伸指数关联衰减；在给定衰减、支撑与均匀性假设下，对迹态微扰建立关联衰减、LPL与局部不可区分性的循环蕴含；均匀聚集条件下得到局域平衡期望的可微路径，其变化按微扰相互作用范数线性有界。

Conclusion: 量子信念传播与Lieb-Robinson界为无限费米子格点热平衡态的微扰稳定性提供了统一框架，可在相共存下工作，并给出关联衰减、局域不可区分性与局域平衡响应之间的等价关系与定量界。

Abstract: We study how thermal equilibrium states of infinite fermionic lattices respond to perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our main result concerns extensive perturbations of a chosen KMS state. We show that exponential decay of correlations and exponential local indistinguishability under the extensive perturbation yield stretched-exponential decay of correlations for the perturbed state. Neither the initial nor the perturbed state is required to be unique, allowing stability to be studied in the presence of phase coexistence. Our approach uses Quantum Belief Propagation (QBP), which we establish directly on the underlying $C^*$-algebra. Combining QBP with Lieb--Robinson bounds, we show implications between decay of correlations, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally, and local indistinguishability, closing the cycle for perturbations of the tracial state under the stated decay, support and uniformity assumptions. For extensive perturbations under uniform clustering conditions, we also obtain a differentiable path of local equilibrium expectations and bound their change linearly in the interaction norm of the perturbation.

</details>


### [2] [Local Determinant Defects and Low-Energy Subspace Stability in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32095)
*Seyed Mahmoud Manjegani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性的判据，利用两个Gibbs态α-z Rényi核的局部行列式缺陷，界定其低能投影之间的迹L2距离，并给出半有限von Neumann代数中谱展平原理、精确两投影公式及行列式不等式等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断低能谱子空间的稳定性（如基态重叠、有效低能模型）通常需要Hamiltonian在算子范数下接近，但实际物理系统中Hamiltonian可以有界下且Gibbs算子迹类，未必范数接近。因此需要一种不依赖算子范数接近性的有限温度稳定性判据。

Method: 核心工具是半有限von Neumann代数中针对有界正单射算子的谱展平原理，将局部乘积缺陷约化为精确的两投影公式。由此构造局部行列式缺陷（基于α-z Rényi核），用于控制两个Gibbs态低能投影间的迹L2距离；结果适用于有下界仿射Hamiltonian且Gibbs算子迹类的情形。同时证明矩阵、有限及半有限情形下的行列式不等式、等号条件与z单调性。

Result: 得到有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性的判据；对矩阵情形给出主角度余项之和；对qubit情形，缺陷与热隙精确决定基态重叠；同一估计为有效低能模型中的有界可观测量提供一致误差界；并给出含无界Hamiltonian的无限能级示例。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了无需Hamiltonian范数接近的有限温度低能谱子空间稳定性理论，通过谱展平与局部行列式缺陷得到精确两投影公式、基态重叠刻画及有效模型误差界，并系统证明了相关行列式不等式与z单调性。

Abstract: We give a finite-temperature test for the stability of low-energy spectral subspaces. The test uses a local determinant defect for the alpha-z Renyi kernel of two Gibbs states and bounds the tracial L2 distance between their low-energy projections. The main tool is a spectral-flattening principle for bounded positive injective operators in a semifinite von Neumann algebra. It reduces the local product defect to an exact two-projection formula. The thermal result applies to lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians with trace-class Gibbs operators and does not require the Hamiltonians to be close in operator norm. We give an infinite-level example with unbounded Hamiltonians. For matrices, the result gives a sum of principal-angle remainders. For qubits, the defect and the thermal gaps determine the ground-state overlap exactly. The same estimate gives a uniform error bound for bounded observables in an effective low-energy model. We also prove the determinant inequalities, their equality cases, and z-monotonicity in matrix, finite, and semifinite settings.

</details>


### [3] [The Hamilton--Jacobi and Symplectic Analysis for Extended Hořava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32218)
*Diana Vanessa Castro-Luna,Alberto Escalante*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 对线性化非投影λR模型（含BPS最低阶项与Cotton张量平方高阶项）分别用Hamilton-Jacobi分析和Faddeev-Jackiw量子化，在λ≠1/3与λ=1/3两种情形下求解并比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩展的线性化非投影λR理论在两类约束量子化框架下的结构，特别是λ=1/3这一特殊值是否导致量子路径积分测度的差异。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi分析（获得完整Hamilton量、广义括号、基本微分、特征方程与规范变换）与Faddeev-Jackiw量子化（构造辛张量、识别基本括号并计算量子路径积分测度）。

Result: HJ与FJ括号一致；由辛张量得到了量子路径积分测度；并讨论了λ≠1/3与λ=1/3在量子路径积分上的差异。

Conclusion: 两种量子化方法对该模型给出自洽且一致的括号结构，λ=1/3情形的量子路径积分与一般情形存在可辨别的差异。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi [HJ] analysis and the Faddeev-Jackiw [FJ] quantization for the linearized, non-projectable $λR$ model, extended by the lowest-order term of Blas, Pujolàs, and Sibiryakov and the higher-order term involving the square of the Cotton tensor, were carried out. The cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ are analyzed. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi framework, we obtain the complete set of Hamiltonians, generalized brackets, fundamental differential, characteristic equations, and gauge transformations of the theory. In the Faddeev-Jackiw framework, a symplectic tensor is constructed from which the fundamental brackets are identified. The HJ and FJ brackets coincide, and from the symplectic tensor, the measure for the quantum path integral is calculated. The differences between the cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ at the quantum path integral are discussed.

</details>


### [4] [Absolute and relative invariants for low-dimensional topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32442)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了低维环面上投影值映射的三种等价关系，并刻画了相应的绝对与相对拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拓扑绝缘体启发，需要在Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan对称性下统一理解低维环面投影值映射的等价分类及其拓扑不变量。

Method: 通过构造满足伪周期性约束的对称基，对d≤2环面上的投影值映射分别分析Murray-von Neumann等价、酉等价与同伦等价。

Result: d=1时所有AZC类均存在完全周期对称基；d=2时出现由Z（陈数）或Z2（Fu-Kane-Mele）刻画的拓扑障碍，它们分类Murray-von Neumann与酉等价；同伦等价在非最小维Hilbert空间中退化为酉等价，在最小环境中则产生额外的相对同伦不变量。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维有能隙量子系统提供了统一几何框架，揭示了不同等价关系如何产生特定的绝对与相对拓扑不变量。

Abstract: [An extended abstract is provided in-text]
  Motivated by topological insulators, this paper analyzes classification schemes for projection-valued maps on $d$-dimensional tori, $d \le 2$, subject to Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan (AZC) symmetries (time-reversal, particle-hole, and chiral). The main objective is to compare three notions of equivalence for these maps: Murray-von Neumann, unitary, and homotopy equivalence. The analysis relies on constructing symmetric bases for the projection ranges under a pseudo-periodicity constraint, where select basis vectors pick up a phase when looped around the torus. If $d=1$, fully periodic symmetric bases exist across all AZC classes. If $d=2$, topological obstructions appear as non-trivial phases characterized by $\mathbb{Z}$ (Chern number) or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ (Fu-Kane-Mele) invariants. These serve as absolute invariants that classify both Murray-von Neumann and unitary equivalences. For homotopy equivalence, the behavior depends on the ambient Hilbert space: if this has non-minimal dimension, homotopy equivalence reduces directly to unitary equivalence, while in minimal ambient spaces additional relative homotopy invariants emerge. Their precise values change under unitary conjugation, leaving only relative topological phases between maps well-defined. Ultimately, this provides a unified geometric framework for gapped quantum systems in low dimensions, demonstrating how distinct equivalence relations generate specific absolute and relative topological invariants.

</details>


### [5] [Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization for fractional and long-range Anderson models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33084)
*Peter D. Hislop,Rodrigo Matos,Constanza Rojas-Molina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究长程Anderson模型（含分数Anderson模型）的局部本征值统计与动力学（退）局域化，证明在强无序且β>d/2时，局部本征值统计为强度由态密度n(E)给出的Poisson点过程；同时在强无序下不存在动力学局域化，验证了Disertori等人的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Anderson模型（仅最近邻跳跃）在强无序下表现出动力学局域化。本文旨在探讨具有长程跳跃项|T(n,m)|~||n-m||^{-(d+2β)}的随机Anderson型扰动系统的本征值统计与动力学行为，检验长程跳跃是否改变局域化性质，并验证Disertori等人关于强无序下无动力学局域化的猜想。

Method: 研究d维格点上的长程Anderson模型，跳跃项满足|T(n,m)|~||n-m||^{-(d+2β)}（β>0）。在强随机势场下模型具有稠密纯点谱且特征向量多项式衰减。作者针对β>d/2，通过建立格林函数分数矩的显式、尖锐下界，推导位置算子的大矩发散，从而分析局部本征值统计与动力学性质。

Result: 在强无序且β>d/2时，以确定性谱中任意E为中心的局部本征值统计为Poisson点过程，强度由态密度函数n(E)给出；强无序下不存在动力学局域化。特别地，在一维d=1情形下，分数Anderson模型（分数Laplacian (-Δ)^α的随机扰动，1/2<α<1）在强无序时呈现Poisson本征值统计和动力学退局域化。这与仅含最近邻跳跃的通常Anderson模型形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃导致强无序下的动力学退局域化，本征值统计为Poisson分布，与最近邻Anderson模型的动力学局域化行为截然不同，验证了Disertori等人的猜想。

Abstract: We study local eigenvalue statistics and dynamical (de)-localization properties for long-range Anderson models, including the fractional Anderson model. These systems are random Anderson-type perturbations of operators with long-range (non random) hopping terms of the form $|T(n,m)| \sim \|n-m\|^{-(d+2β)}$ for $β>0$, on the $d$-dimensional lattice. In the presence of a strong enough random potential, these models exhibit dense pure point spectrum with polynomially decaying eigenvectors, almost surely. We show that in this strong disorder regime, and for $β>d/2$, the local eigenvalue statistics centered at any $E$ in the deterministic spectrum is a Poisson point process with intensity given by the density of states function $n(E)$. Moreover, we prove that, at strong disorder, there is no dynamical localization for these models, verifying a conjecture of Disertori et al. We achieve this by establishing explicit, sharp lower bounds on the Green's functions fractional moments which imply that large moments of the position operator are infinite. Hence, these models exhibit Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization, that is, the absence of dynamical localization. In particular, this shows that in dimension $d=1$, the fractional Anderson model, a random perturbation of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^α$ with $α\in (0,1)$, exhibits Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization if the disorder is strong and the exponent $\frac{1}{2}<α<1$. This is in stark contrast with what is known for the usual Anderson model that has only nearest-neighbor hopping.

</details>


### [6] [Minimal output entropy for the channels that add or remove a box of a Young diagram, and a Pauli principle for every permutation symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33498)
*Robin Reuvers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 $U(d)$ 两个相差一个方块的不可约表示之间的联系，证明四类协变量子信道中相干态输出均被其他输出优占（majorize），从而最小化输出熵，并给出用钩长表示的显式最优输出，进而导出约化密度矩阵谱的尖锐界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $U(d)$ 相邻不可约表示之间由“加/去一个方块”联系，这类操作对应物理上添加或移除一个粒子（或保持盒子的 $\mathbb{C}^d$ 及其对偶），在纠缠、粒子数变化与对称性约束问题中具有基础意义；作者希望确定这些信道输出熵的极小值及其极值态，并应用于单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的刻画。

Method: 作者考虑四类协变信道：去盒或加盒，且保留新图或保留盒子（即 $\mathbb{C}^d$ 或其对偶）。利用优占（majorization）这一偏序工具比较不同输出的本征值排序，证明相干态的输出的本征值向量优占任意其他输出；并通过钩长公式显式计算最优输出谱。对保留盒子的两类信道，进一步刻画所有极小化态。

Result: 对每一类信道，相干态输出的本征值优占任意其他输出，因此相干态最小化输出熵；最优输出可显式地用钩长表示。对保留盒子的两类信道，确定了全部极小化态，且这些极小化态不必是相干态，即使当其为纯态时亦然。作为应用，对给定置换对称性的 $N$ 粒子系统，得到单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的尖锐上下界，并给出混合态可达到谱的精确集合；费米子情形对应泡利原理与 Coleman 定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将相邻 $U(d)$ 不可约表示间的协变信道纳入统一的优占/熵极小化框架，给出钩长形式的最优输出及完整极小化态分类，并由此精确刻画了对称多粒子系统中单粒子约化密度矩阵的容许谱，推广了泡利原理和 Coleman 定理。

Abstract: Two irreducible representations of $U(d)$ whose Young diagrams differ by a single box are connected by four covariant quantum channels: the box can be removed or added, and one keeps either the new diagram or the box ($\mathbb{C}^d$ or its dual). We prove that for each of these channels the output of a coherent state majorizes every other output, so that coherent states minimize the output entropy, and we compute the optimal output explicitly in terms of hook lengths. For the two channels that keep the box we also determine all minimizers; these need not be coherent, even when they are pure. As an application we consider $N$ particles with a given permutation symmetry and find sharp bounds on the spectrum of the reduced density matrix of a single particle, as well as the exact set of spectra that mixed states can reach (for fermions these are the Pauli principle and Coleman's theorem).

</details>


### [7] [Effective atom-dimer bands of three-body lattice systems at strong coupling: exact critical mass ratios and a statistics-quasimomentum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33576)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究正方晶格上三粒子无穷大强度接触相互作用系统，涵盖三种统计（全同玻色子、两个全同费米子加一粒子、两个全同玻色子加一粒子）及任意总准动量。通过Feshbach-Schur约化将阈值问题化为基于正方晶格两点电阻的4×4厄米矩阵判据（对应四次多项式），完整求解原子-二聚体区带与临界质量比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅处理三聚体问题和零准动量情形，原子-二聚体扇区在整个布里渊区上尚未被分析。本文旨在将原子-二聚体扇区推广至完整布里渊区，建立无穷大接触相互作用下三种统计的完整阈值理论。

Method: 采用Feshbach-Schur约化方法将问题投影到配对接触算子的无穷简并特征值1上，得到具有一阶误差界证明的显式有效哈密顿量；将阈值问题转化为由正方晶格两点电阻构成的4×4厄米矩阵判据，进而化为四次多项式求解。

Result: 给出区带的闭式解；临界质量比在零准动量处精确值为π/(π-2)，与数值结果一致；对任意准动量和质量比至多存在两个原子-二聚体态；得到起界律——区带边界上的本质奇异性与纯p波中的线性对数起界；证明两个精确对偶性，表明统计改变等价于准动量平移π；给出第二费米阈值的全局范围。所有常数经晶格求积、计算机代数、精确对角化和无穷晶格求解器独立验证。

Conclusion: 起界律具有重整化群解读：逆晶格格林函数为边缘s波耦合，束缚能通过维度嬗变产生（晶格尺度16），区带边界属边缘（BCS/Kondo）型而非BKT型。该工作完整刻画了无穷大接触相互作用下三粒子系统的原子-二聚体谱结构与阈值行为。

Abstract: We study three particles on the square lattice with contact interactions of infinite strength, for all total quasimomenta, for three statistics: three identical bosons, two identical fermions plus a third particle, and two identical bosons plus a third particle. Previous work treated only the trimer and zero quasimomentum. Here the atom-dimer sector is analyzed on the whole Brillouin zone. A Feshbach-Schur reduction onto the infinitely degenerate eigenvalue 1 of the pair-contact operator gives explicit effective Hamiltonians with proved first-order error. The threshold problem reduces to a 4x4 Hermitian matrix criterion built from the two-point resistances of the square lattice, and hence to a quartic polynomial. This yields the complete atom-dimer zone and the critical mass ratio on the Brillouin zone, with bound between 1 and pi/(pi-2), and closed forms at all symmetry points. We prove: the zones in closed form; the critical mass ratio with exact value pi/(pi-2) at zero quasimomentum, reproducing a numerical value; at most two atom-dimer states for every quasimomentum and mass ratio; the onset laws, an essential singularity on the zone boundary and a linear-over-logarithm onset in the pure p-wave; two exact dualities showing that a change of statistics is equivalent to a shift of quasimomentum by pi; and the global range of the second fermionic threshold. The onset laws admit a renormalization-group reading: the inverse lattice Green function is a marginal s-wave coupling, binding energies arise by dimensional transmutation with lattice scale 16, and the zone boundary is of marginal (BCS/Kondo) type rather than of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. All constants are verified independently by lattice quadrature, computer algebra, exact diagonalisation, and infinite-lattice solvers.

</details>


### [8] [$p$-adic spectral zeta functions via the inverse Stieltjes transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33660)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 $p$-adic 谱 zeta 函数框架从离散谱推广到连续谱，通过逆 Stieltjes 变换构造 $p$-adic 谱分布，并定义玻色型与费米型 zeta 函数及泛函行列式，建立其解析性质、特殊值公式与 Stirling 展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱 zeta 函数是量子物理中正则化微分算子行列式的标准工具；此前工作仅通过局部解析插值函数处理离散谱，本文旨在将框架扩展至连续谱情形。

</details>

Below the free or one-excitation threshold, the point spectrum in every higher sector is described by a bounded self-adjoint nonnegative Birman--Schwinger operator. In every sector with at least two excitations we prove absence of sub-threshold spectrum for arbitrary coupling when the renormalized gap is not smaller than the boson mass, and under an explicit weak-coupling condition when it is smaller. Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging in terms of accurate boundary localization and complex-structure preservation, as target regions often exhibit weak boundaries, fine-grained structures, and irregular shapes, while high-quality images and precise annotations are usually limited. Existing generative segmentation approaches based on Flow Matching mainly learn velocity fields in the state space but lack explicit constraints on the spatial regularity and distance-field properties of the recovered representation. This limitation may lead to spatial oscillations, boundary displacement, and discontinuities in fine structures. To address these issues, we propose an image-conditioned Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching framework, termed GR-FM, which formulates segmentation as a continuous probability transport process from an initial distribution to a target implicit representation distribution. Instead of directly modeling binary masks, GR-FM adopts the SDF to describe the target structure, where each pixel is represented by its signed distance to the object boundary, resulting in a continuous geometric field. Flow Matching and ordinary differential equations are then employed to achieve deterministic and efficient distribution transport. The training objective further incorporates a biharmonic regularization term and an Eikonal constraint to enhance the spatial smoothness, structural consistency, and distance-field characteristics of the recovered representation. Moreover, we analyze the continuous transport process under geometric constraints and investigate the evolution of the zero level set. Experiments conducted on the MoNuSeg, GlaS, and DRIVE datasets demonstrate that GR-FM achieves competitive performance in both region overlap and boundary accuracy, reduces performance variation, and maintains stable segmentation results with only a small number of integration steps.

</details>

Abstract: Motivated by the systematic study of spiral minimal products, we consider harmonic maps obtained by coupling two spherical eigenmaps through a profile curve in $\mathbb S^3$, with a doubly warped product metric on the source. We obtain global reconstructions from a prescribed magnitude, one source warp, or a monotone volume factor, together with periodic families in which profiles closing on finite covers are dense. In the fixed-metric problem, a change of time gives an autonomous Neumann system. One fixed source metric then carries continuous families of closed profiles and infinitely many closed graph geodesics. For fixed even $p,q\geq 2$, every fixed metric in this Neumann class on $\mathbb S^1\times\mathbb S^p\times\mathbb S^q$ supports harmonic maps into $\mathbb S^{p+q+1}$ of degree $4m$ for every $m\geq 1$. Conclusion: $\bigwedge_A G$上的Gerstenhaber括号结构与$G$上的Lie--Rinehart结构存在一一对应，且该对应可通过分次导子与内导子的交换子关系自然刻画。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a commutative associative unital algebra over a field $K$ of characteristic zero, and let $(G,ρ)$ be a Lie--Rinehart algebra over $A$. We revisit the Gerstenhaber structure carried by the exterior algebra $\bigwedge_A G$ from the point of view of graded derivations. Particular attention is paid to the well-definedness of the Schouten--Nijenhuis extension over the coefficient algebra $A$: the Lie--Rinehart identity is shown to provide exactly the correction terms needed for the bracket to descend to the $A$-balanced exterior algebra. After establishing graded antisymmetry, the Leibniz rule and the graded Jacobi identity without circularity, we study the inner graded derivations $\adSN(P)=[P,\cdot]_{SN}$ and prove \[ [\adSN(P),\adSN(Q)]=\adSN([P,Q]_{SN}). \] Finally, we give a complete proof of the converse construction: Gerstenhaber brackets on $\bigwedge_A G$ are in one-to-one correspondence with Lie--Rinehart structures on $G$.

</details>

Abstract: Assume that smooth metrics $g_k$ converge in measure to a smooth metric $g$. However, if one assumes in addition that the identity maps $(M,g_k)\to (M,g)$ are uniformly bi-Lipschitz, then indeed $g$ must have non-negative scalar curvature. Motivation: Frank 与 Loss 提出了关于球面上 Dirac 零模的定量下界问题。该问题与 Dirac 算子、向量场曲率项及 sharp 几何不等式密切相关，且在 n=3 时已由作者用不同方法证明；本文旨在解决其余所有维数 n≥4 的情形，并建立统一、sharp 的估计。

Method: 论文分三种情形处理：i) n≥5 奇数；ii) n≥5 偶数；iii) n=4，各情形采用不同方法。对 n≥5，核心是化归为球面 S^n 上一个带重心约束的改进 Sobolev 不等式。定义泛函商 𝔞_n，在约束 ∫x|u|^{2n/(n-2)}=0 且 ω_n^{2/n}‖u‖_{2n/(n-2)}²−∫u²>0 下取 infimum，并证明普适估计 𝔞_n>n(n−2)/(4(n²−3n+1))。

Result: 得到 sharp 下界：‖dA^♭‖_{n/2} ≥ 2[n/2]^{−1/2}(n−1)/(n−2) S_n = [n/2]^{−1/2}·n(n−1)/2·ω_n^{2/n}。等号可达当且仅当 n 为奇数；此时在共形变换与规范变换意义下，φ 为 Killing spinor，A 为与 φ 相关的 Reeb 场的实常数倍。对 n≥5 得到 𝔞_n>n(n−2)/(4(n²−3n+1))，足以推出目标下界。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了 Frank–Loss 的 sharp 下界问题，覆盖 n=3 之外的所有维数，并给出等号情形的刚性刻画。带重心约束的改进 Sobolev 不等式是 n≥5 情形的关键工具，但其最优常数 𝔞_n 的精确值仍未确定，留作开问题。

Abstract: We resolve an open problem posed by Frank--Loss. Let $n\ge 3$ and let $\varphi\in L^p(\mathbb{S}^n)$, with $\frac{n}{n-1}<p<\infty$, be a nontrivial Dirac zero mode on $\mathbb{S}^n$, i.e. a nonzero spinor satisfying \begin{equation*}
  D\varphi = iA\cdot\varphi, \end{equation*} where $D$ is the Dirac operator and $A$ is a vector field with $\mathrm{d} A^\flat\in L^{n/2}$. We prove the sharp lower bound \begin{equation*}
  \|\mathrm{d} A^\flat\|_{\frac{n}{2}} \ge 2\left[\frac{n}{2}\right]^{-\frac12}\frac{n-1}{n-2}S_n
  = \left[\frac{n}{2}\right]^{-\frac12}\frac{n(n-1)}{2}ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}. \end{equation*} Equality is attainable if and only if $n$ is odd; in that case, modulo conformal and gauge transformations, $\varphi$ is a Killing spinor and $A$ is a real multiple of the Reeb field associated with $\varphi$. The case $n=3$ was proved in our recent paper using a different method. In the paper we divide the remaining cases into 3 cases: i) $n\ge 5$ is odd, ii) $n\ge 5$ is even and iii) $n=4$. All these cases need to use different methods.
  For $n\ge 5$, the argument crucially reduces to an improved Sobolev inequality on $\mathbb{S}^n$ under a barycenter constraint. Specifically, for $u\in W^{1,2}(\mathbb{S}^n)$ we consider \begin{equation*}
  \mathfrak{a}_n := \inf\Bigg\{ \frac{ \int \Big(|\nabla u|^2 + \frac{n(n-2)}{4}u^2\Big) - \frac{n(n-2)}{4}ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}\|u\|_{\frac{2n}{n-2}}^2 }{ ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}\|u\|_{\frac{2n}{n-2}}^2 - \int u^2 } \,\Bigg|\, \int x|u|^{\frac{2n}{n-2}}=0,\ ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}\|u\|_{\frac{2n}{n-2}}^2 - \int u^2>0 \Bigg\}, \end{equation*} and we obtain the following universal estimate \begin{equation*}
  \mathfrak{a}_n > \frac{n(n-2)}{4(n^2-3n+1)}, \end{equation*} which is enough for our aim. Determining the exact value of $\mathfrak{a}_n$ remains open.

</details>

Abstract: We study properties of Extremally Ricci-Pinched (ERP) $G_2$-Structures on compact $7$-manifolds. Abstract: We establish a parameterized Heintze--Karcher-type inequality for positive solutions of nonlinear Dirichlet problems involving the $p$-Laplacian, with $p\geq2$, on bounded Riemannian domains. We compare the resulting bound with previous estimates and investigate the geometry associated with equality. For $p=2$, an exact deficit identity allows the pointwise condition $f'\leq nk$ to be replaced by a weighted integral condition. Abstract: We study the local geometry of Euclidean submanifolds of rank at least two whose curvature normals with respect to the flat part of the normal bundle have constant length, equivalently, whose adapted third fundamental form has constant eigenvalues. The complete case was fully settled by Di Scala and the third author, who showed that such a complete submanifold has constant principal curvatures. Around a regular point, we show that $M$ is generated, via parallel manifolds, by a hypersurface $N$ whose extrinsic factors are contained in spheres and, when of dimension at least two, have rank one; conversely, such factors can be assembled to construct examples of $M$. The proof relies on the isoparametric rank theorem to rule out non-umbilical factors. As a further application, when the inner products of the curvature normals are constant, we show that there are exactly two eigendistributions, one of which is one-dimensional and autoparallel, extending, for arbitrary codimension when the normal bundle is flat, curvature-homogeneity results of Tsukada and the recent ones of Bryant, Florit, and Ziller for hypersurfaces.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静止方程 f(x)r^α σ_k(κ)=βu 的严格凸解存在性已由 Bryan-Ivaki-Scheuer 通过扩张型流从障碍出发得到；但归一化收缩流从任意初值的收敛性、显式指数收敛率以及极限唯一性尚属空白，本文旨在补齐这一部分。

Method: 考虑归一化流 X_t=-f(ν)|X|^α σ_k(κ)ν+βX，其中 β=C_n^k，并将相对残差 p=(log u)_t=β-f(x)r^α σ_k(κ)/u 作为核心量。在 α>k+1 的超临界范围内，先独立于曲率估计证明残差的 L^∞ 范数单调不增且以显式指数 β(α-k-1) 衰减，再在此残差估计基础上进行曲率估计。

Result: 对每个光滑严格凸且包围原点的初始超曲面，流对所有时间存在，并在 C^∞ 中指数收敛到静止方程的唯一光滑严格凸解；收敛由残差 p 驱动，其 L^∞ 范数单调不增且以指数 β(α-k-1) 衰减；残差估计先于且独立于曲率估计得到；极限的唯一性同时建立。

Abstract: Let k be an integer from 1 to n, let f be a positive smooth function on the unit sphere, and consider the normalized flow X_t = -f({nu})|X|^{alpha} sigma_k({kappa}) {nu} + beta X, beta = C_n^k. In the supercritical range alpha > k+1, we prove the flow exists for all time and converges exponentially in C^{infty}, from every smooth strictly convex initial hypersurface enclosing the origin, to the unique smooth strictly convex solution of f(x)r^{alpha} sigma_k({kappa}) = beta u. The convergence is driven by the relative residual p = (log u)_t = beta - f(x)r^{alpha} {sigma}_k({kappa})/u, whose L^{infty} norm is nonincreasing and decays with the explicit exponent beta(alpha-k-1); the residual estimates are proved before, and independently of, the curvature estimates. Existence of a strictly convex solution of the stationary equation in this range, for arbitrary positive angular data, was previously obtained by Bryan-Ivaki-Scheuer through an expanding-type flow started from a barrier; the contribution here is the convergence of the normalized contracting flow from arbitrary initial data, with an explicit exponential rate, together with uniqueness of the limit.

</details>

Abstract: We study a conjecture of Qiongling Li concerning the pointwise monotonicity of the energy density along the $\mathbb C^*$-flow of stable $\mathrm{SL}(n,\mathbb C)$ Higgs bundles. We prove that the conjecture holds in rank two: for every stable $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb C)$ Higgs bundle, the energy density is pointwise nondecreasing along the $\mathbb C^*$-orbit. In contrast, we show that this phenomenon is genuinely rank-dependent. For every rank $n\geq 3$, we construct stable $\mathrm{SL}(n,\mathbb C)$ Higgs bundles for which the energy density fails to be monotone along the $\mathbb C^*$-flow.

</details>

Abstract: In this note, we prove that for n > 2 a harmonic map $\mathbb{S}^n \to \mathbb{CP}^m$, of index n+1 is pseudo horizontally weakly conformal, that $n$ must be odd. Abstract: We extend Gualtieri's deformation theorem for generalized complex structures on exact Courant algebroids to the more general class of $\mathcal{G}$-flat transitive Courant algebroids. Our approach relies on the framework of tame Fréchet spaces and Hamilton's Nash-Moser (implicit function) theorem. As an important step of the proof of our deformation theorem, we endow the group of autoequivalences of a $\mathcal{G}$-flat transitive Courant algebroid with a tame Fréchet Lie group structure and show that there exists a tame subgroup of exact autoequivalences, whose infinitesimal action is by inner derivations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射的正则性理论中，奇异集的维数估计是核心问题。对一般目标流形，稳定驻调和映射的奇异集通常只能得到余维至少二的估计；当目标为具有双不变度量的紧李群时，几何结构更特殊，已有工作暗示可能有更强的正则性，因此需要确定这一情形下奇异集维数的精确上界。

Result: 建立了关键定理：对任意稳定驻调和映射 u: M → G，其奇异集的Hausdorff余维至少为四；并通过构造例子说明该余维界不能被改进，即估计是sharp的。

Conclusion: 稳定驻调和映射映入紧李群时具有良好的正则性，其奇异集维数远低于一般目标流形情形，余维四的界是最优的。这一结果将此前关于余维二的估计显著改进，并为李群目标下调和映射正则性理论提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a compact Riemannian manifold, and let $G$ be a compact Lie group with bi-invariant metric. We show that the singular set of any stable stationary harmonic map $u : M \to G$ has Hausdorff codimension at least four. This is sharp.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构造colex向量场：基于单纯复形下星滤过对应的全序，定义无环离散向量场；证明该向量场是滤过无环的，并由其Morse复形计算下星滤过的持续同调。进一步研究当诱导下星滤过的顶点函数通过顺序相邻顶点交换修改时，colex向量场的快速重计算；同时提出存储与计算复形中胞腔间路径数的框架，以及当colex向量场改变时快速更新这些路径数的方法。最后给出公开的概念验证代码。

Result: colex向量场诱导出滤过无环向量场，其Morse复形可计算下星滤过的持续同调；该向量场在顶点函数经顺序相邻交换修改时可快速重计算；胞腔间路径数可存储、计算并在向量场变化时快速更新；概念验证代码在持久同调变换上的运行时间与更标准的矩阵归约方法相当。

Conclusion: 该工作将离散Morse理论与持续同调计算相结合，提供了一个可快速更新的下星滤过持续同调计算框架；在持久同调变换应用中，其初步实现展示了与标准矩阵归约方法相当的性能，为动态/局部滤过修改场景提供了可行思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a construction of an acyclic discrete vector field that is compatible with a total order associated with a lower star filtration on a simplicial complex, called the colex vector field. We show that the colex vector field induces a filtered acyclic vector field, whose resulting Morse complex computes the persistent homology of the lower star filtration on the underlying simplicial complex. We show that the colex vector field can be recomputed quickly when the vertex function that induces the lower star filtration is modified via order-adjacent vertex swaps. We provide a framework for storing and computing the number of paths between cells in the simplicial complex, as well as a method to update these values quickly when the colex vector field changes. Finally, we provide publicly available proof-of-concept code for the ideas shown. When applying it to the Persistent Homology Transform, we show that its runtime is comparable to a more standard matrix reduction approach.

</details>

Motivation: 作者希望刻画并计数特定偏序集（Hasse图为有根树）上的transfer系统，并探究transfer系统在偏序集取反序时的格结构性质，从而深化对transfer系统与weak factorization系统之间关系的理解。

Method: 对Hasse图为有根树的偏序集，构造transfer系统与受秩函数约束的保序函数之间的双射，并建立相应函数集合的递推关系用于枚举。对一般有限偏序集，通过建立weak factorization系统与transfer系统之间的双射，证明P^{op}上的transfer系统格同构于P上transfer系统格的反格。

Result: 证明了树上transfer系统与有界保序自然数函数之间的双射；建立了计数递推并计算了若干树族的transfer系统数量；证明了对任意有限偏序集P，L(Tr(P^{op})) ≅ L(Tr(P))^{op}。

Conclusion: 该工作为树上transfer系统的计数提供了统一框架，并将weak factorization系统与transfer系统的对应推广到一般有限偏序集，揭示了transfer系统格在反序下的对偶性质。

Abstract: We study transfer systems on partially ordered sets whose Hasse diagrams are rooted trees. We prove there is a bijection between transfer systems on a tree and order-preserving functions to the natural numbers that are bounded by the rank function. We then develop a recurrence for the set of functions and use this to enumerate transfer systems on some families of trees. In addition to our study of trees, we prove for any finite poset $\mathcal{P}$, the lattice of transfer systems on the opposite poset $\mathcal{P}^{op}$ is isomorphic to the opposite lattice of transfer systems on $\mathcal{P}$. We show this by establishing a bijection between weak factorization systems and transfer systems, which builds on the results for lattices shown in previous work of Franchere, Ormsby, Osorno, Qin, and Waugh.

Abstract: Let $P_q$ be the graded polynomial algebra $\mathbb F_2[x_1,x_2,\ldots ,x_q]$ over the prime field of two elements, $\mathbb F_2$, with the degree of each $x_i$ being 1. We study the {\it hit Peterson problem} of finding a minimal set of generators for $P_q$ as a module over the mod-$2$ Steenrod algebra, $\mathcal{A}.$ In this note, we present the minimal sets of $\mathcal{A}$-generators for $P_5$ in the cases where the degree are sufficiently general.

</details>


### [66] [A data structure for quotient flag complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32733)
*Konstantin Sorokin,Aleksandr Levin,Maxim Beketov,Anton Ayzenberg*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究将flag complex \(K\) 沿子复形 \(A\) 的每个连通分支压缩为一点所得的商空间：该商是CW复形，但粘接映射不再隐含。作者证明当 \(K\) 为flag时，商的面序严格分次当且仅当 \(A\) 为flag，且存活标号胞腔由维数不超过3的胞腔决定；对 \(m\)-flag对阈值为 \(2m+1\)，\(K\)为flag时降为 \(m+2\)；仅当 \(A\) 在 \(K\) 中full时这些指定胞腔构成正则CW分解。据此提出QF-tree，一种索引于商顶点词trie的胞腔表，为每个存活单形存储其 \(d+1\) 个面的有序列表并标记塌缩面。有界维数下规模线性于存活单形数加保留来源；给出并验证了使其小于同伦等价锥模型的塌缩比例公式。由于塌缩只改变 \(A\) 的闭星上的粘接数据，QF-tree可局部用于simplex tree中；对Vietoris–Rips采样中球状 \(A\)，闭星为薄壳，各采样半径下中位紧凑预算均低于锥模型。配套库基于Gudhi实现QF-tree、局部变体、可编辑层、粘接、圆盘附着、诱导映射、cup积、基本群表示与zigzag持久性，实验区分了维护商与在其上计算代数的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vietoris–Rips滤流是拓扑数据分析中的标准对象，本质上是flag complex，而simplex tree可无需粘接数据地存储它们，具有很高的存储经济性。本文关注的问题是：当把flag complex \(K\) 沿子复形 \(A\) 作商、把 \(A\) 的每个连通分支压缩为一点时，这种经济性还能保留多少。该问题的动因在于商空间虽由 \(K\setminus A\) 的单形作为胞腔构成，但其粘接映射不再像flag complex那样隐含，因此需要明确刻画哪些胞腔与附着信息是必要的，以及能否设计一种紧凑数据结构在保留可计算性的同时避免退化为通用的CW表示。

Method: 论文对flag complex \(K\) 及其子复形 \(A\) 的商进行组合与拓扑分析。核心结论包括：\(K\) 为flag时商的面序严格分次当且仅当 \(A\) 为flag；存活标号胞腔由维数至多3的胞腔决定；对 \(m\)-flag对阈值为 \(2m+1\)，\(K\)为flag时降为 \(m+2\)；指定胞腔构成正则CW分解当且仅当 \(A\) 在 \(K\) 中full。基于这些结果提出QF-tree：一种胞腔表，对每个存活单形存储其 \(d+1\) 个面的有序列表，并标记塌缩面，整体索引于商顶点词的trie。作者给出有界维数下的线性规模界，并推导及验证塌缩比例公式以比较其与同伦等价锥模型的大小。进一步利用塌缩仅影响 \(A\) 的闭星这一局部性，将QF-tree嵌入simplex tree局部使用。配套实现基于Gudhi风格，包含可编辑层、局部商更新、粘接、圆盘附着、诱导映射、cup积、基本群表示与zigzag持久性。

Result: 理论上，论文建立了flag商的面序分次判据、存活胞腔的有限维决定性与阈值、正则CW分解的充要条件，并给出QF-tree在有界维数下线性于存活单形数加保留来源的规模界及塌缩比例公式。实验上，在球状 \(A\) 的Vietoris–Rips采样情形中，闭星为薄壳，QF-tree在各个采样半径下的中位紧凑预算均低于锥模型，验证了其空间优势。配套库的实验还将维护商的成本与在其上计算代数的成本分离开来，表明QF-tree可支持包括zigzag持久性在内的一系列代数计算。

Conclusion: 论文表明，flag complex沿子复形作商后仍可保留相当程度的表示经济性：通过面序分次性、低维决定性与full条件等组合刻画，可以界定必需保留的附着数据；QF-tree以trie索引的胞腔表紧凑地存储这些数据，并在有界维数下达到线性规模，且因局部性可嵌入simplex tree。实验显示其在特定Vietoris–Rips采样情形下优于同伦等价锥模型。配套库进一步验证了该表示在粘接、诱导映射、cup积、基本群与zigzag持久性等计算中的可用性。

Abstract: Vietoris-Rips filtrations, which are standard in topological data analysis, are flag complexes, and a simplex tree stores these without any attaching data. In this paper we ask what survives of this economy when a flag complex $K$ is divided by a subcomplex $A$, each connected component of $A$ being crushed to a point. Such a quotient is a CW complex whose cells are the simplices of $K\setminus A$, but their attaching maps are no longer implicit. We show that for flag $K$ the face order of the quotient is strictly graded exactly when $A$ is flag, and that the surviving labelled cells are determined by those of dimension at most 3. For $m$-flag pairs the threshold is $2m+1$, and it drops to $m+2$ when $K$ is flag. The prescribed cells form a regular CW decomposition only when $A$ is full in $K$. These results justify the QF-tree: a cell table that stores, for each surviving simplex, its ordered list of $d+1$ facets with collapsed facets flagged, indexed by a trie of quotient-vertex words. For bounded dimension its size is linear in the number of surviving simplices plus the retained provenance, and we derive and verify a simple formula for the collapsed fraction above which it is smaller than the homotopy-equivalent cone model. Because a collapse changes the attaching data only on the closed star of $A$, the QF-tree can also be applied locally inside a simplex tree. For a ball-shaped $A$ in the sampled Vietoris--Rips regime the closed star is a thin shell, and the median compact budget is below the cone model at every sampled radius. An accompanying library, modelled on Gudhi, implements the QF-tree, its local variant, an editable layer with local quotient updates, gluing, disc attachment, induced maps, cup products, fundamental-group presentations and zigzag persistence, and provided experiments separate the cost of maintaining a quotient from the cost of the algebra computed on it.

</details>

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{F}^{m}=\{A\subset[m]\}$, endowed with the symmetric difference metric and the Cardinality Reverse-Lexicographic order. That way, we compute the homotopy type $\operatorname{Čech}\left(\mathcal{F}_{\preceq a}^m; \frac{r}{2}=1\right)$ by relating it to consecutively adding vertices of $\mathbb{I}^m$, the unit hypercube of $m$th dimension, to point $0$. Further, for higher $r$, we propose a conjecture regarding contractibility of a subcomplex implying a formula for the exact number of wedges of $\mathbb{S}^ 3$'s and $\mathbb{S}^ 4$'s in the $\operatorname{Čech}$ complex.

*Julia Semikina,Don Zagier*

TL;DR: 本文研究了偶数维闭定向流形 $M$ 的对称积 $M(n)=M^n/S_n$ 的 $L$-类，通过对偶同调类给出更简洁的描述：其生成函数为仅依赖 $M$ 的欧拉示性数的初等因子与 $L$-类庞加莱对偶的奇数 Adams 扭的指数之积，并由此得到同调 $L$-类更简的证明与表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数维闭定向流形的对称积是有理同调流形，因而有典范的 $L$-类，但此前仅有一个复杂的计算公式（来自第二作者早年学位论文）。作者希望简化该结果并给出更易理解的结构性描述。

Method: 转而在同调中研究对偶类：将每个 $H_*(M(n);\mathbb{Q})$ 嵌入 Hopf 代数 $H_*(M(\infty);\mathbb{Q})$，考察这些同调 $L$-类的生成函数。利用 Hopf 代数结构与 Adams 运算对生成函数进行因子分解。

Result: 同调 $L$-类的生成函数可分解为两部分：一个仅依赖 $M$ 的欧拉示性数的初等因子，以及由 $M$ 的 $L$-类的庞加莱对偶的奇数 Adams 扭所构成级数的指数。该分解较原公式大幅简化。

Conclusion: 该生成函数公式同时给出同调 $L$-类的更简证明，以及同调 $L$-类较以往更简洁的显式表达，统一并简化了对称积 $L$-类的计算。

Abstract: If $M$ is a closed oriented manifold of even dimension, then its $n$th symmetric product $M(n)=M^n/S_n$ is a rational homology manifold for any $n \geq 0$ and hence has a canonically defined $L$-class in $H^*(M(n);\mathbb{Q})$. A complicated formula for these classes was given many years ago in the second author's thesis. We obtain a simpler result by studying the dual classes in homology, showing that their generating function (after embedding each $H_*(M(n); \mathbb{Q})$ into the Hopf algebra $H_*(M(\infty); \mathbb{Q})$) is the product of an elementary factor depending only on the Euler characteristic of $M$ and the exponential of a series consisting of odd Adams twists of the Poincaré dual of the $L$-class of $M$. We also use this to obtain both a simpler proof and a simpler expression for the cohomological $L$-classes than the previous one.

</details>

Abstract: We deformalize an LLM-generated proof within the formalism of Homotopy Type Theory that an iterated pushout construction for $\operatorname{SP}^3(X)$ is a set whenever $X$ is a set. </details>


### [70] [Critical Diameters and Vietoris-Rips Filtrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33009)

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文将有限构型的临界直径与Vietoris-Rips复形的拓扑联系起来，证明了当区间[r,s)不含弱斜率零的直径时，尺度r到s的包含映射为同伦等价；并在黎曼流形、球面等情形下刻画了临界直径谱的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vietoris-Rips复形随尺度变化时其同伦型何时改变是一个核心问题。作者希望用"直径函数临界点"这一分析工具刻画同伦型保持的尺度区间，从而连接度量几何中的临界直径与拓扑数据分析中的复形稳定性。

Method: 引入直径函数的弱斜率（weak slope）驻点概念，证明[r,s)内无此类临界直径则包含映射为同伦等价。在黎曼流形上建立弱斜率驻点与Clarke临界性、一阶驻点（无同时减小所有实现直径的方向）之间的关系，并利用Clarke临界直径谱分析其Hausdorff维数与基数。在单位球面S^m上，用非负平衡应力刻画正的非对径弱斜率驻点构型，并通过改编自Lovász的球面堆叠构造进行提升。

Result: 闭连通光滑流形上Clarke临界直径谱Hausdorff维数为零（即使在割迹处），实解析情形下固定标签数时有限、总标签数时可数。S^m上最小正临界直径为arccos(-1/(m+1))，首个聚点为arccos(-1/m)。堆叠构造产生从下方逼近原值的驻点直径。另给出一般度量空间中弱斜率驻点直径不必然引起Vietoris-Rips复形同伦型改变的例子。

Conclusion: 临界直径（弱斜率驻点）是控制Vietoris-Rips复形同伦型变化的关键量；在光滑流形上其谱是稀疏的（维数为零），但在一般度量空间中临界性未必对应拓扑变化，说明该对应依赖于流形结构。

Abstract: We relate critical diameters of finite configurations to the topology of Vietoris-Rips complexes. For a compact metric space and $0<r<s$, we prove that the canonical inclusion from scale $r$ to scale $s$ is a homotopy equivalence whenever $[r,s)$ contains no diameter of a finite labelled configuration at which the diameter function has zero weak slope.
  On Riemannian manifolds, weak-slope stationarity implies Clarke criticality. At positive diameter, when the distances realizing the diameter are smooth, both are equivalent to first-order stationarity: the absence of a direction decreasing all these distances to first order. For closed connected smooth manifolds, the Clarke critical diameter spectrum has Hausdorff dimension zero, even at the cut locus. In the real-analytic case, it is finite at each fixed number of labels and countable over all label numbers.
  On the unit round sphere $S^m$, $m\geq1$, positive nonantipodal weak-slope stationary configurations are characterized by nonzero nonnegative equilibrium stresses. The least positive critical diameter is $\arccos(-1/(m+1))$, and the first accumulation point is $\arccos(-1/m)$. We lift every such stress through a spherical stack construction adapted from Lovász, producing stationary diameters that approach the original value from below in the next dimension as the number of layers increases. We also give example in general metric space showing that weak-slope stationary diameters need not correspond to changes in the homotopy type of Vietoris-Rips complexes.

</details>


### [71] [Morse-Bott inequalities on Lefschetz complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33329)
*Yuto Nishikawa*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为带实值关联函数、每维有限胞元的Lefschetz复形发展了离散Morse-Bott理论，提出基于四种基本操作（Moves）的归约程序计算Morse-Bott不等式中的剩余级数$R_t$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散Morse理论已有丰富成果，但对Lefschetz复形（带实值关联函数）的Morse-Bott推广尚不完善，尤其是Morse-Bott不等式及剩余级数$R_t$系数的非负性缺乏构造性证明。

Method: 定义四种基本操作（Moves），它们保持剩余级数$R_t$不变；在每个维度上有限次递归应用这些Moves，将复形分解为若干两胞元基本块的不交并，这些块的Poincaré级数为零。

Result: 归约分解通过统计相应维度上的块数得到$R_t$的系数，并通过统计作为孤立胞元被移除的胞数得到原复形的Betti数；由此证明$R_t$系数的非负性及Lefschetz复形的Morse-Bott不等式。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于CW复形，其中非负性通过将$R_t$的每个系数等同于相应维度基本块的数目而得到。

Abstract: We develop a discrete Morse-Bott theory for Lefschetz complexes with real-valued incidence functions and finitely many cells in each dimension. Our main result is a reduction procedure, based on four elementary operations called Moves, for computing the remainder series $R_t$ associated with the Morse-Bott inequality. These Moves preserve $R_t$, and the procedure recursively constructs, using finitely many Moves in each dimension, a disjoint union of two-cell elementary blocks with vanishing Poincaré series. The resulting decomposition computes the coefficients of $R_t$ by counting the blocks in the corresponding dimensions. The same procedure also computes the Betti numbers of the original complex by counting the cells removed as isolated cells. As consequences, we obtain the nonnegativity of the coefficients of $R_t$ and the Morse-Bott inequality for Lefschetz complexes. The method applies in particular to CW complexes, where the nonnegativity is obtained by identifying each coefficient of $R_t$ with the number of elementary blocks in the corresponding dimension.

</details>


### [72] [A study of $n$-valued non-split maps and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34013)
*Daciberg Lima Gonçalves,Bartira Maués,Daniel Vendrúscolo*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文利用覆叠空间与甲板变换群作用研究n值映射的分解结构，构建中间覆叠空间与构型空间的塔式结构，并应用于Borsuk-Ulam性质与Nielsen数计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n值映射（split map）可分解为n个单值映射的复合，但非分裂n值映射的拓扑性质（如Borsuk-Ulam性质、Nielsen数）缺乏系统性工具。作者希望借助覆叠空间与群作用，建立统一的塔式分解框架，从而将n值映射问题化归为单值映射问题。

Conclusion: 通过覆叠空间与甲板变换群作用构造的塔式结构，为n值映射提供了有效的分解与提升工具，可将Borsuk-Ulam性质与Nielsen数问题化归为单值映射的重合问题，并给出非分裂情形的精确Nielsen数公式。

Abstract: For a given $n$-valued map, there exists a suitable minimal covering of the domain such that the composition of this covering with the map yields a split map, composed of $n$ single-valued maps, called the lift factors. Using the group action of the deck-transformation group we construct intermediate covering spaces as well as intermediate configuration spaces, allowing the original map to lift to these spaces, building a tower to illustrate at which level the lift factors split off. We then display two applications of these new tools. The first is to derive results concerning the Borsuk-Ulam property for certain coverings of the tower. The second is to obtain a sharp formula for the Nielsen number of an $n$-valued non-split map, using the coincidence number of specific single-valued maps that arise in the lifts of the tower.

</details>

Abstract: We extend Sullivan's rational homotopy theory by constructing geometrically and algebraically minimal models for diagrams of simply connected spaces over a class of finite indexing categories. Both classes classify rational diagram homotopy types, but their minimality conditions can differ. We also show that, in contrast to classical rational homotopy theory, H-diagrams need not split into products of Eilenberg--MacLane diagrams, and characterize the indexing categories for which every H-diagram admits such a splitting. Main category: math.AT

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般有向空间中，遗忘路径参数化会改变其同伦型，这阻碍了有向同伦论中轨迹（trace）与路径（path）之间建立良好范畴关系。作者希望通过引入"时钟"（clock）与"计时空间"（timed space）结构，将参数化信息固定下来，从而在保持有向同伦型的前提下研究路径到轨迹的商映射的纤维化性质，并统一立方（cubical）与球状（globular）两类框架。

Result: 固定时钟上的计时空间类为局部可表现范畴；固定端点的有向路径到轨迹商映射是带连续截面的平凡Hurewicz纤维化，在Hausdorff饱和有向空间（拟紧子空间可度量化，含第二可数情形）下为平凡q-纤维化。有向圆是立方时钟，但存在有限胞腔球状例子无到它的正则映射。所构造的球状时钟通过单射有向映射包含立方时钟，接纳所有q-余纤维球状实现的正则映射，因此其上计时空间同时涵盖立方与球状例子。规范化给出q-余纤维多尖d-空间执行路径商定理的新证明，并证明非降满射的商是带连续截面的平凡Hurewicz纤维化。

Conclusion: 通过时钟与计时空间的框架，论文在保持有向同伦型参数化的前提下统一处理了立方与球状结构，建立了路径到轨迹商映射的纤维化理论，并借助球状时钟的构造与规范化方法，重新证明了执行路径商定理及非降满射商的平凡Hurewicz纤维化性质。该框架为使有向同伦论中参数化路径与轨迹的商运算具备良好范畴与纤维化性质提供了统一基础。

Abstract: For a general directed space, forgetting the parametrization of paths can change their homotopy type. A clock is a directed space with submetrizable underlying space; a timed space is a saturated directed space equipped with a regular directed map to a clock. Over every fixed clock, timed spaces form a locally presentable category, and their quotients from directed paths with fixed endpoints to traces are trivial Hurewicz fibrations with continuous sections. For Hausdorff saturated directed spaces with metrizable quasicompact subspaces, these quotients are trivial q-fibrations; this includes the second countable case. The directed circle is the cubical clock, but a finite cellular globular example admits no regular map to it. We construct a globular clock from a cellular q-cofibrant replacement of the terminal multipointed $d$-space. It contains the cubical clock by an injective directed map and admits regular maps from all q-cofibrant globular realizations, which are themselves saturated clocks. Thus timed spaces over this clock include both cubical and globular examples. Normalization gives a new proof of the quotient theorem for execution paths of q-cofibrant multipointed $d$-spaces and proves that the quotient by nondecreasing surjections is a trivial Hurewicz fibration with a continuous section.

</details>

### [77] [A construction of polyptych lattices via a pair of Gorenstein PL cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32549)

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在多重格（polyptych lattice）理论框架下，提出了 Gorenstein PL 锥及其 Gorenstein PL 锥扩张的构造，给出严格对偶对 (E, F) 的具体实例，并联系到 Gorenstein-Fano 多面体与簇数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重格理论旨在将推广环面几何的诸多理论（如 Newton-Okounkov 体、环面退化、簇簇等）中出现的分段线性双射（突变）统一到一个组合框架中。本文的动机是为该理论提供具体可构造的严格对偶对实例，从而为去热带化及其紧化提供更丰富的来源。

Method: 引入 Gorenstein PL 锥的概念（经典 Gorenstein 锥在多重格情形下的类比），并据此构造 Gorenstein PL 锥扩张，从而显式给出严格对偶对 (E, F) 的多重格。若原多重格可去热带化，则通过一个简单显式公式保证新多重格 E、F 也可去热带化。

Result: 给出严格对偶对 (E, F) 的具体构造，即 Gorenstein PL 锥扩张；证明若原多重格可去热带化则 E、F 亦可去热带化；由任意一对维数分别为 n 与 n' 的 Gorenstein-Fano 多面体 Δ、Δ' 可得到秩为 n+n'+1 的可去热带化多重格严格对偶对 (E(Δ,Δ'), F(Δ,Δ'))。

Conclusion: Gorenstein PL 锥扩张为严格对偶多重格提供了丰富实例来源，可与 Gorenstein-Fano 多面体及簇数据等经典对象建立联系，推进多重格理论在推广环面几何诸理论中的统一应用。

Abstract: The theory of polyptych lattices seeks to incorporate, into a single combinatorial framework, the piecewise-linear bijections (mutations) that appear in many theories that generalize toric geometry, such as Newton-Okounkov bodies, toric degenerations, cluster varieties, and other areas. The link to algebraic geometry comes from the accompanying notion of detropicalizations of polyptych lattices, and their associated compactifications. The main result of this manuscript gives a concrete construction of a strict dual pair $(\mathcal{E}, \mathcal{F})$ of polyptych lattices, which we call a Gorenstein PL cone extension. To define our construction, we introduce the notion of Gorenstein PL cones, which are a polyptych analogue of a Gorenstein cone in the classical setting. If the original polyptych lattices are detropicalizable, then the new polyptych lattices $\mathcal{E},\mathcal{F}$ are also detropicalizable, via a simple explicit formula. Our Gorenstein PL cone extensions give a rich source of examples of strict dual pairs of polyptych lattices. In particular, any pair of Gorenstein-Fano polytopes $Δ,Δ'$ of dimension $n,n'$ respectively, gives rise to a strict dual pair $(\mathcal{E}(Δ,Δ'), \mathcal{F}(Δ,Δ'))$ of detropicalizable polyptych lattices of rank $n+n'+1$. Other examples can be built from cluster data.

</details>

Weibel and the author in 1985, we construct a Chern class map $c_d:K_0(X)\to \text{CH}^d(X, X^*)$, a cycle class map from $\text{CH}^d(X, X^*)$ onto a subgroup $F^dK_0(X)$ of $K_0(X)$, and prove a Riemann-Roch theorem computing the two compositions of these two maps.

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the geometry of the Gieseker moduli space of semistable rank $2$ torsion-free sheaves on $\mathbb{P}^3$ with Chern classes $(c_1, c_2, c_3) = (-1, c_2, c_2^2-2)$ for $c_2 \geq 2$. We use the modular Serre correspondence to relate these moduli spaces to spaces of pairs and to suitable Hilbert schemes of one-dimensional subschemes in $\mathbb{P}^3$. For $c_2 \geq 4$, we prove that the moduli space is irreducible of dimension $c_2^2+3c_2+5$. For the case $c_2 = 3$, relying on a known geometric description of a relevant Hilbert scheme, we show that the moduli space $\mathcal{M}(-1,3,7)$ consists of exactly two irreducible components, namely the generic component of reflexive sheaves and a $T$-component, and we prove that their intersection is nonempty.

</details>

*Adrian Langer*

TL;DR: 该论文在noetherian环（含mixed characteristic）上的quasi-projective概形中研究Viehweg弱正性（weak positivity），证明弱正性在张量积、对称幂、除幂、外幂下保持；引入弱平坦丛（weakly flat bundles）推广数值平坦丛，构造S-基本群概形（S-fundamental group scheme），并与强数值平坦性比较，建立Demailly-Peternell-Schneider定理的类似结果。

Method: 在quasi-projective概形上定义并研究向量丛的弱正性；证明其在张量积、对称幂、除幂、外幂等运算下的保持性；引入弱平坦丛概念以推广数值平坦丛，并据此构造正规簇的S-基本群概形（需small projective compactification）；将弱平坦性与强数值平坦性进行比较，建立Demailly-Peternell-Schneider型定理；对光滑复簇（具small compactification）分类弱平坦丛中的半单对象为unitary flat丛；刻画带可积代数联络与不变 filtration（商为unitary flat）的向量丛的弱平坦性。

Result: 1) 弱正性在tensor products、symmetric powers、divided powers、exterior powers下保持；2) 引入weakly flat bundles并构造S-fundamental group scheme；3) 比较weak flatness与strong numerical flatness，建立Demailly-Peternell-Schneider定理的类似版本；4) 光滑复簇上弱平坦丛的半单对象恰为unitary flat bundles；5) 带可积代数联络及以unitary flat为商的invariant filtration的向量丛是weakly flat；6) 应用：用标准向量丛的弱正性刻画Abel簇的商。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了mixed characteristic下Viehweg弱正性理论，引入弱平坦丛与S-基本群概形，统一并推广了数值平坦性、unitary flatness等概念，给出Demailly-Peternell-Schneider定理的类比，并以Abel簇商的刻画作为应用，显示出弱正性/弱平坦性是研究基本群与正性几何的有力工具。

Abstract: We study Viehweg's weak positivity for vector bundles on quasi-projective schemes over noetherian rings, including mixed characteristic. We prove that weak positivity is preserved under tensor products, symmetric powers, divided powers, and exterior powers. We introduce weakly flat bundles, generalizing numerically flat bundles, and we use them to construct an S-fundamental group scheme for normal varieties admitting a small projective compactification. We compare weak flatness with strong numerical flatness and establish analogues of the Demailly-Peternell-Schneider theorem. For smooth complex varieties admitting a small compactification, we prove that the semisimple objects in the category of weakly flat bundles are precisely the unitary flat bundles. We also show that a vector bundle equipped with an integrable algebraic connection and an invariant filtration with unitary flat quotients is weakly flat. As applications, we characterize quotients of abelian varieties in terms of weak positivity of some standard vector bundles.

</details>

Abstract: Let $\cX$ be a curve of genus $g\ge2$ and zero $p$-rank over an algebraically closed field of odd characteristic $p$. We classify the pairs $(\cX,G)$ for which $G\le\Aut(\cX)$ has no common fixed point and $|G|>24g(g-1)$. For $g\ge4$, the curves are explicit cyclic covers of the projective line, the Hermitian curve, or the Ree curve. We determine their full automorphism groups and the possible subgroups $G$, including the central extensions. The exceptional $A_7$ action in characteristic $5$ determines the Hermitian curve of degree $6$. The cases of genus $2$ and $3$ are treated separately. These results give an odd-characteristic counterpart to the classification of large automorphism groups of zero $2$-rank curves. Abstract: We prove Landau-Ginzburg mirror symmetry conjecture for ADE singularities with the nontrivial symmetry groups. In particular, we consider $(f,G)$ with $f \in \mathbb{C}[z_1,z_2,z_3]$ defining ADE singularity and $G$ --- nontrivial group of diagonal symmetries of $f$. For a connected reductive algebraic group $H$ over $\mathbb{C}$, we classify the isomorphism classes of framed toric principal $H$-bundles over $\mathscr{X}(\boldsymbolΣ)$ in terms of piecewise linear maps from $|Σ|$ to the cone over the Tits building of $H$. Using this classification, we describe the equivariant automorphism group of a toric principal $H$-bundle, and give a necessary and sufficient condition for an equivariant reduction of the structure group. We also show that every toric principal $\mathrm{GL}(r)$-bundle over the weighted projective stack $\mathbb{P}(w_0,\dots,w_n)$ splits equivariantly whenever $r < n$, and that every toric principal $H$-bundle over the weighted stacky projective line $\mathbb{P}(a,b)$ splits equivariantly when $H$ is a connected reductive algebraic group.

</details>

Method: 将每个n≥4的低次系数块延拓为多项式坐标系统，构造几何度为n(n-2)的三维Keller映射；计算原映射和各次坐标下降的一般单值群（奇数n为循环圈积，偶数n为指数2显式子群）；构造光滑有限正规化，分析其在源仿射空间中的补集为n-2个不相交仿射除子；计算正规化的Picard群与同伦型；通过基变换描述任意子variety的原像，并分析目标多项式可分解时含常数unit的分量（与简单根Kummer覆盖的开子集相关）。

Result: 单值群：奇数n为循环圈积，偶数n为指数2显式子群，阶为(n-2)^n n!/gcd(2,n-2)。正规化：光滑有限，补集为n-2个不相交仿射除子，其中一个分歧，其余记录逆层未分歧损失；Picard群为Z^{n-2}，同伦型为n-2个二维球的楔积。有理deck群循环，而源映射的多项式deck群平凡。基变换可描述任意目标子variety的原像；目标多项式可分解时，仅含常数unit的分量是某因子简单根Kummer覆盖的开子集。单值群可将n≥4的映射与稳定多项式左右等价下的单加权提升区分开。

Abstract: We study the dimension-indexed marked-root polynomial maps introduced by Harish. For every $n\geq 4$, their lower coefficient block extends to a polynomial coordinate system, giving three-dimensional Keller maps of geometric degree $n(n-2)$ for every choice of the fixed coefficients. We determine the generic monodromy of the original maps and all successive coordinate descents: it is the cyclic wreath product for odd $n$, and an explicit subgroup of index two for even $n$. Its order is $(n-2)^n n!/\gcd(2,n-2)$. We construct the smooth finite normalization and identify its complement of the affine source as $n-2$ disjoint affine divisors. One is ramified; the others record unramified loss of inverse sheets. The normalization has Picard group $\mathbb{Z}^{n-2}$ and the homotopy type of a wedge of $n-2$ two-spheres. Its rational deck group is cyclic, while the polynomial deck group of the source map is trivial. By base change, the normalization describes the preimage of any subvariety of the target; when the target polynomial factors, a component with only constant units is an open subset of a Kummer cover of the simple roots of one factor. Finally, the monodromy separates these maps, for $n\geq 4$, from single weighted lifts under stable polynomial left-right equivalence.

</details>

Abstract: As a non-abelian analogue of the Hodge locus, Simpson introduced the non-abelian Noether--Lefschetz locus and conjectured its algebraicity for $\mathbb Z$PVHS. In this paper, we study this question on the moduli space of curves $\mathcal M_g$. For a non-unitary representation \[ ρ:π_1(Σ_g)\longrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb R) \] which admits a $\mathbb R$PVHS of weight one, we prove that algebraicity of a positive-dimensional non-abelian Noether--Lefschetz component is equivalent to discreteness of $\operatorname{im}ρ$, and in this case the component is precisely a marked fixed-target orbifold Hurwitz component. As applications, we construct two explicit rational families by slit surgery: one with non-discrete monodromy and non-algebraic Noether--Lefschetz image, and another with discrete monodromy whose period map is nevertheless non-uniformizing. The latter gives an affirmative answer to a question of Baldi--Lam concerning $\mathbb Q$PVHS.

</details>

Abstract: For a line arrangement $\mathcal{A} \colon f=0$ in $\mathbb{P}^2$, let $ν(\mathcal{A})$ be the maximal dimension of a graded piece of the Jacobian module of $f$. It is known that $\mathcal{A}$ is free if and only if $ν(\mathcal{A})=0$, and hence this conjecture is a strengthening of Terao's conjecture for line arrangements. We show that the conjecture holds for arrangements of at most $13$ lines, except possibly for arrangements of exactly $13$ lines whose intersection points have maximal multiplicity $5$.

Abstract: A Belyĭ function on a smooth projective algebraic curve defined over a number field determines a bipartite graph called a dessin d'enfant.

In previous papers, we proved that every passport of the form $[n,n,n]$, $[n,b^{q},n]$, or $[b^{q},b^{q},n]$ of genus at least $2$ admits a dessin with trivial automorphism group. In this paper, we prove the analogous result for passports of the form $[a^{p},b^{q},n]$. Motivation: Diverio–Trapani 曾猜想不变射流微分束 $E_{k,m}T^*_X$ 当 $m\gg0$ 时丰沛，该猜想是双曲性/射流微分理论中的核心问题之一。需检验其在最小非平凡情形（低阶 $k$、$\mathbb P^4$ 中超曲面）是否成立。

Result: 对任意整数 $q\ge1$，$\mathbb P^4$ 中每张光滑超曲面 $X$ 上 $E_{3,5q}T^*_X$ 均不丰沛；该结论在 $k=3$ 时构成对 Diverio–Trapani 猜想“对 $m\gg0$，$E_{k,m}T^*_X$ 丰沛”的反例。障碍进一步推广至任意维完全交、4 阶射流微分及所有足够可除的权。

Conclusion: $E_{k,m}T^*_X$ 的丰沛性并非对所有 $m\gg0$ 成立，而是依赖 $m$；文中讨论了这种对 $m$ 的依赖性，表明 Diverio–Trapani 猜想需作修正。译者注：关于丰沛性随 $m$ 的变化规律，应由原文后续讨论给出。

Abstract: We show that on every smooth hypersurface $X\subset\mathbb P^4$ the Demailly--Semple bundle $E_{3,5q}T^*_X$ of invariant jet differentials of order $3$ and weighted degree $5q$ is not ample, for every integer $q\ge1$. This contradicts the Diverio--Trapani conjecture in the form ``$E_{k,m}T^*_X$ is ample for all $m\gg0$'' for hypersurfaces in $\mathbb P^4$ at $k=3$. We then extend the obstruction to complete intersections of any dimension, to jet differentials of order $4$, and to all sufficiently divisible weights, and we discuss how ampleness of $E_{k,m}T^*_X$ depends on $m$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变Euler示性数 χ_G(X,L) 的计算在一般情况下较为困难，尤其涉及商曲面奇异点、固定点与固定曲线时，缺乏统一且可操作的计算手段，因此需要一套结合双有理几何与留数理论的方法来处理。

Method: 对 G-线性化线丛 L 给出等变除子表示；采用"除子剥离"逐层使用除子正合列并按 G-轨道整体处理，将问题归约为结构层与低维等变贡献。在 X/G 的分辨上研究同型直像层自然扩张的Euler示性保持性及其诱导联络（一般非对数，通过 D_4 计算说明）。先爆破固定点再取商，将局部计算约化为对角作用与循环 Hirzebruch-Jung 奇异；给出 ADE 奇异点的显式修正类函数及一般对角作用（含准反射）的统一流程。对角情形下自然扩张为对数联络，其留数由 Hirzebruch-Jung 分辨上的单项赋值计算。结合留数、分支曲线项与除子剥离得到完整计算程序。

Result: 建立了同型直像层自然扩张保持Euler示性数但联络未必对数的结论并给出 D_4 反例；得到 ADE 奇点的显式修正类函数；证明对角情形自然扩张为对数联络并可由单项赋值计算留数；将局部类与全纯Lefschetz公式及 Lim-Rota 的 Kleinian 系数进行比较；在 P^2 上给出示例，包括含固定曲线的例子。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective surface over $\mathbb{C}$ with an effective action of a finite group $G$, and let $L$ be a $G$-linearized line bundle. We develop a residue-theoretic and birational approach to computing the equivariant Euler characteristic $χ_G(X,L)$. Every such $L$ admits an equivariant divisor presentation. Divisor peeling applies the divisor exact sequences one layer at a time, treating whole $G$-orbits of components together. It reduces the problem to the structure sheaf and lower-dimensional equivariant contributions.

Blowing up fixed points before taking the quotient reduces the local calculation to diagonal actions and cyclic Hirzebruch-Jung singularities. We obtain explicit correction class functions for the ADE singularities and a uniform procedure for general diagonal actions, including quasi-reflections. In the diagonal case, the natural extensions are logarithmic, and their residues are computed from monomial valuations on the Hirzebruch-Jung resolution. Combining these residue calculations with the branch-curve terms and divisor peeling gives a procedure for computing the equivariant Euler characteristic of every $G$-linearized line bundle. We compare the local classes with the holomorphic Lefschetz formula and the Kleinian coefficients of Lim and Rota, and give examples on $\mathbb{P}^2$, including one with fixed curves.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a two-parameter family of complex structures on the standard six-sphere, starting from a rational elliptic surface with singular fibers $III^*,I_1,I_1,I_1$. Abstract: We prove that any proper Shimura variety admitting a limpid integral model in the sense of Madapusi Pera becomes a perfectoid space at infinite level at $p$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qin提出猜想：约化生成级数应为混合权有界的拟模形式，但此前缺乏证明。该猜想联系Hilbert概型上tautological丛的相交理论、Nakajima算子代数与拟模形式，具有重要理论意义。

Result: 建立了Nakajima算子正规序乘积迹的Wick定理型公式；给出超迹的Z、P、T函数表达式；证明约化生成级数确为拟模形式且满足预测的混合权上界，从而验证Qin猜想；并提供计算一般约化生成级数的显式算法。

Conclusion: 论文成功证明了Qin猜想，揭示了Hilbert概型相交数的拟模性结构，并给出可计算的一般算法，为相关计数几何问题提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective complex surface with numerically trivial canonical class. With the help of GPT-5.6 Sol, we prove Qin's conjecture that the reduced generating series of intersection numbers of Chern characters of tautological bundles against the total Chern class of $X^{[n]}$ is a quasimodular form with the predicted mixed-weight bound. The main ingredient is a Wick's theorem-type formula for computing traces of normally ordered products of Nakajima operators on $\bigoplus_n H^*(X^{[n]})$. Following the argument of Li-Qin-Wang, the computation of the reduced generating series is reduced to computing the constant term of the supertrace of a product of certain operator-valued currents and the Carlsson-Okounkov operator. Our trace formula shows that this supertrace can be expressed in terms of two quasi-elliptic functions $\widehat Z$, $P$ and a quasimodular function $T$, whose constant terms are quasimodular forms by the theorem of Goujard-Moller. Finally, we give an explicit algorithm for computing the general reduced generating series.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Ree曲线R_{p,s}（p>3）的几何自同构群、分歧滤链、Castle性质及Jacobi簇的F_q-同源分解与Newton斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有射线类与大作用文献中出现该两层方程结构，且p=3时给出经典Ree曲线，但p>3情形下的几何与算术性质尚不明确，作者希望类比广义Suzuki曲线对其进行系统刻画。

Method: 对光滑射影曲线R_{p,s}: F_q(x,y,z)，y^q-y=x^{q_0}(x^q-x)，z^q-z=x^{2q_0}(x^q-x)（q=p q_0^2），计算Aut_{F_q}(R_{p,s})，确定R_{p,s}->P^1_x的完全分歧滤链，证明(R_{p,s},P_∞)为Castle，并利用Jacobi簇的同源分解与Newton斜率估计。

Result: p>3时Aut_{F_q}(R_{p,s})≅U⋉F_q^*，|U|=q^3；U为big action；经典Ree群在特征3中仅为P_∞的稳定子，呈现特征3二分性；Jac(R_{p,s})~Jac(Y_{p,s})×Jac(Z_{p,s})^q，第一因子超奇异且三条曲线p-rank为0；1/(pq_0+2)≤λ_min(Z_{p,s})≤1/3，故Z_{p,s}与R_{p,s}非超奇异；固定p>3、s→∞时|Aut|~2^{8/5}p^{-4/5}q^{8/5}。

Conclusion: 广义Ree曲线在p>3时的自同构群较经典Ree情形显著缩小，并在特征3处出现明显二分；其Jacobi簇具有明确的同源分解且非超奇异，自动群阶随s呈q^{8/5}渐近，表明该曲线族在几何与算术上兼具特殊结构与一般性。

Abstract: Let p be an odd prime, let q_0 := p^s with s >= 1, and put q := p q_0^2. We study the smooth projective curve R_{p,s} with function field F_q(x,y,z), y^q - y = x^{q_0}(x^q - x), z^q - z = x^{2q_0}(x^q - x). The two-equation layer is already present in the ray-class and big-action literature; for p = 3 it gives the classical Ree curve. In analogy with the generalized Suzuki curve, we call R_{p,s} the generalized Ree curve associated with p and s. For p > 3 our principal geometric result determines the full geometric automorphism group: Aut_{F_q}(R_{p,s}) is isomorphic to the semidirect product of U and F_q^*, with |U| = q^3. The natural p-group U gives a big action; we determine the complete ramification filtration of the elementary abelian cover R_{p,s} -> P^1_x and prove that (R_{p,s}, P_infinity) is Castle. In characteristic 3 the same subgroup is only the stabilizer of P_infinity in the full Ree group, so the automorphism structure exhibits a sharp characteristic-three dichotomy. On the arithmetic side, writing Y_{p,s} : y^q - y = x^{q_0}(x^q - x), Z_{p,s} : z^q - z = x^{2q_0}(x^q - x), we prove the F_q-isogeny Jac(R_{p,s}) ~ Jac(Y_{p,s}) x Jac(Z_{p,s})^q. The first factor is supersingular and all three curves have p-rank zero. For p > 3 the first Newton slope of Z_{p,s} satisfies 1/(pq_0 + 2) <= lambda_min(Z_{p,s}) <= 1/3, so Z_{p,s} and R_{p,s} are not supersingular. For fixed p > 3 and s -> infinity, the order of the full automorphism group is asymptotic to 2^(8/5) p^(-4/5) q^(8/5).

</details>


### [95] [The monodromy of Xiao's genus-two fibrations in degrees 3, 4 and 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34042)

Result: 得到 $d=3,4,5$ 时分别为 $7$、$13$、$31$ 条消失圈，并将其表示为标号参考纤维中的显式曲线。对于 $d=4,5$，这些结果给出亏格二映射类群中恒等元的新的正因子分解，类型分别为 $(6,7)$ 和 $(12,19)$。

Abstract: We determine the vanishing cycles of Xiao's genus-two fibrations $X_d\to P^1$, $d=3,4,5$, whose fibers admit degree-$d$ maps to a fixed elliptic curve $E$. We tile the base $P^1$ by triangles and realize the surface $X_d$ as a degree-$d$ branched cover of $P^1\times E$. The covering description gives an explicit algorithm for the vanishing cycles. A vanishing path is recorded by the sides it crosses in the tiling; each crossing changes a labelled picture of the fiber by a local hexagon move, and the vanishing cycle is read from the terminal picture. We obtain the $7$, $13$, and $31$ vanishing cycles as explicit curves in a marked reference fiber. For $d=4,5$, these give new positive factorizations of the identity in the genus-two mapping class group, of types $(6,7)$ and $(12,19)$.

</details>

Abstract: We prove that the pushforwards of Gauss--Manin connections on smooth rigid analytic varieties along Zariski-open immersions are coadmissible and holonomic over Ardakov--Wadsley's sheaf D-cap of infinite order differential operators. This may be viewed as a rigid analytic variant of the classical theorem of Griffiths, Deligne, and Katz that Gauss-Manin systems have regular singularities after compactification. The proof combines p-adic de Rham comparison and Diao--Lan--Liu--Zhu's logarithmic Riemann--Hilbert correspondence with extendability results for log-connections with nilpotent residues. In the algebraic snc case, we also establish weak holonomicity via a p-adic Bernstein-Sato criterion of Bitoun and the first author.

</details>
