Abstract: We study the wall crossing for the moduli space of unmarked cubic surfaces at the log Calabi-Yau threshold. We prove the existence of a good moduli space for the corresponding moduli stack of boundary polarized Calabi-Yau pairs with Gorenstein underlying surfaces and describe the wall crossing morphisms explicitly. More precisely, the morphism from the K-moduli space to the log Calabi-Yau moduli space is an isomorphism, whereas the morphism from the KSBA moduli space contracts a divisor isomorphic to $\mathbb{P}^3$ to a point and is an isomorphism away from this divisor.

</details>


### [98] [Higher dimensional local class field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34437)
*Morten Lüders*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文综述高维局部类域论，即局部域上光滑射影概形的类域论，给出ℓ部分的新证明并提出关于p系数的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典局部类域论已较完善，而高维情形（局部域上光滑射影概形）的结果散见于Jannsen--Saito、Forré等人工作中，缺乏统一且具启发性的阐述。作者希望梳理经典与高维理论的类比，并厘清单位滤链与分歧（即p部分与ℓ部分）在高维框架下的解释。

Method: 文章以综述为主：先概述经典局部类域论的主要结果，再给出高维情形的主要定义与陈述；针对ℓ部分给出一个新且简单的证明；并基于该证明提出一个猜想。

Result: 给出了ℓ部分（原属Jannsen--Saito与Forré）的新简单证明；提出一个可推出p系数剩余开放情形的猜想，从而加深对p部分的理解；阐明了经典与高维理论之间的类比关系。

Conclusion: 该文系统梳理了高维局部类域论，并通过新证明与猜想推进了对p部分的认识，强化了经典与高维理论在单位滤链和分歧方面的类比。

Abstract: In this largely expository article we give an introduction to higher dimensional local class field theory, more precisely class field theory for smooth projective schemes over local fields. We begin with a quick summary of some of the main results of classical local class field theory. Then we explain the main definitions and statements in the higher dimensional setting. We give a new and simple proof of the $\ell$-part, which is originally due to Jannsen--Saito and Forré. Inspired by this proof, we propose a conjecture which would imply the remaining open cases with $p$-coefficients and which improves our understanding of the $p$-part. A particular goal of the exposition is to outline analogies between the classical and the higher dimensional theory and on how the unit filtration and ramification, i.e. the $p$- and the $\ell$-part, can be interpreted in the higher dimensional setting.

</details>


### [99] [Equivariant multiplicities and torsion at attractive fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35600)
*Tao Gui,Peter L. Guo,Zhuowei Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在带环面作用、具有吸引不动点 x 的有理光滑复仿射簇 X 上，若 X\{x\} 是 p-光滑且其整等变上同调无 p-挠，则其总 p-主上同调挠的阶等于 x 处等变重数的约化分子的 p-部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究等变上同调中挠元的结构与计数，特别是验证 Juteau–Williamson 提出的挠阶猜想及其在 Schubert 簇正规切片上的局部版本。

Method: 假设 X 有理光滑、有环面作用与吸引不动点 x，且 X\{x\} 为 p-光滑、整等变上同调无 p-挠；利用 Fiebig–Williamson 的等变无挠性结果，将等变重数约化分子的 p-部分与总 p-主上同调挠的阶对应起来。

Result: 证明了总 p-主上同调挠的阶等于 x 处等变重数约化分子的 p-部分，从而解决了 Juteau–Williamson 的挠阶猜想及其关于 Schubert 簇正规切片的局部情形。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了等变重数的 p-部分与上同调挠阶之间的精确等式，验证并推广了挠阶猜想。

Abstract: Let \(X\) be a rationally smooth complex affine variety with a torus action and an attractive fixed point \(x\). Suppose that \(X\setminus\{x\}\) is \(p\)-smooth and that its integral equivariant cohomology has no \(p\)-torsion. We prove that the order of its total \(p\)-primary cohomology torsion is the \(p\)-part of the reduced numerator of the equivariant multiplicity at \(x\). In particular, this resolves the torsion-order conjecture of Juteau--Williamson and its local version for normal slices in Schubert varieties, using the equivariant torsion-freeness result of Fiebig--Williamson.

</details>


### [100] [Connectivity of the slice filtration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34535)
*Dipankar Maity*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文利用类Morel稳定连通性定理的方法，证明Tate截断函子$f_{0/n}$在任意域上保持$S^1$-谱的motivic连通性；对复形得到$L^{p,n}$与$L_{bir}^n$局部化的Hurewicz定理；在完美域上对具有$\mathcal{C}$-转移的motivic谱建立更强连通性结论，并借助motivic重构定理推出$f_n$、$s_n$、$f_{0/n}$保持有效（进而$\mathbb{P}^1$-）motivic谱的连通性，同时给出切片版的重构定理与$S^1$/$ \mathbb{P}^1$-识别定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivic连通性（Morel稳定连通性定理）刻画了谱的连通层次，是motivic同伦论的核心工具。Tate截断函子$f_{0/n}$及其相关切片函子是否保持连通性，直接关系到截断、切片与局部化运算的相容性，并影响Hurewicz型定理与重构定理的适用范围，因此作者在一般域与完美域上系统研究该问题。

Method: 模仿Morel稳定连通性定理的证明策略；在任意域上处理Tate截断函子对$S^1$-谱的作用；对复形情形分析$L^{p,n}$-与$L_{bir}^n$-局部化；在完美域上引入带$\mathcal{C}$-转移的motivic谱范畴并假设$\mathcal{C}$满足cancellation；使用motivic重构定理将结论从带转移情形推广到有效及$\mathbb{P}^1$-motivic谱；同时建立切片版的motivic（有效）重构定理以及$S^1$-、$\mathbb{P}^1$-识别定理的切片对应。

Result: 证明Tate截断函子$f_{0/n}$在任意域上保持$S^1$-谱的motivic连通性；在复形情形得到$L^{p,n}$-与$L_{bir}^n$-局部化的Hurewicz定理（完美域）；在完美域上证明若$\mathcal{C}$满足cancellation，则$f_n^\mathcal{C}$保持带$\mathcal{C}$-转移motivic谱的连通性；进一步推出$f_n$、$s_n$、$f_{0/n}$保持有效motivic谱及$\mathbb{P}^1$-motivic谱的连通性；并建立切片版motivic（有效）重构定理与切片版motivic $S^1$-、$\mathbb{P}^1$-识别定理。

Conclusion: Tate截断与切片相关函子在较一般域上具有良好的连通性保持性质，该结论可推广至带转移、有效及$\mathbb{P}^1$-motivic谱框架，并伴随若干重构与识别定理，为motivic同伦论中的连通性、局部化与切片理论提供统一处理。

Abstract: Using methods similar to Morel's stable connectivity theorem, we prove that, over an arbitrary field, the Tate truncation functors $f_{0/n}$ preserve motivic connectivity of $ S^1$-spectra. An analogous result for complexes yields a Hurewicz theorem for the $L^{p,n}$- and $L_{bir}^n$-localizations over perfect fields. Over such fields, we establish a stronger connectivity property for categories of correspondences, showing that $f_n^\mathcal{C}$ preserves connectivity of motivic spectra with $\mathcal{C}$-transfers, whenever $\mathcal{C}$ satisfies cancellation. We then use the motivic reconstruction theorem to deduce that $f_n$, and consequently $s_n$ and $f_{0/n}$, also preserve connectivity for effective (and thereby, $\mathbb{P}^1$-) motivic spectra. Along the way, we also establish the slice analog of the motivic (effective) reconstruction theorem, as well as the slice analog of motivic $S^1$- and $\mathbb{P}^1$-recognition theorems.

</details>


### [101] [Large cyclic automorphism groups and cyclic subgroups of index two in characteristic two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34709)
*Marco Timpanella*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在特征2的代数闭域上对亏格 g≥2 的射影、几何不可约、非奇异代数曲线的自同构群进行分类：分类了阶 N≥2g+1 的循环子群，并进一步分类了含指数2循环子群且 |H|>4g+4 的群 H；同时给出二面体群的尖锐上界及其达到情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究特征2（正特征）代数曲线自同构群的阶与结构的限制，特别是大阶循环子群及含指数2循环子群的群的分类，以完善正特征下自同构群理论，并确定二面体群作用下的尖锐上界与极值曲线。

Method: 设 X 为特征2代数闭域上亏格 g≥2 的射影几何不可约非奇异曲线。先分类 Aut(X) 中阶 N≥2g+1 的循环子群，通过 Artin–Schreier 方程与除子/分支点分析归约为 Kummer 扩张及两类方程；再由此分类含指数2循环子群且 |H|>4g+4 的群 H，并借助方程 y^k=x^M+x^{-M} 与亏格公式 2g=M(k-1) 刻画其结构与阶，同时讨论 Hermitian 曲线情形及有限域上极大性条件；最后对二面体群用 Artin–Schreier 方程确定等号情形。

Result: 阶 N≥2g+1 的循环子群除奇数次、三点分支的 Kummer 扩张外，仅两类：y^2+y=x^m，N=2m=4g+2；以及 y^2+y=c/(x^m+1)（m 奇，c≠0），N=2m=2g+2；特别地 4∤N。含指数2循环子群且 |H|>4g+4 的群恰为 C_k×D_{2M}，作用于曲线 y^k=x^M+x^{-M}（k,M≥3 奇且互素，2g=M(k-1)），其阶为 4g+2M，其中 M 取 g 的某些奇因子，且 |H|≤6g；该族曲线均非 ordinary，包含 Hermitian 曲线，并给出有限域上极大性的两个充分条件。二面体群的尖锐上界为偶亏格 4g+4、奇亏格 4g，等号情形由显式 Artin–Schreier 方程给出，且此时二面体群即全自同构群。

Conclusion: 在特征2下，大阶循环子群与含指数2循环子群的群结构被完全刻画，阶受到强限制（如 4∤N、|H|≤6g），且二面体群作用达到尖锐上界并给出极值曲线的显式方程与全自同构群结论，同时 Hermitian 曲线作为特例出现并提供有限域极大性判据。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{X}$ be a projective, geometrically irreducible, nonsingular algebraic curve of genus $g\ge2$ over an algebraically closed field of characteristic two. We classify cyclic subgroups of $\Aut(\mathcal{X})$ of order $N\ge2g+1$. Besides Kummer extensions of odd degree ramified over three points, precisely two cases occur: $y^2+y=x^m$, with $N=2m=4g+2$, and $y^2+y=c/(x^m+1)$, with $m$ odd, $c\ne0$, and $N=2m=2g+2$. In particular, $4\nmid N$. We then classify groups $H$ with a cyclic subgroup of index two and $|H|>4g+4$. They are precisely the groups $C_k\times D_{2M}$ on the curves $y^k=x^M+x^{-M}$, where $k,M\ge3$ are odd and coprime, and $2g=M(k-1)$. Their possible orders are $4g+2M$, where $M$ runs over certain odd divisors of $g$, and $|H|\le6g$. No curve in this family is ordinary. Hermitian curves occur in the family, and we give two sufficient conditions for maximality over finite fields. For dihedral groups the sharp bound is $4g+4$ in even genus and $4g$ in odd genus. The equality cases are given by explicit Artin--Schreier equations, and in each case the dihedral group is the full automorphism group.

</details>


### [102] [On the De Rham Cohomology of Zero-Dimensional Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34819)
*Martin Kreuzer,Le Ngoc Long*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究零维概型的de Rham上同调，重点讨论仿射情形。对于$R=P/I$，基于$dI\wedge\Omega^m_{P/K}$的Macaulay基形状证明了$H^m_{\rm dR}(R)$的消没定理；对Artinian局部代数$A=P/\langle f_1,\dots,f_n\rangle$（超正则序列）分析了$H^\bullet_{\rm dR}(A)$的非平凡性与标准基条件下的平凡性，并详研$\ker(d_A)$的维数。利用Galois分裂将任意仿射零维概型归约至此情形，得到fat point概型的de Rham上同调，并给出大量算例与反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零维概型的（朴素）de Rham上同调定义为其坐标环Kähler微分代数作为复形的同调；当环拟齐次时高次上同调消失，因此需聚焦一般仿射情形，厘清其结构并探究何时消没或非消没。

Method: 对$R=P/I$（${\rm char}(K)=0$）通过$dI\wedge\Omega^m_{P/K}$的Macaulay基的形状刻画$H^m_{\rm dR}(R)$的消没；对超正则序列定义的Artinian局部代数，研究$\Omega^m_{A/K}$关系模自然生成元系是否为标准基；细致分析$H^0_{\rm dR}(A)=\ker(d_A)$的维数；并用Galois分裂将任意仿射零维概型归约到Artinian局部环情形。

Result: 证明了基于Macaulay基形状的$H^m_{\rm dR}(R)$消没定理；对超正则序列的Artinian局部代数，$H^\bullet_{\rm dR}(A)$一般非平凡，但当关系模的自然生成元系为标准基时平凡；获得$\ker(d_A)$维数的详细刻画；通过Galois分裂与归约得到fat point概型的de Rham上同调，并提供大量显式算例与反例。

Conclusion: 零维概型的de Rham上同调在一般情况下高度微妙，其消没或非消没取决于理想生成元结构（如Macaulay基、标准基）与关系模的性质；本文为仿射零维情形（含fat point）提供了系统的判定与计算方法。

Abstract: The (naive) de Rham cohomology of a zero-dimensional scheme is the homology of the Kähler differential algebra of its coordinate ring, viewed as a complex. Since it is well-known to vanish in higher degrees if the ring is quasi-homogeneous, we concentrate on the affine case. For a general affine $K$-algebra $R=P/I$, where ${\rm char}(K)=0$ and $P=K[x_1,\dots,x_n]$, we prove a vanishing theorem for $H_{\rm dR}^m(R)$ based on the shape of a Macaulay basis of $dI\wedge Ω^m_{P/K}$. For an Artinian local algebra $A=P/\langle f_1,\dots,f_n\rangle$, where $\{f_1,\dots,f_n\}$ is a super regular sequence, we show that $H_{\rm dR}^{\bullet}(A)$ is non-trivial in general, but trivial when the natural system of generators of the relation module of $Ω^m_{A/K}$ is a standard basis. Moreover, we provide a detailed study of the dimension of $H_{\rm dR}^0(A)=\ker(d_A)$ for Artinian local rings $A=P/I$. The case of arbitrary affine zero-dimensional schemes is reduced to this case using a Galois splitting, and as a result we obtain the de Rham cohomology of a fat point scheme. Many explicitly computed examples and counterexamples support the results and indicate how subtle the de Rham cohomology of a zero-dimensional scheme is in general.

</details>


### [103] [On deformations of foliated complex analytic structures I: Theorems of existence and completeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34830)
*Chunghoon Kim*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在Kodaira-Spencer形变理论基础上研究由切丛与余切丛的局部自由子层定义的叶状复解析结构的形变，并证明了相应的存在性与完备性定理，包括同时形变情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kodaira-Spencer关于复解析结构形变的存在性与完备性定理推广到叶状复解析结构，处理由切丛与余切丛局部自由子层所定义的叶状结构及其同时形变问题。

Method: 以Kodaira-Spencer形变理论为基础，分别对由切丛局部自由子层和余切丛局部自由子层定义的叶状复解析结构建立形变理论，并进一步研究当紧复流形上的奇异全纯叶状结构同时由切丛与余切丛局部自由子层定义时的同时形变。

Result: 证明了三类存在性与完备性定理：由切丛局部自由子层定义的叶状复解析结构形变；由余切丛局部自由子层定义的叶状复解析结构形变；以及二者同时定义时的同时形变。

Conclusion: 叶状复解析结构的形变理论可作为Kodaira-Spencer经典理论的类比推广，且在切丛与余切丛局部自由子层两种刻画下均具有存在性与完备性，同时形变情形亦成立。

Abstract: We study deformations of foliated complex analytic structures defined by locally free subsheaves of tangent sheaves and locally free subsheaves of cotangent sheaves on the basis of Kodaira-Spencer's deformation theory. We prove theorems of existence and completeness for deformations of foliated complex analytic structures defined by locally free subsheaves of tangent sheaves as analogues of theorems of existence and completeness for deformations of complex analytic structures by Kodaira-Spencer. We prove theorems of existence and completeness for deformations of foliated complex analytic structures defined by locally free subsheaves of cotangent sheaves. We also prove theorems of existence and completeness for simultaneous deformations when singular holomorphic foliations on compact complex manifolds are defined by locally free subsheaves of tangent and cotangent sheaves at the same time.

</details>


### [104] [Degeneration of Calabi-Yau threefolds with Galois action induced by modular form of weight 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35157)
*Marcin Oczko*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究一类含两个K型退化的双八次Calabi-Yau三维簇族，构造半稳定模型，计算极限混合Hodge结构，并证明极限上同调的ℓ进Galois表示（半单化后）同构于某奇异K3曲面所对应的权3模形式Galois表示与其Tate扭的直接和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Calabi-Yau三维簇的K型退化与K3曲面之间预期关系的显式实现。

Method: 对一族单参数双八次Calabi-Yau三维簇的两个K型退化，构造半稳定模型并计算极限混合Hodge结构；分析极限上同调上的ℓ进Galois表示结构。

Result: 极限上同调的ℓ进Galois表示（模去半单化）同构于与某奇异K3曲面相关联的权3模形式的Galois表示及其Tate扭的直接和。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了Calabi-Yau三维簇K型退化与K3曲面之间预期关系的显式实现。

Abstract: We study a one-parameter family of double octic Calabi-Yau threefolds with two degenerations of type $K$. For each of these degenerations we construct a semistable model and compute the limiting mixed Hodge structure. We show that the $\ell$-adic Galois representation on the limiting cohomology is isomorphic (up to semisimplification) to the direct sum of the Galois representation attached to a weight-three modular form associated with a singular K3 surface and its Tate twist. This gives an explicit realization of the expected relation between type $K$ degenerations of Calabi-Yau threefolds and K3 surfaces.

</details>


### [105] [Artin vanishing along the $\ell$-adic tower of an abelian variety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35404)
*Ashutosh Roy Choudhury,K. V. Shuddhodan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明阿贝尔簇上constructible层的上同调在高次时消失（稳定化后为零），给出Fourier–Mellin变换支撑的余维数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Bhatt–Schnell–Scholze提出的问题：阿贝尔簇上层的上同调是否在超过支撑维数后消失；并改进Esnault–Kerz关于Fourier–Mellin变换的估计。

Method: 利用$\ell^n$-倍映射的拉回$[\ell^n]^*$，构造带指标$e$的零映射（pullback稳定化），结合constructible $\mathbb{F}_\ell$-层与perverse层的Fourier–Mellin变换分析。

Result: 对任意constructible $\mathbb{F}_\ell$-层$F$，存在$e$使$[\ell^e]^*:H^i(A,[\ell^n]^*F)\to H^i(A,[\ell^{n+e}]^*F)$对所有$n\ge0$、$i>\dim\mathrm{Supp}F$为零，故$\varinjlim_n H^i(A,[\ell^n]^*F)=0$；并给出Fourier–Mellin变换上同调层支撑的尖锐余维数估计。

Conclusion: 解决了Bhatt–Schnell–Scholze的问题；以$\ell$-adic系数去除了Esnault–Kerz的算术性假设，$\mathbb{F}_\ell$系数下completed transform的估计为新结果且不依赖Hard Lefschetz。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an abelian variety of dimension $g$ over an algebraically closed field and let $\ell$ be a prime invertible in the field. For every constructible $\mathbb{F}_{\ell}$-sheaf $F$ on $A$ we prove that there is an integer $e$, depending on $F$, such that the pullback map $[\ell^{e}]^{*}\colon\mathrm{H}^{i}(A,[\ell^{n}]^{*}F)\to\mathrm{H}^{i}(A,[\ell^{n+e}]^{*}F)$ is zero for all $n\geq0$ and all $i>\dim\operatorname{Supp}F$. In particular $\varinjlim\limits_{n}\mathrm{H}^{i}(A,[\ell^{n}]^{*}F)=0$ for $i>\dim\operatorname{Supp}F$, which answers a question of Bhatt--Schnell--Scholze.
  Our methods also give sharp codimension estimates for the supports of the cohomology sheaves of the Fourier--Mellin transform of a perverse sheaf. With $\ell$-adic coefficients, we remove the arithmeticity hypothesis from the estimates of Esnault--Kerz. The corresponding estimates for the completed transform with $\mathbb{F}_{\ell}$-coefficients are new and hold even when Hard Lefschetz fails.

</details>


### [106] [Maximality and configuration spaces on real algebraic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35442)
*Aloïs Demory*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究实代数曲线 $C$ 上 $n$ 个互异无标号点构成的构型空间，其上的实结构由 $C$ 的实结构诱导。作者证明该构型空间达到 Smith-Thom 不等式中的极大值，当且仅当 $C$ 为极大曲线，或 $g(C)=0$ 且 $n$ 为偶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实代数簇的拓扑（尤其是实点集的拓扑型与其复化之间的比较）是实代数几何的核心问题。Smith-Thom 不等式给出了实点集同调维数之和的上界，达到该上界的"极大"簇具有重要几何意义。构型空间是曲线上自然且重要的空间，但其极大性判据此前并未完全刻画，故本文填补这一空白。

Method: 在实代数曲线 $C$（亏格 $g$，非奇异）上取 $n$ 个互异无标号点，得到构型空间，其复化对应于 $C_\mathbb{C}$ 上的构型空间。实结构由 $C$ 上的实结构（对合）诱导。通过分析该构型空间的实点集与复化空间的同调，利用 Smith-Thom 不等式，并结合曲线极大性（实点集在同调意义下达到最大，即 $g+1$ 个连通分支）与 $g=0$ 时的特殊情况，分别讨论奇偶 $n$ 的情形，导出极大性的充要条件。

Result: 构型空间是极大的（即满足 Smith-Thom 等号），当且仅当 $C$ 是极大实代数曲线，或者 $g(C)=0$ 且 $n$ 为偶数。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了实代数曲线上无标号构型空间极大性的充要条件，将曲线本身的极大性与亏格零的偶点情形对应起来。这说明除射影直线上的偶数点构型这一特例外，构型空间的极大性完全由底曲线 $C$ 的极大性决定。

Abstract: Let C be a non-singular real algebraic curve of genus g. The real structure on C induces a real structure on the configuration space of n distinct unlabeled points on C. We prove that this configuration space is maximal (in the sense of the Smith-Thom inequality) if and only if C is maximal or the genus of C is zero and n is even.

</details>


### [107] [Quasi-$F$-split primitive symplectic varieties in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35467)
*Haitao Zou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明好约化射影hyperkähler簇的准-$F$-分裂性等价于Frobenius分裂，等价于晶体上同调存在斜率零部分，其准-$F$-分裂高度为$1$或$\infty$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正特征下hyperkähler簇及primitive symplectic簇的Frobenius分裂性质与晶体上同调斜率的关系，并探讨Hodge形变保持primitive symplectic性质的问题。

Method: 结合Verbitsky斜率比较与Witt–Euler恒等式，无需晶体无挠假设。

Result: 证明准-$F$-分裂与Frobenius分裂等价，且等价于$\operatorname{H}^2_{\operatorname{crys}}(X/W)[1/p]$含斜率零部分；该二分性对特征$p$的primitive symplectic簇也成立；Hodge-良好性在光滑proper族中开；$K3$曲面Hilbert概型$S^{[n]}$（$p>n$）及广义Kummer簇$K_n(A)$（$p>n+1$）的Hodge形变保持primitive symplectic，具有无挠晶体上同调及无阻碍混合特征形式形变。

Conclusion: 给出了hyperkähler簇Frobenius分裂的斜率刻画及高度二分性，并将结果推广到primitive symplectic簇与Hodge形变族。

Abstract: Let $X$ be the good reduction of a projective hyperkähler variety of dimension $2n\geq4$. We prove that $X$ is quasi-$F$-split if and only if it is Frobenius split, equivalently if $\operatorname{H}^2_{\operatorname{crys}}(X/W)[1/p]$ has a slope-zero part. Thus its quasi-$F$-split height is $1$ or $\infty$. The proof combines a Verbitsky slope comparison with a Witt--Euler identity and requires no crystalline torsion-freeness. The same dichotomy holds for primitive symplectic varieties in characteristic $p$, and Hodge-goodness is open in smooth proper families. Hodge-deformations of Hilbert schemes $S^{[n]}$ of $K3$ surfaces ($p>n$) and generalised Kummer varieties $K_n(A)$ ($p>n+1$) remain primitive symplectic, with torsion-free crystalline cohomology and unobstructed mixed-characteristic formal deformations.

</details>


### [108] [Algebraic Dimension and Reduction of LVMB manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35605)
*Federico Thiella*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LVMB流形的代数维数，给出其上界并证明其是sharp的，结合Meersseman的下界，在很多情形下确定了精确的代数维数，并描述了代数约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVMB流形是一大类紧复非Kähler、非代数的流形，由代数-组合数据构造。其代数维数是重要的双亚纯不变量，但此前仅有Meersseman给出的下界，缺乏上界与精确值。本文旨在确定其代数维数并刻画代数约化。

Method: 通过代数-组合的方法，为LVMB流形的代数维数建立上界并证明其sharp性；将所得上界与Meersseman的下界比较；进一步描述LVMB流形的代数约化。

Result: 1) 给出了LVMB流形代数维数的sharp上界；2) 与Meersseman下界结合，在很多相关情形下得到精确代数维数；3) 刻画了代数约化，并发现Meersseman下界等于有理部分的维数；4) 用关联toric quasifold的非有理性度（nonrationality degree）计算该双亚纯不变量，并给出了Battaglia–Prato近期引入的这一不变量的显式公式。

Conclusion: LVMB流形的代数维数可由上界与下界夹逼在很多情形下精确确定，其代数约化的有理部分维数等于Meersseman下界，并可通过toric quasifold的非有理性度显式表达。

Abstract: LVMB manifolds are a large class of compact complex non-Kähler and non-algebraic manifolds, constructed from an algebro-combinatorial datum. We provide an upper bound on their algebraic dimension and prove it to be sharp. Comparing our result with the lower bound found by Meersseman, we obtain the exact algebraic dimension of an LVMB manifold in many relevant cases. Pursuing our analysis further, we also obtain a description of the algebraic reduction of an LVMB manifold. From this, it emerges that Meersseman's lower bound coincides with the dimension of the rational component. This bimeromorphic invariant of an LVMB manifold is then computed in terms of the nonrationality degree of the associated toric quasifold. This is an invariant recently introduced by Battaglia and Prato, for which we provide an explicit formula.

</details>


### [109] [Integral Bott--Chern cohomology of Generalized Calabi--Eckmann threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35625)
*Federico Thiella*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文计算了一类复三维广义 Calabi–Eckmann 流形的整 Bott–Chern 上同调，发现尽管这些流形彼此不双全纯，其整 Bott–Chern 上同调环却同构；随后通过一个连接奇异、Dolbeault 与整 Bott–Chern 上同调的特征映射获得上同调层面的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义 Calabi–Eckmann 流形是复几何中的重要非 Kähler 例子，其双全纯分类与上同调不变量之间的关系尚不完全清楚；作者希望考察整 Bott–Chern 上同调能否作为区分这类流形的有效不变量。

Method: 对复维数为 3 的一类广义 Calabi–Eckmann 流形直接计算其整 Bott–Chern 上同调环；并构造一个上同调特征映射，将奇异上同调、Dolbeault 上同调与整 Bott–Chern 上同调联系起来。

Result: 所研究的这族流形虽然彼此不双全纯，但其整 Bott–Chern 上同调环相互同构，即该不变量无法区分它们；借助特征映射，在奇异/Dolbeault 上同调层面得到了可区分这些流形的上同调判据。

Conclusion: 整 Bott–Chern 上同调环不足以区分这类复三维广义 Calabi–Eckmann 流形，需结合特征映射所联系的其他上同调理论才能实现区分。

Abstract: We compute the integral Bott--Chern cohomology of a class of generalized Calabi--Eckmann manifolds of complex dimension \(3\). Although these manifolds are mutually non-biholomorphic, we show that their integral Bott--Chern cohomology rings are nevertheless isomorphic. A cohomological distinction is then obtained via a characteristic map in cohomology, connecting singular, Dolbeault, and integral Bott--Chern cohomologies.

</details>


### [110] [Global log canonical thresholds for Burniat surfaces and some infinite families of surfaces of general type as $\mathbb{Z}_2^n$-covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35678)
*Nguyen Bin,Jheng-Jie Chen,YongJoo Shin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文完成了Burniat曲面全局对数典范阈值的计算，并确定了一类由P²或P¹×P¹的Z₂ⁿ覆叠构造的一般型曲面的全局对数典范阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局对数典范阈值是双有理几何中的重要不变量，与曲面的K稳定性等性质密切相关；Burniat曲面的该阈值此前尚未被完整计算，同时对其推广的无穷族曲面也缺乏系统结果。

Method: 通过代数几何方法，利用曲面作为P²或P¹×P¹的Z₂ⁿ覆叠的显式构造，结合对有效除子与典范类相关性质的分析来计算全局对数典范阈值。

Result: 完整给出了Burniat曲面的全局对数典范阈值，并确定了由Z₂ⁿ覆叠构造的若干无穷族一般型曲面的全局对数典范阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作补齐了Burniat曲面全局对数典范阈值的计算，并将结果推广到更广的覆叠曲面族。

Abstract: In this paper, we complete the computation of the global log canonical thresholds of Burniat surfaces. Additionally, we determine the global log canonical thresholds for certain infinite families of surfaces of general type, constructed as $ \mathbb{Z}_2^n $-covers of $\mathbb{P}^2$ or $\mathbb{P}^1 \times \mathbb{P}^1$.

</details>


### [111] [On orbits of the automorphism group of toroidal spherical varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35683)
*Alexander Chernov,Klim Rolgin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了环面球簇上自同构群的作用轨道。在仿射情形，证明了两个点位于单位连通分支的同一轨道当且仅当除数类群到其局部除数类群的同态核相等，并用B-稳定素除子描述了这些核；对光滑完备环面球簇，利用不含给定G-轨道的B-稳定素除子类生成的幺半群得到了类似判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画代数群自同构群在球簇上的轨道结构是球簇理论的核心问题之一，已有工作多关注代数群自身作用的轨道。本文转向自同构群作用，旨在给出轨道相等的可计算判据。

Method: 研究对象为环面球簇（toroidal spherical variety）。仿射情形下，比较除数类群到各点局部除数类群的同态核，并借助B-稳定素除子显式描述该核；光滑完备情形下，改用不含给定G-轨道的B-稳定素除子类所生成的幺半群作为判据工具。

Result: 在仿射情形证明了轨道相等的等价条件：两点属于单位连通分支的同一轨道当且仅当其局部除数类同态的核相同；对光滑完备环面球簇，建立了以相应幺半群表述的类似轨道判据。

Conclusion: 自同构群的轨道可由局部除数类群同态的核（仿射情形）或特定B-稳定素除子类生成的幺半群（完备情形）统一刻画，为环面球簇上的轨道分类提供了代数化判据。

Abstract: In this paper we study the orbits of the automorphism group on a toroidal spherical variety. In the affine case, we prove that two points lie in the same orbit of the identity component if and only if the kernels of the homomorphisms from the divisor class group to their local divisor class groups coincide. These kernels are described in terms of $B$-stable prime divisors. For a smooth complete toroidal spherical variety, we obtain a similar criterion using the monoids generated by the classes of $B$-stable prime divisors that do not contain a given $G$-orbit.

</details>


### [112] [Abelian canonical covers of minimal rational surfaces and $\mathbb F_1$ up to degree $8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35724)
*Alexandre Dorothée,Francisco Javier Gallego*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文分类了极小次数光滑曲面、$\mathbb P^2$ 及非极小嵌入的 Hirzebruch 曲面的阿贝尔典范覆盖（次数 $\le 8$），并给出了更一般框架下的分支除子消失与 $\pi_*\mathcal O_X$ 的模结构结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次数 $n \le 4$ 的阿贝尔典范覆盖已有分类，但 $5 \le n \le 8$ 尚不完整；同时在非极小嵌入的 $\mathbb P^2$、Hirzebruch 曲面上的阿贝尔典范覆盖缺乏系统刻画，且需要一般性的分支除子与模结构理论工具。

Method: 将问题归结为对阿贝尔典范覆盖 $\pi: X \to W$（群 $G$、次数 $n$）的分类研究。在更一般的设定（$X$ 正规、局部 Gorenstein、任意维数，$W$ 光滑且 $p_g(W)=0$）下，确定哪些分支除子必须消失；并研究当 $\pi_*\mathcal O_X$ 分解为线丛之和时其 $\mathcal O_W$-模结构，在 $W=\mathbb P^2$ 或 Hirzebruch 曲面时完全算出。再应用于 $n \le 8$ 的具体分类。

Result: 对于极小次数 $W$：$n$ 为素数且 $>3$ 或 $G=\mathbb Z_8$ 时不存在此类覆盖；对 $G=\mathbb Z_6,\ \mathbb Z_2^3,\ \mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_4$ 给出完整覆盖列表，仅 $W=\mathbb P^2,\mathbb F_0,\mathbb F_1$ 出现，含多个 $p_g$ 无界的新族，$X$ 在 $G=\mathbb Z_2^3$ 一般光滑，$G=\mathbb Z_6$ 及（除一例外）$G=\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_4$ 时奇异。对于 $\mathbb P^2$ 或非极小嵌入的 Hirzebruch 曲面：阿贝尔典范覆盖仅当 $n=2$ 或 $G=\mathbb Z_2^2$ 时存在，后者被完全分类为无限多族，大多为新族，$p_g$ 无界，多数不规则度无界。所有 $X$ 的斜率 $K^2/\chi$ 在 $6$、$8$ 及 $8\alpha/(\alpha+1)$（$\alpha\ge 2$）处聚集。

Conclusion: 完成了度 $\le 8$ 阿贝尔典范覆盖在极小次数曲面及 $\mathbb P^2$、Hirzebruch 曲面上的分类，揭示了斜率的聚集现象，并建立了一般性的分支除子消失与 $\pi_*\mathcal O_X$ 模结构理论作为基础工具。

Abstract: We classify abelian canonical covers of degree at most $8$ of smooth surfaces of minimal degree, and of $\mathbb P^2$ and Hirzebruch surfaces not embedded as surfaces of minimal degree.
  Let $π: X \longrightarrow W$ be an abelian canonical cover of a smooth surface of minimal degree $W$ with group $G$ and degree $n$. Degrees $n \le 4$ were classified earlier; we complete the picture for $5 \le n \le 8$:
  - No such covers exist if $n$ is prime and $n > 3$, nor if $G=\mathbb Z_8$.
  - For $G=\mathbb Z_6$, $\mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_2$ and $\mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_4$ we give the complete list of covers. Only $W =\mathbb P^2$, $\mathbb F_0$ and $\mathbb F_1$ arise. The list contains several families with unbounded $p_g$, most of them new; $X$ is smooth for general covers with $G=\mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_2$, but singular if $G=\mathbb Z_6$ and, with one exception, if $G=\mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_4$.
  When $W$ is $\mathbb P^2$ or a Hirzebruch surface not embedded as a surface of minimal degree, we show that abelian canonical covers exist only for $n = 2$ or for $G =\mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_2$, and we classify the latter completely: they form infinitely many families, most new, with unbounded $p_g$ and, most of them, with unbounded irregularity.
  The slopes $K^2/χ$ of all these $X$ accumulate at $6$, at 8, and at $8α/(α+1)$ for every integer $α\ge 2$.
  These results rest on more general ones, valid for $X$ normal, locally Gorenstein and of arbitrary dimension, and $W$ smooth with $p_g(W) = 0$: we determine which branch divisors of an abelian canonical cover must vanish, and, for canonical covers whose $π_*\mathcal O_X$ splits as a sum of line bundles, we describe the $\mathcal O_W$-module structure of $π_*\mathcal O_X$, computing it completely when $W$ is $\mathbb P^2$ or a Hirzebruch surface.

</details>


### [113] [A uniform lower bound for the Zhang-Kawazumi invariant and applications to the Bogomolov conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35736)
*Robert Wilms*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了亏格 g≥2 的紧致连通 Riemann 曲面的 Zhang-Kawazumi 不变量 φ(X) 的严格下界，并对超椭圆情形给出更强估计，同时应用于数域上曲线典范丛的自交数与 Bogomolov 猜想的统一高度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhang-Kawazumi 不变量是 Arakelov 几何与算术几何中的重要量。论文旨在给出该不变量的精确下界，并由此改进数域上曲线典范丛 admissible adelic 度量的自交数估计，从而获得 Bogomolov 猜想中的统一高度界。

Method: 核心是将 φ(X) 表示为 X 上光滑 Hermitian 矩阵值函数空间上的一个二次型在某些投影矩阵处的取值这一新表达式；一般情形下推导严格下界，超椭圆情形下利用该表达式进一步得到更强下界。

Result: 证明了对亏格 g≥2 的紧致连通 Riemann 曲面，φ(X) 严格大于 [g(g+2)-(2g+1)H_g]/(g-1)（H_g 为第 g 个调和数）；若 X 为超椭圆，则 φ(X)>(g/2)(H_g-1)。并由此导出数域上亏格 g≥2 光滑射影曲线 admissible adelic 典范丛 ω_a 自交数 ω_a² 的新下界及 Bogomolov 猜想中的新统一高度界。

Conclusion: 论文通过 φ(X) 的二次型新表达式统一处理了 Zhang-Kawazumi 不变量的下界问题，给出一般与超椭圆两套严格下界，并在算术层面获得自交数下界与统一高度界，为 Arakelov 几何与 Bogomolov 猜想提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove that the Zhang-Kawazumi invariant $\varphi(X)$ of a compact and connected Riemann surface $X$ of genus $g\ge 2$ is strictly larger than \[\frac{g(g+2)-(2g+1)H_g}{g-1},\] where $H_g=\sum_{k=1}^g \frac{1}{k}$ denotes the $g$-th harmonic number. If $X$ is hyperelliptic, we give the stronger bound $\varphi(X)>\frac{g}{2}(H_g-1)$. The proof relies on a new expression of $\varphi(X)$ in terms of a quadratic form on the space of smooth Hermitian matrix-valued functions on $X$, evaluated at certain projector matrices. As an arithmetic application, we deduce new lower bounds for the self-intersection number $ω_a^2$ of the admissible adelic metrized canonical bundle $ω_a$ of a smooth projective curve of genus $g\ge 2$ over a number field and hence new uniform height bounds in the Bogomolov conjecture.

</details>

### [114] [Trotterisation Thresholds and Dissipative Quantum Chaos in Open-System Digital Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34623)
*Angsar Manatuly,Lukas M. Sieberer,Cahit Kargi,Nathan K. Langford*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含噪声开放量子系统中的Trotter化数字量子模拟（DQS），证明Trotter化阈值在开放系统效应下依然存在，并提出基于随机矩阵理论（RMT）的统计方法严格刻画量子混沌的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotter化DQS通过增大Trotter步长可减少门数量，但超过某阈值会因量子混沌而突然崩溃。此前该现象仅在无环境噪声与退相干的封闭系统中研究，而真实量子计算机对环境噪声高度敏感。作者希望填补开放系统下Trotter化阈值研究的空白。

Method: 作者研究一系列实验相关的开放量子模拟模型，涵盖直至稳态、完全失去相干性的长时极限；引入基于随机矩阵理论（RMT）的统计技术，扩展自封闭系统的方法，分别刻画近幺正量子混沌与真正耗散量子混沌的出现；并分析耗散强度与底层幺正量子混沌之间的平衡条件。

Result: Trotter化阈值即使包含开放系统效应、在长时稳态极限下依然存在；开放DQS在阈值之前能准确再现开放目标系统的动力学，但超过阈值仍会突然性能崩溃。耗散量子混沌的出现要求耗散与底层幺正量子混沌相平衡：耗散须强到足以引发真正耗散行为，又不能强到抑制底层混沌结构。

Conclusion: 开放系统DQS同样受Trotter化阈值限制，量子混沌是由模拟误差突降的根源；RMT统计方法为刻画开放系统Trotter阈值提供了严格工具，并揭示了耗散量子混沌出现的平衡条件，对实际量子模拟的资源与精度权衡具有指导意义。

Abstract: Digital quantum simulation (DQS) is one of the most promising applications of quantum computing. Trotterised DQS models quantum dynamics by decomposing the evolution into a sequence of gate-based Trotter steps. Using larger Trotter steps reduces the required gate count, but it is now understood that the Trotterisation step size cannot be increased beyond a certain threshold value, beyond which there is a sudden breakdown in simulation accuracy, due to the onset of quantum chaos. To date, this behaviour has only been studied for closed systems not subjected to the environmental noise and decoherence to which real-world quantum computers are highly sensitive. In this work, we study a range of experimentally relevant open-system quantum simulator models and show that Trotterisation thresholds persist even when open-system effects are included, even in the long-time, steady-state limit, once all coherence has been lost. We show that open DQS can accurately reproduce the dynamics of open target systems prior to the threshold, but still exhibits a sudden performance breakdown beyond it. We introduce new, statistical techniques to rigorously characterise the onset of quantum chaos beyond the Trotterisation threshold for both approximately unitary and genuine dissipative quantum chaos, based on random matrix theory (RMT), building on techniques developed for closed systems. Finally, we demonstrate that dissipative quantum chaos emerges when the dissipation is in balance with the presence of underlying unitary quantum chaos; the dissipation must be strong enough to induce genuinely dissipative behaviour, yet not so strong as to suppress the underlying chaotic structure.

</details>


### [115] [Probing the classical complexity of quantum dynamics experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31830)
*Thomas Schuster,Andreas Elben*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新的量子实验复杂度度量——反应性（reactivity），用于刻画近期经典算法通过追踪局部信息高效模拟量子电路与动力学的现象。反应性针对整个量子实验而非孤立态或算符，低反应性实验可被高效经典模拟、学习与前推。作者进一步提出"泡利路径谱学"协议以高效测量任意实验的反应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复杂度度量（如纠缠、magic）无法解释为何某些量子电路和动力学在纠缠与magic较高时仍可被追踪局部信息的经典算法高效模拟。因此需要一个能捕捉这种新型经典攻击方式的新复杂度度量，并指导寻找难以被经典模拟的高反应性量子实验。

Method: 引入新的复杂度度量"反应性"（reactivity），它不是描述孤立量子态或算符的性质，而是刻画整个量子实验。通过数值与严格证明建立低反应性与可经典模拟、可学习、可快速前推之间的关联；并提出名为"泡利路径谱学"（Pauli path spectroscopy）的实验协议来高效测量任意量子实验的反应性，该协议即便在实验本身无法经典模拟时仍然适用。

Result: 提供了数值与严格证据，表明低反应性的量子实验在多种意义下都是简单的：可被高效经典模拟、学习和前推。所提出的泡利路径谱学协议可高效测量任意感兴趣量子实验的反应性，且适用于实验本身超出经典模拟能力的情形。

Conclusion: 反应性为理解量子实验的经典可模拟性提供了新视角，填补了传统复杂度度量的空白。该工作激励研究者寻找高反应性的量子实验以规避这些简化特征，同时提供了可实验实现的反应性测量工具。

Abstract: A confluence of recent works has shown that many quantum circuits and dynamics are efficiently simulable by classical algorithms that track local information, even when conventional complexity measures such as the entanglement and magic are high. Here, we introduce a novel measure of complexity, the reactivity, to capture this new method of classical attack. Unlike conventional complexity measures, the reactivity does not capture a property of a quantum state or operator in isolation, but rather a quantum experiment as a whole. We provide numerical and rigorous evidence that quantum experiments with low reactivity are simple by a host of measures: they are efficient to classically simulate, learn, and fast-forward. This motivates the search for quantum experiments with high reactivity, which may evade these simplistic features. To this end, we introduce easily implementable experimental protocols---dubbed Pauli path spectroscopy---that allow one to efficiently measure the reactivity of any quantum experiment of interest. Our protocols are applicable even when the experiment itself is beyond the reach of classical simulation.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum error mitigation from information dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31934)
*Philippe Suchsland,Thomas Schuster,Manuel S. Rudolph,Nicholas Noll,Thomas E. O'Brien,Zlatko K. Minev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于"反应性函数"（reactivity function）这一紧凑表示来理解与缓解量子电路实验误差，并据此设计两种互补的误差缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型复杂量子电路中量子信息沿指数级多路径扩散，导致难以直接理解误差影响；传统误差缓解方法（如零噪声外推、概率误差消除）存在偏差大、采样成本高的问题。

Method: 引入反应性函数作为量子电路信息动力学的紧凑表示，其形状决定噪声响应及对不同误差缓解方法的适用性。基于此提出两种方法：(1) Pauli路径零噪声外推，对电路反应性函数拟合参数化模型并外推至零噪声；(2) 可调误差消除，对反应性函数进行滤波以在阈值局域性内抵消噪声。两者均含调优参数，可系统性提升精度直至收敛。

Result: 在来自已发表实验的三个电路族上，方法克服了传统零噪声外推高达30%的偏差；在约10^-2均方根误差下，相比概率误差消除将采样成本降低最多达10^3倍。

Conclusion: 反应性函数为理解与缓解量子电路误差提供了统一且紧凑的视角，所提两种方法精度可收敛且显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Overcoming experimental errors is a central challenge in quantum science. This challenge is particularly acute in large and complex quantum circuits, where quantum information spreads into exponentially many paths, foiling any direct attempt to understand errors' impact. In this work, we present a novel approach to understanding and mitigating experimental errors, based on a surprisingly compact representation of a quantum circuit's information dynamics, the reactivity function. The shape of a circuit's reactivity function governs its noise response, and its amenability to different error mitigation methods. From this viewpoint, we introduce two complementary such methods. The first, Pauli-path zero-noise extrapolation, fits a parameterized model to a circuit's reactivity function and uses it to accurately extrapolate to zero noise. The second, tunable error cancellation, filters a circuit's reactivity function to cancel noise on all information up to a threshold locality. Crucially, both methods feature a tuning parameter that allows one to systematically improve their accuracy until convergence. Across three circuit families from published experiments, our methods overcome conventional zero-noise extrapolation biases as large as 30%; at $\sim$$10^{-2}$ root-mean-square error, they reduce sampling cost relative to probabilistic error cancellation by up to a factor of $10^3$.

</details>


### [117] [Characterising the precision of a clock without any external time reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31832)
*Pablo Á. Domínguez,Christopher Chubb,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外部时间基准或第二台时钟即可自评估时钟精度的方法，仅需信号分束器与延迟触发器，并由专用电子电路验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量时钟精度通常需要与更优的时间基准比较，但物理上不存在理想参考基准，因此亟需一种摆脱外部参考、实现单时钟自评估精度的手段。

Method: 利用信号分束器与延迟触发器将单个时钟信号自身作为比较对象，并在估计器中显式计入触发器延迟的不确定度，构造仅依赖计次统计（tick-count statistics）的精度估计量。

Result: 在无外部授时设备、无第二台时钟的条件下，通过专门搭建的电子电路验证了该估计方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 单台时钟可借助极简硬件实现自身精度的自洽表征，为摆脱对外部时间基准依赖的时钟精度评估提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Determining a clock's precision usually requires comparison with a better time reference, yet no ideal reference is physically attainable. We show how a single clock can characterise its own precision using only a signal splitter and delayed trigger, whose uncertainty is explicitly accounted for. The resulting estimator depends only on tick-count statistics. A purpose-built electronic circuit validates the method without an external timekeeper or a second clock.

</details>


### [118] [Measuring Clock Precision Without an Ideal Time Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31837)
*Pablo Á. Domínguez,Christopher Chubb,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究无绝对参考时钟条件下，利用多个同类非完美时钟的集体比较来量化时钟精度，并给出解析界与协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理器件通常通过与更精确的参考标准比较来表征，但时钟缺乏物理上完美的时间参考，真实时钟具有有限且依赖模型的不确定度，因此需要无需绝对参考的精度量化方法。

Method: 提出仅使用同一时钟的多个副本进行时钟精度量化的解析界（analytic bounds）与协议；这些副本无需同步，且可存在制造差异或环境耦合。

Result: 给出了多个非完美时钟集体确定自身精度的解析界；除时钟表征外，该方法还可用于亚周期时间间隔的精确测量以及时间膨胀、多普勒频移等相对论效应的测量。

Conclusion: 多个非完美时钟可在无绝对参考条件下集体量化其精度，为量子计量学与基础物理研究提供工具。

Abstract: Characterising physical devices typically requires comparison with more precise reference standards. For clocks, however, this approach is problematic because no physically perfect time reference exists; real clocks have finite, model-dependent uncertainty. We address whether multiple imperfect clocks can collectively determine their precision without an absolute reference. This paper provides analytic bounds and protocols for quantifying clock precision using only multiple copies of the same clock, which need not be synchronised and may have manufacturing variations or environmental coupling. Beyond clock characterisation, these methods enable precise measurements f sub-period intervals and relativistic effects such as time dilation and Doppler shifts, providing tools for quantum metrology and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [119] [A Universal Budget for Entanglement and Nonlocal Non-Stabilizerness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31841)
*Salvatore Marco Giampaolo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子比特系统中建立了纠缠与非局域非稳定子性（nonlocal non-stabilizerness）两种资源同时分配的普适二次约束，并给出张量网络截断的资源感知控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与非局域非稳定子性虽同为编码于二分纯态Schmidt谱中的量子资源，但二者能否被同时自由分配此前缺乏定量的普适性刻画；同时张量网络截断通常只关注纠缠而忽略非稳定子性等资源的损耗，需要资源感知的控制准则。

Method: 以Schmidt谱为出发点，引入"逻辑容量"概念（即编码占据的Schmidt支撑每侧所需的最少逻辑量子比特数），推导纠缠与非局域非稳定子性的普适二次约束及附加谱约束；对单条分离虚拟边切割的张量网络，将逻辑容量与占据的bond dimension对应；并由保留的Schmidt谱与丢弃权重构建对未截断态资源的单侧证书。

Result: 1）证明二者同时分配受普适二次约束支配，约束由逻辑容量决定；2）揭示分配的强不对称性：纠缠可耗尽逻辑容量，而非局域非稳定子性的最大值仅随逻辑容量对数增长；3）对单虚拟边情形，逻辑容量由占据的bond dimension决定；4）给出基于保留Schmidt谱和丢弃权重的单侧资源证书，小丢弃权重下修正项关于权重线性。

Conclusion: 这些结果将一个普适的资源预算与张量网络截断的资源感知控制联系起来，为在截断过程中同时追踪纠缠与非局域非稳定子性提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Entanglement and nonlocal non-stabilizerness are distinct quantum resources encoded in the Schmidt spectrum of a bipartite pure state. For qubit systems, we establish a universal quadratic constraint on their simultaneous allocation, governed by the minimum number of logical qubits required on each side to encode the occupied Schmidt support. Additional spectral constraints reveal a strong asymmetry in their allocation with the Entanglement that can exhaust the logical capacity, whereas the maximal nonlocal non-stabilizerness grows only logarithmically with it. For a tensor-network cut crossed by a single separating virtual edge, the logical capacity is determined by the occupied bond dimension. We further derive a one-sided certificate for the resources of the untruncated state from a retained Schmidt spectrum and its discarded weight. For small discarded weight, the resulting corrections are linear in that weight. These results connect a universal resource budget to resource-aware control of tensor-network truncations.

</details>


### [120] [In-plane optimal qubit operation with control fidelities exceeding 99 %](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33229)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Zhenzhen Kong,Rong-Long Ma,Hao-Tian Jiang,Ao-Ran Li,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Tao Wu,Rui Zheng,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在平面锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，通过优化面内磁场取向抑制电荷噪声敏感性，使Hahn回波相干时间提升5倍、控制错误率降低近10倍，门保真度达99.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点空穴自旋量子比特因全电操控适用于大规模量子计算，但价带空穴的多种物理机制导致比特性质各向异性，需要寻找延长相干时间、实现高保真操控的机会。

Method: 在平面锗（Ge）量子点中制备单空穴自旋量子比特，研究其在面内磁场下对电荷噪声的各向异性敏感性；将纵向自旋-电敏感性（spin-electric susceptibility）与比特相干性及操控性能相关联，确定电荷噪声敏感度最低的最优工作点；采用门集层析（gate set tomography）评估门保真度。

Result: 优化磁场取向后自旋-电敏感性降低，Hahn回波相干时间提升5倍，控制错误率被抑制近10倍；在最优工作点，门集层析显示最大门保真度达99.82%。

Conclusion: 该发现表明通过优化磁场取向可有效抑制电荷噪声、提升空穴自旋量子比特性能，增强了其在可扩展量子信息处理中的应用前景。

Abstract: Hole spin qubits based on semiconductor quantum dots are promising for building future large-scale quantum computers owing to their all-electrical manipulation. However, abundant physical mechanisms of valence band holes lead to anisotropic qubit properties. There is an opportunity to prolong the coherence time and achieve high-fidelity qubit manipulations. Here, we report a single-hole spin qubit in a planar germanium quantum dot and investigate its anisotropic susceptibility to charge noise under an in-plane magnetic field. By correlating the longitudinal spin-electric susceptibility with qubit coherence and control performance, we identify an optimal operating point where the sensitivity to charge noise is minimized. We find that optimizing the magnetic-field orientation reduces the spin-electric susceptibility, resulting in a five-fold enhancement of the Hahn-echo coherence time and a nearly tenfold suppression of control infidelity. At the optimal operating point, gate set tomography demonstrates the maximum gate fidelity of 99.82 %. Our finding enhances the prospects of hole spin qubits for scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Fisher Information as the Speed Limit for Multipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31853)
*Zain H. Saleem,Da-Wei Luo,Anjala M. Babu,Ting Yu,Stephen K. Gray,Anil Shaji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了多体纠缠生成速度的普适上界：对任意可微纯多体态的参量依赖演化，广义 concurrence 的变化率被量子 Fisher 信息（QFI）的平方根所界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注两体纠缠与 QFI 的关系，而多体纠缠生成速度是否存在普适极限、量子临界增强的参量敏感度是否源于多体纠缠的快速变化，尚不清楚。

Method: 基于对称对数导数（SLD）形式体系给出严格证明，揭示该上界源自量子估计理论；辅以 Schmidt 谱分析确定饱和条件，并对秩二二分给出更紧的界；在相互作用多体自旋模型及横场 Ising 模型量子相变中加以验证。

Result: 广义 concurrence 变化率 ≤ √QFI；Schmidt 谱分析给出饱和条件与秩二二分的更紧界；在横场 Ising 模型临界点，QFI 显著增强而广义 concurrence 近似静止、其对耦合的敏感度被压制。

Conclusion: 量子临界性带来的参量敏感度增强并不必然来自多体纠缠模的快速变化，二者可以解耦。

Abstract: We derive a universal upper bound on the speed of multipartite entanglement generation. For arbitrary differentiable pure multipartite states undergoing parameter-dependent evolution, we prove that the rate of change of generalized concurrence is bounded by the square root of the quantum Fisher information (QFI). The proof follows directly from the symmetric logarithmic derivative formalism, revealing the bound as a consequence of quantum estimation theory, while a complementary Schmidt-spectrum analysis identifies the conditions for saturation and yields a tighter bound for rank-two bipartitions. We illustrate these results in interacting many-body spin models and investigate their behavior across the quantum phase transition of the transverse-field Ising model. At the critical point, we find that the QFI can be strongly enhanced while the generalized concurrence is nearly stationary and its sensitivity to the coupling is suppressed. This demonstrates that the enhanced parameter sensitivity associated with quantum criticality need not arise from rapid changes in the magnitude of multipartite entanglement.

</details>


### [122] [Indefinite causal order with output-signalling instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31863)
*Ricardo Silveira,Patrick Andriolo,Davide Poderini,Dmitry Melnikov,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅允许局域操作经典结果通信的约束下,量子过程的不定因果序如何产生新型非因果统计,并构造了可违反新因果不等式的因果多面体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定因果序是否产生真正的非因果统计,不仅取决于全局因果结构,还取决于各方可用的局域操作。现有研究多在较宽松的操作假设下讨论,可能遗漏在更受限通信条件下才能显现的不定因果序形式。

Method: 将通信限制为局域操作的经典结果,构建具有新因果不等式的因果多面体;采用测量-重制备、破坏纠缠的仪器(无需相干传输穿过实验室)来违反这些不等式;并使用半定规划框架对可实现违反程度进行界定。

Result: 所提出的因果不等式可被测量-重制备且破坏纠缠的仪器违反;半定规划给出了可实现违反的上界;并揭示了满足所有常规输入信号不等式的非因果关联。

Conclusion: 限制可用于通信的信息可以揭示在较宽松操作假设下无法获得的不定因果序形式,说明局域操作与通信约束在判定非因果性中起关键作用。

Abstract: Quantum processes can exhibit indefinite causal order, allowing coherent superpositions of distinct orderings of events. Whether such processes generate genuinely noncausal statistics, however, depends not only on their global causal structure but also on the local operations available to the parties. Here, we restrict communication to the classical outcomes of local operations, yielding a causal polytope with new causal inequalities that can be violated using measure-and-reprepare, entanglement-breaking instruments requiring no coherent transmission through the laboratories. Using a semidefinite-programming framework, we bound the achievable violations and reveal noncausal correlations that satisfy all conventional input-signalling inequalities, demonstrating that restricting the information available for communication can uncover forms of indefinite causal order inaccessible under less restrictive operational assumptions.

</details>


### [123] [High-fidelity geometric quantum gates exceeding 99.9% in germanium quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33251)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Rong-Long Ma,Zhenzhen Kong,Ao-Ran Li,Chengxian Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yang Liu,Hao-Tian Jiang,Zhi-Tao Wu,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Guang-Can Guo,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在锗量子点单空穴自旋量子比特中，门集层析显示I门离共振噪声限制性能，引入几何量子计算后可在宽Rabi频率范围内实现保真度>99%的噪声鲁棒操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现容错量子计算要求高保真且鲁棒的量子比特操控。常规操控受离共振噪声等影响，连续I门操作时噪声显著降低量子比特性能，因此需要一种对噪声不敏感的操控方案。

Method: 在锗量子点中制备单空穴自旋量子比特，并利用门集层析（gate set tomography）表征其控制保真度；针对离共振噪声问题，引入几何量子计算来实现噪声鲁棒的量子比特操控，并测试其在宽Rabi频率范围及微波频率失谐条件下的保真度。

Result: 最大控制保真度I、X/2、Y/2门分别达97.48%、99.81%、99.88%；门集层析序列中连续I门的离共振噪声严重限制性能。几何门控制保真度在宽Rabi频率范围内均高于99%，最高超过99.9%；几何X/2、Y/2（I）门在微波频率失谐±2.5 MHz（±1.2 MHz）时保真度仍超过99%。

Conclusion: 几何量子计算是实现半导体量子计算中高保真、可重复量子比特操控的潜在方法。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity and robust qubit manipulations is a crucial requirement for realizing faulttolerant quantum computation. Here, we demonstrate a single-hole spin qubit in a germanium quantum dot and characterize its control fidelity using gate set tomography. The maximum control fidelities reach 97.48%, 99.81%, 99.88% for the I, X/2 and Y /2 gate, respectively. These results reveal that off-resonance noise during consecutive I gates in gate set tomography sequences severely limits qubit performance. Therefore, we introduce geometric quantum computation to realize noiseresilient qubit manipulation. The geometric gate control fidelities remain above 99% across a wide range of Rabi frequencies. The maximum fidelity surpasses 99.9%. Furthermore, the fidelities of geometric X/2 and Y /2 (I) gates exceed 99% even when detuning the microwave frequency by +-2.5 MHz (+-1.2 MHz), highlighting the noise-resilient feature. These results demonstrate that geometric quantum computation is a potential method for achieving high-fidelity qubit manipulation reproducibly in semiconductor quantum computation.

</details>


### [124] [Scattering amplitudes from quantum hardware a la RESOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31889)
*Raul A Briceno,Ivan M Burbano,Anthony N Ciavarella,Ermal Rrapaj,Thomas R Richardson,Andre Walker-Loud*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次在量子硬件上实现实时散射可观测量估计器(RESO)，在一维格点非相对论自旋-1/2费米子模型上计算时空关联函数，并提取束缚态与散射振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射振幅的传统提取通常依赖欧氏时空或格点QCD方法，需要解析延拓或有限体积技巧，难以直接实时计算。本文旨在探索在量子硬件上直接实现实时散射可观测量估计器的可行性。

Method: 物理模型为一维格点非相对论自旋-1/2费米子理论，含单一接触相互作用，格点数最多N_x=54，时间步最多20步。使用IBM量子计算机在109个量子比特上执行量子线路，应用10,806个CZ门，双量子比特门深度达246，计算时空关联函数。

Result: 成功在量子硬件上计算了时空关联函数，并从中研究了束缚态、提取了散射振幅。

Conclusion: 验证了在近期量子硬件上实现实时散射可观测量估计器的可行性，为后续量子计算应用于散射问题的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We report the first quantum-hardware implementation of Real-time Estimators for Scattering Observables (RESOs). The physical model is a one-dimensional lattice theory of non-relativistic spin-1/2 fermions with a single contact interaction. The lattice sizes studied are up to $N_x=54$ lattice sites and up to $20$ time steps. Spacetime correlation functions were computed on IBM's quantum computers, using $109$ qubits with $10,806$ CZ gates applied, reaching a two-qubit gate depth of $246$. We demonstrate how to study bound states and extract scattering amplitudes from these correlation functions.

</details>


### [125] [Temperature--Hamiltonian Ambiguity in Strong-Coupling Quantum Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33389)
*Jinshuang Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 强耦合下约化平衡态只固定无量纲Gibbs生成元，无法唯一确定局域Hamiltonian与温度；作者提出以独立物理信息确定局域Hamiltonian，进而唯一定义局域温度的框架，并用两个精确可解模型阐明该模糊性及其解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合使子系统偏离其在浴温下裸Hamiltonian的Gibbs态，因而局域温度与局域Hamiltonian的定义存在内在模糊性：同一约化平衡态可对应不同的(H, T)参数化。论文旨在明确这一"温度–Hamiltonian模糊性"，并给出可操作的解决方案，以区分量子态的表征与热力学赋值。

Method: 提出一个操作性的解决思路：局域Hamiltonian由与稳态Gibbs参数化无关的物理信息（如延迟动力学）独立识别；若精确约化态与该独立识别Hamiltonian生成的Gibbs族相容，则唯一确定局域温度。通过两个精确可解模型验证：(1) 二能级系统–谐振子模型，用基于指定局域Hamiltonian的温度重整化与浴温下mean-force Hamiltonian能级重整化给出状态等价描述；(2) 精确玻色子模型，由延迟动力学独立固定重整化的局域激发尺度，从而在稳态布居确定温度之前指定局域Hamiltonian。

Result: 在二能级–振子模型中，明确展示了温度重整化与Hamiltonian-of-mean-force能级重整化两种状态等价描述的模糊性；在精确玻色子模型中，延迟动力学可独立确定重整化的局域激发尺度，进而配合稳态布居唯一确定局域温度，给出互补情形的操作性判定。

Conclusion: 论文区分了"态表征"与"热力学赋值"，表明局域热力学量在强耦合下需借助独立物理信息（尤其是动力学）来锚定局域Hamiltonian，才能唯一定义局域温度；该框架为超越弱耦合的局域热力学提供了可操作基础。

Abstract: Strong coupling generally drives a subsystem away from the Gibbs state of its bare Hamiltonian at the bath temperature. We formulate a temperature--Hamiltonian ambiguity: the reduced equilibrium state fixes the dimensionless Gibbs generator, but does not by itself uniquely determine the local Hamiltonian and temperature entering a Gibbs representation. We propose an operational resolution in which the local Hamiltonian is identified from physical information independent of the stationary Gibbs parametrization. Whenever the exact reduced state is compatible with the Gibbs family generated by that independently identified Hamiltonian, it then determines a unique local temperature. An exactly solvable two-level-system--oscillator model makes the ambiguity explicit through state-equivalent descriptions based on temperature renormalization relative to a specified local Hamiltonian and Hamiltonian-of-mean-force level renormalization at the bath temperature. An exact bosonic model provides the complementary case in which retarded dynamics independently fixes a renormalized local excitation scale and thereby specifies the local Hamiltonian before the stationary populations determine the temperature. These results distinguish state representation from thermodynamic assignment and provide an operational framework for local thermodynamics beyond weak coupling.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Encoding Agents: A Natural Language Interface for Data Embedding Strategy Selection in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31902)
*Ana Paula Appel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantum Encoding Agents，一个开源系统，将量子机器学习中的数据编码选择转化为自然语言交互，结合硬件感知策略与 KTA 评分自动推荐可复制的 Qiskit 电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据编码选择是量子机器学习中的核心但缺乏有效工具支持的决策，它决定了希尔伯特空间几何、量子核的可表达性以及电路能否在近期硬件上运行。

Method: 系统接收数据集和可选任务描述，对数据做剖析；采用基于门错误阈值 p*≈10^-3 的硬件感知策略；返回可复制的 Qiskit 电路并给出葡萄牙语或英语理由，用 Kernel-Target Alignment（KTA）对量子核评分。当存在数值矩阵时，估计相关分形维数 D_2，并用 FD-ASE 选择原始列，以 q* = max(2, ceil(D_2)) 作为量子比特预算，避免在保真度核已崩溃的宽度下提出 angle 与 IQP 映射。系统实现七种编码族：amplitude、angle、dense angle、IQP、basis、data re-uploading 和 custom feature map。

Result: 在基准数据集上，KTA 能在真实 NISQ 约束下区分这些编码。

Conclusion: Quantum Encoding Agents 通过自然语言交互与硬件感知、分形维数驱动的策略，为量子编码选择提供了可用的开源工具，并以 KTA 验证了其区分能力。

Abstract: Selecting a data encoding is a central and poorly tooled decision in quantum machine learning. The feature map fixes the geometry of the Hilbert space, the expressibility of quantum kernels, and whether the circuit can run on near-term hardware. This paper presents Quantum Encoding Agents, an open-source system that turns encoding selection into a natural-language interaction. Given a dataset and an optional task description, it profiles the data, applies a hardware-aware policy using the gate-error threshold p* approximately 10^{-3}, returns a copyable Qiskit circuit with a justification in Portuguese or English, and scores the quantum kernel by Kernel-Target Alignment (KTA). When a numerical matrix is available, it estimates the correlation fractal dimension D_2 and selects original columns with FD-ASE, using q* = max(2, ceil(D_2)) as a qubit budget so that angle and IQP maps are not proposed at a width where the fidelity kernel has collapsed. The service implements seven encoding families: amplitude, angle, dense angle, IQP, basis, data re-uploading, and custom feature map. On benchmark datasets, KTA separates these encodings under realistic NISQ constraints.

</details>


### [127] [Engineering Defect-Phonon Interactions Through Heterophase-Interfaces in Silicon Carbide Membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33545)
*Kirlie Iulius Figuera Michal,Jin Hee Lee,Keiju Sato,Takuji Maekawa,Je-Hyung Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过远程外延构建3C/4H-SiC异相界面，调控缺陷-声子相互作用，使Vsi色心的零声子跃迁显著增强，室温零声子线宽窄至4.71 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽禁带半导体中的点缺陷可提供固态自旋与光子量子比特，且能在室温工作；但本征电子-声子相互作用导致声子边带发射展宽、零声子线跃迁弱，限制了高效自旋-光子界面与可扩展的光子介导相互作用。

Method: 提出基于远程外延技术的可控异相界面，作为晶体异质性工程策略。以堆垛层错这一通常被视为需消除的缺陷为切入点，利用晶相界面为缺陷-声子相互作用提供超越单晶本征性质的额外调控自由度；对比单相4H-SiC薄膜与3C/4H异相界面SiC中的Vsi色心。

Result: 与单相4H-SiC薄膜相比，3C/4H异相界面SiC中的Vsi零声子光学跃迁大幅增强，即使在室温下也获得显著更窄的零声子线宽，为4.71 meV。

Conclusion: 异相界面工程可作为调控缺陷-声子相互作用的有效途径，用于实现具有增强零声子跃迁的明亮室温量子发射体。

Abstract: Point defects in wide-bandgap semiconductors offer spin and photonic qubits in a solid-state platform, making them important building blocks for quantum information processing, communication, and sensing. While these systems have the strong advantage of room-temperature operation, intrinsic electron-phonon interaction induces broad phonon-sideband emission and weak zero-phonon-line transitions, limiting efficient spin-photon interfaces and scalable photon-mediated interaction. Here, we introduce a controlled heterophase interface based on the remote epitaxy technique as a crystal heterogeneity-engineering strategy. While stacking faults are treated as imperfections to be eliminated, our results instead show that crystal-phase interfaces can provide an additional degree of freedom to engineer defect-phonon interaction beyond the intrinsic properties of a single crystal. In comparison with single-phase 4H-silicon carbide (SiC) membranes, Vsi in 3C/4H heterointerface SiC exhibits a drastic enhancement of zero-phonon optical transitions, with a substantially narrower zero-phonon linewidth of 4.71 meV even at room temperature. These results establish heterophase-interface engineering as an effective route for tailoring defect-phonon interactions and realizing bright room-temperature quantum emitters with enhanced zero-phonon optical transitions.

</details>


### [128] [Gate Synthesis in Dephasing-Limited Nitrogen-Vacancy Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31913)
*Ankita Chakravarty,Arnaud Carignan-Dugas,Eva Dupont-Ferrier,Yves Bérubé-Lauzière*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于ZXZXZ门分解的控制框架，仅需单个校准的π/2原语加虚拟Z操作即可实现普适单比特控制；在Rabi频率与超精细劈裂相当的高功率区，复合脉冲相较标准脉冲在长序列中抑制误差累积超70%，而随机基准测试对相干控制误差不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系综NV色心广泛用于量子传感与磁强计，其性能依赖微波脉冲序列的准确实现。但系综中非均匀展宽与短相干时间限制多脉冲可靠控制，硬件不完美与离共振激发又使实际旋转失真，导致任意门的直接校准极其繁琐。因此需要一种既便于校准又能抵抗误差的实用控制方案。

Method: 采用ZXZXZ门分解的控制框架，用固定角度、相位参数化的脉冲，从单一校准的π/2原语（X_{π/2}）配合虚拟Z操作实现普适单比特控制。对比标准脉冲与针对高功率工作区（Rabi频率与超精细劈裂相当、导致超精细能级间演化不均）设计的复合脉冲，并使用预定的π/2脉冲序列组合进行脉冲级表征。

Result: 在重复施加下，标准脉冲迅速偏离理想行为，而复合脉冲尽管持续时间更长，在长序列中仍将误差累积抑制超过70%。相反，随机基准测试对两种实现给出相近的衰减因子，表明其对相干控制误差的敏感度有限。

Conclusion: 研究为短相干系综系统提供了实现鲁棒控制的可行路径，并强调脉冲级表征的重要性，同时指出随机基准测试在评估相干控制误差方面存在局限。

Abstract: Ensemble nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers are widely used for quantum sensing and magnetometry, where performance depends on the accurate implementation of microwave pulse sequences. In these systems, inhomogeneous broadening and short coherence times limit reliable multi-pulse control, while hardware imperfections and off-resonant excitation distort implemented rotations, making direct calibration of arbitrary gates tedious. To address this, we implement a control framework with a ZXZXZ gate decomposition which uses fixed-angle, phase-parameterized pulses, enabling universal single-qubit control from a single calibrated $ π/2$ primitive ($X_{π/2}$) along with virtual Z operations. We compare a standard pulse with a composite pulse designed for high-power operation in a regime where the Rabi frequency is comparable to the hyperfine splitting, leading to non-uniform evolution across hyperfine levels. Using pre-determined combinations of $π/2$ pulse sequences, we observe that standard pulses rapidly deviate from ideal behavior under repeated application, while the composite pulse suppresses this error accumulation by more than 70$\%$ for longer sequences despite its longer duration. In contrast, randomized benchmarking yields similar decay factors for both implementations, highlighting its limited sensitivity to coherent control errors. These results establish a practical route to robust control in short-coherence ensemble systems and emphasize the importance of pulse-level characterization.

</details>


### [129] [Interactions Protect Periodically Driven Time Crystals Against Dephasing but Destabilise Multipolar-Driven Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33574)
*Karthikeya Machiraju,Kaustav Bhowmick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在周期性驱动下更强的相互作用保护时间晶体序抵抗退相干，但在随机多极驱动下反而破坏序；且配位数（相互作用几何）的影响超出总相互作用强度本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究周期驱动时间晶体序在退相干环境下的稳定性如何受相互作用强度与相互作用几何（配位方式）影响，区分"强度"与"配位数"两个通常混杂的因素。

Method: 对N=12和16自旋的无序kicked Ising模型进行精确对角化，包含不完美π脉冲与逐点退相干；比较周期性驱动、n=0,1,2阶随机多极驱动及Thue-Morse序列；并通过在相同总相互作用强度zJ下对比网格与环状几何，将耦合强度与配位数分离。

Result: 周期驱动下相干时间近似按1/γ标度，强耦合降低退相干作用的横向倾斜。对n≥1的多极驱动，环上耦合加倍使相干时间在N=16时缩短至0.54(n=1)、0.37(n=2)、0.44(Thue-Morse)；而随机符号(n=0)不受影响。无序扫描支持"翻转框架"图像，即相互作用破坏脉冲误差的块状抵消。在相同zJ下，周期驱动中网格寿命比环长2.15(N=12)和2.78(N=16)倍，尽管横向倾斜相同，显示超出该图像的配位效应。所有比值在三种相干时间定义下均成立。

Conclusion: 相互作用对时间晶体序的影响依赖驱动类型：周期驱动中强相互作用起保护作用，随机多极驱动中则起破坏作用；配位数在总相互作用强度之外产生独立的稳定化效应，需扩展现有翻转框架理论以涵盖几何效应。

Abstract: We show that stronger interactions protect periodically driven time-crystal order against dephasing but destabilise order under random multipolar drives, and that coordination matters beyond the total interaction strength. We studied a disordered kicked Ising model of $N=12$ and $16$ spins with imperfect $π$ pulses and per-site dephasing by exact diagonalisation, comparing a periodic drive, random multipolar drives of order $n=0,1,2$ and the Thue-Morse sequence, and separated coupling strength from coordination by comparing a grid with a ring at equal total interaction strength $zJ$. For the periodic drive the coherence time scales approximately as $1/γ$, and stronger coupling reduces the transverse tilt on which dephasing acts. For multipolar drives of order $n\ge1$, doubling the coupling on a ring shortens the coherence time to $0.54$ ($n=1$), $0.37$ ($n=2$) and $0.44$ (Thue-Morse) at $N=16$, while random signs ($n=0$) are unaffected. A disorder scan supports a toggling-frame picture in which interactions spoil the block cancellation of the pulse error. At equal $zJ$ the grid outlives the ring by $2.15$ ($N=12$) and $2.78$ ($N=16$) for the periodic drive despite an identical transverse tilt, a coordination effect beyond this picture. All ratios hold under three definitions of the coherence time.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Trotter Orderings for Heisenberg Hamiltonians with a Ranking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31977)
*Shamminuj Aktar,Reuben Tate,Stephan Eidenbenz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出物理感知排序Transformer，直接学习Trotterization的哈密顿量项最优排序，在链和三角/矩形晶格上泛化到更大规模系统，单次前向传播即可生成排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotterization中非对易项引入排序依赖误差，寻找最优项排序是阶乘搜索空间上的组合问题；现有方法依赖固定启发式或在预定义结构化排序中选择，均需先模拟候选再择优，代价高且不灵活。

Method: 使用物理感知的排序Transformer为每个哈密顿量项打标量分数并排序得到顺序；通过交换子偏置注意力与反对易加权排序损失注入物理结构；以模拟退火(SA)参考排序为监督；分别训练一阶与二阶Trotter模型，联合训练最长14量子比特链与12量子比特晶格。

Result: 在未见过的16-20量子比特链和16、20量子比特晶格上评估，以相对SA参考的保真度中位数差距衡量：一阶在链上汇合差距低于10^-4，三角晶格0.0144与0.0088，矩形晶格0.0948与0.0823；二阶对应为0.0114、0.0364与0.0244，0.1348与0.1239。预测在34%一阶和8%二阶链实例上超过SA参考，且因对易项可重排，序列不同时保真度仍可持平。

Conclusion: 学习模型在三种几何结构上均能泛化到更大系统，在链和三角晶格上表现最佳，且无需候选枚举、模拟退火或保真度评估，单次前向传播即可产出排序。

Abstract: Trotterization approximates quantum time evolution by sequentially applying Hamiltonian terms. Because noncommuting terms introduce ordering-dependent errors, selecting an optimal term ordering is a combinatorial problem over a factorial search space. Prior approaches rely on fixed heuristics or on selection among predefined structured orderings, both of which require simulating candidates before choosing among them. For 1D and 2D Heisenberg-style Hamiltonians, we instead learn the ordering directly with a physics-aware ranking transformer that assigns a scalar score to each Hamiltonian term and predicts an ordering by sorting these scores. Physical structure enters through commutator-biased attention and an anticommutation-weighted ranking loss, and the models are trained on simulated-annealing (SA) reference orderings. We train separate models for first- and second-order Trotterization, each jointly on chains up to 14 qubits and lattices up to 12 qubits, and evaluate them on unseen chains with 16-20 qubits and lattices with 16 and 20 qubits. Predicted orderings are evaluated by the median gap in simulation fidelity to the SA reference. At first order, the model reaches a pooled gap below 10^-4 on chains, 0.0144 and 0.0088 on triangular lattices, and 0.0948 and 0.0823 on rectangular lattices at 16 and 20 qubits; at second order, the gaps are 0.0114, 0.0364 and 0.0244, and 0.1348 and 0.1239. The prediction exceeds the SA reference on 34% of first-order and 8% of second-order chain instances and can match its fidelity even when the two sequences differ because commuting terms may be rearranged without changing the Trotter unitary. The learned models generalize to larger systems across all three geometries, performing best on chains and triangular lattices, and produce an ordering in one forward pass without candidate enumeration, simulated annealing, or fidelity evaluation.

</details>


### [131] [Autonomous phase discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33802)
*Shiyu Zhou,Yuxuan Zhang,Sebastian Wetzel,Roger Melko,Xiu-Zhe Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将可微编程与无监督学习结合，构建了完全自主的量子相发现系统，沿自适应轨迹而非预设参数网格搜索基态数据，在多个模型上以等预算显著优于随机采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相研究依赖对称性、拓扑等数学工具与物理直觉，缺乏在未知组织原理的哈密顿量空间中通用探索的手段，且通常需要相标签或预设目标相。

Method: 提出将可微编程与无监督学习结合的自主搜索框架：在自适应轨迹上评估基态数据，而非固定参数网格；以三种不同求解器实现，并在相等基态评估预算下与随机采样进行基准对比。

Result: 在最多含200个不同相的广义团簇链上，等预算下多发现至多25个相，且在三十倍预算下与随机采样持平；在50参数Chern绝缘体上，可到达简单谐振构造未获得的扇区（该族逆问题仍开放），同时覆盖采样找到的所有扇区。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无相标签、无预设目标相的自主梯度驱动哈密顿量空间探索，是发现量子相的实用途径。

Abstract: Understanding quantum phases of matter has long relied on physicists' intuition and mathematical tools such as symmetry and topology. Remarkably successful as these approaches have been, they provide no universal way to explore a Hamiltonian space whose organizing principle is not known in advance. In this work, we introduce a fully autonomous system combining differentiable programming and unsupervised learning for quantum phase discovery. The search evaluates ground-state data along an adaptive trajectory rather than on a predetermined parameter grid. We demonstrate the system with three different solvers and benchmark it against random sampling at equal ground-state-evaluation budgets. On a generalized cluster chain hosting up to $200$ distinct phases, the search finds up to $25$ more phases at the same budget, and matches random sampling given thirty times its budget. On a $50$-parameter Chern insulator, it reaches sectors not obtained by the simple harmonic constructions considered here, in a family whose inverse problem remains open, while recovering all sectors found by sampling. Our results establish autonomous, gradient-driven exploration of Hamiltonian space as a practical route to discovering quantum phases without phase labels or a prescribed target phase.

</details>


### [132] [Robust Entanglement Witnessing via Dense Network Coding with Graph States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32024)
*Brian Doolittle,Ian George*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含噪环境下量子稠密网络编码协议，提出可稳健估计图态有效可见度的半设备无关方法，噪声鲁棒性随发送者数量指数放大，且适用于任意图态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际通信网络中验证与应用基于纠缠的方案，需要含噪条件下可操作的纠缠见证测试；现有方法通常假设理想态或需完全表征测量设备。

Method: 考虑使用图态纠缠实现一族仿射变换的稠密网络编码协议，推导经典网络可达成功概率上界，并将结果应用于半设备无关协议，用以稳健估计图态与测量基的有效可见度。

Result: 噪声鲁棒性随发送者数目呈指数级放大；所提半设备无关协议能够稳健估计有效可见度；方法对噪声鲁棒，支持未表征的态制备或测量。

Conclusion: 该框架为含噪网络中纠缠见证与量子稠密网络编码的实用验证提供了通用、鲁棒且半设备无关的工具，可推广至任意图态。

Abstract: Practical tests for witnessing entanglement in the presence of noise are needed to verify and apply entanglement-based applications in real-world communication networks. Quantum dense network coding is a communication protocol that uses an entangled state preparation together with quantum communication to halve the amount of communication needed to evaluate certain bijective functions in networks having multiple senders and one receiver. In this work, we consider a dense network coding protocol that uses graph state entanglement to implement a family of affine transformations, we derive a bound on the success probability that can be achieved by classical networks, and we show that the noise robustness exponentially amplifies with the number of senders. We apply our results in a semi-device-independent protocol for robustly estimating the effective visibility of graph states and measurement bases. Overall, our approaches are robust to noise, can support the usage of uncharacterized state preparations or measurements, and can be applied to any graph state.

</details>


### [133] [Swapping Quantum Annealing Errors into a Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33975)
*Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"腔量子退火"方案：在退火中段耦合一个频率远大于最小能隙Δ的腔模，将因小能隙导致的错误转入光子，从而显著缩短退火时间并提供结果认证手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火在退火过程中遇到小能隙Δ时会大幅减速，且此时产生的错误无法被检测，限制了其保真度与效率。

Method: 引入腔量子退火：将一个频率为O(1)≫Δ的腔模在退火中段耦合进系统，把错误交换为光子；在p-spin模型上分析退火时间与光子计数；并利用预置光子反向执行交换以制备激发态。

Result: 在p-spin模型中，退火时间缩短至无腔情形的近平方根；通过光子计数实现结果认证，高光子事件（产率O(1)）可更早达到超过99.99%的基态保真度，且该增益随系统规模指数增长。

Conclusion: 腔耦合能够增强基于量子比特的计算，为量子退火实现高保真量子态制备开辟了道路。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising form of quantum computation, but it slows down at a small energy gap $Δ$ during the anneal, and the errors it makes there remain undetected. We introduce cavity quantum annealing, in which a cavity mode of frequency $O(1)\ggΔ$, coupled mid-schedule, swaps these errors into photons. In the $p$-spin model, this reduces the annealing time to nearly the square root of that required without the cavity. Moreover, counting the photons certifies the result: high-photon runs, with an $O(1)$ yield, reach a ground-state fidelity above $99.99\%$ orders of magnitude sooner, a gain that grows exponentially with system size. A pre-loaded photon instead runs the swap in reverse, preparing an excited state. More broadly, our work shows that cavities can enhance computations performed with qubits, paving the way toward high-fidelity quantum state preparation by quantum annealing.

</details>


### [134] [Nonstabilizerness of the entangling layer in quantum circuit Born machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32025)
*Marcin Płodzień*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 纠缠层在量子电路Born机中可仅提供纠缠而不提供非稳定子性：即便采用Clifford纠缠器，只要电路深度足够，训练损失与近Haar纠缠器相当；非稳定子性的增加（纠缠功率不变）不改变损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路Born机常被认为需要纠缠层同时提供纠缠与非稳定子性（nonstabilizerness/magic）。作者要探究：纠缠层是否必须贡献非稳定子性，抑或非稳定子性可由可训练的单量子比特旋转独立提供，从而厘清纠缠、非稳定子性与生成性能之间的关系。

Method: 采用固定（不可训练）纠缠器在每层重复使用、仅优化单量子比特旋转的架构。比较Clifford纠缠器、近Haar纠缠器与自由费米子matchgate纠缠器在足够电路深度下的表现；并设计"掺杂控制"，在保持每个门纠缠功率不变的前提下提升纠缠器的非稳定子性，观察损失变化。此外，理论分析给定Born分布下纯态的最小稳定子Rényi熵。

Result: Clifford纠缠器在足够深度下达到与近Haar纠缠器相同的KL散度；覆盖从零到Haar典型非稳定子性的纠缠器损失相同，仅自由费米子matchgate纠缠器有微小偏移。提升非稳定子性而保持纠缠功率不变的掺杂控制使损失保持不变。使用Clifford纠缠器时，训练态的非稳定子性完全来自旋转，使训练后电路超出稳定子形式体系。并证明在所有给定Born分布目标的纯态中，振幅为概率正平方根的态具有最小稳定子Rényi熵，故该最小值无需训练即可计算。

Conclusion: 纠缠层的非稳定子性并非量子电路Born机性能的必要条件：纠缠层只需提供纠缠，非稳定子性可由单量子比特旋转贡献。损失主要由纠缠功率而非纠缠器的非稳定子性决定，目标Born分布对应的最小稳定子Rényi熵可直接解析计算。

Abstract: The entangling layer of a quantum circuit Born machine can supply both entanglement and nonstabilizerness. In this work we show that the entangling layer can be a Clifford circuit, which supplies no nonstabilizerness. We use an architecture in which a fixed, non-trainable entangler is reused in every layer and only single-qubit rotations are optimized. There, a Clifford entangler reaches the same Kullback--Leibler divergence as a near-Haar one at sufficient circuit depth, and entanglers spanning zero to Haar-typical nonstabilizerness reach the same loss, up to a small offset for a free-fermion matchgate entangler. A doping control that raises the entangler's nonstabilizerness while leaving the entangling power of every gate unchanged leaves the loss flat. With a Clifford entangler the nonstabilizerness of the trained state comes entirely from the rotations, which take the trained circuit outside the stabilizer formalism. We also show that, among all pure states whose Born distribution is a given target, the one whose amplitudes are the positive square roots of the probabilities has the smallest stabilizer Rényi entropy, so this minimum can be computed without training.

</details>


### [135] [Particles, Localization and "Collapse"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32101)
*Anthony Rizzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在本科可理解的层次上，运用量子力学的系综诠释（最小诠释），澄清Hegerfeldt定理及相关结论所依赖的基本原理与前提，并给出一个因坍缩假设而导致错误结果的新例证，从而化解粒子与定域场定域化所引发的"悖论"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hegerfeldt定理表明，在量子力学与狭义相对论的非常一般假设下，粒子（或场）的定域化是不可能的。该定理及后续相关结果对理论的理解与应用提出尖锐质疑：坍缩假设的适用性、粒子乃至定域场的存在性、以及定域测量的可能性均受到挑战。这些根本性问题困扰着研究者与学生，可能损害学生的学习能力乃至对物理学的兴趣，使量子场论及其量子力学极限显得难以理解甚至自相矛盾。

Method: 采用量子力学的自然（最小）诠释即系综诠释，系统阐释并澄清量子力学、Hegerfeldt定理及其在测量中应用所使用的基本原理与前提；并以本科教学可接受的方式展开分析，同时构造一个定性上新的、因坍缩假设而产生错误结果的例子。

Result: 给出了一个定性上新的例子，说明由坍缩假设所导致的错误结果；并通过系综诠释与对基本前提的澄清，消解了围绕粒子定域化、定域场存在性与定域测量的表面矛盾。

Conclusion: Hegerfeldt定理所带来的困扰并非源于量子场论或量子力学本身的不一致，而是源于对坍缩假设的不当使用与对基本前提的混淆；借助系综诠释可对这些根本问题给出连贯、自洽且在本科层面可理解的处理。

Abstract: Hegerfeldt's theorem shows that the localization of a particle (or field) is not possible under very general assumptions of quantum mechanics and special relativity. This theorem, and further developments and associated theorems force penetrating questions on the proper understanding of the theory and its use. In particular, the use of the collapse postulate is called into question. The existence of particles and even localized fields are called into question. And, this in turn challenges local measurements. It generally forces one to consider the profound problems and even wrong results that arise from the collapse postulate. We give a qualitatively new example of such a wrong result. Researchers and students alike are bothered by these fundamental issues. Students' ability to learn the material, and even their interest in physics, can be compromised by such disturbing conundra that seem to make physics, or at least QFT and its QM limit, seem incomprehensible and even inconsistent. This article resolves these issues in a way accessible to undergraduates, by applying the natural (minimal) interpretation of quantum mechanics (QM) (the ensemble interpretation) and explicating and clarifying the basic principles and premises used in QM and the Hegerfeldt theorem and its application to measurements.

</details>


### [136] [Counterdiabatic quasi-Floquet control for the generation of entangled BICs using giant atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32125)
*Alexis R. Legón,Pedro Orellana,Ariel Norambuena*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 采用双编织巨原子构型与量子控制技术，提出鲁棒制备高保真纠缠BIC的方案，无损模型中全态BIC保真度0.99992，Bell保真度0.99588，并验证了有限模式与噪声下的控制容限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC为波导量子电动力学中保持纠缠提供机制，但需鲁棒且高保真地制备纠缠态（含完整缀饰BIC），并明确噪声与控制误差下的可行窗口。

Method: 在双编织巨原子构型中，将准Floquet调制有效原子-波导耦合与独立相位控制的原子交换结合，提供受限counterdiabatic辅助以制备完整缀饰BIC；并通过有限模式微观模拟及含弛豫、退相干的标定误差扫描进行验证。

Result: 无损有效模型快控协议下，全态BIC保真度0.99992，无条件原子Bell保真度0.99588；有限模式模拟验证穿越过程的中心-边带描述；静态标定误差扫描给出控制容限；含弛豫与退相干的微观研究（实验尺度参数）识别出有限有用纠缠窗口，且耦合包络整形有益。

Conclusion: 在所述控制与噪声假设下，支持缀饰纠缠的可控制备、保持与回收。研究为BIC纠缠的鲁棒量子控制提供可行路径，但结论受模型与噪声假设约束。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) provide a mechanism for preserving entanglement in waveguide quantum electrodynamics. Here, using quantum control techniques in a configuration of two braided giant atoms, we propose a robust approach to create high-fidelity entangled BICs. The protocol combines quasi-Floquet modulation of the effective atom--waveguide couplings with an independent phase-controlled atomic exchange, which provides restricted counterdiabatic assistance for preparing the complete dressed BIC. In the lossless effective model, we obtain a full-state BIC fidelity of $0.99992$ and an unconditional atomic Bell fidelity of $0.99588$ using a fast control protocol. Microscopic finite-mode simulations validate the central-sideband description during the passage, while static calibration-error scans quantify its control tolerances. A separate microscopic study with relaxation and dephasing, using experimentally motivated component scales, identifies a finite useful entanglement window and a benefit from shaping the coupling envelopes. These results support controlled preparation, retention, and retrieval of dressed entanglement within the stated control and noise assumptions.

</details>


### [137] [Inverse-Designed Nanobeam Cavities for High-Cooperativity Spin-Photon Interfaces in Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32205)
*Nail Letty,Md Sakibul Islam,Wayesh Qarony*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对硅中T色心与Al1色心，采用统一伴随优化框架逆向设计纳米梁腔，实现高Purcell增强、共振对准与定向光子提取，获得高协同度与近满的腔耦合发射效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信波段硅自旋缺陷（T色心、Al1色心）相干零声子发射有限，需强腔增强才能实现高效自旋-光子接口，亟需可规模化、工艺兼容的腔设计方法。

Method: 建立统一的伴随优化（adjoint-optimization）框架，同时以Purcell增强、共振对准和光子提取为目标，对两种色心分别设计对称腔（最大化Purcell增强）与非对称腔（定向耦合至集成波导），并用三维有限差分时域（FDTD）仿真验证。

Result: 负载品质因子最高达3.25×10^6，Purcell因子最高1.43×10^5，协同度约280–5000，腔耦合发射比例超过99.6%；非对称腔将约90%发射功率导入单一片上波导且协同度仍高于280。在纳米级制造扰动及吸收受限品质因子10^5约束下，四种设计仍保持高协同度。

Conclusion: 该统一且工艺兼容的框架为硅电信自旋-光子接口提供了可规模化的腔设计路径，并据作者所知是首个针对Al1色心的腔设计。

Abstract: Telecom-band spin defects in silicon offer a compelling platform for integrated quantum networks by combining scalable silicon photonics with spin-based quantum memories and multiqubit registers. The T center and recently demonstrated Al1 center are particularly promising, but their limited coherent zero-phonon emission requires strong cavity enhancement for efficient spin-photon interfaces. Here, we inverse-design silicon nanobeam cavities for both centers using a unified adjoint-optimization framework targeting Purcell enhancement, resonance alignment, and photon extraction. For each emitter, symmetric cavities maximize Purcell enhancement, while asymmetric cavities provide directional coupling to an integrated waveguide. Three-dimensional finite-difference time-domain simulations yield loaded quality factors up to 3.25 x 10^6, Purcell factors up to 1.43 x 10^5, and cooperativities of approximately 280-5000, corresponding to cavity-coupled emission fractions exceeding 99.6%. The asymmetric cavities direct approximately 90% of emitted power into a single on-chip waveguide while maintaining cooperativity above 280. Under nanometer-scale fabrication perturbations, all four designs remain within the high-cooperativity regime, and high cooperativity persists when the cavity quality factor is constrained to a realistic absorption-limited value of 10^5. This unified, fabrication-compatible framework establishes a scalable cavity-design route for silicon telecom spin-photon interfaces and, to our knowledge, the first cavity designs for the Al1 center.

</details>


### [138] [Contour-integral and Fourier transform based multivariable quantum eigenvalue transformation for commuting matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32262)
*Shan Jiang,Dong An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子计算机上多变量矩阵值函数的实现，提出两种作用于两两交换矩阵元组的量子算法：基于多变量围道积分的全纯函数算法，以及基于高维傅里叶变换的光滑厄米函数算法，并分析其复杂度及混合量子-经典变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量矩阵函数在量子模拟与量子线性代数中具有重要应用，但其高效量子实现尚缺乏系统性研究，需要针对任意全纯函数与光滑厄米矩阵函数设计可行算法并控制资源开销。

Method: 第一种算法利用多变量围道积分，适用于 C^n 上的任意全纯函数；第二种算法基于高维傅里叶变换，针对交换厄米矩阵的光滑函数。两者均借助量子奇异值变换、压缩 gadget 与酉算子线性组合进行量子实现并分析复杂度。此外研究混合量子-经典变体。

Result: 混合量子-经典变体将所需辅助量子比特数降至对变量数的对数依赖，代价是查询复杂度增加；作为应用，多变量矩阵多项式可在无显式次数依赖下实现，仅取决于多项式的全局性质。

Conclusion: 所提算法为多变量矩阵值函数提供了可行的量子实现方案，在辅助比特与查询复杂度之间可实现权衡，并给出多变量矩阵多项式的高效实现途径。

Abstract: We study the problem of implementing multivariable matrix-valued functions on a quantum computer and propose two quantum algorithms for multivariable matrix eigenvalue transformations acting on tuples of pairwise commuting matrices. The first algorithm is based on multivariable contour integrals and applies to arbitrary holomorphic functions on $\mathbb{C}^n$. The second algorithm is based on high-dimensional Fourier transforms and designed for smooth functions of commuting Hermitian matrices. For both algorithms, we discuss and analyze the complexity of their quantum implementation leveraging quantum singular value transformation, compression gadgets, and linear combination of unitaries. We additionally study their hybrid quantum--classical variants that reduce the number of required ancilla qubits to logarithmic dependence on the number of variables, at the cost of increased query complexity. As an application, we show that multivariable matrix polynomials can be implemented with no additional explicit degree dependence, depending only on global properties of the polynomials.

</details>


### [139] [Correlation measure for statistical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34859)
*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种适用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡统计系统的"关联指数"（correlation index）作为关联性度量，给出其一般定义并通过多个密度算符与约化密度矩阵示例展示计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计系统中需要一个既能刻画量子体系又能刻画经典体系、且同时适用于平衡态与非平衡态的普适关联度量，现有度量往往局限于特定体系或特定情形。

Method: 对任意迹类算符（trace-class operators）定义关联指数的一般概念，并针对多种密度算符与约化密度矩阵进行具体计算；平衡态方面以含玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的玻色系统、超导系统和自旋系统为例，非平衡态方面以自旋与赝自旋系统的非线性动力学为例。

Result: 在所列举的玻色凝聚、超导、自旋等平衡系统及自旋/赝自旋非线性动力学等非平衡系统中，均给出了关联指数的具体计算示例，表明该度量具有普适性与可计算性。

Conclusion: 关联指数是一种普适的关联度量，可统一处理量子与经典、平衡与非平衡统计系统，并可通过密度算符直接计算。

Abstract: A measure of correlations for statistical systems is advanced, called correlation index. The measure is general, being applicable to quantum and classical, equilibrium and nonequilibrium statistical systems. After formulating the general notion of correlation indices for arbitrary trace-class operators, the calculation of the correlation indices are illustrated by several examples of density operators and reduced density matrices. The computation of the correlation indices for equilibrium systems is exemplified for bosonic systems with the Bose-Einstein condensate, superconducting systems, and for spin systems. Correlation indices for nonequilibrium systems are considered for nonlinear dynamics of spin and pseudospin systems.

</details>


### [140] [Toroidal-Core Certificates for PPT-Squared Diagonal Orthogonal Covariant Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32979)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对DOC类量子信道，给出了PPT-squared复合为纠缠破坏（EB）的一个充分条件，并构造了一个两两复合均为EB但单个信道非EB的信道族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT-squared猜想（两个PPT信道的复合是否必然EB）在一般情况下仍未解决，而在对角正交协变（DOC）类中，首个未解决的确定性维度是四维，因此需要一个可用于高维的可验证充分条件。
