TL;DR: 研究稳定子码最小距离的近似计算复杂度，闭合了此前加法近似缺口的差距，并开创了细粒度复杂度研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最小距离问题即使有线性加法误差也是NP难的，而量子码此前仅能达到加法O(√N)缺口，故需闭合这一差距并开展细粒度研究。

Method: 通过复杂性归约证明：(1) 除非NP ⊆ coRP，否则无随机算法可在加法αN（α>0常数）内近似距离；(2) 细粒度下，除非SETH失败，否则任何随机2^{(1-ε)κ}poly(N)算法无法计算含κ逻辑量子比特码的距离；(3) 除非非均匀Gap-ETH失败，否则无随机2^{o(N)}算法在块长度、逻辑量子比特数与缺口同时线性时近似距离。

Result: 证明了加法αN近似为NP难（除非NP ⊆ coRP）；细粒度下分别建立基于SETH和非均匀Gap-ETH的下界。

Conclusion: 稳定子码最小距离的近似问题在加法线性缺口下是NP难的，细粒度下也需亚指数时间，闭合了此前O(√N)缺口的空白。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of approximating the minimum distance of a stabilizer code. Classically, the minimum distance problem is NP-hard to approximate even to within an additive gap linear in the block length. For quantum codes, however, the reductions of Kapshikar and Kundu and of Grigorescu, Jha and Samperton reach only an additive $O(\sqrt{N})$ gap in the block length $N$. We close this gap and, in a separate direction, initiate the fine-grained study of the problem.
  We show that no randomized algorithm approximates the distance to within an additive $αN$, for a constant $α> 0$, unless $\mathsf{NP} \subseteq \mathsf{coRP}$. In the fine-grained setting, for every $\varepsilon > 0$, no randomized $2^{(1-\varepsilon)κ}\,\mathrm{poly}(N)$-time algorithm computes the distance of a code with $κ$ logical qubits unless SETH falls; and no randomized $2^{o(N)}$-time algorithm approximates the distance to within a linear additive gap, on instances where block length, number of logical qubits, and gap are simultaneously linear, unless non-uniform Gap-ETH falls.

</details>


### [178] [QBX: A Compiler for 2-local Qubit Hamiltonian Simulation on Quantum Chiplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33997)
*Zikun Li,Zhuoming Chen,Zhihao Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 QBX，首个面向量子 chiplet 架构的 2-local qubit Hamiltonian 模拟编译器，通过分层优化与 highway 机制降低跨 chiplet 通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-local qubit Hamiltonian 模拟是量子计算的基础任务，应用广泛。现有通用 chiplet 编译器仅工作在门级，无法利用 Hamiltonian 模拟的高层语义进行优化；现有领域专用 Hamiltonian 模拟编译器面向单体架构，可扩展性有限且难以适配 chiplet 的异构性，导致电路输出次优。

Method: QBX 采用可扩展的分层方法，同时减少跨 chiplet 通信的数量与距离；集成面向 chiplet 的 highway 机制以实现高效长距离 qubit 通信，降低跨 chiplet 交互成本；并将 2-local Pauli 串高效聚合为多目标受控门，利用 highway 进行深度优化。

Result: 评估显示 QBX 在电路深度和操作数上显著优于通用编译器与领域专用编译器，并可扩展到更大规模的 2-local Hamiltonian 模拟。

Conclusion: QBX 是首个面向 chiplet 架构的 2-local qubit Hamiltonian 模拟编译器，验证了结合高层语义与 chiplet 特性的分层优化在性能与可扩展性上的优势。

Abstract: 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation, a fundamental task in quantum computing, is widely applied in various applications. This paper presents QBX, the first quantum compiler designed for 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation on quantum chiplet architectures. Existing general-purpose quantum compilers for chiplet architectures target quantum programs at the gate level and miss optimizations requiring high-level semantics of Hamiltonian simulation. Conversely, existing domain-specific compilers for Hamiltonian simulation are designed for monolithic architectures, and their limited scalability and inability to adapt to the heterogeneity of chiplet architectures lead to sub-optimal circuit outputs. QBX implements a scalable hierarchical approach to reduce both the amount and distance of cross-chiplet communications. It integrates the highway mechanism, a chiplet-oriented solution for efficient long-range qubit communication, to diminish cross-chiplet interaction costs. Furthermore, QBX efficiently aggregates 2-local Pauli strings into multi-target controlled gates, harnessing highways for deep optimization. Our evaluations show that QBX markedly outperforms both general-purpose and domain-specific compilers in circuit depth and operation count, while scaling to much larger 2-local Hamiltonian simulations.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum memory in an effective $Λ$-type system using dynamic detuning control of a superconducting qubit pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34201)
*Hsin Chang,Kai-I Chu,Ite A. Yu,Yen-Hsiang Lin,Yung-Fu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了由一对可调超导量子比特与半无限传输线耦合、间隔半波长构成的有效Λ型系统中的量子光存储，通过调控比特失谐实现慢光、动态映射与按需读取，可作为最简可调量子存储架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为可扩展超导量子网络寻求一种结构最简、参数可调的量子存储架构，实现入射量子场的长期存储与按需读取。

Method: 构建有效Λ型系统：两个可调超导量子比特耦合到半无限传输线，间距为半个波长；利用量子比特间的频率失谐直接耦合超辐射激发态与亚辐射亚稳态，并调节失谐以改变系统光谱响应与慢光特性，动态控制失谐完成量子场的写入、存储与读取。

Result: 系统呈现可控的光谱响应并伴随慢光行为；通过动态调节失谐，入射量子场可被映射到亚辐射集体态，作为长寿命激发存储，并可随后按需读取。

Conclusion: 该方案可作为一种最简且可调的量子存储架构，适用于可扩展的超导量子网络。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate quantum-light storage in an effective $Λ$-type system formed by a pair of tunable superconducting qubits coupled to a semi-infinite transmission line and separated by half a wavelength. The frequency detuning between the qubits directly couples the superradiant excited state and the subradiant metastable state. By adjusting the qubit detuning, the system exhibits a controllable spectral response accompanied by slow-light behavior. Dynamically controlling the detuning enables an incident quantum field to be mapped onto the subradiant collective state, stored as a long-lived excitation, and subsequently retrieved on demand. Our results establish the proposed system as a minimal and tunable quantum-memory architecture for scalable superconducting quantum networks.

</details>


### [180] [Monotonicity of the Rényi channel capacity under non-signaling assisted channel simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34213)
*Tina Shariat,Matt Hoogsteder-Riera,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究非信号盒（non-signaling boxes）能否提升经典信道的Rényi容量，证明其不能在任何阶数上提升Rényi容量，因此也无法改善随机编码、球填充或强逆指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非信号盒类严格大于共享随机性辅助的编解码器对，因此基于独立预处理和后处理的传统论证不适用；同时非信号盒约束对盒是线性的，而Rényi容量对信道呈非线性，这一不匹配使得问题难以直接处理。

Method: 采用基于Legendre变换的变分方法，将容量重新表述为信道线性出现的一族仿射泛函上的极值问题；并结合对Shannon容量与端点阶数的单独论证。

Result: 证明非信号盒不能在任意阶数上提升Rényi容量，范围覆盖从零阶、Shannon容量到无穷极限阶。由此，非信号模拟不能提升容量以下的随机编码指数或球填充指数，也不能降低容量以上的强逆指数。

Conclusion: 非信号关联无法增强经典信道的Rényi容量，这一结论统一覆盖了从零阶到无穷阶的整个阶数范围，并推广至相关的编码与逆定理指数。

Abstract: We ask whether non-signaling correlations shared between the sender and receiver can increase the Rényi capacity of a classical channel. Non-signaling boxes form a strictly larger class than that of encoder and decoder pairs assisted by shared randomness, so the usual argument based on independent preprocessing and postprocessing is insufficient, and the constraints defining such boxes are linear in the box, while the Rényi capacity depends on the channel nonlinearly. We resolve this mismatch with a variational approach based on the Legendre transform, which recasts the capacity as an extremum over a family of affine functionals in which the channel appears linearly. Combining this method with separate arguments for the Shannon capacity and endpoint orders, we prove that no non-signaling box can raise the Rényi capacity at any order, from order zero through the Shannon capacity to the limiting order at infinity. Consequently, non-signaling simulation cannot increase the random-coding or sphere-packing exponent below capacity, or decrease the strong-converse exponent above capacity.

</details>


### [181] [A threshold for maximal Schmidt number from spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34255)
*Junhyeong An,Soojoon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双分体量子态谱与Schmidt数非最大性之间的关系，证明当最大特征值不超过p个最小特征值之和（p ≤ ⌊d²/2⌋）时，态属于ASN_{d-1}，并给出APPT结果的推广及反例说明全局酉变换条件的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schmidt数刻画双分体量子态纠缠的维度，判定其是否非最大（即 ≤ d-1）对纠缠检测具有重要意义。本文旨在探究仅凭谱信息能否保证Schmidt数非最大，并推广已有的绝对正部分转置（APPT）态结果。

Method: 通过推导(d-1)-块正算子的谱界，证明LS_p ⊆ ASN_{d-1}（p ≤ ⌊d²/2⌋），其中LS_p为最大特征值不超过p个最小特征值之和的态集，ASN_{d-1}为在所有全局酉变换下Schmidt数保持≤ d-1的态集。由此得到仅涉及最大特征值的充分条件。

Result: 证明了LS_p ⊆ ASN_{d-1}（p ≤ ⌊d²/2⌋）；给出仅依赖最大特征值的简单充分条件；证明在任意全局酉变换下满足reduction criterion的态属于ASN_{d-1}（任意维度），推广了APPT态属于ASN_{d-1}的先前结论；并构造反例：对任意d ≥ 3，存在满足两子系统reduction criterion但Schmidt数最大的态。

Conclusion: 谱条件可在一定范围内保证Schmidt数非最大，全局酉变换下满足reduction criterion的态属于ASN_{d-1}，但全局酉要求是本质的，因为存在满足reduction criterion却具有最大Schmidt数的态。

Abstract: The Schmidt number quantifies the dimensionality of entanglement in bipartite quantum states. We investigate when the spectrum of a state on $\mathbb{C}^d \otimes \mathbb{C}^d$ alone guarantees that its Schmidt number is not maximal. By deriving spectral bounds for $(d-1)$-block positive operators, we prove that $\mathrm{LS}_p\subseteq \mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ for $p\leq \lfloor d^2/2\rfloor$, where $\mathrm{LS}_p$ denotes the set of states whose largest eigenvalue does not exceed the sum of their $p$ smallest eigenvalues, and $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ denotes the set of states whose Schmidt number remains at most $d-1$ under all global unitaries. This also yields a simple sufficient condition involving only the largest eigenvalue. As an application, we show that states satisfying the reduction criterion under arbitrary global unitaries belong to $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ in every dimension. This subsumes the earlier result that absolutely positive partial transpose states belong to $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$. The global unitary requirement is nevertheless essential: for every $d\geq3$, we construct states of maximal Schmidt number satisfying the reduction criterion on both subsystems.

</details>


### [182] [Optimal Quantum Algorithms for Ordered Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34293)
*Joseph Carolan,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了有序搜索问题的量子查询复杂度精确常数问题，提出两种达到最优查询数 (1/π)ln n + o(log n) 的量子算法：一种为零错误连续松弛算法，另一种为基于多项式规划解析解的精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序搜索中经典二分搜索需 ⌈log₂ n⌉ 次查询且最优；量子算法可带来常数因子加速，但该常数长期未确定，本文旨在精确刻画该最优常数。

Method: 提出两种量子算法：其一是由问题连续松弛导出的简单零错误算法；其二基于 Farhi、Goldstone、Gutmann 与 Sipser 多项式规划的解析解构造精确算法。

Result: 两种新算法均使用最优的 (1/π)ln n + o(log n) 次查询，从而闭合了量子有序搜索最优常数这一长期开放问题。

Conclusion: 量子有序搜索的最优查询复杂度常数为 1/π，两种算法分别在零错误与精确设定下达到该界，完整刻画了该问题的量子查询复杂度。

Abstract: Ordered search is the problem of locating a target element in a sorted list of size $n$ using comparison queries. Classically, binary search requires $\lceil \log_2 n\rceil$ queries, which is optimal. Quantum algorithms offer a constant-factor speedup, but the precise constant has been a longstanding open question. We close this gap by exhibiting two new quantum algorithms for ordered search, each using the optimal $\frac{1}π\ln n+o(\log n)$ queries. The first, discovered by Claude Fable 5, is a simple zero-error algorithm derived from a continuum relaxation of the problem. The second, discovered by GPT-5.6-Sol (informed by Claude's zero-error algorithm), is an exact algorithm based on an analytic solution of the polynomial program of Farhi, Goldstone, Gutmann, and Sipser.

</details>


### [183] [Observation of a topological edge state among localized bulk states in the anisotropic quantum Rabi model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34332)
*Sungjoo Lim,Chanyang Im,Christopher G. Yale,Brian K. McFarland,Edward C. Tortorici,Daniel S. Lobser,Melissa C. Revelle,Susan M. Clark,Mahn-Soo Choi,Junki Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在囚禁离子量子模拟器中研究各向异性量子Rabi模型（AQRM）的拓扑边缘态（TES）与本征态，提出用内禀拓扑特征（手性与自旋-玻色可分性）而非空间局域性来区分TES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相由离散对称性保护，但当平移周期性缺失时，体态也变为局域态，使得TES无法仅凭空间局域性加以区分，因此需要寻找新的、内禀的拓扑判据。

Method: 在一维合成晶格中利用囚禁离子量子模拟器实现AQRM；该模型的平移对称性被随格点指数变化的非均匀耦合所破坏，随后对本征态进行表征并结合相空间层析（tomography）。

Result: TES与本征态均呈局域分布，但TES表现出明确的手性以及近乎完全的自旋-玻色可分性，而体态不具备这些特征；相空间层析显示TES的玻色子部分为压缩真空态，压缩度高达6.45 dB。

Conclusion: 通过内禀拓扑特征可识别TES，并确立“本征态层面表征”作为探测拓扑现象的有效途径。

Abstract: Topological phases are governed by discrete symmetries that protect boundary modes against local perturbations. When translational periodicity is absent, the bulk states also become localized, so that a topological edge state can no longer be distinguished from them by spatial localization alone. Here, we investigate the topological edge state (TES) and bulk eigenstates of the anisotropic quantum Rabi model (AQRM) in a trapped-ion quantum simulator. The AQRM hosts a topological phase in a one-dimensional synthetic lattice, whose translational symmetry is broken by the non-uniform couplings scaling with the site index. While both the TES and bulk states show localized distributions, we find that the TES exhibits well-defined chirality and near-complete spin--boson separability as signatures of the topological phase, in contrast to the bulk states. Phase-space tomography further reveals that the bosonic component of the TES is a squeezed vacuum state, with squeezing up to 6.45 dB. These results identify the TES through its intrinsic topological signatures and establish eigenstate-level characterization as a route to probing topological phenomena.

</details>


### [184] [Cavity magnonics and bound states in the continuum with Bragg and anti-Bragg mirrors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34365)
*B. -Y. Wu,K. -P. Li,G. Chen,W. -M. Zhou,C. -X. Run,K. -M. Hsieh,A. F. Kockum,Z. -R. Lin,W. Nie,I. -C. Hoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双开放波导腔磁子学平台上，利用两个周期性铁磁球阵列构成Bragg与anti-Bragg反射镜，实验实现了束缚态于连续谱（BIC），并通过可移动探针球对腔场进行位置依赖探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性谐振发射体阵列耦合于一维波导可充当反射镜（如Bragg镜），两个此类镜构成可承载BIC的有效腔；但BIC空间分布的生成与探测此前极具挑战，需要一种灵活可调、可空间扫描的实验平台。

Method: 采用双开放波导架构，以两个周期性亚铁磁球阵列作为反射镜、一个探针球置于腔区。分别构造晶格常数不同的Bragg腔与anti-Bragg腔，通过移动探针球实现位置依赖的腔场探测，并测量超模与探针磁子耦合形成的极化子劈裂随探针尺寸和反射镜球数的变化。

Result: 在Bragg腔中，反射镜球形成简并超模，单一暗超模与腔区探针磁子相干耦合，产生两个极化子，其劈裂随探针球尺寸与反射镜球数同时增大；anti-Bragg腔则在带隙中出现明、暗超模，显著改变磁子-腔相互作用；移动探针球实现了腔场的位置依赖探测，凸显BIC的作用。

Conclusion: 该可扫描探针的灵活实验平台为探测光-物质相互作用产生的其他奇异态、与超导电路接口构建混合量子网络，以及利用Bragg与anti-Bragg腔研究非厄米物理提供了可能。

Abstract: A periodic resonant emitter array coupled to a one-dimensional waveguide can act as a mirror. A typical example is a Bragg mirror, where emitters are spaced by multiples of half wavelengths and collectively enhance light reflection. Two such mirrors form an effective cavity that hosts bound states in the continuum (BICs). However, generating and detecting the spatial profiles of such BICs has proven challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate BICs in cavity magnonics using two periodic ferrimagnetic-sphere arrays and a probe sphere in a dual-open-waveguide architecture. We realize both Bragg and anti-Bragg cavities, whose mirrors have different lattice constants. In the Bragg cavity, there are degenerate supermodes formed by mirror spheres. We show that a single dark supermode coherently couples to the probe magnon in the cavity region, forming two polaritons whose splitting scales with both the probe-sphere size and the number of mirror spheres. By contrast, the anti-Bragg cavity has bright and dark supermodes in a bandgap, substantially changing the magnon-cavity interaction. Moreover, by moving the probe sphere, we perform position-dependent detection of the cavity field, highlighting the role of BICs in these cavities. Our flexible experimental setup with a scanning probe opens possibilities to detect other exotic states created by light-matter interaction, to interface with superconducting circuits in hybrid quantum networks, and to study non-Hermitian physics with Bragg and anti-Bragg cavities.

</details>


### [185] [Experimental Realization and Phase-Space Winding of a Topological Defect State in the Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34389)
*Kyungmin Lee,Jiyong Kang,Sunkyu Yu,Jaehun You,Wonhyeong Choi,Taehyun Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单个囚禁${}^{171}\mathrm{Yb}^{+}$离子中实验实现了量子Rabi模型的拓扑缺陷态，利用Fock态晶格表示中振荡器的内禀依赖粒子数耦合构建可调界面，并通过特征函数层析重构运动相空间分布，观测到缠绕数为1和0的Wigner质心轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非均匀合成晶格中的拓扑缺陷态物理与混合自旋-玻色系统的可测相空间几何联系起来，探索量子Rabi模型中拓扑缺陷态的实验实现与表征。

Method: 在单个囚禁${}^{171}\mathrm{Yb}^{+}$离子平台上，利用振荡器在Fock态晶格表示中的内禀依赖粒子数耦合形成可调界面，独立制备各边带相位设置下的态，采用特征函数层析重构运动相空间分布；通过改变载波幅度进行动力学探测。

Result: 重构的Wigner质心轨迹在两种耦合区域分别表现出缠绕数为1和0；改变载波幅度仅重缩放轨迹而保持缠绕数不变；载波幅度调制给出与缺陷态激发间隙及可及跃迁通道一致的自旋响应。

Conclusion: 实验将非均匀合成晶格中的拓扑缺陷态物理与混合自旋-玻色系统的可测相空间几何相联系，为量子Rabi模型中拓扑缺陷态的相空间表征提供了实验依据。

Abstract: We experimentally realize a topological defect state of the quantum Rabi model in a single trapped $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}^{+}$ ion. The state is supported by a tunable interface arising from the oscillator's intrinsic number-dependent coupling in the Fock-state-lattice representation. We independently prepare the state at each sideband-phase setting and reconstruct its motional phase-space distribution using characteristic-function tomography. The resulting Wigner-centroid trajectories exhibit winding numbers of one and zero in the two coupling regimes. Changing the carrier amplitude rescales the trajectories while preserving their windings. Carrier-amplitude modulation provides a complementary dynamical probe, yielding a spin response consistent with the excitation gaps and accessible transition channels from the defect state. The experiment connects topological defect-state physics in a nonuniform synthetic lattice to the measurable phase-space geometry of a hybrid spin-boson system.

</details>


### [186] [Coherence Rather Than Error Rate Governs Privacy in Multi-Tenant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34411)
*Farhad Farokhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于量子态可区分性的信息论泄漏度量，量化多租户量子云中跨租户边界的信息泄漏；在IBM Kingston（156量子比特）与IQM Garnet（20量子比特）上实测表明，平均门错误率无法反映泄漏程度，并证明全寄存器Pauli随机化可将泄漏降至零但会带来误差累积导致的保真度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业量子云计算允许供应商将同一量子处理器的不相交分区租给不同用户。云厂商公布的平均门错误率并不能说明一个租户能从另一个租户处获知多少信息，因此需要一种能刻画共租边界信息泄漏的度量，以评估隐私与安全风险。

Method: 提出基于量子态可区分性的信息论信息泄漏度量，用于量化共租边界处的泄漏；在商用IBM Kingston（156量子比特）与IQM Garnet（20量子比特）设备上实测该泄漏；对比具有相同基准错误率的不同边界；并采用覆盖受害者整个寄存器的统一Pauli随机化作为防御手段。

Result: 基准错误率相同的边界可表现出显著不同的信息泄漏量，因为标准报告的错误度量对错误是相干性还是随机性不敏感，而所提泄漏度量可以区分。统一Pauli随机化可将信息泄漏降为零，理论上无保真度代价，但实验显示误差累积会导致非平凡的保真度退化。

Conclusion: 平均门错误率不足以刻画多租户量子云的信息泄漏风险。作者呼吁量子云计算供应商在设备数据表中除标准错误率外，还应报告信息泄漏指标，以便用户在与设备交互前评估隐私与安全风险。

Abstract: Multi-tenant computing enables providers of commercial cloud quantum processors to rent disjoint sectors of a device to independent users. Average gate error, which cloud quantum computing providers report, does not determine how much one tenant learns about another. We propose an information-theoretic notion of information leakage across co-tenancy boundaries stemming from quantum state distinguishability. We measure this leakage on commercially-available 156-qubit (IBM Kingston) and 20-qubit (IQM Garnet) devices. Boundaries with identical benchmarked error can offer significantly different amount of information leakage because standard reported measures of error are blind to coherent-versus-stochastic nature of the error while the proposed notion of information leakage is not. A uniform Pauli randomisation implemented over the victim's whole register is used as a defence mechanism to reduce the information leakage to zero. The defence theoretically does not incur a fidelity cost, but the experiments show a non-trivial degradation caused by accumulation of errors. We provide a specific call-for-action to the providers of quantum cloud computing to report information leakage in addition to standard error rates in their device datasheet to enable users to compute privacy and security risks prior to engagement with the device.

</details>


### [187] [Quantum Security of XOR of Permutations via Fourier Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34413)
*Wonseok Choi,Minki Hhan,Junyoung Jang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $r\\ge 2$ 个独立随机置换的异或（XoP）构造在量子叠加查询下仍具超越生日界的安全性，给出了任意 $q$-查询量子算法的区分优势上界，是首个达到量子超越生日界安全性的基于置换的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XoP 是从置换构建伪随机函数的原型构造，其经典安全性已确立，但其在量子叠加查询攻击下的安全性长期未决。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索 XoP 能否在量子环境中保持超越生日界的安全性。

Method: 采用傅里叶分析形式的多项式方法：任意 $q$-查询量子算法的优势由至多 $2q$ 次傅里叶分量或 $2q$ 组输入输出数据控制。对高次分量用范数估计直接界住；对低次分量（度-2 与度-4 项），将其重新解释为带/不带植入碰撞的随机函数问题的优势，再用 Zhandry 的小范围分布界定，并证明了大范围下的小范围不可区分性新界。

Result: 证明了对所有 $q\\ll 2^n$，$q$-查询量子算法的区分优势为 $O(\\min\\{q^3/2^{rn},\\ q^{1.5}/2^{(r-0.5)n},\\ 1/2^{(r-1.5)n}\\})$。XoP 在整个查询范围内保持安全，远超量子碰撞搜索攻击的 $2^{n/3}$ 界限。同时给出若干启发式攻击，表明在 $q\\le 2^{n/2}$ 及约 $2^n$ 范围内界是紧的。

Conclusion: XoP 是首个在量子环境中实现超越生日界安全性的基于置换的构造；所提傅里叶多项式方法及大范围小范围不可区分性新界具有独立价值。

Abstract: The XOR of two or more independent random permutations (XoP) is the prototypical pseudorandom function built from permutations achieving security beyond the birthday bound. The classical security of the XoP construction is well established, but its security against quantum attacks that query XoP in superposition has remained widely open.
  We prove that the XOR of $r\ge 2$ random permutations over $\{0,1\}^n$ is indistinguishable from a random function by any $q$-query quantum algorithm with advantage \[O\left(\min\left\{\frac{q^3}{2^{rn}},\frac{q^{1.5}}{2^{(r-0.5)n}},\frac{1}{2^{(r-1.5)n}}\right\}\right)\] for all $q\ll 2^n$. In particular, XoP remains secure throughout the entire query range, far beyond the $2^{n/3}$ bound due to quantum collision finding attacks. This is the first construction from permutations that achieves the quantum version of the beyond birthday bound security. We also present several heuristic attacks suggesting the tightness of our bounds in ranges $q\le 2^{n/2}$ and $\approx 2^n$.
  We use a Fourier-analytic variant of the polynomial method: the advantage of any $q$-query quantum algorithm is controlled by the Fourier components of degree at most $2q$, or by $2q$ input-output data of the construction. The norms of most components are bounded well, proving the bound $2^{-(r-3/2)n}$.
  The norm of low-degree components turn out to be too large for the bounds $q^3/2^{rn}$ and $q^{1.5}/2^{(r-0.5)n}$. We reinterpret these low-degree components as (sums of) advantages of the other problems. For example, the degree-2 and degree-4 terms are interpreted as the advantages against random functions with and without \emph{planted collisions}, which in turn are bounded using Zhandry's small-range distributions. Along the way, we prove a new bound for the small-range indistinguishability for (ironically) large ranges, which is of independent interest.

</details>


### [188] [Excitation Gap of a Confined Chiral Mode Measures Critical Zero-Point Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34416)
*Shu He,Liwei Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在量子Rabi环中，均匀与手性超辐射边界的交汇处，二次理论给出具有有限径向宽度的简并手性梯；加入一个手性量子使径向波函数变宽，四次耦合提高均匀二次系数。所得能隙正比于均匀零点方差除以原子-腔频比η，随η^{-2/3}闭合，而方差按η^{1/3}增长。四次Schrödinger方程确定两个可观测量及其比值的一阶修正，并获全自旋-光子对角化验证。三角自旋团簇在Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换下遵循同一关系，但系数不同、修正符号相反。环的三坐标谱将该能隙与邻近的η^{-1}和η^{-1/3}律及光子数饱和联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究均匀超辐射相与手性超辐射相在量子Rabi环中相遇时的临界行为，特别是二次理论存在简并手性梯解的情形，需要引入手性量子与四次耦合来刻画能隙与波函数宽度的标度关系，并探索其与其它标度律及光子数饱和的联系。

Method: 在量子Rabi环框架下，从二次理论出发引入一个手性量子以拓宽梯的径向波函数，并加入四次耦合提高均匀二次系数；建立四次Schrödinger方程解析求解能隙与零点方差及其比值的一阶修正；采用全自旋-光子对角化进行数值验证；并考察具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换的三角自旋团簇这一类比体系；最后通过环的三坐标谱分析关联邻近标度律与光子数饱和。

Result: 能隙正比于均匀零点方差除以原子-腔频比η，随η^{-2/3}闭合，而方差按η^{1/3}增长；四次Schrödinger方程同时确定这两个可观测量及其比值的一阶修正，并与全自旋-光子对角化结果一致；三角自旋团簇在Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换下遵循相同关系，但系数不同且修正符号相反；环的三坐标谱将该能隙与η^{-1}和η^{-1/3}标度律及光子数饱和联系起来。

Conclusion: 在均匀与手性超辐射边界交汇的量子Rabi环中，手性量子与四次耦合主导了能隙与径向波函数的标度行为，给出的η^{-2/3}闭合律与η^{1/3}方差增长由四次Schrödinger方程统一描述并获数值验证；该关系在Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换的三角自旋体系中普适成立（系数与修正符号不同），并可通过环的三坐标谱与邻近标度律及光子数饱和相连接。

Abstract: Where uniform and chiral superradiant boundaries meet in a quantum Rabi ring, the quadratic theory leaves a degenerate chiral ladder with finite radial width. We show that adding one chiral quantum widens the ladder's radial wave function; the quartic coupling then raises the uniform quadratic coefficient. The resulting gap is proportional to the uniform zero-point variance divided by the atomic-to-cavity frequency ratio $η$: it closes as $η^{-2/3}$ while the variance grows as $η^{1/3}$. A quartic Schrödinger equation fixes both observables and the first correction to their ratio, as confirmed by full spin-photon diagonalization. A triangle of collective spins with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya exchange obeys the same relation, with a different coefficient and a correction of opposite sign. The ring's three-coordinate spectrum connects this gap to the neighboring $η^{-1}$ and $η^{-1/3}$ laws and to photon-number saturation.

</details>


### [189] [Near-Optimal Bounds on the Density of Low-Energy States of $k$-Local Hamiltonians and Faster Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34532)
*Sevag Gharibian,François Le Gall,Ranitha Mataraarachchi,Suguru Tamaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对一般 k-local 哈密顿量的低能估计与态制备问题，提出以二元熵函数控制运行时间指数的更快的指数级量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能估计与态制备是量子复杂度理论的基本问题。Buhrman 等人 [BGLGST, PRL 2025] 打破了 Grover 界 O*(2^{n/2})，其改进依赖相对精度 ε 与局域性 k。本文旨在进一步提升指数上的加速效果。

Method: 核心是一般 k-local 哈密顿量的算法设计，并通过对基态进行去极化操作，得到低能子空间维数的、由熵控制的下界。

Result: 当 ε/k 足够小时，算法指数较 [BGLGST, PRL 2025] 改进 log(k/ε) 因子；对固定 k，低能子空间维数的下界在指数上最优（至多相差常数因子）；同一框架对任意交互图上的 Heisenberg、XY 与 Ising 模型给出更紧的界。

Conclusion: 以熵控制指数为核心，本文同时给出了更快的量子算法与匹配（至常数因子）的下界，并推广到若干典型自旋模型。

Abstract: Low-energy estimation and state preparation for general $k$-local Hamiltonians are fundamental challenges in quantum complexity theory. Buhrman et al.~ [BGLGST, PRL 2025] recently broke the natural Grover bound $O^\ast(2^{n/2})$ for both problems, with the improvement depending on the relative accuracy $\varepsilon$ and the locality $k$. In this work, we present faster exponential quantum algorithms for these problems, where the binary entropy function governs the runtime exponent. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon/k$, our algorithms improve the exponent by a factor of $\log(k/\varepsilon)$ over [BGLGST, PRL 2025]. Our main technical result is an entropy-governed lower bound on the dimension of the Hamiltonian's low-energy subspace, obtained by depolarizing its ground state. For fixed $k$, this bound is optimal up to constant factors in the exponent. The same framework yields tighter bounds for Heisenberg, $XY$, and Ising models on arbitrary interaction graphs.

</details>


### [190] [Microsecond-Programmable Synthetic Quantum Matter: Circumventing the Adiabatic Bottleneck for Heisenberg-Limited Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34559)
*Soi-Chan Lei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出单腔BEC中的非绝热Floquet方案，通过快速旋转与相位跳变从原生纵向耦合合成有效TAT相互作用，并发展虚拟维数严格为O(1)的有限状态自动机MPO编码，实现大系统精确DMRG/TDVP模拟。对N=10^3系综跟踪Floquet TAT动力学与退相干，证明淬火可快速产生海森堡极限自旋压缩（ξ_R^2 ~ 1/N），微秒级时间尺度使生成过程与毫秒级退相干解耦，最优提取时间约为3.26 μs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观量子纠缠的生成是量子度量的核心，穿越量子临界点虽可带来海森堡极限精度，但绝热方案受限于慢动力学与退相干，难以实用。

Method: 在单腔玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中设计非绝热Floquet协议，利用快速旋转和相位跳变从原生纵向耦合动态合成有效Two-Axis Twisting（TAT）相互作用；为突破平均场近似，构建有限状态自动机形式的矩阵乘积算符（MPO）精确编码，将虚拟维数严格约束在O(1)量级，从而支持大系统精确DMRG与TDVP模拟；对N=10^3系综跟踪非平衡Floquet TAT动力学及环境退相干。

Result: 淬火过程能够快速产生海森堡极限自旋压缩，ξ_R^2 ~ 1/N；协议的微秒级时间尺度使纠缠生成过程与典型毫秒级退相干机制有效解耦；唯象模型给出最优提取时间约为t ≈ 3.26 μs。

Conclusion: 该工作为在当前腔量子电动力学平台上实现海森堡极限度量提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Generating macroscopic quantum entanglement is central to quantum metrology. While traversing a quantum critical point yields Heisenberg-limited precision, adiabatic schemes are restricted by slow dynamics and decoherence. Here, we propose a non-adiabatic Floquet protocol in a single-cavity Bose-Einstein condensate to circumvent these limitations. By applying rapid rotations and phase jumps, we dynamically synthesize effective Two-Axis Twisting (TAT) interactions from native longitudinal couplings. To evaluate this beyond mean-field limits, we develop an exact finite-state automaton Matrix Product Operator (MPO) encoding that strictly bounds the virtual dimension to $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling exact DMRG and TDVP simulations for large systems. Tracking non-equilibrium Floquet TAT dynamics and environmental decoherence for $N=10^3$ ensembles, we demonstrate that the quench rapidly generates Heisenberg-limited spin squeezing ($ξ_R^2 \sim 1/N$). The microsecond timescale of this protocol decouples the generation process from typical millisecond decoherence, with phenomenological models revealing an optimal extraction time near $t \approx 3.26~μs$. This work establishes a feasible route for realizing Heisenberg-limited metrology in current cavity-QED platforms.

</details>


### [191] [Quantum Entanglement in Variational Quantum Classification for Breast Cancer Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34617)
*Zineb Hazmoun,Zoubida Sakhi,Mohamed Bennai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究VQC纠缠结构与乳腺癌诊断性能的关系：三种三量子比特配置（Ising单层、Ising两层线性、Heisenberg全连接）共享同一ansatz、优化器与交叉验证。从A到C，平均纠缠熵从0.40升至0.71，准确率从92.98%升至93.68%，F1从90.32%升至91.28%，C最稳定；但折间纠缠-性能相关性弱且不显著，纠缠拓扑同时提升了表达力，故只能得出关联而非因果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习中，纠缠常被视为变分量子电路性能的关键资源，但其与分类性能之间究竟是因果还是伴随关系尚不明确。作者希望在一个受控的医学诊断任务中，考察纠缠结构如何影响VQC的表现。

Method: 在WDBC数据集上构建三个三量子比特VQC配置，均采用相同的EfficientSU2 ansatz、COBYLA优化器和分层五折交叉验证，仅纠缠结构不同：(A) 单次重复Ising型耦合；(B) 两次重复线性Ising型耦合；(C) 全连接Heisenberg型耦合。用von Neumann熵和Wootters concurrence量化纠缠，并与准确率、F1分数、折间稳定性做对比分析。

Result: 从A到C，平均纠缠熵由0.40增至0.71，准确率由92.98%增至93.68%，F1分数由90.32%增至91.28%，配置C在交叉验证各折间最稳定。然而折级别的纠缠-性能相关系数较弱且未达显著性；同时更丰富的纠缠拓扑也提高了电路表达力，两种效应无法完全分离。

Conclusion: 纠缠结构更丰富的拓扑与更高的VQC诊断性能之间存在关联，但不能证明因果关系；表达力与纠缠的混淆限制了因果归因，需进一步设计解耦实验加以验证。

Abstract: This study looks at how the entangling structure of a variational quantum classifier (VQC) relates to its performance in breast cancer diagnosis, using the Wisconsin Diagnostic Breast Cancer (WDBC) dataset. We tested three three-qubit configurations that share the same EfficientSU2 ansatz, COBYLA optimizer, and stratified five-fold cross-validation, but differ in their entangling structure: a single- repetition Ising-type coupling (A), a two-repetition linear Ising-type coupling (B), and a fully connected Heisenberg-type coupling (C). Entanglement was measured with the von Neumann entropy and the Wootters concurrence. From A to C, the mean entropy rose from 0.40 to 0.71, accuracy rose from 92.98% to 93.68%, and F1-score rose from 90.32% to 91.28%. Configuration C was also the most stable across folds. However, the fold-level correlations between entanglement and performance were weak and not significant. Richer topologies also increase circuit expressivity, so the two effects cannot be fully separated. These results show an association, not a proven causal link, between entangling structure and VQC performance.

</details>


### [192] [Depth-Optimal Quantum Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34659)
*Francisca Vasconcelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了任意单量子比特门合成的常数深度线路，构造是酉的且无需预置催化态，并证明了界定宽度下的紧致深度下界与常数深度的可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前单量子比特门合成的线路深度与精度、辅助比特等资源之间存在权衡，难以在常数深度内实现任意单量子比特门合成，且缺乏匹配的下界刻画。本文旨在探究能否以常数深度、酉且无需催化态的方式实现任意单量子比特门合成，并厘清界定宽度与对数宽度多比特门下的深度下界。

Method: 构造常数深度线路：使用O(log^{1+δ}(1/ε))个干净辅助比特、Hadamard与T门、O(log(1/ε))宽度的广义Toffoli门以及次对数宽度的Fan-Out门进行任意单量子比特门的ε近似；进一步去除Fan-Out，仅用Hadamard、T与广义Toffoli门完成常数深度合成；并给出界定宽度门模型下深度为O(loglog(1/ε))的构造，同时证明匹配的Ω(loglog(1/ε))深度下界。此外，发展一种保深度的实数模拟，将深度为d的QAC线路用Hadamard、X与广义Toffoli门以深度O(d)模拟。

Result: 得到首个常数深度的任意单量子比特门合成线路；在界定宽度门模型下，构造深度为O(loglog(1/ε))，并证明匹配的Ω(loglog(1/ε))下界；表明仅用界定宽度门时Θ(loglog(1/ε))深度不可避免，而允许对数宽度多比特门即可达到常数深度合成；并证明深度d的QAC线路可用Hadamard、X与广义Toffoli门以深度O(d)模拟。

Conclusion: 任意单量子比特旋转与复振幅不会提升QAC的界定误差判定能力（即使常数深度下也不提升），从而将Parity∉QAC^0猜想归约为仅含Hadamard、X与广义Toffoli门线路的Parity下界；实数范式揭示了浅层量子线路层级与受限于特定oracle族的Forrelation线路层级之间的直接对应关系。

Abstract: We achieve the first constant-depth circuit for arbitrary single-qubit gate synthesis. Unlike prior approaches, the construction is fully unitary and requires no pre-supplied catalyst. For any constant $δ>0$, it $\varepsilon$-approximates an arbitrary single-qubit gate using $O(\log^{1+δ}(1/\varepsilon))$ clean ancillae, Hadamard and $T$ single-qubit gates, $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$-width generalized Toffoli gates, and sublogarithmic-width Fan-Out gates. We further eliminate Fan-Out entirely, showing that Hadamard, $T$, and generalized Toffoli gates alone suffice for constant-depth synthesis. When restricted to the standard bounded-width gate model, our construction has depth $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$, and we prove a matching $Ω(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$-depth lower bound. Overall, we establish that $Θ(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$-depth is unavoidable with only bounded-width gates, yet allowing even logarithmic-width multi-qubit gates suffices to achieve constant-depth synthesis.
  These results also reveal new structure in shallow quantum circuit complexity. We give a depth-preserving real simulation of bounded-error decision computation, showing that every depth-$d$ QAC circuit can be simulated in depth $O(d)$ using only Hadamard, $X$, and generalized Toffoli gates. Thus arbitrary single-qubit rotations and complex amplitudes do not increase the bounded-error decision power of QAC, even at constant depth. In particular, this reduces the long-standing conjecture Parity$\notin$QAC$^0$ to proving a Parity lower bound against circuits consisting only of Hadamard, $X$, and generalized Toffoli gates. More generally, this real normal form exposes a direct correspondence between the standard shallow-depth quantum circuit hierarchy and a hierarchy of Forrelation circuits with restricted oracle families.

</details>


### [193] [Log-Euclidean Rényi Conditional Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34699)
*Roberto Rubboli,Amir Arqand,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个满足所有理想结构性质的量子Rényi条件互信息(QCMI)推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子条件互信息是度量条件独立性的基本量，但在量子信息论和多体物理中，一直缺乏一个满足全部理想结构性质的完全量子Rényi推广。这一空白促使作者构造新的Rényi QCMI。

Method: 引入一种量子Rényi条件互信息，验证其在局部量子信道下单调、张量积下可加、随Rényi参数单调、并在α→1时收敛到QCMI；建立其与Petz恢复态相对熵的界；推导基于恢复的QCMI上下界；并建立与Markov态、可分态集合相对熵的界以及Rényi链式法则。

Result: 该量满足局部信道单调性、张量积可加性、Rényi参数单调性及α→1收敛性；给出与Petz恢复态相对熵的界，表明其刻画了近似条件独立性的独特度量；导出基于KL散度量子推广的QCMI恢复式上下界，在某些区间改进了已知最佳结果；建立与Markov态和可分态相对熵的界及Rényi链式法则，后者即使经典情形下也似为新结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子条件互信息提供了一个结构性质完备的Rényi推广，并由此获得更紧的恢复式界与新的Rényi链式法则，对量子信息论和多体物理具有应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum conditional mutual information (QCMI) is a fundamental measure of conditional independence, with central applications throughout quantum information theory and many-body physics. Despite its importance, finding a fully quantum Rényi generalization that satisfies all desirable structural properties has remained an open question. In this work, we introduce a quantum Rényi conditional mutual information possessing these desirable properties: it is monotone under local quantum channels, additive under tensor products, monotone in the Rényi parameter, and converges to the QCMI in the limit $α\to 1$. We establish bounds on this quantity in terms of relative entropies to the Petz-recovered state, showing that it provides a distinct measure of approximate conditional independence. As a consequence, we derive recovery-based upper and lower bounds on the QCMI in terms of quantum generalizations of the Kullback-Leibler divergence, improving upon the best known results in certain regimes. Finally, we establish bounds in terms of relative entropies to the sets of Markov and separable states and derive Rényi chain rules, which appear to be novel even in the classical setting.

</details>


### [194] [Cubic Phase Gate with a Trapped Ion Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34774)
*Nigel Benjamin Lee Junsheng,Eugene Koh Jun Wei,Mu Young Kim,Dzmitry Matsukevich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在囚禁离子运动模式上，通过数值优化一组状态依赖力脉冲的相位与幅度，实验演示了立方相位门的近似实现，并在真空态与低振幅相干态输入下验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子的运动模式是实现连续变量（CV）量子计算与量子模拟的候选平台，但要构建CV量子计算的通用门集合，需要具备非谐非线性幺正操作。立方相位门是其中的关键非线性门，因此其实验实现具有重要意义。

Method: 采用一组状态依赖力脉冲（state-dependent force pulses），对其相位角和幅度进行数值优化，从而近似实现立方相位门。该方法验证了真空态输入与低振幅（低-α）相干态输入两种情形。

Result: 实验上成功演示了立方相位门的近似实现，并在真空态和低-α相干态输入下验证了其性能表现。

Conclusion: 该协议能够有效近似的实现立方相位门，并且可以进一步推广用于近似其他非线性幺正操作，为囚禁离子体系中的连续变量量子计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The motional modes of trapped ions are promising candidates for continuous-variable (CV) quantum computation and simulation. However, to achieve a universal gate set for CV quantum computation, anharmonic nonlinear unitaries are required. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a protocol that allows us to approximate the cubic phase gate. By numerically optimizing the phase angles and amplitudes of a set of state-dependent force pulses, we demonstrate the cubic phase gate for both vacuum and low-$α$ coherent state inputs and verify its performance. Our protocol can also be extended to approximate other nonlinear unitaries.

</details>


### [195] [Efficient Simulation of Hybrid Continuous- and Discrete-Variable Quantum Circuits via Gaussian Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34793)
*Kazufumi Tanji,Dot Belin Pio,Shohei Kiryu,Ulrik Lund Andersen,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯线性组合（LCoG）分解的CV-DV混合量子系统模拟框架，将混合密度矩阵在DV基下分块并对每块做LCoG展开，给出受控高斯操作下复高斯函数的解析变换公式，并扩展到状态依赖高斯噪声的受控高斯信道，避免CV子系统的Fock空间截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DV与CV混合的量子系统广泛存在于多种物理平台，可实现超越纯量子比特架构的量子信息处理；但其经典模拟困难，因为CV和DV系统的计算成本都随规模指数增长，亟需高效的模拟方法。

Method: 以高斯线性组合（LCoG）分解为基础构建模拟框架：将混合密度矩阵在离散变量基下分块，每块表示为LCoG展开；在该表示下玻色子子系统上的高斯操作独立作用于每个高斯函数，并推导复高斯函数在受控高斯操作下的解析变换公式；进一步扩展至含状态依赖高斯噪声的受控高斯信道。方法无需对CV子系统做Fock空间截断，每个高斯项更新的代价随模式数多项式增长。

Result: 建立了可处理受控高斯操作与状态依赖高斯噪声的CV-DV混合系统模拟框架，更新单个高斯项的计算成本随模式数呈多项式增长，且无需Fock空间截断；并以腔QED系统中的GKP态生成为例完成模拟验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析混合量子算法、量子模拟协议以及非高斯态生成方案提供了通用计算工具，可有效缓解CV-DV混合系统经典模拟中的指数复杂度问题。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum systems combining discrete-variable (DV) and continuous-variable (CV) subsystems arise across a broad range of physical platforms and enable quantum information processing beyond purely qubit-based designs. Their classical simulation, however, is challenging since computational costs of both CV and DV systems exponentially increase. In this paper, we introduce a simulation framework for CV--DV hybrid systems based on linear combination of Gaussian (LCoG) decomposition. We decompose the hybrid density matrix into blocks in a DV basis and represent each block as an LCoG expansion. Within the representation, Gaussian operations on the bosonic subsystem independently act on each Gaussian function. We derive analytic formulas describing how complex Gaussian functions transform under controlled Gaussian operations. We further extend the framework to controlled Gaussian channels with state-dependent Gaussian noise. The method avoids a Fock-space truncation for the CV subsystem, and the cost of updating each Gaussian term scales polynomially with the number of modes. The framework provides a general computational tool for analyzing hybrid quantum algorithms, quantum simulation protocols, and non-Gaussian state generation schemes. As an example, we simulate Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill (GKP) state generation based on a cavity-QED system.

</details>


### [196] [General Photon Subtraction from a Gaussian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34803)
*Niklas Budinger,Ulrik L. Andersen,Peter van Loock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯模糊与滤波的数学形式化，统一描述光子减除高斯态，并据此改进猫态、立方相位态与GKP态的产生方案，同时给出近确定性生成目标态所需资源的非高斯性下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量光学实现通用容错量子计算的关键瓶颈是缺乏近确定性的非高斯性来源。由于缺少强非线性，概率性光子减除高斯态是最有希望的方案，且与高斯测量和单模前馈结合可在高速率下提供足够非高斯性。但现有"增殖"（breeding）协议损耗容忍度不足，难以实验实现。

Method: 作者建立一个数学形式化框架：对测量得到的Fock维格纳函数施加高斯模糊与高斯滤波，以此描述一般的光子减除高斯态。在该表示下，初始压缩、高斯测量与光子损耗都统一归结为对总模糊的贡献，并可相互转换。利用该框架在成功率、前馈兼容性与损耗容忍度之间折衷，改进猫态、立方相位态和GKP态的产生；对多次光子减除，引入介于breeding与后选择之间的混合方案；并引入期望维格纳对数负性作为非高斯性的可加单调量，建立资源下界。

Result: 该直观方法能够为猫态、立方相位态和GKP态的产生找到改进方案；多次光子减除情形下，混合方案弥合了breeding与后选择协议之间的差距；并给出了近确定性生成给定目标态所需的资源下界。

Conclusion: 以高斯模糊/滤波统一描述光子减除高斯态，可将压缩、测量与损耗相互换算，为高损耗容忍的非高斯态产生提供设计工具与资源下界，推动连续变量光学的容错量子计算。

Abstract: A near-deterministic source of non-Gaussianity is the missing piece to reach universal and fault-tolerant quantum computing with continuous-variable optics. Due to a lack of strong non-linearities, the probabilistic photon-subtracted Gaussian states remain the most promising contender. In combination with Gaussian measurements and single-mode feed-forward operations, they have been shown to provide sufficient non-Gaussianity at high rates. However, these existing breeding protocols lack the necessary loss tolerance to be experimentally feasible. In this work, we introduce a mathematical formalism based on the application of a Gaussian blur and filter on the measured Fock Wigner function to describe general photon-subtracted Gaussian states. Within our representation, the initial squeezing, Gaussian measurements, and photon loss can all be understood as contributions to the total blurring and interchanged accordingly. We find that this intuitive approach can be used to find improvements to cat, cubic phase, and GKP state generation by compromising between success rates, feed-forward compatibility, and loss tolerance. In the case of multiple photon subtractions, this is achieved by introducing a hybrid setup variant bridging the gap between protocols based on breeding and post-selection. Finally, we establish a general lower bound on the resources needed to generate a given target state near-deterministically by introducing the expected Wigner logarithmic negativity as an additive monotone of non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [197] [Directional pumping of a two-level system by a fluctuation-regulated quantum source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34885)
*Sarfraj Fency,Rangeet Bhattacharyya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将驱动源保留为量子子系统并求迹掉后，可产生定向布居泵浦；数对角全激发源下仅剩向上耗散通道，实现单调泵浦，且稳态激发态布居可超过共振驱动光学Bloch极限1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典共振场对二能级系统的相干驱动在普通弛豫下只产生饱和，而非定向布居转移。作者希望说明定向性如何在驱动源被当作量子子系统处理时出现。

Method: 把源建模为单激发自由度（等价于二能级辅助粒子流），与目标二能级系统交换耦合。源的一阶矩给出通常相干驱动，二阶矩产生向上与向下耗散通道。用精确碰撞映射和涨落调控的二阶量子主方程两种方法描述；并分析指数衰减源关联下泵浦速率与失谐的关系。

Result: 对于数对角、全激发的源，一阶矩消失，仅保留向上通道，导致目标单调泵浦。指数衰减源关联下，泵浦速率呈洛伦兹失谐依赖，由关联时间τ_c控制，并伴随色散频移。存在热弛豫时，稳态激发态布居可超过共振驱动光学Bloch极限1/2。在涨落调控主方程中，该定向泵浦表现为驱动诱导的耗散贡献，其强度与谱响应由τ_c控制。

Conclusion: 将驱动源作为量子子系统求迹，可在二阶关联中产生定向耗散通道，实现超越传统共振驱动极限的布居泵浦；该效应是涨落调控主方程中的驱动诱导耗散项。

Abstract: A resonant classical field drives a two-level system coherently and, with ordinary relaxation, produces saturation rather than directional population transfer. Here we show that directionality emerges when the excitation source is retained as a quantum subsystem and subsequently traced out. We model the source as a single-excitation degree of freedom, or equivalently a stream of two-level ancillas, exchange-coupled to a target two-level system. The source first moment generates the usual coherent drive, while its second moments produce upward and downward dissipative channels. For a number-diagonal, fully excited source, the first moment vanishes and only the upward channel remains, yielding monotonic pumping of the target. The same structure follows from both an exact collision map and a fluctuation-regulated second-order quantum master equation. For exponentially decaying source correlations, the pumping rate shows a Lorentzian detuning dependence governed by the correlation time $τ_c$, accompanied by a dispersive frequency shift. In the presence of thermal relaxation, the steady-state excited-state population can exceed the resonantly driven optical-Bloch limit of one half. Within the fluctuation-regulated master equation, this directional pumping arises as a drive-induced dissipative contribution whose strength and spectral response are controlled by $τ_c$.

</details>


### [198] [Classical simulation of amplitude-damped bucket-brigade quantum random access memories via predictable branch evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34935)
*Zhao-Yun Chen,Ming-Yang Tan,Sheng Zhang,Peng Wang,Yun-Jie Wang,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出bucket-brigade QRAM在幅度阻尼噪声下"好分支"可预测性的完整理论，并据此修正剪枝准则，使含噪经典模拟大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含噪QRAM经典模拟中，分支剪枝可加速轨迹系综采样：避开所有采样故障的"好分支"末态可解析预测，无需演化。但在幅度阻尼下，无跳变算符K0在每个时刻作用于每条分支，好分支的可预测性不再自动成立，需明确其成立条件与形式。

Method: 针对qutrit与qubit两种编码，分析阻尼信道的可预测性。qutrit编码中K0在节点基下对角、|W>为其不动点，好分支沿无噪声轨道演化至一个标量衰减因子；qubit编码中两Hadamard墙间的相位回踢使K0非对角，但结构可精确闭式求解，并据此修正qubit编码的剪枝准则。所得算法仅演化坏分支加一条参考分支。

Result: 无跳变条件下，好分支的分支内不保真度为阻尼率γ的二阶量，每个数据比特的相干泄漏约为n²γ²/4，闭式解无近似地刻画该泄漏。端到端基准测试中剪枝相对完整演化加速最高达285×；轨迹级测试证实所有闭式解达到机器精度。

Conclusion: 阻尼信道下好分支的可预测性可被完整解析刻画：qutrit编码为对角衰减、qubit编码可闭式求解；修正后的剪枝算法在保持与完整演化相同其它开销的同时仅演化坏分支与一条参考分支，并获得显著加速与机器精度验证。

Abstract: Classical simulation of quantum random access memory (QRAM) under noise can be accelerated dramatically by branch pruning: noise histories are sampled as trajectory ensembles, and good branches, whose routing paths avoid every sampled fault, need not be evolved because their final states are analytically predictable. Under amplitude damping this predictability is not automatic, because the no-jump operator $K_0$ acts on every branch at every time slice. Here we give a complete account of damping-channel predictability in bucket-brigade QRAM simulation, for both qutrit and qubit encodings. For the qutrit encoding, $K_0$ is diagonal in the node basis, and the wait state $|W\rangle$ is its fixed point, so a good branch follows the noiseless orbit up to a scalar attenuation. In the qubit encoding, phase-kickback memory fetching between two Hadamard walls makes $K_0$ no longer diagonal, yet the resulting structure is exactly solvable in closed form. Conditional on no jump, the in-branch infidelity of a good branch is second order in the damping rate $γ$, with coherent leakage $\sim n^2γ^2/4$ per data qubit, and the closed form captures it without approximation. We also revise the pruning criterion for the qubit encoding. The resulting algorithm evolves only the bad branches plus one reference branch and shares every other cost with the full evolution it replaces. It is validated by end-to-end benchmarks with pruned-over-full speedups up to $285\times$ and by trajectory-level tests confirming every closed form to machine precision.

</details>


### [199] [All-vs-Nothing Operational Manifestation of Preparation Contextuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34964)
*Jayashree Karmakar,Rafiuddin Gazi,Biswadeep Chatterjee,Subhendu B Ghosh,Anandamay Das Bhowmik,Ananya Chakraborty,Manik Banik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"奇偶无知"(parity-oblivious)隐藏匹配任务，使制备上下文性呈现出全有或全无的定性分离：量子协议能以单条⌈log₂n⌉量子比特消息完美成功，而任何制备非上下文模型均无法对偶数n≥6达到完美成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备上下文性通常只表现为信息处理任务中相对非上下文模型的定量优势，量子理论往往无法达到完美成功，缺乏"全有或全无"式的强分离证据。作者希望构造一个任务，使量子与制备非上下文模型之间出现定性而非仅定量的差异。

Method: 提出奇偶无知的隐藏匹配任务：Alice将n比特串编码为消息，除两比特奇偶性外不泄露任何输入奇偶信息；Bob在给定完美匹配下需输出一条边及其两端比特的奇偶性。构造使用一条⌈log₂n⌉量子比特消息、满足奇偶无知约束且确定成功的量子协议；并对任意ontic态空间、不假设结果确定性或测量非上下文性，证明非上下文模型的不可能性。渐近上界通过布尔超立方体上的超压缩性导出Fourier分析求和规则获得。

Result: 对任意偶数n≥6，无制备非上下文模型可完美成功。n=6与n=8时最优非上下文成功概率分别为4/5与3/4；渐近最优非上下文成功概率为1/2+Θ(1/√n)。此外在n=8时，即使Bob仅限使用105个完美匹配中合适的4个，非上下文界仍为3/4，使该效应进入实验可达范围。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了制备上下文性的定性分离：量子理论可完美成功，而制备非上下文模型不能，超越了以往的定量优势；同时通过限制匹配数量将效应带入实验可验证范围。

Abstract: Preparation contextuality is often manifested operationally through a quantitative advantage over preparation-noncontextual models in information-processing tasks, yet quantum theory typically falls short of perfect success. Here we introduce a parity-oblivious Hidden Matching task that instead yields an all-vs-nothing manifestation of preparation contextuality. Alice encodes an $n$-bit string so that her message reveals no information about any input parity other than the two-bit parities. Bob, given a perfect matching on the input positions, must output an edge of the matching together with the parity of its two bits. A quantum protocol using a single $\lceil\log_2 n\rceil$-qubit message satisfies the parity-obliviousness constraint and succeeds with certainty. We prove that no preparation-noncontextual model achieves perfect success for any even $n\ge6$. The result holds for arbitrary ontic state spaces and assumes neither outcome determinism nor measurement noncontextuality. For $n=6$ and $n=8$, the optimal noncontextual success probabilities are $4/5$ and $3/4$, respectively, while asymptotically the optimal noncontextual success probability is $1/2+Θ(1/\sqrt n)$. Our asymptotic upper bound follows from a Fourier-analytic sum rule derived via hypercontractivity on the Boolean hypercube. The resulting separation is qualitative rather than merely quantitative: quantum theory achieves perfect success, whereas preparation-noncontextual models cannot. We aslo show that at $n=8$ the noncontextual bound remains $3/4$ even when Bob is restricted to just suitable $4$ of the $105$ possible perfect matchings, bringing the effect within experimental reach.

</details>


### [200] [Simulation-Based Quantum System Inference with Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34995)
*Hang Zou,Anton Frisk Kockum,Martin Rahm,Simon Olsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"基于模拟的量子系统推断"框架：用多项式代价经典模拟器配合归一化流等神经密度估计器，免似然地直接从模拟数据学习参数后验，单次训练后前向一次即可完成任意新测量记录的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统正向模型能将参数映射到观测量，但反向的参数推断因希尔伯特空间指数级增长导致似然不可计算，成为根本性难题。需要一种避免显式似然、又能覆盖多类量子表征任务的高效推断方法。

Method: 统一的无似然（likelihood-free）模拟推断框架：将多项式代价的经典模拟器（如Pauli propagation、张量网络）与归一化流或其他神经密度估计器配对，从经典模拟数据中直接学习参数后验分布；训练一次后，模型以单次前向传播将任意新测量记录映射为其后验，把逐次实验推断转化为一次性固定开销。

Result: 在Pauli噪声学习、量子误差缓解、量子态层析和哈密顿量学习四类任务上数值验证了框架的通用性，示例涵盖81比特浅层电路和735参数推断；各任务均给出可辨识参数的准确估计，后验不确定性还可诊断不可辨识性并指示需进一步表征之处。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了量子实验的数据采集需求并加速参数推断，为大规模量子系统的表征与改进提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Models of quantum systems faithfully map system parameters to observations, but the inverse problem of parameter inference from measurement data presents a fundamental challenge: computationally intractable likelihoods due to an exponentially large Hilbert space. Here, we introduce simulation-based quantum system inference, a unified, likelihood-free framework that learns parameter posteriors directly from classical simulation data. The central idea is to pair polynomial-cost classical simulators, such as Pauli propagation and tensor networks, with normalizing flows or other neural density estimators for accurate, reusable inference. A single model, trained once, maps any new measurement record to its posterior in one forward pass---turning per-experiment inference into a fixed, up-front cost. We numerically demonstrate the framework's versatility across Pauli noise learning, quantum error mitigation, quantum state tomography, and Hamiltonian learning, with examples involving 81-qubit shallow circuits and 735-parameter inference. In each case, the approach yields accurate estimates of identifiable parameters, while posterior uncertainty provides additional diagnostics of non-identifiability and indicates where further characterization is needed. Our framework reduces data-acquisition requirements in quantum experiments and accelerates parameter inference, providing a practical route to characterizing and improving large-scale quantum systems.

</details>


### [201] [Cyclotomic Cosets: Hidden Subgroup and Quantum Sieving Algorithm for Prime-Power Moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34996)
*Mathias Boucher,Pierre-Alain Fouque,Yixin Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出分圆陪集问题（CCP），作为DCP的分圆推广，保持精确的隐藏子群结构，并利用π-adic理想链构造量子筛，对CCP、均匀EDCP及高斯S|LWE>给出拟多项式时间算法，但不直接适用于标准LWE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LWE是后量子密码学的基础假设。Regev给出LWE到DCP的量子归约，Brakerski等人提出的EDCP虽与LWE等价，但不再具有陪集结构，限制了隐藏子群问题技术的直接应用。因此需要一种既与LWE相关、又保留精确隐藏子群结构的推广问题。

Method: 引入分圆陪集问题CCP。设ζ_p为本原p次单位根，π=ζ_p−1，q=p^t，L=t(p−1)。在R_q=Z_q[ζ_p]≅Z[ζ_p]/(π^L)上工作，利用全分歧恒等式(p)=(π)^{p-1}得到的π-adic理想链，构造量子筛，逐次将相位态模π^L, π^{L-1}, …, π进行约化。

Result: 对每个固定素数p和模数q=p^t，算法以时间和样本复杂度2^{O_p(log n log q)}求解CCP，使用多项式量子空间。该筛也适用于均匀EDCP和高斯S|LWE>，当q=poly(n)时给出拟多项式时间算法，将Bai等人（CRYPTO 2025）的2的幂EDCP筛推广到分圆情形。

Conclusion: 该结果本身不能得到标准LWE的拟多项式时间算法，因为目前已知的归约只能产生有限数量的近似CCP态。因此工作主要贡献在于建立保留隐藏子群结构的分圆框架并解决相关变体问题。

Abstract: The Learning With Errors (LWE) problem is a fundamental assumption in post-quantum cryptography. Regev established a quantum reduction from LWE to the Dihedral Coset Problem (DCP). Later, Brakerski et al. introduced the Extrapolated Dihedral Coset Problem (EDCP), proving its equivalence to LWE. However, unlike DCP, EDCP no longer admits a coset structure. This limits the direct application of techniques for hidden subgroup problems.
  In this work, we introduce the Cyclotomic Coset Problem (CCP), a cyclotomic generalization of DCP that preserves an exact hidden-subgroup structure. Let $ζ_p$ be a primitive $p$-th root of unity, let $π=ζ_p-1$, and write $q=p^t$ and $L=t(p-1)$. We work over $R_q=\mathbb Z_q[ζ_p] \cong \mathbb Z[ζ_p]/(π^L)$, where the isomorphism follows from the total ramification identity $(p)=(π)^{p-1}$. We exploit the resulting $π$-adic ideal chain to construct a quantum sieve that successively reduces phase states modulo $π^{L},π^{L-1},\ldots,π$. For every fixed prime $p$ and modulus $q=p^t$, our algorithm solves the CCP in time and sample complexity $2^{O_p(\log n\log q)}$, using polynomial quantum space. The sieve also applies to uniform EDCP and Gaussian S|LWE>, yielding quasi-polynomial time algorithms for all the above problems when $q=\text{poly}(n)$. This extends the power-of-two EDCP sieve of Bai et al. (CRYPTO 2025) to a cyclotomic setting.
  However, we emphasize that our result does not, by itself, yield a quasi-polynomial-time algorithm for standard LWE, because the currently known reduction produces only a limited number of approximate CCP states.

</details>


### [202] [Enantiomer-specific pumping of chiral molecules in high-$J$ rotational states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35008)
*Fen Zou,Yan Gao,Peng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于手性分子 J=1 与 J=2 转动态的对映体选择性态转移（ESST）方案，利用三束线偏振微波场与一束激光驱动的对映体选择性泵浦，将一种对映体泵入 J=1,2 子流形内的对映体特异暗态，而另一种对映体被耗散性地泵出该流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ESST 实验演示主要局限于最低转动角动量态（J=0、J=1），而真实分子样品在高 J 转动态可能存在可观测布居；由于高 J 流形包含更多磁子能级且电偶极跃迁网络更复杂，无法通过低 J 方案的简单直接推广获得适用方案，因此需要专门设计高 J 的 ESST 方案。

Method: 针对涉及 J=1 与 J=2 转动态的手性分子，设计基于对映体选择性泵浦的 ESST 方案：采用三束线偏振微波场与一束激光束驱动，在 J=1,2 子流形内构建对映体特异暗态；一种对映体被相干泵浦至该暗态，另一种对映体则被耗散性泵出该流形。

Result: 在所提方案下，一种对映体被泵浦进入 J=1,2 子流形内的对映体特异暗态，另一种对映体被耗散性地泵出该流形，从而实现基于 J=1 与 J=2 转动态的对映体选择性态转移。该策略可推广至涉及更高角动量转动态的系统。

Conclusion: 该工作将 ESST 从最低转动态拓展到 J=1 与 J=2 流形，为存在较高 J 布居的真实分子样品提供了可行的对映体操控与检测/分离途径，并具有向更高转动角动量系统扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Enantiomer-specific state transfer (ESST) based on electric-dipole transitions has attracted considerable interest in the studies of manipulating molecules with electromagnetic fields. It also holds promise as a powerful tool for enantiomer detection and separation. To date, experimental demonstrations of ESST have focused mainly on molecules in the lowest rotational angular-momentum states, namely, $J=0$ and $J=1$. Realistic molecular samples, however, may contain appreciable populations in higher-$J$ rotational states, making it desirable to develop ESST schemes applicable to such states. Nevertheless, one cannot derive such schemes via a simple and straightforward extension of existing low-$J$ schemes, because higher-$J$ manifolds contain more magnetic sub-levels and involve more complicated networks of electric-dipole transitions. Here, we propose an ESST scheme for chiral molecules involving the $J=1$ and $J=2$ rotational states. Specifically, our scheme is based on enantiomer-specific pumping driven by three linearly polarized microwave fields and one laser beam. Under this scheme, one enantiomer is pumped into an enantiomer-specific dark state within a sub manifold with $J=1,2$, whereas the other enantiomer is dissipatively pumped out of this manifold. Our strategy can be extended to systems involving rotational states with even higher angular momenta.

</details>


### [203] [Experimental quantum-computing-enhanced sensing using Grover's algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35016)
*Mathieu Ouellet,Purnendu Sen,Xiangqin Wang,Saswata Roy,Xingrui Song,Vladimir Kremenetski,Sridhar Prabhu,Valla Fatemi,Peter L. McMahon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了利用Grover搜索算法改进量子传感，在超过10 MHz的探测带宽下超越非Grover基线，且优势随带宽超线性增长，可减少所需信号量10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子传感与量子计算结合，以期在不依赖大规模或容错硬件的前提下，获得优于传统量子传感的性能，是量子计算一个有前景的近-term应用方向。

Method: 基于单超导量子比特与单超导腔耦合的系统，实验实现了一种理论提议：重新利用Grover搜索算法，提升在大探测带宽内探测未知频率信号的能力。

Result: 对于探测带宽>10 MHz，Grover传感优于自然非Grover基线，且优势在超越收支平衡点后随带宽超线性增长；在更大探测带宽和更高探测精度需求下，Grover协议使做出准确探测判决所需的信号量减少10倍以上。

Conclusion: 结果原理性验证了Grover-based量子计算传感可在近-term硬件上实现，并在存在额外协议复杂性的情况下仍提供远超收支平衡的计量优势。

Abstract: The combination of quantum sensing with quantum computing to provide an enhancement over conventional quantum sensing has recently emerged as a promising potential application of quantum computing that could give advantages without needing large-scale or fault-tolerant hardware. In this work, we report an experimental demonstration of a recent theoretical proposal to repurpose Grover's search algorithm to improve the ability to detect signals with unknown frequency within a large detection bandwidth. Our experiments were based on a system comprising a single superconducting qubit coupled to a single superconducting cavity, highlighting the modest hardware requirements for realizing the protocol. We found that Grover-based sensing was able to outperform the natural non-Grover baseline for our experimental platform for detection bandwidths $>$10~MHz, with an advantage that empirically grew superlinearly with the bandwidth beyond that break-even point. The use of the Grover-based protocol reduced the amount of signal that needed to be sensed to make an accurate detection decision by more than 10$\times$ for choices of larger detection bandwidth and higher desired detection accuracy. Our results provide a proof-of-principle validation that Grover-based quantum computational sensing can be realized in near-term hardware and provide a metrological advantage well beyond break-even in spite of the additional protocol complexity.

</details>


### [204] [Classical Bound on the Fisher Information Rate of a Dephasing-Enhanced Quantum Neuron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35076)
*Peng Wang,Yu-Xuan Zhang,Hai-Tao Ding,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文推导了部分重置更新单量子比特神经元在重复投影读出下的响应带宽阈值与精确局部Fisher信息率，给出信息率上界 R ≤ ν(1-s)(1+s)^2/2 ≤ 16ν/27，并指出理想读出下最优在 s=1/3 处以任意快速读出逼近该上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退相干可锐化耗散量子神经元的激活，但稳态响应并不能刻画其输出单位时间内传递多少输入信息；需要区分"噪声增强激活"与"运行层面的信息吞吐量"，并为有限时间量子神经处理提供硬件相关的性能基准。

Method: 针对以速率 ν 进行部分重置更新的单量子比特，推导响应带宽阈值，并计算来自重复投影读出的完整记录（含测量反作用/backaction）的精确局部Fisher信息率；对读出间隔与保留幅度 s 进行优化，并在存在有限测量死时间时做无量纲双参数优化。

Result: 在激活阈值附近，信息率满足 R ≤ ν(1-s)(1+s)^2/2 ≤ 16ν/27；对任意固定的有限马尔可夫退相干率，优化读出间隔与 s 得到相同上确界，在 s=1/3 处以任意快速的理想读出逼近；有限测量死时间则选出有限的最优读出间隔。该理想上界可由经典两态跳跃过程饱和，量子相干性改变有限节奏下的性能但无法提升优化后的上界。

Conclusion: 结果将噪声增强的激活与量子神经元的运行信息吞吐量区分开来，为有限时间量子神经处理提供了硬件感知的基准；量子相干不提升最优信息率上界，该上界本质上由经典两态跳跃过程达到。

Abstract: Dephasing can sharpen the activation of a dissipative quantum neuron, but a stationary response does not determine how much input information its output delivers per unit time. For a single qubit with partial-reset updates at rate $ν$, we derive the threshold response bandwidth and the exact local Fisher information rate of the complete record from repeated projective readouts, including their backaction. Near the activation threshold, the rate obeys $\mathcal R\leqν(1-s)(1+s)^2/2\leq16ν/27$, where $s$ is the retained amplitude of each update. For every fixed finite Markovian dephasing rate, optimization over the readout interval and $s$ gives the same supremum, approached at $s=1/3$ with arbitrarily rapid ideal readout. A finite measurement dead time instead selects a finite optimal interval, which we obtain by a dimensionless two-parameter optimization. The ideal bound is saturated by a classical two-state jump process: quantum coherence changes finite-cadence performance but cannot raise the optimized bound. These results separate noise-enhanced activation from the operational information throughput of a quantum neuron and provide a hardware-aware benchmark for finite-time quantum neural processing.

</details>


### [205] [From Simple Sources to Quantum Advantage: Homomorphic Polynomial Transduction via Relative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35101)
*Zhong-Xia Shang,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将DQI/HDQI重铸为通过代数同态进行的量子态转导，引入相对距离d_rel作为保内积判据，证明其优于普通距离，并给出去除关系限制的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI与HDQI通过傅里叶变换和相干解码制备目标函数低次多项式振幅态，但依赖复杂的导频态制备，且受普通距离d_ord限制。作者希望以更模块化的方式（制备源多项式态）替代导频态，并将(H)DQI纳入更一般的代数框架。

Method: 把该过程表述为经由代数同态的量子态转导：设π为有限维源C*-代数到目标C*-代数的单位*-同态，将源哈密顿量H_A的度D多项式算子态转导为目标哈密顿量H_B=π(H_A)的多项式态；使用算子傅里叶变换与相对解码恢复源算子而非单项选择标签，并定义相对距离d_rel为源与目标迹首次不一致的度数。

Result: 证明2D<d_rel等价于保持度D多项式间所有内积；给出d_rel≥d_ord，并构造d_ord=O(1)而d_rel=Θ(n)的高效解码族；框架支持非均匀系数与非对易相互作用，可推广至费米子、qudit与玻色子系统，应用于近似优化与Gibbs采样。在非线性成对最优多项式交集变体中，相对解码的量子得分为0.643，优于最佳测试经典启发式的0.606，超出3个百分点。

Conclusion: 相对解码将(H)DQI推广为代数同态下的状态转导，用源多项式态制备替代导频态，放宽距离限制并拓展适用范围，展现出对经典启发式方法的实证优势。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) and its Hamiltonian extension (HDQI) prepare states whose amplitudes are low-degree polynomials of an objective, using Fourier transforms and coherent decoding. We recast this approach as quantum state transduction through algebra homomorphisms. We transfer an efficiently preparable operator state of a degree-$D$ polynomial in a source Hamiltonian $H_A$ to the corresponding polynomial state of a target Hamiltonian $H_B=π(H_A)$, where $π$ is a unital $*$-homomorphism between the finite-dimensional source and target $C^*$-algebras. The transduction uses operator Fourier transforms and relative decoding to recover source operators rather than individual term-selection labels. The relative distance $d_{\mathrm{rel}}$ is the first degree at which source and target traces disagree. We prove that $2D<d_{\mathrm{rel}}$ is equivalent to preserving all inner products between degree-$D$ polynomials. Moreover, $d_{\mathrm{rel}}\ge d_{\mathrm{ord}}$, where $d_{\mathrm{ord}}$ is the ordinary distance used in (H)DQI, and we give families with $d_{\mathrm{ord}}=O(1)$ but $d_{\mathrm{rel}}=Θ(n)$ and efficient decoders. Our framework replaces the complicated pilot state preparation by the more modular task of preparing a source polynomial state. DQI and HDQI arise as relation-free special cases. The framework accommodates nonuniform coefficients and noncommuting interactions and extends to fermionic, qudit, and bosonic systems, with applications to approximate optimization and Gibbs sampling. As evidence of advantages, for a nonlinear pairwise variant of optimal polynomial intersection where constant $d_{\mathrm{ord}}$ limits DQI-style preparations, relative decoding yields an ideal quantum score of 0.643 versus 0.606 for the best tested classical heuristic, a gap exceeding 3 percentage points.

</details>


### [206] [From Quantum to Classical Dissipative Spin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35112)
*Alireza Ataei,Leila Ataei,Olle Eriksson,Vahid Azimi Mousolou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（QLLG）方程在大自旋经典极限下导出为经典LLG方程，从而为耗散磁化动力学的LLG方程提供微观量子基础，并用解析证明与相互作用自旋系统的数值验证加以支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLG方程是描述耗散磁化动力学的基础方程，而新提出的量子类比QLLG方程将框架推广到量子自旋系统，但QLLG方程在相互作用自旋系统的大自旋经典极限下是否能还原为LLG方程尚属未解问题。

Method: 作者对QLLG方程取大自旋经典极限，解析地推导出经典LLG方程，并针对一个相互作用自旋系统进行数值计算以验证该对应关系。

Result: 解析推导表明LLG方程确实是QLLG方程的大自旋经典极限，数值结果在相互作用自旋系统中验证了这一对应；数值计算还表明，哈密顿量中自旋数目越多，量子效应越不明显。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了耗散自旋动力学的量子到经典描述，为LLG方程提供了微观基础，并说明量子效应随自旋数增大而减弱。

Abstract: The Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation provides the fundamental description of dissipative magnetization dynamics. A recently proposed quantum analog, the quantum Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (QLLG) equation, extends this framework to quantum spin systems. However, whether the QLLG equation recovers the LLG equation in the classical large-spin limit for interacting spin systems remains an open question. Here, we demonstrate this connection by deriving the LLG equation as the classical large-spin limit of the QLLG equation. We establish this correspondence analytically and verify it numerically for an interacting spin system. These results provide a quantum-to-classical description of dissipative spin dynamics, offering a microscopic foundation for the LLG equation. From numerical calculations provided here, we also conclude that quantum effects become much less noticeable for systems with larger number of spins in the Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [207] [Accurate and bounded approximation of quantum correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35114)
*Alexander van Lomwel,Florian Mintert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对非可积量子系统精确模拟的指数级难度，本文为无限温度关联函数提出有限区域近似方法，并推导出严格的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非可积量子系统的精确经典模拟在系统规模稍大时即变得不可行，但其对预测多体动力学至关重要。近似方法虽能降低成本，却通常缺乏对与精确结果偏差的严格保证。

Method: 针对无限温度关联函数，将完整动力学限制在一个可计算的有限尺寸区域内进行近似，并推导该近似的严格误差界；以自关联为重点进行验证，并可推广至为其他近似方法（如矩阵乘积算符模拟）提供误差界。

Result: 该有限区域方法在自关联的计算中，在一维系统及更高维情形下均表现出很高的精度；同时能够为矩阵乘积算符等近似方法给出严格的误差界。

Conclusion: 有限区域近似为无限温度关联函数提供了兼具精度与严格误差保证的计算途径，并可作为评估其他近似方法误差的通用工具。

Abstract: The exact classical simulation of non-integrable quantum systems rapidly becomes intractable beyond small system sizes, yet its role is central to predicting many-body dynamics. Approximate methods alleviate this cost but typically lack rigorous guarantees on their deviation from the exact result. For the infinite-temperature correlation function, we derive a rigorous error bound for approximating the full dynamics within a computable finite-sized region. Applied to autocorrelations, this finite-region approach is especially accurate across one-dimensional systems and beyond, and can further provide rigorous error bounds for other approximate methods, demonstrated here for matrix-product-operator simulations.

</details>


### [208] [Asymptotically Good Quantum Codes with Addressable Transversal T Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35129)
*Tongyin Lin,Bujiao Wu,Bin Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构建了一类具有常数速率和线性距离的二进制 CSS 码族，支持完全可寻址的横向 T 门，且无需后续纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错量子计算中，同时实现横向非 Clifford 门与良好纠错参数是重要目标，传统码难以兼顾。

Method: 结合代数几何码与合适的二进制嵌入，获得二进制码的广义可除性；将最小二进制嵌入长度问题转化为仿射空间上的最小权重问题。

Result: 构造出显式的常数速率、线性距离二进制 CSS 码族，可完全可寻址地横向实现任意指定的逻辑 T 门张量积；并数值改进了渐近速率与相对距离界中的常数。

Conclusion: 该构造表明横向非 Clifford 门与良好纠错参数可同时实现，且数值优化可提升参数界。

Abstract: Designing quantum codes with both transversal non-Clifford gates and good error-correcting parameters is an important goal in fault-tolerant quantum computation. Here, we construct an explicit family of binary CSS codes with constant rate and linear distance that admit fully addressable transversal $T$ gates. Specifically, any prescribed tensor product of logical powers of $T$ is implemented by a tensor product of physical powers of $T$, without any subsequent correction. Our construction combines algebraic-geometry codes with suitable binary embedding to obtain generalized divisibility of binary codes. This divisibility then enables fully addressable transversal $T$ gates. Furthermore, we formulate the minimum binary embedding length problem as a minimum-weight problem over an affine space and numerically improve the constants in the asymptotic rate and relative-distance bounds.

</details>


### [209] [The Overlap Gap Property: Separating Quantum and Quantum-Inspired Approximate Optimization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35131)
*Mark Goh,Thorge Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明MF-AOA（QAOA的量子启发经典算法）无法对具有Overlap Gap Property的问题给出任意近优解，因为它等价于受OGP限制的有限记忆AMP算法，从而说明此类问题上QAOA可超越MF-AOA与AMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究QAOA相对其量子启发的经典对应算法（MF-AOA）是否具有真正的量子优势，并寻找OGP问题作为区分二者性能的具体场景。

Method: 将MF-AOA与有限记忆的Approximate Message Passing (AMP)算法建立联系，利用AMP受OGP限制的已知结论进行理论论证；并在具有OGP的Max-4-XORSAT实例上数值模拟QAOA性能，辅以有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: QAOA能够越过OGP障碍，而MF-AOA/AMP不能；有限尺寸标度分析表明深度依赖更倾向于超多项式而非多项式；在OGP障碍附近观察到QAOA性能的不可微点，最优参数从绝热调度转变为非绝热调度。

Conclusion: 具有OGP的问题是一类QAOA可普遍超越MF-AOA与AMP算法的实例，且该超越伴随从绝热到非绝热调度的相变特征。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that the Mean-Field Approximate Optimization Algorithm (MF-AOA), a quantum-inspired classical algorithm of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), is unable to give arbitrary near optimal solution for problems exhibiting the Overlap Gap Property (OGP). We show this by relating the MF-AOA to finite-memory Approximate Message Passing (AMP) algorithms, which are known to be limited in performance by the OGP. Thus, our paper argues that problems exhibiting the OGP are one such instance where the QAOA can outperform the MF-AOA and AMP algorithms in general. We supplement this by numerically simulating the performance of the QAOA on instances of Max-$4$-XORSAT that exhibit the OGP and find that the QAOA is able to surpass the barrier, while our finite-size scaling analysis favors a super-polynomial over a polynomial depth dependence. In addition, we observe a non-differentiable point in the performance of the QAOA near by the OGP barrier, where the optimal QAOA parameters change from an adiabatic to a non-adiabatic schedule.

</details>


### [210] [Weakly interacting fermionic Gibbs states are Gaussian mixtures and classically simulable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35159)
*Zhengyi Han,Yuanchen Zhao,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了弱相互作用费米子体系的吉布斯态可表示为费米高斯态的凸混合，并据此构造了多项式时间的经典采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定相互作用费米子体系可被高效经典模拟的参数区间是量子多体物理中的重要问题，本文旨在严格刻画弱相互作用费米子的高斯性与经典可模拟性。

Method: 针对局域相互作用费米子哈密顿量 H=H0+V（H0 为二次型，V 为相互作用项）的吉布斯态，在任意有限温度和任意空间维度下，证明存在正相互作用强度阈值，使得低于该阈值时吉布斯态是费米高斯态的凸混合；并基于该结构，通过在叶子为高斯态的树上进行 Metropolis 更新构造经典采样器。

Result: 证明了存在一个正的相互作用强度下界，低于该强度时吉布斯态可写为费米高斯态的凸混合；并据此得到该吉布斯态的多项式时间经典采样算法。

Conclusion: 弱相互作用费米子体系具有高斯性与经典可模拟性，其吉布斯态可在多项式时间内被经典采样，表明该参数区间属于可高效经典模拟的区域。

Abstract: Identifying regimes in which interacting fermionic systems admit efficient classical simulation is a crucial task in quantum many-body physics. In this work, we rigorously establish the Gaussianity and classical simulability of sufficiently weakly interacting fermions. For the Gibbs state of a local interacting fermionic Hamiltonian $H=H_0+V$ at any finite temperature and in any spatial dimension, where $H_0$ is quadratic and $V$ is the interaction term, we show that there exists a positive interaction strength below which the Gibbs state is a convex mixture of fermionic Gaussian states. Moreover, based on this structure, we construct a polynomial-time classical sampler for the Gibbs state via Metropolis updates on a tree whose leaves are Gaussian states.

</details>


### [211] [Deployment of Large IQP Circuit Born Machines on Qubit-Limited Hardware Using Noise Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35163)
*Ju-Young Ryu,Wooyeong Song,Kwangil Bae,Wonhyuk Lee,Ilkwon Sohn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种IQP量子电路Born机（QCBM）部署协议：通过在逻辑层注入噪声将大电路概率性分解为可在有限量子比特硬件上采样的子簇，并结合轻量重采样缓解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IQP型QCBM的损失函数可经典高效估计，但推理阶段仍需量子计算机，因此可经典训练出超越现有硬件量子比特数的大型模型，这促使研究如何用有限量子比特硬件采样更大规模的IQP QCBM。

Method: 借鉴含噪IQP电路经典模拟的洞见，提出部署协议：在逻辑层注入噪声，概率性将原电路分解为更小的、可在有限量子比特处理器上采样的子簇；并对已知含噪量子比特进行轻量重采样以缓解引入误差。

Result: 对浅层IQP QCBM的数值模拟，以及在IBM Heron上将316量子比特IQP电路部署到156量子比特硬件的实验表明：误差缓解提升了经验分布与目标分布的相似度；且相比具有准确单比特边缘分布的经典基线，该协议更准确地保持了相关结构。

Conclusion: 该部署协议可有效利用有限量子比特硬件采样更大规模IQP QCBM，误差缓解改善分布相似性，并在保持相关结构方面优于经典基线。

Abstract: Quantum circuit Born machines (QCBMs) built with instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) circuits have loss functions that can be efficiently estimated classically, but require quantum computers during inference. Thus, classical computers can train large IQP QCBMs that exceed the qubit numbers of available quantum hardware. This motivates research into how qubit-limited quantum computers can be used to sample from larger IQP QCBMs. Leveraging insights from classical simulation of noisy IQP circuits, we propose a deployment protocol for IQP QCBMs that injects noise at the logical level to probabilistically decompose the original circuit into smaller clusters that can be sampled on qubit-limited quantum processors. We also apply lightweight resampling of known noisy qubits to mitigate the impact of the introduced error. Numerical simulations of our protocol applied to shallow IQP QCBMs and IBM Heron experiments deploying a 316-qubit IQP circuit onto 156-qubit hardware show that mitigation improves the similarity of the empirical distribution to the target distribution. The proposed deployment protocol also crucially preserves correlation structures more accurately than a classical baseline that has accurate single-qubit marginals.

</details>


### [212] [Positronium Laser Deceleration, Cooling and Trapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35202)
*Barna Mendei,Helmut Ritsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究对正电子素（Ps）束的激光减速、冷却与光学捕获，证实利用现有mJ级、约100 ns脉冲激光技术即可在原子湮灭前实现高效减速与捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正电子素可实现极冷高密集合体，为超辐射激光和玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚提供基础，但其快速基态湮灭及高反冲位移与线宽比给时序控制和激光功率带来新挑战，需评估激光冷却与捕获的可行性。

Method: 扩展的数值模拟，在标准多普勒冷却几何下基于1³S–2³P跃迁实现减速、冷却与捕获；纳入完整塞曼态流形，并加入2³P–3³D跃迁的额外横向激光以改善时序与最终温度。

Result: 数值研究确认可同时在1³S–2³P跃迁上减速、冷却并光学捕获Ps原子；横向激光改进后时序与最终温度满足末端光捕获要求；所需激光功率与几何参数处于当前技术可达范围（约100 ns、mJ级脉冲）。

Conclusion: 现有激光技术足以在多数原子湮灭前实现Ps的有效减速与捕获；未来通过光学腔或空芯光纤集体增强冷却与捕获，有望快速制备超冷Ps体系。

Abstract: We numerically study laser deceleration, cooling and trapping of host fast ortho-positronium (Ps) bunches as produced in silica microchannel targets using positron bunches from a Surko trap. While Doppler laser cooling routinely allows to cool neutral atom species below milli-Kelvin kinetic temperatures, the fast ground state annihilation and the high recoil shift to line-width ratio in Ps pose new challenging limitations on timing and the required laser power. Earlier theoretical work and first experiments already exhibit viable prospects for radiation pressure cooling to create unprecedented cold and dense Ps ensembles. Our extended numerical studies confirm very good prospects to simultaneously decelerate, cool and optically trap Ps atoms in a standard Doppler cooling geometry on the $1^3\mathrm{S}$--$2^3\mathrm{P}$ transition. Including the full Zeeman state manifolds and adding additional transverse lasers on the $2^3\mathrm{P}$--$3^3\mathrm{D}$ transition improves timing and final temperature to allow for optical trapping at the end. The required laser powers and geometry parameters to implement effective slowing and trapping within a time frame, where the majority of atoms is not annihilated, is in reach of current technology of $\mathrm{mJ}$ energy pulses of approximately $100\ \mathrm{ns}$ duration. Future extensions to collectively enhance cooling and trapping in optical cavities or hollow-core fibres should finally allow fast preparation of ultra-cold Ps systems as future basis of superradiant lasing and Bose--Einstein condensates.

</details>


### [213] [Computational Complexity of Clifford Template Compilation: Are Quantum Computers Useful for Compiling Quantum Circuits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35239)
*Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究Clifford模板编译问题：给定可重复Clifford操作的有序族，如何选择各操作的重复次数以实现目标Pauli算子变换，并刻画该逆问题的复杂度分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford模板编译问题源于量子纠错中受限物理或容错约束下仅用允许的Clifford操作搜索逻辑操作的需求。尽管正向Clifford动力学可高效经典模拟，但其逆问题存在显著复杂度转变，值得系统刻画。

Method: 论文从可交换模板出发，区分指数为无限制整数与限定为{0,1}两类情形：前者利用Abelian群闭包与有限域离散对数构造量子算法，后者去掉群闭包并结合CNOT-only及X型Pauli等特例进行复杂度归约；同时对自逆Clifford作用分别分析二元可行性与总重复次数约束下的复杂度。

Result: 对可交换模板且指数无限制，可行性属于NP∩BQP，并能给出一个解及完整的指数关系格；即使k=1也涉及F_{2^r}^×上的有限域离散对数。一般情形下，将指数限制为{0,1}会破坏Abelian群闭包，使变k时可行性为NP完全，即便仅含严格可交换的CNOT操作与X型Pauli。对可交换自逆Clifford作用，二元可行性与恢复一个解均可在经典多项式时间内完成，但加入总重复次数上界后变为NP完全，即使仅用CNOT操作。

Conclusion: Clifford模板编译问题呈现丰富的复杂度图景：既包含经典可 tractable 的情形，也包含可用量子多项式时间算法求解的问题，还存在多种NP完全变体，为受约束量子操作合成提供了复杂度层面的完整分类依据。

Abstract: A Clifford template is a finite ordered family of repeatable Clifford operations, and an instantiation specifies how many times each operation is applied. The Clifford template compilation problem asks how to choose these repetition numbers so that the template realizes a target transformation of Pauli operators. This problem arises, for example, when searching for logical operations in quantum error correction using only Clifford operations permitted by physical or fault-tolerance constraints. Although forward Clifford dynamics is efficiently classically simulable, this inverse problem has sharp complexity transitions. For commuting templates with unrestricted integer exponents, feasibility lies in $\mathrm{NP}\cap\mathrm{BQP}$ and a constructive quantum algorithm returns a particular solution together with the full exponent-relation lattice; already at $k=1$, recovering the repetition number contains finite-field discrete logarithm over $\mathbb{F}_{2^r}^{\times}$. In general, restricting every exponent to $\{0,1\}$ removes the Abelian-group closure and makes feasibility NP-complete for variable $k$, even for exactly commuting CNOT-only operations and X-type Paulis. For commuting self-inverse Clifford actions, both binary feasibility and recovery of one solution are classically polynomial-time solvable, but imposing a bound on the total repetition count is NP-complete, even for CNOT-only operations. These results reveal a rich complexity landscape within Clifford template compilation, spanning classically tractable cases, problems admitting quantum polynomial-time algorithms, and NP-complete variants.

</details>


### [214] [An Exact Polynomial Task-Risk Bridge for Time-Multiplexed Photonic Quantum Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35243)
*Yuqi Zhang,Tianyu Zhou,Yilun Jiang,Tian Chen,Hao Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对时间复用（TDM）光子储备池，建立了从任务到预测风险的精确解析桥梁，将架构设计从"枚举仿真"转变为"基于精确陈述的剪枝"，从而高效筛选候选TDM架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDM光子储备池通常仅靠少数数据集上的得分来评判，难以反映芯片整体性能；其设计依赖枚举候选电路并逐一仿真，效率低且无法保证找到优良结构。因此需要一个能精确连接任务与预测风险、从而指导架构选择的理论框架。

Method: 针对时间展开的无源线性网络（含数据调制门或源）与岭回归读出，分三步推导任务到预测风险的精确关系：(i) 任务特征——有限样本风险仅通过训练窗口的有限统计量及其与目标的相关性依赖于时间序列，由编码与光路选择决定；(ii) 编码——每个输出矩是数据调制位点特征的多项式，阶数与支撑由光路元组界定；门编码读取特征函数并跨时间延迟产生交互，压缩编码读取矩生成函数，位移编码读取低阶矩，配合正交接收机分别给出加性与线性模型；(iii) 读出——对零差、外差和光子数接收机，风险精确闭合，其对样本数的依赖表现为信噪比模式上的显式和；阈值点击允许带认证误差的有限字典近似。此外，不共享光的部分具有独立状态，接收机仅通过连接它们的测量事件进行组合。

Result: 在七个数据集上的验证表明，所有精确陈述均达到机器精度一致。所得三步框架给出了一个有用的候选TDM架构空间，可由精确陈述而非试错仿真进行剪枝。

Conclusion: 该工作将TDM光子储备池的评估与设计从经验仿真提升为精确解析，为未来根据目标需求选择TDM芯片提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Time-multiplexed (TDM) photonic reservoirs are usually judged by their scores on a few datasets, which say little about a chip's overall performance, and designed by enumerating candidate circuits and simulating each one, which is inefficient and gives no guarantee of finding a good structure. For a time-unrolled passive linear network with data-modulated gates or sources and a ridge readout, we derive an exact bridge from the task to the prediction risk in three steps. (i) Task features: the finite-shot risk depends on the time series only through finitely many statistics of its training windows and their correlations with the target, selected by the encoding and the optical paths. (ii) Encoding: every output moment is a polynomial in the features of the data-modulated sites, with degree and support bounded by tuples of optical paths. Gate encoding reads the characteristic function and creates interactions across time lags; squeezing encoding reads the moment-generating function and displacement encoding low-order moments, and with quadrature receivers they yield only additive and linear models, respectively, for every topology. (iii) Readout: for homodyne, heterodyne and photon-number receivers the risk closes exactly, and its dependence on the number of shots is an explicit sum over signal-to-noise modes; threshold clicks admit finite-dictionary approximations with certified error. Parts of the network that share no light carry independent states, which a receiver combines only through measurement events that join them. Checks on seven datasets confirm every exact statement to machine precision.
