The three steps thus yield a useful space of candidate TDM architectures, pruned by exact statements rather than by trial simulation, which can in future guide the selection of TDM chips for target requirements.

</details>


### [215] [Gauge freedom and efficient algorithms for Lindbladian learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35265)
*Steven T Flammia,Savar D Sinha,Yu Tong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在SPAM噪声下学习局域Lindbladian的问题，刻画了SPAM导致的内禀不可学习性（规范依赖性），并为所有普适规范不变量成分提供了SPAM鲁棒的、仅需单量子比特操作的样本高效学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作虽提出了在理想访问假设下的可扩展Lindbladian学习算法，但SPAM噪声会使不同的Lindbladian在实验上无法区分，导致部分成分从根本上不可学习。因此需要刻画这一障碍，并设计对SPAM鲁棒的学习算法。

Method: 针对有界度局域Lindbladian，识别出一族与可信单量子比特控制对易的保局域性规范变换，据此对Lindbladian成分进行分类；对一般规范依赖成分，构造Lindbladian（即使施加完全正性约束）证明其具有与系统尺寸无关的Ω(1)不确定度；并设计仅使用可信单量子比特操作、无需辅助比特与纠缠控制的SPAM鲁棒算法来学习所有普适规范不变量成分。

Result: 对规范依赖成分，展示了即使满足完全正性仍存在与系统尺寸无关的Ω(1)不确定度；算法使用Õ(ε^{-2}log(N/δ))次实验及总演化时间（常数局域度与度数下），允许全局SPAM随N增大任意偏离理想，仅要求非消失的局部可见度；并给出物理正性约束下边界诱导的额外哈密顿量系数规范不变性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该规范感知框架与SPAM对消技术为开放量子系统的可扩展刻画提供了实用工具；普适规范不变量可高效学习，而一般额外的规范不变参数的学习仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a local Lindbladian in the presence of state-preparation-and-measurement (SPAM) noise. Although recent work has developed scalable learning algorithms under idealized access assumptions, SPAM can make distinct Lindbladians experimentally indistinguishable, thus making part of the Lindbladian fundamentally unlearnable. We give a sharp characterization of this obstruction for bounded-degree local Lindbladians. We identify a family of locality-preserving gauge transformations that commutes with trusted single-qubit control, and use it to classify Lindbladian components. For the generically gauge dependent components, we construct Lindbladians to show that they can have $Ω(1)$ uncertainty independent of system size, even after imposing complete positivity.
  We complement this characterization with SPAM-robust algorithms for learning every universally gauge-invariant component. Our algorithms use only trusted single-qubit operations, require neither ancillas nor entangling control, and use $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\left(ε^{-2}\log(N/δ)\right)$ experiments and total evolution time for constant locality and degree. Our guarantees allow global SPAM to become arbitrarily far from ideal as $N$ grows, requiring only nonvanishing local visibility. We also establish sufficient conditions for boundary-induced gauge invariance of additional Hamiltonian coefficients under physical positivity constraints, though learning these additional parameters in general remains an open problem. More broadly, our gauge-aware framework and SPAM-cancellation techniques offer a practical toolkit for the scalable characterization of open quantum systems.

</details>


### [216] [Generalized LIMDDs: Succinctness and Canonicity for Decision Diagrams Modulo a Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35271)
*Arend-Jan Quist,Alexis de Colnet,Thomas Reps,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究将ROBDD的Myhill–Nerode商按群作用进行粗化，构造以群元素标注边的决策图，并完整刻画由C^{≤k}R_q与X生成的群族在量子函数表示上的简洁性与算法性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROBDD是最小自动机式表示，而Pauli-LIMDD通过Pauli群粗化已在量子函数表示上取得优势。作者希望将群粗化推广到更一般的两参数群族，量化其相对于Pauli-LIMDD的简洁性提升，并判断查询、变换与范式计算是否仍保持可处理性，从而理解粗化粒度与表示效率/算法难度之间的权衡。

Method: 以residuals为结点、按群G轨道合并结点并在边上记录群元素，形成群粗化决策图框架；取G为由C^{≤k}R_q（至多k个控制比特的q阶相位旋转）与比特翻转X生成的两参数群族；用超图态作为分离对象，比较其相对于Pauli-LIMDD的简洁性阶；考察五个查询与八种变换的可处理性；给出五条归约规则并证明范式唯一性及多项式时间可计算性。

Result: 该群族相对Pauli-LIMDD可带来指数级简洁性提升，并完全确定了整个族的简洁性阶；超图态总能被族中某个成员高效表示，充当分离对象。五个查询与八种变换的可处理性在整个族内保持不变，且与Pauli-LIMDD表现相同。五条归约系统的范式对族中每个成员唯一，且可在LIMDD规模的多项式时间内计算。

Conclusion: 群粗化决策图为量子函数表示提供了可调的简洁性—算法性权衡框架：在两参数群族内可获得指数级压缩且保持查询/变换可处理性与唯一范式。但当粗化超出（反）对角群时，最小规模范式的计算是非局部的，可能需要重建整个图，表明更强的粗化会以局部可计算性为代价。

Abstract: A reduced ordered decision diagram is the minimal automaton of a function on words of fixed length: its nodes are the residuals, and reduction is the Myhill--Nerode quotient. We study what happens when that quotient is coarsened by a group. Fix a group $G$ acting on residuals, merge two nodes when their residuals lie in one $G$-orbit, and record the group element on the edge. For functions $\{0,1\}^n\to\mathbb{C}$ and $G$ the Pauli group this is the Local Invertible Map Decision Diagram. We take $G$ from the two-parameter family generated by $C^{\leq k}R_q$, the phase rotation of order $q$ with up to $k$ control qubits, with and without the bit flip $X$. We show that this gives exponential succinctness improvements compared to Pauli-LIMDD, and we determine the succinctness order of the family completely. The separating objects are hypergraph states, which can always be efficiently represented by some member of the family. We settle the tractability of five queries and eight transformations, which is invariant across the family, and shows the same behavior as Pauli-LIMDD. We give a five-rule reduction system whose normal forms are unique for every member of the family, and we show that this canonical form is computable in polynomial time in the size of the LIMDD. We show that, when coarsening beyond the (anti-)diagonal groups, the calculation of a minimal sized normal form turns out to be non-local and it might to rebuild the whole diagram.

</details>


### [217] [Circuit-level benchmarks of GKP-concatenated qLDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35282)
*Yuan Yao,Ruipeng Xing,Jianshuo Gao,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在统一的仿真框架下，对 BB 码与 tricycle 码作为 GKP 级联外层码进行基准测试，从码容量噪声逐步推进到含噪综合征提取和调度分辨的位移传播。结果表明，模拟信息辅助的 BP-OSD 在三种噪声模型下均优于硬判决解码，电路级阈值中 BB 序列约为 σ_th≈0.212（对应约 10.46 dB 压缩），tricycle 序列约为 0.142（约 13.94 dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 GKP 码的可扩展容错量子纠错需要有限速率的纠错码，能够在真实电路噪声下利用模拟 GKP 信息，但量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）外层码作为电路级 GKP 级联候选尚未被系统比较。

Method: 采用统一仿真框架，对 BB 码和 tricycle 码进行基准测试，噪声模型从码容量噪声依次推进到重复含噪综合征提取，再到调度分辨的位移传播；解码方面比较模拟信息辅助的置信传播结合有序统计解码（BP-OSD）与硬判决解码。

Result: 在三种噪声模型下，模拟信息辅助的 BP-OSD 均给出高于硬判决解码的有限尺寸交叉估计。在完整电路模型下，所选 BB 序列的模拟信息交叉阈值为 σ_th≈0.212，tricycle 序列为 0.142，分别对应约 10.46 dB 和 13.94 dB 的压缩需求。在当前常用仿真假设下，所选 BB 序列的有限尺寸交叉估计也高于所选 tricycle 序列。

Conclusion: 模拟 GKP 信息在电路级噪声下仍能改善解码性能；在所采用的仿真假设下，所选 BB 码序列相比 tricycle 码序列具有更高的有限尺寸交叉阈值估计，表明其作为 GKP 级联外层码更具优势。

Abstract: Scalable fault-tolerant quantum error correction based on Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes requires finite-rate outer codes that can exploit analog GKP information under realistic circuit noise. However, quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) outer codes have not been systematically compared as candidates for circuit-level GKP concatenation. Here, we benchmark BB and tricycle codes using a unified simulation framework that progresses from code-capacity noise to repeated noisy syndrome extraction and schedule-resolved displacement propagation. Across all three noise models, analog-informed belief-propagation with ordered-statistics decoding (BP-OSD) yields higher finite-size crossing estimates than hard-decision decoding. In the full circuit model, the analog-informed crossings are \(σ_{\mathrm{th}}\simeq0.212\) for the selected BB sequence and \(0.142\) for the tricycle sequence, corresponding to squeezing requirements of approximately \(10.46\,\mathrm{dB}\) and \(13.94\,\mathrm{dB}\), respectively. These results show that analog GKP information continues to improve decoding under circuit-level noise. Under the common simulation assumptions used here, the selected BB sequence also yields a higher finite-size crossing estimate than the selected tricycle sequence.

</details>


### [218] [Distributional Variants of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35327)
*Uma Girish,Kunal Mittal,Barak Nehoran,Ran Raz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子查询算法经典模拟猜想在非均匀输入分布下的等价性：证明偏置乘积分布与等重切片分布下的模拟猜想均等价于均匀分布原猜想，并将Aaronson-Ambainis猜想推广到这些分布且证其等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂度理论中长期存在一个猜想：在均匀输入分布下，量子查询算法可被经典查询算法以多项式查询开销模拟。该猜想是理解"决策问题的指数量子优势是否必须依赖额外结构"的核心。然而现实中的输入分布往往并非均匀，因此需要考察该猜想在其他自然分布下是否仍然成立、与原猜想关系如何。

Method: 1) 考虑偏置乘积分布μ_p：各输入比特为独立同分布Bernoulli变量，偏置固定为p；证明对每个固定p∈(0,1)，μ_p下量子查询算法存在多项式开销经典模拟当且仅当均匀分布下同样成立。2) 考虑切片分布ν_p：在Hamming重量为⌊pn⌋的串集合上均匀分布，证明ν_p与均匀分布之间的类似等价。3) 在布尔超立方体上有界低次多项式框架下，将Aaronson-Ambainis猜想（均匀分布下非可忽略方差的多项式必存在有影响力变量）推广到偏置乘积分布与切片分布，并证明这些变体均与原猜想等价。

Result: 对所有固定p∈(0,1)，μ_p分布下的量子查询算法经典模拟猜想等价于均匀分布下的原猜想；ν_p分布（Hamming重量⌊pn⌋切片上均匀）亦与均匀分布等价。对于Aaronson-Ambainis猜想，其在偏置乘积分布和切片分布下的所有类比变体均被证明与原Aaronson-Ambainis猜想等价。

Conclusion: 量子查询模拟猜想及Aaronson-Ambainis猜想对输入分布的选择具有鲁棒性：在偏置乘积分布和等重切片分布下，这些猜想并不比均匀分布情形更强或更弱，而是完全等价。这表明若要在这些自然分布下获得指数量子优势，仍需依赖额外结构。

Abstract: A longstanding conjecture in quantum complexity theory asserts that, under the uniform input distribution, quantum query algorithms can be polynomially simulated by classical query algorithms. More precisely, the acceptance probability of any quantum query algorithm can be approximated, on average over uniformly random inputs, by a classical query algorithm, with only polynomial query overhead. The conjecture is central to understanding whether exponential quantum advantages for decision problems necessarily rely on additional structure.
  We study analogues of this conjecture under other natural input distributions and prove that they are all equivalent to the original uniform-distribution conjecture. We first consider the product distribution $μ_p$, where the input bits are independent Bernoulli variables with fixed bias $p$. We show that for every fixed $p \in (0, 1)$, quantum query algorithms under the $μ_p$ distribution admit polynomial-overhead classical simulations if and only if the same holds under the uniform distribution. Second, we consider the distribution $ν_p$ that is uniform over the slice of strings with Hamming weight $\lfloor pn \rfloor$ and prove a similar equivalence for the $ν_p$ distribution and the uniform distribution.
  The Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture is a stronger statement that implies the above-mentioned conjecture and is formulated in terms of bounded low-degree polynomials on the Boolean hypercube. It asserts that under the uniform distribution, any such polynomial with nonnegligible variance must have an influential variable. We formulate analogues of this conjecture, where the underlying distribution is a biased product distribution or a uniform distribution over a slice, and prove that all these variants are equivalent to the original Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture.

</details>


### [219] [Heuristic lower bounds on real Grothendieck constants of finite and infinite order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35399)
*Erika Bene,Tamás Vértesi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用旋转不变三次核给出实数 Grothendieck 常数 $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ 及其无穷维极限 $K_{\mathrm G}$ 的候选下界；提出半球策略在临界阈值处全局最优的猜想，据此可得 $K_{\mathrm G}\geq 9\pi/16\simeq1.767146$，并通过数值优化与有限维构造给出改进下界及维度见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实数 Grothendieck 常数的严格下界（如 $6\pi/11\simeq1.713596$）与猜想真值之间仍存在差距，需要新的构造与分析手段来提升下界，并探索其在有限维及量子信息维度见证中的意义。

Method: 采用旋转不变三次核作为目标函数：连续构造中识别使半球二值策略在三次边界扰动下失稳的显式阈值；用数值证据支持半球策略在该阈值处全局最优的猜想。有限维方面，由最多 24 维空间中的单位向量构造对称系数矩阵，并用大规模 see-saw 优化进行搜索。

Result: 若猜想成立，可得 $K_{\mathrm G}\geq 9\pi/16\simeq1.767146$，优于严格下界 $6\pi/11\simeq1.713596$；see-saw 优化支持连续预测，并在所研究的 $d=4$ 至 $24$ 维给出改进的 $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ 候选下界（以二值最优值精确为前提）。部分矩阵作为二部关联 Bell 泛函时给出有限维维度见证；若相应二值上界成立，可证明每方局域 Hilbert 空间维度至少为五。

Conclusion: 三次核为提升实数 Grothendieck 常数下界提供了有效途径，半球策略的全局最优性猜想是关键；数值结果同时指向有限维常数改进与量子维度见证的潜在应用。

Abstract: We propose candidate lower bounds on the real Grothendieck constants $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ and their infinite-dimensional limit $K_{\mathrm G}$ using rotationally invariant cubic kernels. For the continuous construction, we identify an explicit threshold above which hemispherical binary strategies are unstable under cubic boundary deformations. Supported by numerical evidence, we conjecture that hemispherical strategies are globally optimal at this threshold. This conjecture would imply $K_{\mathrm G}\geq9π/16\simeq1.767146$, improving the rigorous lower bound $6π/11\simeq1.713596$. We also construct finite symmetric coefficient matrices from unit vectors in dimensions up to $24$. Large-scale see-saw optimization supports the continuous predictions and suggests improved lower bounds on $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ in the studied dimensions from $4$ to $24$, conditional on the conjectured binary optima being exact. Selected matrices also yield finite-setting candidate dimension witnesses when interpreted as bipartite correlation Bell functionals. If the corresponding binary upper bounds hold, these witnesses would certify a local Hilbert-space dimension of at least five for each party.

</details>


### [220] [Fixed-Time Gaussian State Transfer via Collective Dissipation in a Fully Static Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35446)
*Austen Couvertier,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全静态、仅依赖耗散的 Gaussian 态传输机制：共享环境的集体耦合固定单一传输时间，无需相干输运或时变控制，并给出解析传输时间 $t^{*}=\pi/g_a$ 与固定时间 Gaussian 信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续变量通信/态传输通常被假定需要相干输运或主动控制。作者希望检验是否仅凭集体耗散即可实现固定时间的 Gaussian 态传输，从而挑战这一既有假设。

Method: 建立仅耗散的 Gaussian 态传输模型，利用亮-暗（bright-dark）分解使动力学封闭，得到解析传输时间 $t^{*}=\pi/g_a$ 与实数干涉因子；分析系综中幅度不对称与相位失谐的影响；通过零阶 O-operator 展开引入有限 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 记忆，考察非马尔可夫效应与快记忆极限。

Result: 动力学在亮-暗分解下封闭，传输时间 $t^{*}=\pi/g_a$，干涉因子为实数，消除动力学相位累积，相位敏感性仅来自 Gaussian 保真度度量；信道对幅度级不对称稳健（表现为均匀速率修饰），而相位级系统失谐通过动力学相位绕转破坏固定时间机制并产生复现行为；有限 OU 记忆仅带来速率与相位重整化，不引入新动力学通道；快记忆区环境关联被迅速抑制，信道对环境失谐不敏感并恢复马尔可夫极限。

Conclusion: 集体耗散本身足以支持固定时间 Gaussian 态传输，无需相干输运或主动控制；这挑战了连续变量通信必须依赖相干输运或主动控制的假设，并定义了由耗散介导、无主动控制的固定时间 Gaussian 通信信道。

Abstract: We establish a fully static, dissipation-only primitive for Gaussian state transfer in which collective coupling to a shared environment fixes a single transfer time without coherent transport or time-dependent control. The dynamics close in a bright--dark decomposition, yielding an analytic transfer time $t^{*} = π/ g_{a}$ and a real interference factor that eliminates dynamical phase accumulation, so that all phase sensitivity arises solely from the Gaussian fidelity metric. This defines a fixed-time Gaussian communication channel in which dissipation mediates information transfer without active control. Ensemble analysis shows that channel performance remains robust to amplitude-level asymmetries, which act as uniform-rate dressing, whereas phase-level system detuning disrupts the fixed-time mechanism via dynamical phase winding and produces revival behavior. Incorporating finite Ornstein--Uhlenbeck memory via a zeroth-order O-operator expansion yields only rate and phase renormalization without introducing new dynamical pathways. In the fast-memory regime, environmental correlations are rapidly suppressed, rendering the channel insensitive to environmental detuning and restoring the Markovian limit. These results show that collective dissipation alone is sufficient to support fixed-time Gaussian state transfer, challenging the assumption that coherent transport or active control are required for continuous-variable communication.

</details>


### [221] [Efficient classical algorithm for estimating linear statistics of Boson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35447)
*Benoit Seron,Hugo Thomas,Eduardo Araujo,Alex Arkhipov,Changhun Oh,Leonardo Novo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色采样输出的线性统计量（基于光子占据数的线性函数）的经典可模拟性，给出了在不同输入态下以加性误差逼近该线性统计量的高效经典算法，并借此统一了分子振动光谱模拟等近期结果，同时展示了该方法可用于经典破解一个基于玻色采样的单向函数提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样是展示量子计算优势的候选方案，但大规模玻色采样器是否有实用计算价值尚不明确。由于结果空间随规模指数增长且分布具有反集中性质，要估计可观测量的物理量必须对输出分布做粗粒化。核心动机是厘清某一类特定粗粒化（即输出光子占据数的线性函数，称为线性统计量）的经典计算复杂度，从而推动将玻色采样器用于实际计算任务。

Method: 作者针对玻色采样分布中基于输出光子占据数线性函数的粗粒化（线性统计量），设计了一种高效经典算法，可在加性误差范围内逼近该统计量。方法覆盖多种输入态，包括福克态（Fock states）和压缩态（squeezed states）。

Result: 1）提出对玻色采样线性统计量在加性误差内的高效经典逼近算法，适用于福克态、压缩态等不同输入态；2）在同一框架内统一了近期关于模拟分子振动光谱的量子启发式经典算法，以及基于探测器分箱的粗粒化分布高效经典逼近结果；3）证明该算法可经典地评估一个基于玻色采样的单向函数提议，而其他密码学应用提议则不受该经典模拟技术影响。

Conclusion: 线性统计量的玻色采样分布可被高效经典逼近，且在统一框架下涵盖多种已有结果并威胁某类密码学提议；非线性统计量的经典可模拟性仍是开放问题，作者将其与含单层相互作用的线性光学电路跃迁振幅计算问题相联系。

Abstract: Boson Sampling is a prominent candidate for the demonstration of quantum computational advantage but it remains unclear whether a large-scale boson sampler can find useful computational applications. The challenge is that to use a boson sampler to estimate, for example, a physical observable, it is necessary to coarse grain the outcome distribution, owing to the exponential size of the outcome space and the anti-concentration properties of the Boson Sampling distribution. In this work, we analyse the complexity of a specific type of coarse-graining of Boson Sampling distributions based on linear functions of the output photon occupation numbers, which we refer to as linear statistics. We present an efficient classical algorithm for approximating linear statistics of boson samplers within additive error for different kinds of input states, such as Fock states and squeezed states. This allows us to unify in the same framework recent results on efficient quantum-inspired classical algorithms for simulating molecular vibronic spectra, or efficient classical approximations of coarse-grained distributions based on detector binning. Additionally, we show how our algorithm can be used to classically evaluate a proposed one-way function based on Boson Sampling, while other proposals for cryptographic applications escape our classical simulation techniques. We leave open the question of classical simulability of non-linear statistics and connect it to the problem of computing transition amplitudes of linear-optical circuits with one layer of interactions.

</details>


### [222] [Temporal trade-offs in high-dimensional entanglement: a comprehensive noise model for optimal time-bin QKD protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35488)
*Alexandra E. Bergmayr-Mann,Gláucia Murta,Marcus Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高维时间bin纠缠在QKD中的权衡问题（发送更多低维光子还是少量高维光子更优），建立含所有相关参数的灵活噪声模型，以单Franson干涉仪、渐近密钥率和共享纠缠为指标，发现当光子对到达率受限（如损耗或泵浦功率受限）时，高维编码仍优于任何qubit方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时间bin纠缠因易实现、鲁棒且可能比简单qubit协议提供更高密钥率而在QKD中颇具潜力。但时间编码与有限时钟分辨率带来一个权衡：应发送更多低维编码光子，还是少量高维光子？该问题难以回答，因其依赖协议整体背景，包括产生率、损耗、暗计数、时间抖动等众多因素。

Method: 本文针对单个Franson干涉仪展开分析，以渐近密钥率和共享纠缠为主要性能指标，构建了一个灵活噪声模型，纳入全部相关参数。

Result: 分析发现确实存在高维编码优于任何qubit版本的区域，这一优势出现在光子对到达率受限的情形下，例如由损耗或有限泵浦功率导致。

Conclusion: 在到达率受限的条件下，高维时间bin编码相较qubit方案仍具优势，为QKD中维度选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: High-dimensional time-bin entanglement is known for having high potential for quantum key distribution (QKD) applications, being easily implementable, robust and may offer better keyrates than simple qubit protocols. The temporal encoding, combined with limited clock resolution, however, implies a trade-off: Is it better to send more low-dimensionally encoded photons or few high-dimensional ones? Answering this question presents a hard challenge as it depends on the entire context of the protocol, including the production rates, losses, dark counts, timing jitters and many more. We answer this question for single Franson based interferometers and asymptotic key rate and shared entanglement as main figures of merit. We present a flexible noise model incorporating all relevant parameters and find that there are indeed regions where high-dimensional encoding still outperforms any qubit based version when the pair arrival rate is limited, for instance through loss or limited pump power.

</details>


### [223] [Fourier-Geometric Circuit Design for Gate and Entanglement Placement in Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35489)
*Seungcheol Oh,Chaemoon Im,Daeyeun Kim,Soohyun Park,Vaneet Aggarwal,Mohsen Heidari,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用编码生成元的伴随作用，将算符空间分解为按频率索引的二维不变平面，证明每个傅里叶系数等于有效态与可观测量在该频率平面上双线性投影之和，从而给出使目标傅里叶系数可达的电路设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化量子电路（PQC）的输出可表示为有限傅里叶级数，其可及频率由数据编码门固定，而傅里叶系数取决于可训练门与纠缠门的排列方式。已有研究虽给出刻画门结构影响系数的度量，但未转化为明确的设计准则，即如何排列门才能使目标系数可达。

Method: 利用编码生成元的伴随作用（adjoint action），将算符空间分解为按频率索引的二维不变平面；证明每个傅里叶系数恰为有效态与可观测量在该频率平面上双线性投影之和。针对泡利编码，高频平面由混合多量子比特泡利串张成，故目标系数仅在本地旋转与纠缠层使有效态和可观测量同时获得该平面支撑时才能贡献输出。对泡利读出与对易双量子比特纠缠器，给出在给定相互作用图上放置本地旋转与纠缠层、使目标系数可达的电路设计规则。

Result: 使用单个编码层，通过放置消融实验验证该设计；在PDEBench回归任务与物理信息麦克斯韦场建模中验证有效性；并进一步证明其对中等模拟门噪声与阻尼具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作将门结构对傅里叶系数的分析转化为显式的电路设计准则，明确了使目标傅里叶系数可达的门排列方式，并在回归与物理建模任务及噪声场景下验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: The output of a parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) can be expressed as a finite Fourier series whose accessible frequencies are fixed by the data-encoding gates. While the encoder determines which frequencies can appear, the corresponding Fourier coefficients depend on how the trainable and entangling gates are arranged. Although existing studies provide metrics for characterizing how gate structure affects Fourier coefficients, they do not translate these analyses into an explicit design criterion specifying how gates should be arranged to make a target coefficient reachable. In this paper, we provide such a criterion. Using the adjoint action of the encoding generator, we decompose operator space into two-dimensional invariant planes indexed by frequency and show that each Fourier coefficient is exactly a sum of bilinear projections of the effective state and observable onto the planes at that frequency. Because, for Pauli encodings, high-frequency planes are spanned by mixed multi-qubit Pauli strings, a target coefficient can contribute to the output only when local rotations and entangling layers are arranged so that both the effective state and observable acquire support on one of its planes. For Pauli readouts and commuting two-qubit entanglers, this yields a circuit design rule that specifies, for a given interaction graph, a placement of local rotations and entangling layers that makes a target Fourier coefficient reachable. Using a single encoding layer, we validate the proposed design through a placement ablation, regression tasks from PDEBench and physics-informed Maxwell field modeling, and further demonstrate its robustness to moderate simulated gate noise and damping.

</details>


### [224] [How fast can a parent estimate the value of their children? A quantum algorithm for stochastic games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35511)
*Marien Raat,Merlin Incerti-Medici,James R. Wootton,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Daniel Bultrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种求解随机二人博弈（expectiminimax 树）的量子算法，以 $O(K^{D}deg^{D/4}(N/\varepsilon)D^{4D}\log(deg N/\varepsilon)^{4D})$ 次叶值查询估计博弈值至均方误差 $\varepsilon$，深度为常数时达到 $\widetilde O(deg^{m/2}\varepsilon^{-1})$，同时保留分支因子与精度的二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机博弈树（对抗层与随机层交替）的搜索复杂度较高；已知量子算法分别对纯对抗树（分支因子二次加速）和单次期望（精度二次加速）有效，但二者嵌套时的可组合性尚不明确。本文旨在验证这两种加速在 expectiminimax 结构中能否同时保持。

Method: 对随机层使用去随机化的多层蒙特卡洛（derandomised multilevel Monte Carlo）估计器；对对抗层使用相干二分搜索（coherent binary search）；通过引入均方误差与一致误差保证之间的转换，以及与量子均值估计的嵌套组合，将两者复合。算法对满足以下条件的博弈均适用：父节点值是子节点值的 Lipschitz 函数，随机顶点处的值对子节点线性，且确定性步骤需具备亚线性查询复杂度的量子子程序。

Result: 算法查询复杂度为 $O(K^{D}deg^{D/4}(N/\varepsilon)D^{4D}\log(deg N/\varepsilon)^{4D})$，$D=2m$。视深度为常数时，界为 $\widetilde O(deg^{m/2}\varepsilon^{-1})$，相较经典的 $\widetilde O(deg^{m}\varepsilon^{-2})$，在分支因子上获得二次加速、在精度上获得二次加速。

Conclusion: 对抗树的分支因子二次加速与单次期望的精度二次加速在相互嵌套时均可保留。算法虽针对随机博弈表述，但适用于更一般的 Lipschitz—线性结构；加速是否保持取决于确定性步骤能否量子化到亚线性查询复杂度。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for stochastic $2$-player games. Their game trees are the expectiminimax trees of classical game search: $m$ adversarial layers alternate with $m$ chance layers, every node has $deg$ children, and the leaf values lie in an interval of length $N$. The algorithm estimates the value of the game to root mean square error $ε$ with \[ O\!\left( K^{D}\, deg^{\frac{D}{4}} \, \frac{N}ε \, D^{4D} \log\left( \frac{deg N}ε \right)^{4D} \right), \qquad D = 2m \] queries to the leaf values, where $K$ is an absolute constant. If we treat the depth $D$ as a constant as is sometimes done in the literature, the bound is $\widetilde O(deg^{m/2}ε^{-1})$ against the classical $\widetilde O(deg^{m}ε^{-2})$. So the quadratic speedup in the branching factor known for adversarial trees and the quadratic speedup in the accuracy known for a single expectation both survive when we nest one inside the other.
  We use a derandomised multilevel Monte Carlo estimator for chance layers and a coherent binary search for adversarial layers. The composition is done by introducing a conversion between root-mean-square and uniform error guarantees, and a composition to nest it with quantum mean estimation. We state the algorithm for stochastic games, but it is valid for the following conditions: the value of a parent is a Lipschitz function of the values of its children and the value at a chance vertex is linear in its children, however the speedup is preserved only if the deterministic step has a quantum subroutine with sublinear query complexity.

</details>


### [225] [Induced Coherence in Quantum and Classical Interferometry: Origin and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35523)
*Balakrishnan Viswanathan,Ravi Kamal Pandey,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Sibasish Ghosh,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子诱导相干干涉仪中验证了相干性、可预测性与纠缠之间的三重关系（triality relation），并指出经典构型退化为传统对偶性不等式；同时探讨了诱导相干干涉仪中相干性与纠缠的起源及调控手段，揭示路径不可分辨性对干涉与纠缠的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在满足Englert-Greenberger-Yasin不等式的杨氏双缝干涉中，波粒二象性已被证明服从一个涉及相干性、可预测性与纠缠的普适三重关系。本文旨在探究该三重关系是否在量子诱导相干干涉仪中依然成立，并进一步理解其中相干性与纠缠的起源及调控机制。

Method: 作者在量子诱导相干干涉仪框架下显式推导并演示三重关系的持续性，同时分析经典构型如何退化为已知的对偶性不等式。此外，通过考察路径不可分辨性（即源的固有不可分辨性）对干涉与纠缠的影响，研究相干性与纠缠的起源及其调控方式，并比较经典与量子版本诱导相干干涉仪中相干性的本质。

Result: 研究明确演示了三重关系在量子诱导相干干涉仪中依然持续成立，而经典构型则恢复为熟知的对偶性不等式；路径不可分辨性在影响干涉与纠缠方面表现出互补作用；并清晰揭示了经典与量子诱导相干干涉仪中相干性的不同本质。

Conclusion: 三重关系在诱导相干干涉仪中具有普适性，经典极限对应对偶性不等式；路径不可分辨性是同时调控干涉与纠缠的关键资源，相干性在经典与量子诱导相干干涉仪中本质不同。

Abstract: The wave-particle duality that manifests in the Young's double-slit interferometry satisfying the well known Englert-Greenberger-Yasin inequality has recently been shown to obey a universal triality relation involving coherence, predictability and rather surprisingly entanglement. In the present work, we explicitly demonstrate the persistence of the triality relation in the quantum induced coherence interferometer and the classical configuration recovers the well known duality inequality. Furthermore, we explore the origin of coherence and entanglement, in the induced coherence interferometer, and means to control them. We show that path indistinguishability (or intrinsic indistinguishability of the sources) in our setup plays a complementary role in influencing both interference and entanglement. We bring out manifestly the nature of coherence in both classical and quantum versions of induced coherence interferometers.

</details>


### [226] [Analysis of asymmetric errors in NISQ experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35535)
*Gil Kalai,Tomer Shoham,Carsten Voelkmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文延续对NISQ实验（尤其是Google 2019"量子优势"实验）的统计分析，在既有对称读出误差研究基础上，扩展分析非对称读出误差，并结合实验与模拟数据检验门误差效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作提出了分析对称与非对称读出误差的统计工具，并深入研究了对称情形；同时，Fefferman等（arXiv:2306.16659）指出振幅阻尼门误差会导致量子计算机采样中零的比例偏高。作者希望验证这一门误差效应是否在Google实验数据中真实存在。

Method: 采用统计工具与Fourier方法分析非对称读出误差；对Google实验数据在量子比特数12≤n≤53全范围内统计样本中0与1的比例；并使用IBM和Google的模拟器在n=12,14下进行数值模拟对照。

Result: 在Google实验数据中，0与1的比例仅反映所报告读出误差的非对称性，并未观察到Fefferman等所预测的门误差导致的零比例偏大效应；该结论覆盖整个12≤n≤53范围，并得到n=12,14模拟结果的支持。

Conclusion: 非对称读出误差足以解释Google实验采样中的0/1比例，振幅阻尼门误差效应在实验数据中不显著；需区分读出误差与门误差对采样统计的不同影响。

Abstract: This paper continues our analysis of NISQ experiments and especially the Google 2019 ``quantum supremacy" experiment. In arXiv:2008.05177 we proposed statistical tools to analyze symmetric and asymmetric readout errors, and the symmetric case was extensively developed and studied in arXiv:2404.00935, where we used Fourier methods combined with statistical tools.
  Here we extend and complement our earlier study and analyze asymmetric readout errors for data from experiments and simulations.
  Our study was further motivated by a paper by Fefferman et al. arXiv:2306.16659, who showed that the effect of amplitude damping gate errors is to have larger fractions of zeroes in the samples produced by the quantum computer. We show that this effect of gate errors is not observed in the Google experimental data, where, for the entire range of the number of qubits, $12 \le n \le 53$, the fraction of zeroes and ones reflects only the asymmetry of reported readout errors, and report on simulations with $n=12,14$ using IBM's and Google's simulators.

</details>


### [227] [Sublinear Copies Suffice for Fidelity Estimation with Pauli Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35547)
*Jayadev Acharya,Abhilash Dharmavarapu,Yuhan Liu,Nengkun Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种估计量子保真度（已知目标态与未知实验室制备态之间）的协议，达到精度ε所需的泡利基测量次数为次线性复杂度 o(d^{0.9908}/ε²)，优于常规线性量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子保真度是量子态验证与基准测试的核心指标，但直接估计通常需要对维数 d 呈线性甚至更高的测量次数，开销随系统维度增长过快。因此需要降低测量复杂度，实现更高效的保真度估计。

Method: 设计一个基于泡利基测量的估计协议：通过选取合适的泡利基进行测量，对已知目标态与未知实验制备态之间的量子保真度进行估计，并将所需测量次数压缩至次线性规模。

Result: 在精度 ε 要求下，协议所需的泡利基测量次数为 o(d^{0.9908}/ε²)，即相对维度 d 呈次线性，突破了朴素估计的线性测量开销。

Conclusion: 该协议以次线性的泡利基测量复杂度实现了对量子保真度的高精度估计，为高效量子态验证与基准测试提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We present a protocol that estimates the quantum fidelity, up to precision $\varepsilon$, between a known target state and unknown lab-prepared state with sublinear, $o(d^{0.9908}/\varepsilon^2)$, number of Pauli basis measurements.

</details>


### [228] [Quantum Query Complexity Beyond the Worst Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35580)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Yanlin Chen,Amin Shiraz Gilani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开创性地系统研究平滑分析（smoothed analysis）的量子对应物，即"平滑量子查询复杂度"。主要成果包括：构造了一个全函数，其平滑量子查询复杂度比经典查询复杂度指数级小；给出了对称布尔函数的平滑随机与量子查询复杂度的近似紧致刻画，统一了最坏情形（Beals 等）与平均情形（Ambainis 和 de Wolf）的结果；并对模式匹配、编辑距离等字符串问题在不同参数区间给出多项式到超多项式的量子加速。核心技术是近紧致的量子算法，用于 ε-近似计算两条非重复字符串间的碰撞数，改进了 Le Gall 和 Ng 的结果。整体表明平滑分析能揭示比最坏情形更大的量子加速，为在更现实输入上实现量子优势开辟了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑分析在经典算法中是解释算法实际性能（超出最坏情形）的核心框架，能说明算法为何在实践中表现良好。然而，量子计算领域缺乏对平滑分析的对应研究。本文旨在弥补这一空白，系统研究平滑分析的量子对应物，探究在更现实的输入扰动下，量子算法能否展现出比最坏情形分析所揭示的更大的加速，从而为量子优势提供更贴近实际的路径。

Method: 作者定义了"平滑量子查询复杂度"作为平滑分析的量子对应物，围绕查询复杂度模型展开研究：（1）构造一个全函数，通过平滑分布下的查询复杂度对比展示量子与经典复杂度的指数级分离；（2）针对对称布尔函数，推导平滑随机与平滑量子查询复杂度的近似紧致刻画，将最坏情形与平均情形结果统一起来；（3）研究字符串问题，包括模式匹配与编辑距离，在不同参数区间分析量子加速。核心技术手段是设计一个近紧致的量子算法，用于 ε-近似计算两条非重复字符串之间的碰撞数，此算法改进了 Le Gall 和 Ng（QIC'22）的结果，并作为上述多个结果的关键技术支撑。

Result: （1）存在一个全函数，其平滑量子查询复杂度比经典查询复杂度指数级更小。（2）给出了对称布尔函数平滑随机与量子查询复杂度的近似紧致刻画，统一并推广了 Beals 等（FOCS'98）的最坏情形复杂度和 Ambainis 与 de Wolf（STACS'00）的平均情形复杂度结果。（3）对模式匹配、编辑距离等字符串问题，在多种参数区间获得了从多项式到超多项式的量子加速。（4）技术层面，给出了 ε-近似非重复字符串间碰撞数的近紧致量子算法，改进了 Le Gall 和 Ng（QIC'22）的结果。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了平滑分析的量子理论框架。结果表明，平滑分析能够揭示比最坏情形分析所暗示的更大的量子加速，说明在更现实的输入分布下量子优势可能更易实现，为量子算法在现实场景中的应用开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Smoothed analysis is a central framework in classical algorithms for explaining the performance of algorithms beyond the worst case, often explaining why algorithms perform well in practice. We initiate a systematic study of its quantum counterpart and show the following results. $(1)$ We show that there is a total function whose smoothed quantum query complexity is exponentially smaller than its classical query complexity. $(2)$ We give near-tight characterizations of smoothed randomized and quantum query complexities for symmetric Boolean functions, unifying the worst-case complexity results of [Beals et al, FOCS'98] and average-case complexity results of [Ambainis and de Wolf, STACS'00]. $(3)$ We study string problems such as pattern matching and edit distance and, in various regimes, give polynomial to superpolynomial quantum speedups. Our main technical ingredients include a near-tight quantum algorithm for $\varepsilon$-approximating the number of collisions between two non-repetitive strings, improving the result of Le Gall and Ng [QIC'22]. Together, our results show that smoothing can reveal larger quantum speedups than worst-case analysis suggests, opening a path towards quantum advantage on more realistic inputs.

</details>


### [229] [QC-Stark: A Multi-Task Benchmark Revealing Capability Dissociations in LLMs Evaluated on Quantum Computing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35581)
*Pranav Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QC-Stark基准，覆盖11项量子计算任务，通过2750次评估发现总体排名会掩盖任务间差异，4/11任务上总体与单任务排名相关性不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测多依赖总体排名，可能掩盖模型在不同量子计算子任务上的能力差异，缺乏细粒度、可自动验证的QC领域基准。

Method: 构建QC-Stark基准，覆盖电路构建、调试、编译、纠错、模拟等11项任务；进行2750次评估（10模型×11任务×5难度×5随机种子）；采用2参数IRT模型验证测量质量，并做提示敏感性分析以检验排名稳健性；全部任务通过执行自动验证。

Result: 总体排名与单任务排名在11项任务中有4项Spearman相关不显著，说明总体排名掩盖了显著的任务间差异；IRT模型验证了测量质量，提示敏感性分析显示排名在不同提示条件下稳健。

Conclusion: QC-Stark提供了无需人工评估、可自动验证的量子计算LLM评测框架，揭示总体排名不足，强调应关注任务级表现；代码与数据已公开于Huggingface。

Abstract: We introduce QC-Stark, a benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on 11 quantum computing (QC) tasks, spanning circuit construction, debugging, compilation, error correction, and simulation. Across 2,750 evaluations (10 models $\times$ 11 tasks x 5 difficulty levels x 5 seeds), we find that overall rankings mask substantial per-task variation. The Spearman correlation between overall and per-task rankings is statistically insignificant for 4 out of the 11 tasks included in this benchmark. A 2-parameter Item Response Theory (IRT) model validates measurement quality, and prompt sensitivity analysis confirms ranking robustness across prompt conditions. All tasks are auto-verifiable via execution, thus not requiring any manual evaluation. We make the code and data publicly available on Huggingface.

</details>


### [230] [Exponential lower bounds for low-degree strategies in position-based quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35592)
*Isabel M. Moreno-Cuadrado,David Perez-Garcia,Carlos Palazuelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非局域量子计算（NLQC）实现对角±1幺正族的资源下界，在首轮策略对ε依赖度为低阶多项式的假设下，证明至少存在一个U_ε的实现需要exp(Ω(n^{2δ}/(log n)^2))量级的额外量子系统维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC是否存在需要指数级额外量子系统维度的幺正实现是一个核心开放问题，其答案关系到基于位置的量子密码安全性、通用量子模拟器最优性以及全息量子引力的预期性质。当前已知下界仅为n的亚线性量级，亟需更强（指数级）下界。

Method: 考虑由布尔超立方体ε∈{±1}^{n^2}索引的对角±1幺正族U_ε，满足U_ε|i⟩_A|j⟩_B=ε_{ij}|i⟩_A|j⟩_B。假设U_ε能以常数精度在NLQC中实现，且相应策略首轮对ε的依赖是次数为O(n^{1/4-δ})的多项式，则结合特定Banach空间的几何性质（type常数）及取值于该空间的布尔函数随机估计进行证明。

Result: 在该多项式依赖假设下，证明至少存在一个U_ε的NLQC实现所需资源按exp(Ω(n^{2δ}/(log n)^2))规模增长，即给出了指数级下界（在假设条件下）。

Conclusion: 在首轮策略对ε依赖为低阶多项式的条件下，对角±1幺正族中存在需要指数级额外量子系统维度的实例，从而朝解决NLQC指数下界这一开放问题迈出一步，并为位置基量子密码等相关应用提供了新的下界工具。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) consists on the implementation of a bipartite unitary $U_{AB}$ by two cooperating distant players by means of a two-round protocol with a single intermediate round of simultaneous communication. It is a major open question to know whether there exists a unitary $U_{AB}$ whose implementation in NLQC requires extra quantum systems with dimensions ${\rm exp}(n^{Ω(1)})$, where $n$ is the dimension of systems $A$ and $B$. This type of exponential lower bound is essential for security of position-based quantum cryptography, since the capabilities of the adversaries are precisely described by NLQC. It has also been connected to many other problems, such as the optimality of universal quantum simulators, or the expected properties of holographic quantum gravity. Currently the best lower bounds are only sublinear in $n$.
  In this paper we consider the family of diagonal ${\pm 1}$ valued unitaries $U_{\varepsilon}$, indexed by an element of the Boolean hypercube $\varepsilon \in \{\pm 1\}^{n^2}$, i.e. $U_{\varepsilon}|i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B =\varepsilon_{ij} |i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B$. We show that if $U_{\varepsilon}$ can be implemented in NLQC with constant accuracy for all $\varepsilon$, and the dependency on $\varepsilon$ in the first round of the associated strategy is a polynomial of degree $O(n^{\frac{1}{4}-δ})$, then the implementation of at least one $U_{\varepsilon}$ in NLQC requires resources scaling as $\exp\left(Ω\left(\frac{n^{2δ}}{(\log n)^2}\right)\right)$. The proof is based on geometric properties of particular Banach spaces, namely their type constants, together with random estimates of Boolean functions valued on them.

</details>


### [231] [Low-Weight Canonical Logical Bases from Pair-Partition Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35601)
*Koki Okada,Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为量子比特CSS码构造了完整的规范逻辑基：通过给二元循环置换矩阵配对划分(CPM-PP)校验赋予四元系数、构造并归一化逻辑代表元,再展开为二元矩阵。在特定可逆子矩阵条件下,余因子代表元由配对多项式的逆规范配对,张成整个逻辑空间;当配对多项式为系数为1的循环移位单项式时,归一化保持二元权重。文中给出一般构造、示例,并报告了7个二元码的参数、校验秩与基权重,代表例如[[320,80,14]]、[[448,112,18]]、[[2048,512,24]]。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子比特CSS码建立完整且规范的逻辑基(通常的逻辑算符基不完备或非规范),使逻辑空间有可系统构造、可比对的代表元,并便于计算权重等码参数。

Method: 对二元循环置换矩阵配对划分(CPM-PP)校验赋予四元系数;构造并归一化逻辑代表元,再展开为二元矩阵。当两个校验系统在不相交列集上具有可逆子矩阵时,用单一配对多项式的逆对余因子代表元作规范配对;当配对多项式为系数为1的循环移位单项式时,归一化保持二元权重。给出一般块维与CPM尺寸下的构造并演示示例。

Result: 所得代表元对张成整个逻辑空间。报告了7个二元码的参数、校验秩与基权重。代表例参数为[[320,80,14]]、[[448,112,18]]、[[2048,512,24]],三者X侧与Z侧最大校验权重均为10;其规范逻辑代表元的二元权重在X、Z两侧分别为25、27、27。

Conclusion: 该文给出一种系统化构造量子比特CSS码完整规范逻辑基的方法,在满足可逆子矩阵条件时配对多项式可规范确定代表元,并在特定多项式下保持权重,所得构造适用于一般尺寸并给出多个高性能二元码实例。

Abstract: We construct complete canonical logical bases for qubit CSS codes by assigning quaternary coefficients to binary circulant permutation matrix pair-partition (CPM-PP) checks, constructing and normalizing logical representatives, and expanding the result into binary matrices. When the two check systems have invertible submatrices on disjoint column sets, cofactor representatives are canonically paired by the inverse of a single pairing polynomial. The resulting pairs span the entire logical space. When the pairing polynomial is a cyclic-shift monomial with coefficient one, normalization preserves the binary weights of the representatives. We state the construction for general block dimensions and CPM size, work through a corresponding example, and report the parameters, check ranks, and basis weights of seven binary codes. Representative examples have parameters $[[320,80,14]]$, $[[448,112,18]]$, and $[[2048,512,24]]$. All three have maximum check weight 10 on both the X and Z sides. Their canonical logical representatives have binary weights 25, 27, and 27, respectively, on both the X and Z sides.

</details>


### [232] [Succinct Arguments for QMA from Collapsing Hash Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35633)
*James Bartusek,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在仅假设存在塌缩哈希函数的前提下，构造了QMA的简洁论证（succinct arguments），首次将安全性建立在非结构化"Minicrypt"假设之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前QMA的简洁论证方案需要依赖更强的、可推出公钥加密的假设。本文旨在将其弱化到不蕴含公钥加密的"Minicrypt"世界，探索量子密码学中最小假设下的可行性。

Method: 核心技术是"爪态生成协议"（claw-state generation）：仅用经典通信，将少量量子关联自举为任意大量的爪态关联。相比Zhang (STOC 2021)，该协议轮复杂度更优、可在标准模型下证明、且更简洁。由此得到基于单向函数的量子计算盲委托协议，再接入Bartusek-Liu-Malavolta (EUROCRYPT 2026)的通信压缩编译器，最终得到QMA的简洁论证。

Result: 证明了仅假设塌缩哈希函数存在时，QMA存在简洁论证；给出了仅依赖单向函数的量子简洁盲委托协议；改进了已有的爪态生成工作（轮数、标准模型、简洁性）。

Conclusion: 将QMA简洁论证的假设基础降至非结构化Minicrypt假设，表明量子关联自举是连接弱假设与强量子证明系统的关键工具。

Abstract: We prove the existence of succinct arguments for QMA, assuming only the existence of collapsing hash functions. This is the first scheme that relies only on unstructured ``Minicrypt'' assumptions, which are not known to imply public-key encryption.
  Our main technical contribution is a quantum-succinct \emph{claw-state generation} protocol that allows us to bootstrap a small number of quantum correlations into an arbitrarily large number of claw-state correlations, using classical communication only. This improves upon the work of [Zhang, STOC 2021], having better round complexity, a proof in the standard model, and being overall much simpler. This yields a quantum-succinct blind delegation of quantum computation protocol from one-way functions, which we plug into the communication-compression compiler of [Bartusek, Liu, and Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026] to obtain succinct arguments for QMA.

</details>


### [233] [Learning sparse quantum states from single-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35648)
*Su-un Lee,Liang Jiang,Kunal Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏量子态的学习问题，证明仅需单量子比特测量即可高效学习具有长程纠缠的稀疏量子态，无需纠缠门，且与现有量子设备兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏量子态的密度矩阵在未知积基下仅有s个非零元素，虽具有紧凑经典描述，但可携带长程纠缠，使得仅凭局部约化密度矩阵无法重建。以往方法需大量纠缠门来提取信息，代价高且与当前设备不兼容。因此作者希望探索能否仅用单量子比特测量来学习此类态。

Method: 作者提出基于单量子比特测量的学习算法，针对不同稀疏度情形进行分析：当s为常数时，给出多项式样本复杂度与多项式经典计算复杂度的算法；当s随n多项式增长时，给出多项式样本复杂度但经典计算效率一般不保证；进一步地，当未知基为已知固定有限个单量子比特基（如泡利算符本征基）的积时，经典计算复杂度为准多项式。

Result: 当s为常数时，算法能以多项式样本与多项式经典计算复杂度从单量子比特测量中学习稀疏态；当s随n多项式增长时，样本复杂度仍为多项式，但经典计算效率一般不保证，而在未知基为已知固定有限单量子比特基之积的特殊情形下计算复杂度为准多项式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，具有长程纠缠的稀疏态可仅通过单量子比特测量高效学习，无需纠缠门，且测量要求与当前量子设备兼容，为稀疏量子态学习提供了实用新途径。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a sparse quantum state, an $n$-qubit quantum state whose density matrix has at most $s$ nonzero matrix entries in an unknown product basis. While such states admit compact classical descriptions, they can carry long-range entanglement that prevents reconstruction from local reduced density matrices alone. Therefore, previous learning approaches addressed such long-range-entangled states using many entangling gates to extract the necessary information. In this work, we show that sparse states can nevertheless be efficiently learned using only single-qubit measurements. Specifically, when the sparsity $s$ is constant, our algorithm can learn sparse states from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity and classical computational complexity. When $s$ grows polynomially with $n$, sparse states can still be learned from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity, although efficient classical computation is not guaranteed in general. In this regime, however, the classical computational complexity becomes quasipolynomial when the state is sparse in an unknown basis that is a product of a known fixed finite set of single-qubit bases (e.g., eigenbases of Pauli operators). These results establish efficient learning of sparse states with long-range entanglement without entangling gates, and the single-qubit measurement requirements make our algorithms compatible with current quantum devices.

</details>


### [234] [The power of oracle access: Optimal sample and query complexity of the abelian state hidden subgroup problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35656)
*Yuhan Liu,Jose Carrasco,Jens Eisert,Armando Bellante*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在样本模型与新提出的查询模型下彻底解决了有限阿贝尔群上StateHSP的复杂度：查询模型需Θ(log(|G/H|)/√ε)次前向/逆查询，样本模型需Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε)个副本，二者误差依赖的二次差异源于相干访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有超多项式加速的量子算法时，问题复杂度很大程度取决于输入访问模型。作者针对StateHSP（隐藏子群问题的量子推广，目标是识别未知量子态的对称性）研究这一现象，并解决有限阿贝尔群情形下已有Fourier采样算法使用O(log(|G|)/ε)副本是否最优的公开问题。

Method: 在已有的样本模型和新的查询模型（更强、操作上更自然，可访问态制备酉算符及其逆）两个框架下分析阿贝尔StateHSP：(1) 查询模型下设计时间高效的量子算法，使用O(log(|G/H|)/√ε)次正向与逆向查询，并证明匹配的Ω(log(|G/H|)/√ε)下界，该下界在更强的共轭查询与受控查询设置下依然成立；(2) 样本模型下证明Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε)个副本既充分又在信息论上必要，即使允许任意集体测量。

Result: 查询模型达到Θ(log(|G/H|)/√ε)的查询复杂度（算法上界与下界匹配，且下界在共轭查询、受控查询等更强设置下仍成立）；样本模型的复杂度为Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε)，任意集体测量也无法突破。由此证明ε上的二次改进真正来源于对态制备电路的相干访问。作为应用，作者得到学习稳定子群、定位非纠缠性以及识别隐藏平移对称性的更快算法。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔StateHSP的复杂度被完全确定，且在查询模型与样本模型间存在ε依赖的二次分离。该分离本质地源自相干访问态制备电路的能力，并可直接加速稳定子群学习、非纠缠性定位与隐藏平移对称性识别等任务。

Abstract: In the quest to identify further quantum algorithms exhibiting superpolynomial speed-ups, a recurring theme is that the complexity of a problem is largely shaped by the input access model. Here, we study this phenomenon for the state hidden subgroup problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalization of the hidden subgroup problem in which the goal is to identify the symmetries of an unknown quantum state. For finite abelian groups, existing Fourier-sampling algorithms use $O(\log(|G|)/ε)$ copies of the state, but whether this scaling is optimal has remained open. We settle the complexity of the abelian StateHSP in both the previously studied sample model and a new query model, which is a stronger and operationally natural generalization that provides access to the state-preparation unitary and its inverse. In the query model, we give a time-efficient quantum algorithm using $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ forward and inverse queries, and prove a matching $Ω(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ lower bound which holds even in the stronger conjugate-query and controlled-query settings. By contrast, we show that in the sample model, $Θ(\log(|G/H|)/ε)$ copies are both sufficient and information-theoretically necessary, even if one allows for arbitrary collective measurements. Thus, the quadratic improvement in $ε$ genuinely arises from coherent access to the preparation circuit. As applications, we obtain faster algorithms for learning stabilizer groups, locating unentanglement, and identifying hidden translation symmetries.

</details>


### [235] [An Optimal Quantum Linear Systems Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35660)
*Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Aram W. Harrow,Robin Kothari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了量子线性系统问题(QLSP)的精确复杂度为Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))，并解决了Berry-Childs关于N×N酉矩阵实现的公开问题，证明可用O(√N)次矩阵元查询以有界误差实现任意N×N酉矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在QLSP中，此前最优量子算法的查询复杂度为O(κd log(1/ε))与κ√d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}，而已知下界仅为Ω(κ log(1/ε))与Ω(κ√d)，算法与下界之间存在显著差距。作者旨在收紧该差距、给出紧确复杂度，并顺带解决酉矩阵实现中的公开问题。

Method: 通过改进量子算法并构造更强的下界，将QLSP查询复杂度的上界与下界都推进到Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))；同时利用矩阵元查询模型给出任意N×N酉矩阵的有界误差实现算法。

Result: 证明QLSP的复杂度为Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))，即上界和下界均已匹配；并证明任意N×N酉矩阵可在有界误差下用O(√N)次矩阵元查询实现。

Conclusion: 本文给出了量子线性系统问题的紧确查询复杂度，并解决了Berry与Childs提出的酉矩阵实现公开问题。

Abstract: In the quantum linear systems problem (QLSP), we are given query access to a $d$-sparse $N\times N$ matrix $A$ with condition number $κ$, and the ability to prepare a quantum state proportional to a vector $\vec b$. The goal is to output an $ε$-approximation to the quantum state proportional to the solution $\vec x$ of $A\vec{x}=\vec{b}$. Following a long line of work, the best previously known quantum algorithms for the QLSP had query complexities $O(κd\log(1/ε))$ and $κ\sqrt d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}$, while the best known lower bounds were $Ω(κ\log(1/ε))$ and $Ω(κ\sqrt d)$. We improve these bounds and show that the complexity of the QLSP is $Θ(κ\sqrt d\log(1/ε))$. We also resolve an open problem of Berry and Childs by showing that any $N\times N$ unitary can be implemented with bounded error using $O(\sqrt N)$ queries to its matrix entries.

</details>


### [236] [Optimal Query Complexity for Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35668)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在已知能谱间隙内能量阈值时，基态制备达到迹距离误差 ε 的最优查询复杂度，并给出了期望与最坏情况下的上下界匹配算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态制备是量子计算的核心任务之一，此前缺乏在已知能量阈值（位于谱间隙内）情形下的最优查询复杂度刻画，本文旨在确定该设定下的紧致查询复杂度界。

Method: 基于 α-block-encoding U_H 与态制备 oracle U_I，利用常数精度谱滤波器构造 purifier，并在振幅放大过程中顺序组合；期望算法重复"制备+高精度谱滤波"直至成功，最坏情况算法使用精度递增的滤波器并限制总查询数。

Result: 期望查询数 O((α/Δ)(γ⁻¹+log(1/ε)))，最坏情况 O((α/(γΔ))log(1/ε))；U_I 调用分别为 O(1/γ) 与 O(γ⁻¹log(1/ε))；期望查询下界与算法匹配，最坏情况下界由 Somma 和 de Wolf [SdW26] 给出；在 (N+1) 维系统上当 U_H 调用为 o((α/Δ)√N) 时 U_I 界亦最优。

Conclusion: 在已知谱间隙内能量阈值的前提下，基态制备的期望与最坏情况查询复杂度均达到最优，算法构造清晰且下界匹配。

Abstract: We determine the optimal query complexity of ground-state preparation to trace-distance error $\varepsilon$ when an energy threshold in the spectral gap is known. Let $U_H$ be an $α$-block-encoding of a Hamiltonian with unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$, and suppose $|\langleψ_0|U_I|0\rangle|\geγ$ for a state-preparation oracle $U_I$. The threshold lies at least $Δ/2$ above the ground-state energy and at least $Δ/2$ below every excited-state energy. We give two algorithms that prepare a state within trace distance $\varepsilon$ of the ground state. One uses $O((α/Δ)(γ^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ calls to $U_H$ in expectation; the other uses $O((α/(γΔ))\log(1/\varepsilon))$ calls to $U_H$ in the worst case. We prove a lower bound matching the expected query count; the corresponding worst-case lower bound follows from Somma and de Wolf [SdW26]. The respective bounds on calls to $U_I$ are $O(1/γ)$ in expectation and $O(γ^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ in the worst case. On $(N+1)$-dimensional systems, these $U_I$ bounds are also optimal when the expected or worst-case count of $U_H$ calls, respectively, is $o((α/Δ)\sqrt N)$. Both algorithms use a constant-accuracy spectral filter to construct a purifier, which we then sequentially compose during amplitude amplification to prepare a state with constant overlap with the ground state. The expected-query algorithm repeats the preparation followed by one high-accuracy spectral filter until success. The worst-case algorithm uses filters of increasing accuracy and limits the total number of queries.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Gorenstein cotorsion pairs induced by balanced pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31729)
*Konstantinos Goflis*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在局部可表现阿贝尔范畴中的可容纳平衡对上，研究由双边复形循环构成的类$G(\mathcal{X})$，证明其在特定条件下构成相对遗传余挠对，并给出函子完备性判据及Dedekind整环上的显式刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对同调代数中，平衡对诱导的精确结构上的Gorenstein对象与余挠对理论有待系统刻画；本文旨在建立一般框架下相对Gorenstein对象的余挠对性质，并给出函子完备性与具体环上的计算。

Method: 设$(\mathcal{X},\mathcal{Y})$为局部可表现阿贝尔范畴$\mathcal{A}$中的可容纳平衡对，$\mathcal{E}$为诱导的精确结构。定义$G(\mathcal{X})$为$\mathcal{X}$中两项均左、右$\mathcal{X}$-零调的双边复形的循环类。在$\mathcal{X}$对直和项与有限直和封闭、且对充分大正则基数$\kappa$为$\kappa$-Kaplansky的条件下，结合相对纯性（由有限表现模决定）的函子完备性判据进行论证。

Result: 证明$(G(\mathcal{X}),G(\mathcal{X})^{\perp*})$是关于$\mathcal{E}$的遗传余挠对；给出相对纯性函子完备性的两个判据，并证明相对周期模生成函子完备的遗传余挠对；对Dedekind整环上的一族精确结构，计算了相对Gorenstein对象、其右正交类及显式逼近。

Conclusion: 在一般局部可表现阿贝尔范畴与平衡对框架下，相对Gorenstein对象类与余挠对理论得以统一处理，且函子完备性与具体环上的逼近计算均获得完整刻画，推广并统一了经典Gorenstein同调结果。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{X},\mathcal{Y})$ be an admissible balanced pair in a locally presentable abelian category $\mathcal{A}$, and let $\mathcal{E}$ be its induced exact structure. We study the class $G(\mathcal{X})$ of cycles of two-sided complexes with terms in $\mathcal{X}$ that are both right and left $\mathcal{X}$-acyclic. If $\mathcal{X}$ is closed under direct summands and finite direct sums and is $κ$-Kaplansky for every sufficiently large regular cardinal $κ$, we prove that $(G(\mathcal{X}),G(\mathcal{X})^{\perp *})$ is a hereditary cotorsion pair relative to $\mathcal E$. For relative purity determined by finitely presented modules, we give two criteria for functorial completeness and show that relative periodic modules generate a functorially complete hereditary cotorsion pair. We also compute the relative Gorenstein objects, their right orthogonal, and explicit approximations for a family of exact structures over Dedekind domains.

</details>


### [238] [Torsion proliferation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31781)
*Joseph Baine,Daniel Tubbenhauer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究有限旗簇上Schubert簇支撑层的通用行为：随代数群秩增长，几乎所有相交上同调层的 stalks/costalks 含 p-扭，且几乎所有不可分解 parity 层非 perverse，并对若干偏旗簇族给出显式渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Schubert簇上层的通用（渐近）行为，特别是 p-扭现象与 parity 层是否 perverse，此前缺乏随秩增长的定量刻画。

Method: 在有限旗簇中考察Schubert簇上支撑的层，固定素数 p，令代数群秩趋于无穷，通过渐近分析研究相交上同调层的 stalks/costalks 的 p-扭以及不可分解 parity 层的性质；对特定偏旗簇族进行显式渐近计算。

Result: 对任意固定素数 p，当代数群秩增大时，几乎每个相交上同调层的 stalks 或 costalks 都出现 p-扭；几乎每个不可分解 parity 层都不是 perverse；并对若干偏旗簇族确定了显式渐近。

Conclusion: 在有限旗簇的渐近框架下，p-扭与 parity 层非 perverse 是普遍现象，表明随秩增长层论行为具有强通用性。

Abstract: This paper considers the generic behaviour of sheaves supported on Schubert varieties in finite flag varieties. Our main results are that, for any fixed prime $p$, as the rank of the associated algebraic group grows, almost every intersection cohomology sheaf admits $p$-torsion in its stalks or costalks, and almost every indecomposable parity sheaf is not perverse. For certain families of partial flag varieties we also determine explicit asymptotics for this growth.

</details>


### [239] [Classification of \(U(\mathfrak h)\)-Free Modules over the Heisenberg-Virasoro type Lie superalgebra via Polynomial Shift Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32105)
*Yan Kong,Haibo Chen,Yucai Su*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文分类了偶数部分为扭曲 Heisenberg-Virasoro 代数、奇数部分为交换理想的 Heisenberg-Virasoro 型 Lie 超代数 $\mathcal L$ 的 $\mathbb Z_2$-分次模，这些模在 $U(\mathfrak h)=\mathbb C[L_0,F_0]$ 上自由且超秩为 $1|1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Heisenberg-Virasoro 型 Lie 超代数上自由超秩 $1|1$ 的模结构，推广偶数子代数（扭曲 Heisenberg-Virasoro 代数）的秩一分类，刻画奇部作用非平凡与平凡两类模并给出新的矩阵实现。

Method: 利用多项式移位算子将定义关系转化为生成元矩阵元满足的函数方程；在假设偶数子代数的秩一分类成立的前提下，对自由超秩 $1|1$ 的 $\mathbb Z_2$-分次模进行分类，并分析分次同构类、子模结构与可约性。

Result: 在奇部作用非平凡时，模在奇偶反转意义下属于三角族 $\Omega^+(\lambda,\beta,d)$；奇部作用平凡时模为偶数子代数两个秩一模的直和。分次同构类中，三角族内的耦合多项式 $d$ 在同构下仅相差非零标量倍数；并刻画了标准真子模，证明分类中的所有模均可约。

Conclusion: 给出了 Heisenberg-Virasoro 型 Lie 超代数自由超秩 $1|1$ 模的完整分类与同构刻画，并利用含多项式移位-微分算子元素的 $2\times2$ 矩阵给出了该 Lie 超代数的新实现。

Abstract: Let \(\mathcal L\) be the Heisenberg-Virasoro type Lie superalgebra whose even part is the twisted Heisenberg-Virasoro algebra and whose odd part is an abelian ideal. We classify \(\mathbb Z_2\)-graded \(\mathcal L\)-modules that are free of super-rank \(1|1\) over \(U(\mathfrak h)=\mathbb C[L_0,F_0]\). Using polynomial shift operators, we express the defining relations as functional equations for the matrix entries of the generators. Assuming the rank one classification for the even subalgebra, we show that, up to parity reversal, every module with nontrivial odd action belongs to a triangular family \(Ω^+(λ,β,d)\), whereas every module with trivial odd action is a direct sum of two rank-one modules for the even subalgebra. We determine the graded isomorphism classes: within the triangular family, the coupling polynomial \(d\) is invariant up to multiplication by a non-zero scalar. We also describe canonical proper submodules and show that every module in the classification is reducible. In particular, we provide a new realization of the Heisenberg-Virasoro type Lie superalgebra using $2\times2$ matrices with entries associated with polynomial shift-differential operators.

</details>


### [240] [Rootwise estimates for Weyl alternants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32214)
*Xiaocheng Li,Yunfeng Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对约化晶体根系与Weyl群W的Weyl交错和建立逐根估计，量化其振荡求和中的抵消，并将结果推广到混合方向导数、球函数与紧连通半单群的不可约特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weyl交错和出现在Weyl特征标公式的分子及复半单群球函数的显式公式中，其振荡求和的抵消程度缺乏精细的逐根定量刻画，且已有估计（如Cowling–Nevo的导数界）未能统一控制任意混合径向导数在空间与谱根超平面上的行为。

Method: 将第二作者对SU(3)点态特征标界所用的下降策略推广到任意秩；关键工具包括Weyl群关于抛物双陪集的分解与相对BGG–Demazure恒等式；并通过相对于仿射Weylarrangement的局部化论证处理正则整权。

Result: 建立了Weyl交错和的逐根界，每个正根贡献一个同时依赖空间变量与谱参数的因子；进一步对所有混合方向导数给出逐根界；对复半单群球函数恢复了Brumley–Marshall–Matz–Peterson的近期点态界，并细化了Cowling–Nevo的导数界；对正则整权得到周期交错和估计及紧连通半单群不可约特征标所有混合径向导数的逐根界。

Conclusion: 该工作以统一的逐根框架定量刻画了Weyl交错和及其混合导数的抵消现象，统一并改进了球函数与特征标的若干已知估计。

Abstract: For a reduced crystallographic root system with Weyl group $W$, Weyl alternants are signed sums of exponentials indexed by $W$. They occur in the numerators of the Weyl character formula and the explicit formula for spherical functions on complex semisimple groups. We establish rootwise bounds that quantify cancellation in these oscillatory sums, with each positive root contributing a factor depending on both the spatial variable and the spectral parameter. More generally, we establish rootwise bounds for all mixed directional derivatives of normalized Weyl alternants. Our proof extends to arbitrary rank the descent strategy used by the second author for pointwise character bounds on $\mathrm{SU}(3)$. The key ingredients include the decomposition of the Weyl group into parabolic double cosets and the relative BGG--Demazure identity. For spherical functions on complex semisimple groups, these estimates recover the recent pointwise bound of Brumley, Marshall, Matz, and Peterson, and they refine the derivative bounds of Cowling and Nevo by controlling arbitrary mixed radial derivatives uniformly across spatial and spectral root hyperplanes. Furthermore, by a standard localization argument relative to the affine Weyl arrangement, we establish periodic alternant estimates for regular integral weights and corresponding rootwise bounds for all mixed radial derivatives of irreducible characters of compact connected semisimple groups.

</details>


### [241] [Symmetric Branching via Laurent Phenomenon Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32425)
*Jiarui Fei*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在约化分支代数上构造Laurent现象结构，将若干典型分支代数等同于上Laurent现象代数，并通过theta基计算分支重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究约化分支代数（如A_{2n-1}↓C_n及例外包含D_4↓G_2、E_6↓F_4、F_4↓B_4）的分支重数计算问题，试图用Laurent现象代数与簇代数/镜像对称的工具给出统一处理。

Method: 利用投射表示的度纤维化范畴给出统一构造；将分支代数与上Laurent现象代数等同并保持冻结系数为多项式；给出充分条件把特化的Keel–Yu镜像代数等同于具有合适二项式种子的上Laurent现象代数；并证明UFD上LP模式有限上界的变异不变性。

Result: 识别出A_{2n-1}↓C_n（n≥2）及D_4↓G_2、E_6↓F_4、F_4↓B_4的分支代数为上Laurent现象代数，并得到B_3↓G_2与G_2↓A_2的A_1型簇种子；所得theta基同时适配冻结边界赋值；对A_{2n-1}↓C_n（2≤n≤5）及其余例外包含得到由显式有理多面体锥格点参数化的齐次theta基，其权重纤维可计算所有分支重数。

Conclusion: 分支代数可纳入上Laurent现象代数的统一框架，theta基为分支重数提供显式多面体参数化计算，且相关LP模式有限上界具变异不变性。

Abstract: We construct Laurent phenomenon structures on reductive branching algebras. For $A_{2n-1}\downarrow C_{n}$, $n\ge 2$, and the exceptional inclusions $D_4\downarrow G_2$, $E_6\downarrow F_4$, and $F_4\downarrow B_4$, we identify the branching algebras with upper Laurent phenomenon algebras, keeping the frozen coefficients polynomial. Degree-fibred categories of projective presentations give a common construction. It also gives cluster seeds of type $A_1$ for $B_3 \downarrow G_2$ and $G_2\downarrow A_2$. We give uniform sufficient conditions identifying a specialized Keel--Yu mirror algebra with an upper Laurent phenomenon algebra admitting a suitable binomial seed. The resulting theta basis is simultaneously adapted to the frozen boundary valuations. For $A_{2n-1}\downarrow C_{n}$ with $2\le n\le5$ and for the other exceptional inclusions, we obtain homogeneous theta bases parametrized by lattice points of explicit rational polyhedral cones, whose weight fibres compute every branching multiplicity. We also prove mutation invariance of finite upper bounds for LP patterns over a UFD.

</details>


### [242] [On the number of $\ell$-regular conjugacy classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32438)
*Zhicheng Feng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明：当 ℓ>5 时，有限群的 ℓ-正则共轭类数目不少于其 Sylow ℓ-子群正规化子的 ℓ-正则共轭类数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在与有限群共轭类计数相关的若干猜想（如 McKay 猜想及其推广）背景下，人们希望用局部子群（Sylow 子群的正规化子）的共轭类信息来约束整群的共轭类数目。此前结论多对素数 ℓ 有较小或较大限制，需要把 ℓ 的下界推进到更小的范围。

Method: 利用 ℓ-正则共轭类（即元素阶与 ℓ 互素的共轭类）的计数性质，通过有限群的结构分析与局部-整体比较，考察 Sylow ℓ-子群正规化子 N_G(P) 的 ℓ-正则共轭类数目，并借助对 ℓ>5 情形下的分类/归纳论证给出不等式。

Result: 对任意有限群 G 及素数 ℓ>5，成立 |{G 的 ℓ-正则共轭类}| ≥ |{N_G(P) 的 ℓ-正则共轭类}|，其中 P 为 G 的 Sylow ℓ-子群。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了 ℓ>5 时 ℓ-正则共轭类数目与 Sylow ℓ-子群正规化子之间的普适下界关系，推广并强化了相关共轭类计数不等式，为研究 McKay 型猜想提供了新的局部-整体工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that for $\ell>5$, the number of $\ell$-regular conjugacy classes of a finite group is at least that of the normalizer of a Sylow $\ell$-subgroup.

</details>


### [243] [Finiteness properties of profinite blocks and blocks with defect group $\mathbb Z_p^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32587)
*Florian Eisele,John W. MacQuarrie*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明：亏群为拓扑有限生成（甚至有限表现）的有限生成亏群时，profinite 群的块可化为虚拟 pro-p 群的块，并完全分类了亏群为 Z_p^n 的块，证明其对 Donovan 猜想成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 profinite 群（而非有限群）的块表示理论，期望将有限群模表示中的亏群理论、Morita 等价与 Donovan 猜想推广到 profinite 群的框架下。亏群为 Z_p^n 的情形自然对应 p-进整环上的有限生成 torsion-free 群结构，具有代表性。

Method: 先建立一般性归约定理：对任意拥有拓扑有限生成亏群 D 的 profinite 群块，构造一个虚拟 pro-p 群，使该块同构于该虚拟 pro-p 群的一个块；若 D 有限表现，则块的基本代数（basic algebra）在 k 上也有限表现。随后针对 D=Z_p^n 应用该归约结果，并结合有限 p'-群 E 忠实作用与上同调类 α∈H^2(E,k^×) 的分类，确定块的 Morita 等价型。

Result: 任何亏群为 D=Z_p^n 的块都 Morita 等价于 O_α[[Z_p^n ⋊ E]]，其中 E 是有限 p'-群、作用忠实，α∈H^2(E,k^×) 为 2-上循环；并且块的基本代数在 k 上有限表现（当 D 有限表现时）。作为推论，此类 profinite 群块满足 Donovan 猜想。

Conclusion: 该文将亏群理论与 Donovan 猜想推广至 profinite 群设定，给出亏群为 Z_p^n 时的完整 Morita 分类，表明 profinite 群块与虚拟 pro-p 群块之间存在强对应，为 profinite 块表示论提供了统一归约框架。

Abstract: We prove that any block of a profinite group with a topologically finitely generated defect group $D$ is isomorphic to a block of a virtually pro-$p$ group, and if $D$ is finitely presented then so is the basic algebra of the block over $k$. Using these general results we then prove that any block with defect group $D=\mathbb Z_p^n$ is Morita equivalent to $\mathcal O_α[[\mathbb Z_p^n\rtimes E]]$ for a finite $p'$-group $E$ acting faithfully and a $2$-cocycle $α\in H^2(E,k^\times)$. In particular, Donovan's conjecture holds for such blocks of profinite groups.

</details>


### [244] [Derived Delooping Levels of One-Point Extensions and Finitistic Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32744)
*Hanpeng Gao,Dajun Liu,Ruomu Xu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出单点扩张 A=B[M] 中新单模具有导出 delooping level 至多 1 的稳定两步扩张判定准则，并对具体代数计算了 B[M] 的导出与经典 delooping level，构造出反例否定 Guo–Igusa 的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限维代数单点扩张 A=B[M] 的导出 delooping level（ddell）与经典 delooping level（dell），并检验 Guo 与 Igusa 提出的关于有限维数、ddell 与 dell 之间关系的猜想/问题。

Method: 以单点扩张 A=B[M] 为框架，利用稳定两步扩张方法建立新单模导出 delooping level ≤ 1 的判定准则；针对具有自入射 Nakayama 商 R 的具体代数 B，对每个非零 M∈mod R 计算 B[M] 的导出与经典 delooping level。

Result: 得到显式代数 A，满足 Findim(A^op)=1<2=ddell A=dell A，即其表示维数为 1，而导出与经典 delooping level 均为 2。

Conclusion: 该结果给出 Guo–Igusa 问题的否定回答，表明 Findim 与 delooping level 之间并不满足其预期的不等式关系。

Abstract: Let $B$ be a finite-dimensional algebra, $M\in\modu B$, and
  $A=B[M]$ the one-point extension. We give a stable two-step extension
  criterion for the new simple $A$-module to have derived delooping level at
  most one. For a concrete algebra $B$ with a self-injective Nakayama quotient
  $R$, we determine the derived and classical delooping levels of $B[M]$ for
  every nonzero $M\in\modu R$. In particular, we obtain an explicit algebra
  $A$ satisfying
  \[
  \Findim(A^{\mathrm{op}})=1<2=\ddell A=\dell A,
  \]
  which gives a negative answer to a question of Guo and Igusa.

</details>


### [245] [Well-tempered symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32916)
*Alexandre Afgoustidis,Peter Hochs,Shintaro Nishikawa,Yanli Song*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究实约化对称空间 $X=G/H$ 上正则表示的 Plancherel 测度支撑及其 Fell 拓扑，提出"良温"（well-tempered）空间类，并给出其支撑的显式描述与拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实约化对称空间上 Plancherel 公式已由 Delorme、van den Ban–Schlichtkrull 等人建立，但其支撑集合（即谱）的结构及 Fell 拓扑尚缺乏统一而显式的刻画，尤其需要简化并刻画一类特殊对称空间，进而计算相关 $C^*$-代数与 $K$-理论。

Method: 首先回顾已有 Plancherel 公式，将支撑用更小对称空间的离散谱来描述；随后定义并研究"良温"对称空间——即单位陪集处切空间存在 Cartan 子空间，其于 $\mathfrak{g}$ 中的中心化子为交换的；在 $G$ 不连通性温和假设下，用 $G$ 的 Cartan 子群特征刻画支撑，并描述其 Fell 拓扑；对复群 $G$ 及实形式 $H$ 作显式分解。

Result: 良温空间在 Benoist–Kobayashi 意义下是温的；其 Plancherel 支撑可由 Cartan 子群特征描述，Fell 拓扑亦被刻画；当 $G$ 为复群、$H$ 为实形式时，支撑分解为相对连续参数空间对有限 Weyl 群的显式商之并。

Conclusion: 作为应用，给出了 $L^2(X)$ 上正则表示所对应的 $C^*$-代数及其 $K$-理论的计算，表明良温性与复群情形下的显式支撑结构为算子代数不变量提供了可计算框架。

Abstract: Let $X=G/H$ be a real reductive symmetric space. We consider the support of the Plancherel measure for the regular representation of $G$ on $L^2(X)$, and its Fell topology.
  We first recall how the Plancherel formula for $X$ (known from work of Delorme and van den Ban--Schlicktrull, among others) yields a description of the support in terms of the discrete spectra of smaller symmetric spaces. We then point out that this description simplifies for a class of real symmetric spaces $X$ which we call well-tempered. These are the spaces whose tangent space at the identity coset admits a Cartan subspace whose centralizer in the Lie algebra of $G$ is abelian. Well-tempered spaces are tempered in the sense of Benoist and Kobayashi.
  Under mild assumptions on the disconnectedness of $G$, we give a description of the support of the Plancherel measure for well-tempered symmetric spaces in terms of characters of Cartan subgroups of $G$, and describe the Fell topology of the support.
  If $G$ is a complex group and $H$ a real form, then the support decomposes as a union of explicit quotients of relative continuous-parameter spaces by finite Weyl groups. As an application, we describe the $C^*$-algebra associated to the regular representation of $G$ on $L^2(X)$, and its $K$-theory.

</details>


### [246] [Nonunimodal principal spectra of Frobenius parabolic Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33173)
*Vincent E. Coll*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明Frobenius抛物Lie代数的Ooms谱严格单峰性在超越极大抛物类时失效，构造出主重数序列含任意长、二次规模的外向上升段，并给出单峰性的表示论等价判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前同伴论文已证明每个Frobenius极大抛物代数满足严格单峰性，本文旨在考察该性质能否推广到更一般的Frobenius抛物代数并寻找反例。

Method: 构造显式的Frobenius抛物族，分析其主重数序列的一阶差分；利用Kirillov形式将中心化分次变为辛分次，将Ooms单峰性刻画为特征Θ_𝔤在不可约SL_2特征下的展开系数（dim𝔤_m − dim𝔤_{m+1}）的非负性，等价于中心主上特征可延拓为辛SL_2作用。

Result: 对每个L≥1可构造含L个连续负一阶差分的Frobenius抛物，差分均为L的二次规模；在单参数子族中，非单峰性最早出现于𝔰𝔩_136，相邻两重数为541<542。因此超越极大抛物类后存在任意长的负不可约系数块，且负总质量无界。

Conclusion: Ooms严格单峰性并非Frobenius抛物代数的普遍性质，而是极大抛物类的特殊现象；其单峰性等价于Θ_𝔤作为虚拟SL_2特征的有效性或辛SL_2作用的延拓性。

Abstract: Strict unimodality of Ooms spectra fails for Frobenius parabolic Lie algebras beyond the maximal-parabolic class. We construct explicit Frobenius parabolics whose principal multiplicity sequences contain arbitrarily long outward rises: for every $L\ge1$ there are $L$ consecutive negative first differences, each of quadratic size in $L$. In a simple one-parameter subfamily the transition to nonunimodality occurs in $\slie_{136}$, where two adjacent multiplicities are $541<542$. We also give a representation-theoretic criterion for unimodality. If $F$ is a Frobenius functional with semisimple integral principal element $H$ and $\g=\bigoplus_j\g_j$, the Kirillov form makes the centered grading symplectic and its character has the unique expansion \[
  Θ_\g(x)=\sum_{m\ge1}(\dim\g_m-\dim\g_{m+1})\,χ_{2m-1}(x) \] in irreducible $SL_2$ characters. Thus Ooms unimodality is equivalent to effectivity of this virtual $SL_2$ character, or equivalently to extension of the centered principal cocharacter to a symplectic $SL_2$ action. For Frobenius seaweeds the unbroken-spectrum theorem says that, after centering, every odd weight in the support occurs; strict unimodality asks for the stronger positivity of every irreducible coefficient. A companion paper proves that stronger property for every Frobenius maximal parabolic. The families constructed here show that beyond that class there can be arbitrarily long blocks of negative irreducible coefficients, with unbounded total negative mass.

</details>


### [247] [Block Relations and Clifford Theory for H-Triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33502)
*Shi Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群H-三元组之间的块关系，证明了Clifford对应定理并给出归纳与粘贴结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Galois等变局部-整体对应提供特征三元组工具，需要建立H-三元组之间保持相对特征高度与共同亏群的块对应框架。

Method: 通过一对相关联的射影表示，同时满足混合比较函数恒等式与各中间群上的块条件，从而构造Clifford对应；并研究归纳、粘贴、直积、圈积及环境群变化。

Result: 证明了Clifford对应定理：对应保持相对特征高度，每对对应块具有共同亏群；建立了允许不等Clifford指数的归纳判别准则与粘贴结果，并在同一框架下处理直积、圈积与环境群变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为Galois等变局部-整体对应提供了特征三元组工具。

Abstract: We study block relations between H-triples of finite groups. We prove a Clifford correspondence theorem in which a single pair of associated projective representations satisfies both the mixed comparison-function identities and the block conditions on every intermediate group. The resulting correspondence preserves relative character heights and yields a common defect group for each pair of corresponding blocks. We also establish induction and gluing results, including an induction criterion that allows unequal Clifford indices. Direct products, wreath products and changes of ambient group are treated within the same framework. These results provide character-triple tools for Galois-equivariant local-global correspondences.

</details>


### [248] [Explicit $H_3$ relation in the diagrammatic Hecke category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34053)
*Lev Nechitailo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文计算了 $H_3$ 型 Hecke 范畴中缺失的三色关系，将其表示为双叶的显式 $R$-线性组合，并用 GAP 精确算术验证，从而完成了 Elias–Williamson Hecke 范畴的图解呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Elias–Williamson 的 Hecke 范畴图解呈现中，$H_3$ 型的三色关系此前尚未给出，该关系的缺失使得范畴的图解呈现不完整，因此需要补齐这一显式关系。

Method: 在 $H_3$ 型中，将缺失的三色关系表示为双叶的 $R$-线性组合，标量取自 $\mathbb{Z}[\varphi]$（$\varphi = (1 + \sqrt{5})/2$）；并借助计算机代数系统 GAP 以精确算术进行验证。

Result: 所得表达式中包含 $43\,424$ 个非零多项式系数；经 GAP 精确算术验证关系成立。

Conclusion: 该计算补全了 $H_3$ 型中缺失的三色关系，从而完成了 Elias–Williamson Hecke 范畴的图解呈现。

Abstract: We compute the missing three-color relation in type $H_3$ as an explicit $R$-linear combination of double leaves. The expression has $43\,424$ nonzero polynomial coefficients with scalars in $\mathbb{Z}[\varphi]$, where $\varphi = (1 + \sqrt{5})/2$. We verify the relation using exact arithmetic in the computer algebra system GAP. This completes the diagrammatic presentation of the Hecke category of Elias and Williamson.

</details>


### [249] [Quasi-cellular categories and the structure of the Brauer category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34203)
*Kazuo Habiro,Mai Katada*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入准胞腔范畴概念，通过其研究Brauer范畴，构造线性函子L到矩阵Brauer范畴，证明在非奇异参数下L是同构且B与置换张成子范畴Morita等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胞腔代数是重要工具，Brauer代数与Brauer范畴有丰富结构。作者希望将胞腔性推广到范畴层面，用准胞腔结构统一处理图范畴（如Brauer范畴、partition范畴），并推广Brown及König-Xi关于Brauer代数的结果。

Method: 定义准胞腔范畴（胞腔代数与胞腔范畴的变体），验证Brauer范畴与partition范畴等图范畴是准胞腔的。通过Brauer范畴B的准胞腔结构，构造到矩阵Brauer范畴mB的线性函子L，mB与B有相同对象与hom空间但具矩阵式复合。

Result: 在非奇异参数δ下，L是线性范畴的同构；B Morita等价于由置换张成的子范畴S，其自同态代数为对称群的群代数。结果推广了Brown以及König-Xi关于Brauer代数的结论。

Conclusion: 准胞腔范畴为图范畴提供了统一框架，矩阵Brauer范畴刻画了Brauer范畴的结构，非奇异参数下B与置换子范畴Morita等价，推广了Brauer代数的经典结果。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a quasi-cellular category, which is a variant of cellular algebras and cellular categories. Many diagram categories, such as the Brauer category and the partition category, are quasi-cellular.
  We study the Brauer category $B$ over a commutative ring $\mathbb k$ with parameter $δ$ through its quasi-cellular structure. We construct a linear functor $L:B\to mB$ to the \emph{matrix Brauer category} $mB$, which has the same objects and hom-spaces as $B$ but a matrix-like composition. For a non-singular parameter $δ$, we show that $L$ is an isomorphism of linear categories, and that $B$ is Morita equivalent to its subcategory $\mathbb S$ spanned by permutations, whose endomorphism algebras are the group algebras of symmetric groups. These results generalize those of Brown and König and Xi for the Brauer algebras.

</details>


### [250] [On Representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathrm{D})$ admitting a generalized linear period](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34307)
*Prem Dagar,Hariom Sharma*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对四元数除代数上GL_n(D)关于(H_{1,n-1},χ_s)的广义线性周期的不可约光滑表示进行分类：完整解决n=3,4，对n>2提出猜想，并在猜想下用Langlands参数刻画此类表示，同时验证Lapid-Prasad猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p进域上四元数除代数GL_n(D)的周期(period)分类问题，推广已有的线性周期理论，并试图通过Langlands参数从Weil-Deligne表示角度理解周期存在的判别准则，以及验证Lapid-Prasad关于L-包在特定自同构下不变性的猜想。

Method: 设D为特征零非阿基米德局部域F上的四元数除代数，G_n=GL_n(D)，H_{1,n-1}为块对角子群，并定义特征χ_s(diag(g_1,g_2))=ν(g_1)^{2s}ν(g_2)^{-2s}。对n=3,4，通过表示论与周期积分方法分类承认关于(H_{1,n-1},χ_s)广义线性周期的不可约光滑表示；由此对n>2提出完整分类猜想；在假设该猜想下，利用Langlands对应将周期条件翻译为Weil-Deligne参数的条件——即𝔏(π)含同构于𝔏(ν^{-2s})的Weil-Deligne子表示（对应G_{n-2}上ν^{-2s}的(2n-4)维参数），其余四维商为G_2的平凡表示的Langlands参数（此时s=±(n-2)/2）或G_2的一个无限维H_{1,1}-distinguished不可约表示的Langlands参数。

Result: 对n=3,4完成了不可约光滑表示承认(H_{1,n-1},χ_s)-广义线性周期的分类；对n>2给出完整分类猜想；在该猜想下得到判别准则：π承认此类周期当且仅当其Langlands参数𝔏(π)包含同构于𝔏(ν^{-2s})的(2n-4)维Weil-Deligne子表示，且四维商为G_2平凡表示的Langlands参数（s=±(n-2)/2）或G_2的无限维H_{1,1}-distinguished不可约表示的Langlands参数；并在此情形验证了Lapid-Prasad猜想：承认关于H_{1,n-1}线性周期的不可约表示的L-包在ρ↦\widetildeρ^θ下不变。

Conclusion: 四元数除代数GL_n(D)上广义线性周期的表示分类可由Langlands参数中的Weil-Deligne子表示结构精确刻画，且相应的L-包具有ρ↦\widetildeρ^θ不变性，支持Lapid-Prasad猜想；n=3,4的完整结果与n>2的分类猜想为后续一般证明提供了框架。

Abstract: Let $\mathrm{D}$ be a quaternion division algebra over a non-Archimedean local field $\mathrm{F}$ of characteristic zero, and let $\mathrm{G}_n=\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathrm{D})$. Let $\mathrm{H}_{1,n-1}=\{\mathrm{diag}(g_1,g_2)\in\mathrm{G}_1,\ g_2\in\mathrm{G}_{n-1}\}$ and, for $s \in \mathbb{R}$, define $χ_s(\mathrm{diag}(g_1,g_2))=ν(g_1)^{2s}ν(g_2)^{-2s}$. We classify, for $n=3,4$, the irreducible smooth representations of $\mathrm{G}_n$ admitting a generalized linear period with respect to $(\mathrm{H}_{1,n-1},χ_s)$. Motivated by these results, we conjecture a complete classification for all $n>2$. Assuming this conjecture, we characterize such representations in terms of Langlands parameters: an irreducible smooth representation $π$ of $\mathrm{G}_n$ admits such a period if and only if $\mathfrak{L}(π)$ contains a Weil-Deligne subrepresentation isomorphic to $\mathfrak{L}(ν^{-2s})$, the $(2n-4)$-dimensional parameter of $ν^{-2s}$ on $\mathrm{G}_{n-2}$, and the four-dimensional quotient is the Langlands parameter of either the trivial representation of $\mathrm{G}_2$, with $s=\pm\frac{n-2}{2}$, or an irreducible infinite-dimensional $\mathrm{H}_{1,1}$-distinguished representation of $\mathrm{G}_2$. We also verify the Lapid-Prasad conjecture in this setting: the $L$-packet of an irreducible representation of $\mathrm{G}_n$ admitting a linear period with respect to $\mathrm{H}_{1,n-1}$ is invariant under $ρ\mapsto\widetildeρ^θ$.

</details>


### [251] [Bounded functor cohomology and comparison theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34664)
*Karol Janowicz*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文将有限域上有限维向量空间函子范畴的导出范畴与一般线性群表示的导出范畴通过零延拓进行比较，把阿贝尔范畴的recollement提升到导出范畴层面，并给出有限域上一般线性群上同调的若干翻译及与多项式函子范畴的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上函子范畴 $\mathcal{F}_n$ 与一般线性群 $GL_n(k)$ 表示范畴之间的导出范畴联系，旨在通过recollement的导出提升，把函子范畴的构造性工具用于理解一般线性群的上同调，并在大有限域情形下用多项式函子 $\mathcal{P}_{d,n}$ 提供新的表述。

Method: 设 $k$ 为有限域，取维数不超过 $n$ 的 $k$-向量空间范畴到 $k$-向量空间范畴的全体函子构成的范畴 $\mathcal{F}_n$；利用零延拓函子程序，将已知阿贝尔范畴的recollement升级为相应导出范畴的recollement，并进一步与多项式函子范畴 $\mathcal{P}_{d,n}$ 进行比较。

Result: 获得了导出范畴层面的recollement；由此得到有限域上一般线性群上同调的若干翻译；并在大有限域上通过 $\mathcal{F}_n$ 与 $\mathcal{P}_{d,n}$ 的比较给出进一步的重述。

Conclusion: 该工作说明函子范畴的导出结构与群表示导出范畴可通过recollement有效衔接，从而为有限域上一般线性群上同调的研究及多项式函子框架下的表述提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a finite field. We consider the category of all functors $\mathcal{F}_n$ from the category of $k$-vector spaces of dimension $\leq n$ into the category of all $k$-vector spaces. We compare its derived category to the derived category of representations of the general linear group $GL_n(k)$ using the extension by zero functor procedure. We upgrade the well-known recollement of abelian categories into the recollement of the corresponding derived categories. Then we obtain some translations of the cohomology the general linear group over finite field. We also compare $\mathcal{F}_n$ with the category of polynomial functors $\mathcal{P}_{d,n}$, giving further reformulations over big finite fields.

</details>


### [252] [The fields generated by character values of linear and unitary groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35183)
*Eden Ketchum*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文计算了几类有限线性群、酉群及 PSL_n(q)、PSU_n(q) 的 Burnside 域 Q(G) 的生成集，并据此分类了可由非阿贝尔单群的 Burnside 域实现的二、三次数域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burnside 域 Q(G)（由不可约特征标值生成的数域）是群表示论与代数数论交叉的重要对象，但其在经典有限单群上的具体生成结构尚缺乏系统刻画，且在低次数数域的实现问题上缺少应用性结论。

Method: 对有限一般/特殊线性群、一般/特殊酉群以及单群 PSL_n(q)、PSU_n(q)，通过分析其不可约特征标的取值（特征标表结构、本原元素与单位根组合），显式构造并计算 Burnside 域 Q(G) 的生成集。

Result: 给出了上述各类群 Q(G) 的具体生成集；随后利用该结果完全分类了所有次数为 2 或 3 且满足 F = Q(S)（S 为非阿贝尔单群）的数域 F。

Conclusion: 所获生成集为 Burnside 域提供了有效计算途径，并成功将问题归结为低次数数域的实现分类，确立了哪些二、三次数域可作为非阿贝尔单群的 Burnside 域出现。

Abstract: For a finite group $G$, define the field $\mathbb{Q}(G) := \mathbb{Q}(\{χ(g) : χ\in \operatorname{Irr}(G) , g \in G\})$. In this article we compute generating sets for the fields $\mathbb{Q}(G)$ when $G$ is a finite general or special linear group, a finite general or special unitary group, or one of the simple groups $\operatorname{PSL}_n(q)$ or $\operatorname{PSU}_n(q)$. We then apply these results to classify all number fields $F$ of degree $2$ or $3$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ such that $F = \mathbb{Q}(S)$ for some non-abelian simple group $S$.

</details>


### [253] [A proof of Frenkel's bound for the hyperbolic Kac-Moody Lie Algebra A_1^++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35325)
*Gabriel B. Legros*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出秩三双曲Kac--Moody李代数$A_1^{++}$的Frenkel根重数上界的计算机辅助证明，证明对任意根$\alpha$有$\dim g_\alpha\le p(1-(\alpha,\alpha)/2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frenkel提出的根重数猜想给出双曲Kac--Moody代数根空间维数的上界，$A_1^{++}$情形此前未被完整证明，本文旨在完整验证该猜想。

Method: 结合精确的仿射特征标与由抛物同调导出的逐系数对数优函数；经Weyl约化后，用解析估计在深度大或能级大的无限区域建立不等式，剩余有限区域由计算机验证。

Result: 解析估计覆盖大深度或大能级的无限区域，计算机验证处理剩余有限区域，再结合已知的低能级公式，对所有根完成证明。

Conclusion: $A_1^{++}$的Frenkel根重数上界对所有根成立，证明综合了解析方法与计算机辅助验证。

Abstract: We give a computer-assisted proof of Frenkel's root-multiplicity bound for the rank-three hyperbolic Kac--Moody Lie algebra $A_1^{++}$. For every root $α$, we prove $\dim g_α\le p(1-(α,α)/2)$, where $p$ is the ordinary partition function. The proof combines exact affine characters with a coefficientwise logarithmic majorant derived from parabolic homology. After Weyl reduction, analytic estimates establish the inequality on infinite regions of large depth or large level. The remaining finite region is verified by computer. Together with the known low-level formulas, the assembly of these estimates establish the conjecture for every root of $A_1^{++}$.

</details>


### [254] [Generating Functions for Casimir Invariants of Simple Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30635)
*Michael Flattery,Michael P. Tuite*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用Klimyk公式与Okubo公式，为单李代数的Casimir算子特征值推导出有理普通生成函数，该方法不依赖Weyl群知识，并直接重现了Perelomov与Popov关于经典李代数的生成函数表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限维半单复李代数不可约表示对应的广义Casimir算子特征值，旨在寻找一种不依赖Weyl群的生成函数推导途径，从而简化计算并推广已有结果。

Method: 基于Klimyk公式和Okubo公式，推导单李代数Casimir算子特征值的有理普通生成函数；并以此方法直接重构经典李代数的生成函数表达式。

Result: 得到了不依赖Weyl群知识的单李代数Casimir特征值有理普通生成函数，并成功直接重现了Perelomov与Popov给出的经典李代数生成函数表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种规避Weyl群依赖的生成函数构造方法，验证并统一了经典李代数的既有结果，对Casimir算子特征值的系统计算具有方法论意义。

Abstract: We consider generalised Casimir operators associated with irreducible representations for finite-dimensional, semisimple, complex Lie algebras. Using Klimyk's formula and Okubo's formula, we derive a rational ordinary generating function for the Casimir operator eigenvalues for simple Lie algebras which does not rely on knowledge of the Weyl group. We also directly reproduce generating function expressions for the classical Lie algebras originally due to Perelomov and Popov.

</details>


### [255] [Keller's conjecture for split finite-dimensional algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35363)
*Changchang Xi,Jinbi Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明了任意域上每个分裂有限维代数的Keller猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Keller猜想是代数领域中的重要公开问题，此前仅在特殊情形下得到验证，亟需在更一般的代数结构上给出证明。

Method: 对任意域上的分裂有限维代数，通过代数结构分析（结合分裂性条件）进行一般性论证。

Result: 证明了对任意域上的每一分裂有限维代数，Keller猜想均成立。

Conclusion: 该结果将Keller猜想的成立范围推广至任意域上的分裂有限维代数，具有一般性意义。

Abstract: We prove Keller's conjecture for every split finite-dimensional algebra over an arbitrary field.

</details>


### [256] [Jordan induction for $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathcal{O})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35393)
*Zhe Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入Jordan归纳框架，用于构造GL_n(O)的模同余光滑表示，具有不可约性、完备性和互不同构性等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造GL_n(O)的光滑表示（O为完备离散赋值环，剩余域有限）通常较复杂。作者希望借助幂零轨道信息，建立一个简单统一的框架来构造此类表示，并推广已有的Jordan分解与Gérardin构造。

Method: 提出Jordan归纳方法，仅依赖幂零轨道的知识来构造GL_n(O)的光滑表示模同余；在偶级（even level）情形下与Hill的Lusztig-Jordan分解类比相结合，并给出轨映射的显式截面构造；同时提出Jordan归纳与Lusztig归纳相联系的猜想。

Result: Jordan归纳仅产生不可约表示，能给出所有偶级不可约表示，且共轭意义下表示互不同构；在偶级情形下显式实现了Hill的Lusztig-Jordan分解类比，并大幅推广了Gérardin对GL_n(O)的构造；作为应用构造了轨映射的显式截面。

Conclusion: Jordan归纳为GL_n(O)的光滑表示提供了简洁而通用的构造工具，统一并推广了已有结果，同时其与Lusztig归纳的联系猜想有望推广近期与Stasinski合作得到的代数化定理。
