Abstract: Let $\mathcal{O}$ be a complete discrete valuation ring with a finite residue field. We introduce a simple framework of constructing smooth representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathcal{O})$ modulo the knowledge of nilpotent orbits, called Jordan induction, which enjoys several remarkable properties: It yields only irreducible representations, it yields all the even level irreducible representations, and up to conjugation it yields non-isomorphic representations.
  In the even level case, this gives an explicit realisation of Hill's analogue of Lusztig's Jordan decomposition, as well as a vast generalisation of Gérardin's construction for $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathcal{O})$. As a simple application, we construct an explicit section to the orbit map. We also propose a conjecture linking Jordan induction and Lusztig induction, aiming at generalising the algebraisation theorem obtained in recent joint works with Stasinski.

</details>


### [257] [Automorphic $C^*$-algebras of reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35543)
*Jun Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文定义了约化群的自守 C*-代数，并利用 Langlands 谱分解构造了一个单射 *-同态到离散 Levi 数据的直和，且在 GL(n) 及其内形式情形下证明该映射是同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自守表示论中的谱分解（按离散 Levi 数据）提升到 C*-代数层面，以给出自守 C*-代数的结构刻画。

Method: 定义自守 C*-代数 C*_aut(G(A)) 为 G(A) 的全自守表示在 L^2(G(F)\G(A)) 上像的 C*-代数像；利用关于离散 Levi 数据的 Langlands 谱分解，构造从 C*_aut(G(A)) 到带 W(G,M,σ) 不变性的紧算子 C*-代数直和的单射 *-同态。

Result: 构造出单射 *-同态 C*_aut(G(A)) → ⊕_{[M,σ]} K_{C_0(Â_M)}(Ind_P^G C_0(Â_M, H_{M,σ}))^{W(G,M,σ)}；当 G 为 GL(n) 或其内形式时，该映射是同构。

Conclusion: 该结果将自守谱分解组织成 C*-代数同构，为 GL(n) 及其内形式给出自守 C*-代数的显式分解。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a reductive group over a number field $F$. We define $C^*_{\rm aut}(G(\mathbb A))$, the automorphic $C^*$-algebra of $G$, to be the $C^*$-algebraic image of the full automorphic representation of $G(\mathbb A)$ on $L^2(G(F)\backslash G(\mathbb A))$. Using the Langlands spectral decomposition with respect to discrete Levi data, we construct an injective $*$-homomorphism \[ C^*_{\rm aut}(G(\mathbb A)) \longrightarrow \bigoplus_{[M,σ]} K_{C_0(\widehat{A_M})} \left( \operatorname{Ind}_P^G C_0(\widehat{A_M},H_{M,σ}) \right)^{W(G,M,σ)}, \] where $[M,σ]$ ranges over the associate classes of discrete Levi data. When $G$ is $\operatorname{GL}(n)$ or an inner form of it, we prove that this map is an isomorphism of $C^*$-algebras.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Observationanal and Thermodynamic Analysis of Power Law form of $f (Q)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31776)
*Agnidipto Bhattacharya,Shinjini Ghosh,Atreyee Biswas,Sujay Kr. Biswas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究幂律形式 f(Q)=Q+mQ^n 引力，结合观测数据统计分析与不可逆热力学，约束模型参数并研究宇宙学演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 f(Q) 引力幂律模型与标准 ΛCDM 的符合程度，并通过热力学约束进一步限定模型参数范围。

Method: 用 31 点 Hubble 数据、Pantheon 型超新星及 1721 点 SH0ES 数据进行参数拟合与对比；基于 Eckart 非平衡热力学理论与统一热力学定律计算视界熵，考察广义第二定律与热力学平衡，并分析宇宙学参数演化。

Result: 获得模型参数 m、n 的最佳拟合值；确定满足广义第二定律和热力学平衡的 m、n 允许区间，该区间与统计分析结果相容。

Conclusion: f(Q) 幂律模型在观测与热力学双重约束下可给出自洽的参数范围，其宇宙学演化行为在该范围内合理。

Abstract: In this study, the power law form of f(Q) gravity $(f(Q)=Q+mQ^n)$ has been studied in view of statistical analysis with current observed data and irreversible thermodynamics. The best fit values of model specific parameters are found with the use of of 31point Hubble data, Patntheon type Supernva and 1721 point SH0ES data and a comparative study has been performed between $f(Q)$ and standard $Λ$CDM model. The thermodynamic consequence using standard Eckart theory of non-equilibrium thermodynamics has been examined independently for general form of $f(Q)$ gravity followed by power law form of $f(Q)$ where the horizon entropy is calculated using unified law of thermodynamics. Suitable range of power law model specific parameters has been explored through investigating validity of generalized second law of thermodynamics and thermodynamic equilibrium. Finally evolution nature of cosmographic parameters are studied within the specific range of $m,n$ which is compatible with the results obtained from both statistical and thermodynamical analysis.

</details>


### [259] [Chaos bound for spinning particles in a hairy black hole with two photon spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31791)
*Chuanhong Gao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了双光子球毛发黑洞中自旋测试粒子的不稳定圆轨道及混沌界，发现内分支始终满足混沌界而外分支可被违反，且该违反依赖于总角动量、背景形变参数及粒子自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于混沌界违反的研究多集中于近视界区域，而对具有双光子球结构的时空中不同轨道分支是否遵循混沌界尚不清楚，因此需要考察轨道分支对混沌界有效性的影响。

Method: 在允许双光子球结构的毛发黑洞时空中，求解自旋测试粒子的不稳定圆轨道，计算轨道李雅普诺夫指数与黑洞表面引力，据此检验混沌界在内、外两条轨道分支上的满足情况，并分析总角动量、背景形变参数和粒子自旋的影响。

Result: 在所考虑的参数范围内，内分支始终满足混沌界，而外分支可出现违反；总角动量显著影响局部轨道不稳定性并扩大外分支的违反区域；背景形变参数通过改变轨道李雅普诺夫指数和表面引力调节混沌界的有效性；粒子自旋影响较弱，主要经自旋-曲率耦合移动已有不稳定圆轨道及相应临界边界。

Conclusion: 混沌界的有效性明显依赖于轨道分支，外分支的违反行为不同于以往集中于近视界的结论；总角动量与背景形变参数是调控混沌界的主要因素，而自旋作用相对次要。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the unstable circular orbits of spinning test particles and the associated classical orbital chaos bound in a hairy black hole spacetime admitting a double photon sphere structure. This background supports two distinct branches of unstable circular orbits, referred to as the inner and outer branches. We find that, within the parameter ranges considered, the inner branch always satisfies the chaos bound, whereas violations can occur on the outer branch. This behavior contrasts with many previous studies focusing primarily on near-horizon violations of the chaos bound and reveals a clear dependence of the bound on the orbital branch. Our analysis further shows that the total angular momentum of the particle has a pronounced effect on the local orbital instability and can enlarge the bound-violating region on the outer branch. Meanwhile, the deformation parameter of the background geometry regulates the validity of the bound by modifying both the orbital Lyapunov exponent and the black hole surface gravity. By contrast, the effect of the particle spin is comparatively weak and mainly shifts the existing unstable circular orbits and the corresponding critical boundaries through the spin-curvature coupling.

</details>


### [260] [Cosmological Dynamics and Observational Constraints of a Logarithmic Herglotz $f(R,T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31800)
*Vishal M C,Sankarsan Tarai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了对数Herglotz型f(R,T)=R+βln(-T/T₀)引力模型的宇宙学动力学与观测可行性，利用CC、DESI DR2 BAO和Union3 SN数据在MCMC框架下进行约束，模型能一致描述晚期背景膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在标准ΛCDM之外，引入对数Herglotz型f(R,T)修正的引力模型能否在现象学层面一致地描述均匀各向同性宇宙的晚期膨胀历史，并检验其观测可行性。

Method: 在空间平坦FLRW背景和无压物质假设下，将修正场方程表述为包含Herglotz场与归一化物质密度的耦合动力系统；对有效Herglotz部分采用现象学闭合关系，数值求解背景演化；使用宇宙计时器测量、DESI DR2 BAO汇编和Union3超新星样本，在贝叶斯MCMC框架下对单数据集与联合数据集进行约束，并进一步分析Om(z)诊断和statefinder参数。

Result: 联合数据分析给出对背景膨胀统计一致的描述；重建的膨胀历史得到当前有效状态方程w_eff,0≈-0.702，并呈现从晚期加速膨胀向较高红移处减速膨胀的预期转变；Om(z)诊断与statefinder参数分析支持该模型在偏离标准ΛCDM背景的同时仍保持现象学自洽。

Conclusion: 对数Herglotz型f(R,T)模型能够在现象学层面一致地描述均匀各向同性宇宙的晚期膨胀历史，同时允许偏离标准ΛCDM背景。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics and observational viability of a logarithmic Herglotz-type $f(R,T)$ gravity model described by $f(R,T)=R+β\ln(-T/T_0)$. Assuming a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background and pressureless matter, the modified field equations are formulated as a coupled dynamical system involving the Herglotz field and the normalized matter density. A phenomenological closure relation is adopted for the effective Herglotz sector, allowing the background evolution to be determined numerically. The model is constrained using Cosmic Chronometer measurements, the DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation compilation, and the Union3 supernova sample, considering both individual and combined datasets within a Bayesian Markov-chain Monte Carlo framework. The combined analysis provides a statistically consistent description of the background expansion. The reconstructed expansion history yields a present-day effective equation of state $w_{\rm eff,0}\simeq-0.702$ and exhibits the expected transition from accelerated expansion at late times toward decelerated expansion at higher redshifts. We further investigate the $Om(z)$ diagnostic and statefinder parameter. These results indicate that the logarithmic Herglotz model provides a consistent phenomenological description of the homogeneous and isotropic late-time expansion history, while allowing departures from the standard $Λ$CDM background.

</details>


### [261] [Identifiability Limits of Gravitational Wave Phase Deviations: Multiclass Classification with a Multihead Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31993)
*Lavinia Heisenberg,Shayan Hemmatyar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络能否从引力波相位偏差中识别广义相对论偏离的类型与强度：分类头区分GR与六个参数化后爱因斯坦相位族，回归头预测耦合强度的对数。网络以响应函数为输入，在模拟与实测噪声上测试，并通过残差近似分类器解释误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波相位偏差可能揭示广义相对论之外的新物理，但不同参数化偏离模式在波形上高度相似，识别难度与限制来源尚不明确。作者希望评估神经网络识别相位偏差（指数类型与耦合强度）的能力，并厘清识别精度到底受网络能力限制还是受物理简并性限制。

Method: 构建带分类头与回归头的神经网络：分类头区分GR及六个PPE相位族（b=-7,-5,-3,-1,+1,+2），回归头预测log10|β|。输入为衡量波形失配对形变敏感性的响应函数。使用Advanced LIGO设计谱高斯噪声（262个源）与O3实测Hanford谱高斯噪声（132个源），并测试注入实测Hanford应变。对比由六个相位族预测波形残差构造的近似分类器，分析残差相似性与混淆模式；测试十三种网络变体。

Result: 两个高斯数据集上分类准确率分别为0.427与0.329，明显高于噪声水平。网络与近似分类器经常混淆相同的相位族；残差分析表明错误源于底层偏差的相似性。实测Hanford应变上，模拟或实测噪声训练的网络准确率接近模拟Hanford噪声上的结果。十三种变体中循环网络对大偏差的均值分类准确率最高，但均低于近似分类器。

Conclusion: 在已知内禀源参数条件下，相位偏差识别的精度主要受相邻相位族之间的物理相似性限制，而非神经网络能力限制，因为网络精度接近近似分类器的上限。这表明提升识别能力需要更好的相位族区分方法或更丰富的物理信息。

Abstract: We study how well gravitational-wave phase deviations can be identified using a neural network with classification and regression heads. The classification head distinguishes general relativity (GR) from six parametrized post-Einsteinian phase families with exponents $b\in\{-7,-5,-3,-1,+1,+2\}$; the regression head predicts the logarithm of the coupling magnitude, $\log_{10}|β|$. The network input is a response function quantifying the sensitivity of waveform mismatch to waveform deformations. We use stationary Gaussian noise with the Advanced LIGO design spectrum for 262 sources and a measured Hanford spectrum from the third observing run (O3) for 132 sources, and also test injections into recorded Hanford strain. On the two Gaussian datasets, the network identifies the exponent of deviations clearly above the noise with accuracies of $0.427$ and $0.329$. To interpret these results, we compare the network with an approximate classifier built from predicted waveform residuals for the six phase families and same sources. The two classifiers often confuse the same families. Examining residuals left by different phase corrections, we link these errors to similarities between the underlying deviations. On selected recorded Hanford strain, networks trained on simulated or recorded noise achieve overall classification accuracies close to those on simulated Hanford noise. Of thirteen neural-network variants tested on the Advanced LIGO design dataset, the recurrent network has the highest mean classification accuracy for loud deviations, although all variants remain below the approximate classifier. The network nearly matches that classifier's accuracy, indicating that identification is limited by similarities between neighboring phase families rather than by the network, for known intrinsic source parameters.

</details>


### [262] [Relational Frame Equivalence and Exact Cosmology in Brans--Dicke Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32047)
*Yaser Tavakoli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将文献[1]中的"钟扇区"(clock-sector)正则框架应用于Brans-Dicke理论的平直FLRW宇宙学，实现了关系型动力学的显式宇宙学实现，并获得精确可积模型与闭式可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为标量-张量宇宙学中的"关系型标架等价性"提供一个可精确求解的实验室，并给出可用于推广至更一般势能与标量-张量模型的显式宇宙学可观测量。

Method: 从已有完整ADM理论出发限制到均匀扇区，导出正则钟动量 P_Φ = p_φ - a p_a/(2φ)，使Jordan标架与Einstein标架的约化系统共享同一正则相空间；随后取Jordan标架下的二次势(对应Einstein标架为常势)，求解关系型FLRW系统。

Result: 该系统精确可积，完整轨迹可由单一参数 u 表出，得到标度因子、宇宙时哈勃率、哈勃慢滚参数、e折数及标量状态方程参数的闭式表达式；精确解给出从 u = arctanh(1/√3) 开始的暴胀阶段并趋于渐近de Sitter。数值积分(适配的Jordan与Einstein描述、以及基于未平移标量动量的朴素约化Jordan系统)重现了精确轨迹，并显示不同约化正则表示之间由钟依赖变换相联系。

Conclusion: 该分析为标量-张量宇宙学中的关系型标架等价性提供了精确可解范例，并给出可用于扩展至更一般势能与标量-张量模型的显式宇宙学可观测量。

Abstract: We apply the canonical clock-sector framework developed in Ref.~\cite{Tavakoli:2026coc} to spatially flat FLRW cosmology in Brans--Dicke theory. The aim is to obtain an explicit cosmological realization of the frame-adapted relational dynamics and to determine the corresponding cosmological observables in closed form. Starting from the previously developed full ADM theory and restricting it to the homogeneous sector, we obtain the canonical clock momentum as $P_Φ=p_φ-ap_a/2φ$. This identification allows the Jordan and Einstein-frame reduced systems to be represented on a common canonical phase space. We then consider a quadratic Jordan-frame potential for which the Einstein-frame potential is constant. The resulting relational FLRW system is exactly integrable. The complete trajectory can be expressed in terms of a single parameter $u$, yielding closed-form expressions for the scale factor, the cosmic-time Hubble rate, the Hubble slow-roll parameter, the number of e-folds, and the scalar equation-of-state parameter. In particular, the exact solution provides an explicit inflationary regime that begins at $u=\operatorname{arctanh}(1/\sqrt{3})$ and transitions toward an asymptotic de Sitter regime. We finally compare the exact solution with direct numerical integration in the adapted Jordan and Einstein descriptions, and with the naively reduced Jordan system based on the unshifted scalar momentum. The numerical results reproduce the exact trajectory and illustrate how the different reduced canonical representations are related by the clock-dependent transformation implied by the canonical clock-sector construction. The analysis provides an exactly solvable laboratory for relational frame equivalence in scalar--tensor cosmology and gives explicit cosmological observables that can be used in extensions to more general potentials and scalar--tensor models.

</details>


### [263] [Inhomogeneous dust collapse in five-dimensional AdS-Chern-Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32200)
*Luis Avilés,José Díaz,Cristian Quinzacara,Patricio Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了五维 AdS Chern–Simons 引力中球对称非均匀尘埃云在无挠、LTB 坐标下的引力坍缩，给出精确坍缩内部解、奇点与能条件分析，以及静态 AdS 黑洞外部的匹配，并确定视界形成的质量阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在五维 AdS Chern–Simons 引力这一修正引力框架下，理解非均匀尘埃云的引力坍缩、壳聚焦与视界形成机制，并考察初始速度与密度剖面对坍缩与俘获的分别作用。

Method: 采用球对称、无挠假设与共动 Lemaître–Tolman–Bondi 坐标，约化场方程并求得精确坍缩内部解；结合曲率标量（Kretschmann 标量）、能量条件、壳交叉条件、零膨胀分析，并将内部解与静态 AdS Chern–Simons 黑洞分支进行光滑匹配。

Result: 区域半径随时间周期性演化，但物理坍缩分支仅延伸到壳聚焦或更早的奇性丢失；初始密度决定一个径向函数，其平方正比于守恒准局域质量。壳聚焦对应真实曲率奇点，弱、强能量条件在规则坍缩阶段保持。齐次极限退化为五维 FLRW 内部解。零膨胀表明只有当准局域质量超过临界阈值时才形成有限半径表观视界：阈值以上初始非俘获壳在壳聚焦前变为俘获，阈值以下则规则内部无有限半径未来俘获对称球。

Conclusion: 五维 AdS Chern–Simons 引力中的非均匀尘埃坍缩存在精确内部解，可实现与静态黑洞外部的光滑匹配；视界形成由准局域质量阈值控制，壳聚焦为真实曲率奇点，能条件在规则阶段得以维持，齐次情形自洽回归 FLRW。

Abstract: We study the gravitational collapse of a spherically symmetric, inhomogeneous dust cloud in five-dimensional AdS Chern--Simons gravity. Working in the torsion-free sector and using comoving Lemaître--Tolman--Bondi coordinates, we derive the reduced field equations and obtain an exact collapsing interior solution. The areal radius evolves periodically in time, but the physical collapsing branch extends only up to shell focusing or an earlier loss of regularity, while the initial density determines a radial function whose square is proportional to the conserved quasilocal mass. This separates the roles of the initial velocity and density profiles in controlling shell focusing and trapping.
  We derive conditions for central regularity and for avoiding shell crossing, analyze the Kretschmann scalar, and show that shell focusing corresponds to a genuine curvature singularity, the weak and strong energy conditions are preserved throughout the regular collapsing phase. The homogeneous limit reduces consistently to a five-dimensional FLRW interior.
  The collapsing cloud is matched smoothly to the black-hole branch of the static AdS Chern--Simons exterior. The null expansions show that a finite-radius apparent horizon forms only when the quasilocal mass exceeds a critical threshold. Initially untrapped shells above this threshold become trapped before shell focusing, whereas below the total-mass threshold no finite-radius future-trapped symmetry sphere forms in the regular interior.

</details>


### [264] [Stability Analysis of Canonical Acoustic Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32277)
*HengZhong Fang,LiQiang Miao,ChangSen Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究正则声学黑洞在非最小耦合标量场、电磁场和狄拉克场扰动下的稳定性，采用三阶WKB近似计算准正规模（QNMs），结果表明该黑洞稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验正则声学黑洞在多种场（标量场、电磁场、狄拉克场）扰动下的稳定性，并考察非最小耦合标量场中耦合参数ζ的影响。

Method: 采用三阶WKB近似方法计算准正规模（QNMs），并分析相应有效势的性质。

Result: 三类扰动下有效势均为正值、QNMs虚部均为负值，表明正则声学黑洞稳定。标量场扰动的有效势与QNMs依赖于角量子数l、泛音数n和耦合参数ζ，ζ越大扰动衰减越快；电磁场扰动依赖于l和n；狄拉克场扰动依赖于n和总角量子数|k|。

Conclusion: 正则声学黑洞在三类场扰动下均稳定，且不同场扰动下QNMs对参数的依赖关系各不相同。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the stability of canonical acoustic black hole under the perturbation of non-minimally coupling scalar field, electromagnetic field and Dirac field. The quasi-normal modes(QNMs) are computed using the three-order WKB approximation method. The results show that, for all these three kinds of perturbation, the values of the effective potential are positive, while the imaginary parts of QNMs are negative, the canonical acoustic black hole is a table. In details respectively, for scalar field perturbation, the effective potential and the QNMs depend on the angular quantum number $l$, the overtone number $n$, and the coupling parameter ζ, the perturbation decays more rapidly for bigger ζ. For electromagnetic field perturbation, the effective potential and QNMs depend on $l$ and $n$. For Dirac field perturbation, the effective potential and QNMs depend on $n$ and the total angular quantum number | k |.

</details>


### [265] [Memory effect from neutron star merger counterparts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32412)
*T. Brabant,M. Pillas,H. Inchauspé,Z. Lin,F. Foucart,M. Bulla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文量化双中子星并合产生的非线性与线性引力波记忆效应，用ET和LISA评估可探测性，发现ET可探测GW170817组合记忆信号（信噪比10.7，以非线性记忆为主），而线性记忆除极有利情形外仍难观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波记忆效应是广义相对论预言但尚未被观测，下一代探测器（ET、LISA）有望首次探测；需明确双中子星并合中非线性记忆与线性记忆的相对贡献及可探测性。

Method: 系统计算双中子星并合过程中GW、伽马射线暴、余辉、中微子、动力学与盘风抛射物及千新星辐射产生的非线性与线性记忆贡献，并针对ET和LISA开展两方面的可探测性评估：一是分析多信使事件GW170817–GRB 170817A–AT2017gfo，二是评估合成GRB种群的GRB记忆探测能力。

Result: GW170817事件的组合记忆信号在ET中可被探测，信噪比为10.7，且以非线性记忆为主导；对合成GRB种群，仅极端能量且联合有利的构型才可能在ET中产生可探测信号。

Conclusion: 下一代探测器将能够探测非线性记忆，但从双中子星对应体观测线性记忆仍极困难，除非在最有利情形下。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave (GW) memory effect is a prediction of general relativity, characterized by a permanent change in spacetime geometry. Although it has not yet been observed, next-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope (ET) and the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) are expected to provide the sensitivity required for its first detection. GW memory comprises two distinct contributions: the nonlinear memory, generated by the self-interaction of GWs, and the linear memory, originating from the anisotropic emission of matter and radiation associated with astrophysical transients. In this work, we quantify both nonlinear and linear memory contributions produced during binary neutron star mergers (BNSs), including those associated with GWs, gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), afterglows, neutrinos, dynamical and disk-wind ejecta, and kilonova emission. We evaluate the detectability of the resulting memory signals with ET and LISA through two applications. First, we apply our analysis to the multi-messenger event GW170817 - GRB 170817A - AT2017gfo. We find that the combined memory signal from this event would have been detectable by ET with a signal-to-noise ratio of 10.7 dominated by the nonlinear memory. We also assess the detectability of the GRB memory across a synthetic population of GRBs, and find that only extreme-energy and jointly favorable configurations could produce a detectable signal in ET. Together, these results demonstrate that while next-generation detectors will probe nonlinear memory, observing linear memory from BNS counterparts will remain elusive except in the most favorable scenarios.

</details>


### [266] [Energy extraction from Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes: polar region collapse and the capture of negative energy protons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32494)
*Ke Wang,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞在渐近匀强磁场中的粒子加速与能量提取，发现弱磁场下黑洞质量增加、强磁场下发生能量提取，且无需各向异性密度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞与Kerr黑洞的Wald解存在几何差异：磁力线非柱状、|A_φ|等值线在大距离饱和、E·B=0界面随半径收缩消失，需要定量考察这些差异对带电粒子加速与能量提取的影响。

Method: 逐点求解带电粒子加速度，分析质子与电子在极区和赤道区的运动方向随磁场的变化；以规范不变的磁通判据确定捕获域，在(r,θ)平面上积分计算黑洞的质量与角动量变化。

Result: 极区质子被向外驱动、电子向内，赤道区相反，极区随磁场增强而收缩；被捕获质子携带负能量与负角动量，被捕获电子携带正能量与正角动量；弱磁场下黑洞获得质量，强磁场下发生能量提取。

Conclusion: 在Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson背景下，使用通常的球对称密度分布即可实现能量提取，无需像Kerr黑洞的Wald解那样引入各向异性密度分布。

Abstract: The Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black hole is an exact Einstein-Maxwell solution describing a rotating black hole in an asymptotically uniform magnetic field. It differs from the Kerr black hole in the Wald solution: the magnetic field lines are no longer cylindrical, with the constant-\(A_φ\) lines saturating at large distances, and the interface \(\vec{E}\cdot\vec{B}=0\) is no longer a cone but contracts with radius and eventually disappears. Determining the acceleration of charged particles pointwise, we find that protons are driven outward and electrons inward in the polar region, and vice versa in the equatorial region; the polar region shrinks as the magnetic field grows. Captured protons carry negative energy and angular momentum, whereas captured electrons carry positive energy and angular momentum. Fixing the capture domain by the gauge-invariant flux criterion and integrating over the \((r,θ)\) plane, we find that in weak magnetic fields the black hole gains mass, while in strong magnetic fields energy extraction occurs. Energy extraction can be achieved under the usual spherically symmetric density, without introducing the anisotropic density distribution required in the Wald solution for a Kerr black hole.

</details>


### [267] [Exact Solutions for Chiral Gravitational Waves from Spin-2 Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32571)
*Mohammad Ali Gorji,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究暴胀背景下引力子与旁观者自旋2场的线性混合系统，在含宇称破坏参数θ时精确求解任意质量与非微扰混合强度的情形，得到闭合形式的晚期功率谱及手征性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旁观者自旋2场可与度规张量扰动线性混合，此耦合系统在暴胀背景下的精确解尚未得到；先前研究多依赖微扰展开，无法处理强混合区域，因此需要一个非微扰求解框架。

Method: 在暴胀背景中建立含宇称破坏项θ的耦合张量系统，不展开两场间的线性混合，对任意质量精确求解，将混合强度非微扰处理；用取值算符化的高斯超几何函数₂F₁作用于Whittaker模式构造两种螺旋度的模式函数，并解析计算晚期功率谱。

Result: 两种螺旋度的晚期功率谱以广义超几何函数₃F₂闭合形式表示；发现其中一种螺旋度被指数增强，表明与旁观者自旋2场的强混合可产生大且高度手征的原初引力波谱；圆偏振度被tanh(πθ)限制，且与自旋2质量和混合强度无关；同一解还给出旁观者场的晚期功率谱及其与引力子的交叉谱；弱混合极限下精确解退化为微扰Schwinger-Keldysh结果。

Conclusion: 该精确解为研究由额外张量模式产生的原初引力波提供了非微扰框架，并揭示强混合可导致大且高度手征的引力波谱，圆偏振度存在由θ决定的上限。

Abstract: A spectator spin-2 field can mix linearly with the metric tensor perturbations. We study the coupled tensor system in an inflationary background, including a parity violating effect parametrised by $θ$. Without expanding in the linear mixing between the two fields, we solve the system exactly for arbitrary mass while treating the mixing strength nonperturbatively. The mode functions of both helicities are constructed by acting with an operator-valued Gauss hypergeometric function ${}_2F_1$ on Whittaker modes, and the late-time power spectrum of each graviton helicity is expressed in closed form in terms of the generalised hypergeometric function ${}_3F_2$. We find that one helicity is exponentially enhanced, showing that strong mixing with a spectator spin-2 field can generate a large and highly chiral primordial gravitational wave spectrum. The degree of circular polarisation is bounded by $\tanh(πθ)$, independently of the spin-2 mass and the mixing strength. The same solution also gives the late-time power spectrum of the spectator field and its cross spectrum with the graviton. In the weak mixing limit, our exact result reproduces the perturbative Schwinger-Keldysh result. These solutions provide a nonperturbative framework for studying primordial gravitational waves sourced by additional tensor modes.

</details>


### [268] [Thermal equilibrium in general relativity and the special nature of the cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32608)
*M. L. Rodrigues,J. B. Formiga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义相对论框架下讨论热平衡问题，强调宇宙学常数所对应的流体是唯一始终处于热平衡的流体，其能量可表示为 $E_\Lambda=\rho_\Lambda V$，具有普适性；利用广义相对论的远平行等价（TEGR）表述计算了Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞与正则黑洞的准局域能量，并探讨了其对最小长度尺度量子引力理论的启示及零点能困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清广义相对论中热平衡的本质，特别是宇宙学常数流体在其中扮演的独特角色，并探讨空间体积是否具有内在能量这一普适性问题，同时尝试将引力能量问题的讨论与量子引力中的最小长度尺度假设联系起来。

Method: 采用广义相对论的远平行等价表述（teleparallel equivalent of general relativity, TEGR）来分析能量问题，考察宇宙学常数流体的热平衡性质并推导 $E_\Lambda=\rho_\Lambda V$ 关系；进而计算Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞与一种正则黑洞的准局域能量，并讨论零点能困难与引力能量问题的关联。

Result: 表明宇宙学常数流体是唯一始终处于热平衡的流体，其能量可写为普适形式 $E_\Lambda=\rho_\Lambda V$；若宇宙学常数为普适常数，则任意空间体积 $V$ 都具有内在能量；用TEGR方法计算得到的Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞和正则黑洞准局域能量与预期一致。

Conclusion: 宇宙学常数流体在热平衡与引力能量问题中具有独特且普适的地位，其能量表述可能为空间体积的内在能量提供依据，并对假设存在最小长度尺度的量子引力理论有所启示；远平行表述下的准局域能量计算验证了相关预期的一致性。

Abstract: The problem of thermal equilibrium in general relativity is discussed and the unique role played by the fluid associated with the cosmological constant is emphasized. In addition to being the only fluid that is always in thermal equilibrium, it is shown here that its energy can also be seen as a universal quantity given by $E_Λ=ρ_ΛV$, where $V$ is a spatial volume. Furthermore, if the cosmological constant turns out to be a universal constant, then the conclusion that any spatial volume $V$ has an intrinsic energy follows naturally from the approach considered here. A possible application to quantum theories of gravity that postulate the existence of a minimal length scale is discussed. In studying the energies, the so-called teleparallel equivalent of general relativity is used, and the difficulty with the zero-point energy is discussed in light of the gravitational energy problem. The quasi-local energies of the Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole and of a regular black hole are calculated. The results obtained here with the teleparallel theory are consistent with our expectations.

</details>


### [269] [One nat per cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32879)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 从"熵是不可约不确定性"与"视界是完全置乱器"两条前提出发，证明每个普朗克元胞携带1 nat信息，从而在无场方程、无微观理论、无预设希尔伯特空间的情况下导出贝肯斯坦-霍金熵 S=A/4ℓ_P²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞熵的微观起源通常依赖量子引力微观理论（弦论、圈量子引力等）或预设的希尔伯特空间与场方程。本文试图寻找一个不依赖具体微观理论的、更为普适的推导路径，说明"1 nat/普朗克元胞"这一结论为何是必然的。

Method: 以两个输入为基础：(1) 熵定义为不可约不确定性，即在普朗克尺度下任何观测都无法分辨的构型数，是客观界限而非知识匮乏；(2) 视界是完备置乱器，到埃伦费斯特时间其维格纳函数已折叠至普朗克尺度之下，任何元胞都不能被确证为空。考虑单个元胞：未分辨的丝线交叉中，被穿越 n 次的元胞有 n! 种顺序被置乱抹除，故基数 n 的权重为 1/n!，求和得到 Ω₁=Σ 1/n! = e，即 1 nat。N=A/ℓ_P² 个独立元胞给出 Ω=e^N，因此 S=N。再乘以运动学因子 1/4（零测地相空间因果可达比例，另行论证）得 S_BH=A/4ℓ_P²。

Result: 单胞计数 Ω₁=e 给出每普朗克元胞恰好 1 nat；N 个独立元胞给出 Ω=e^N，S=N；乘以因果可达因子 1/4 后得到 S_BH=A/4ℓ_P²，与贝肯斯坦-霍金公式一致。

Conclusion: 该推导不诉诸场方程、微观理论或预设的希尔伯特空间，仅由"不可约不确定性"和"视界置乱"两条一般性原理即可导出黑洞熵的面积律及 1/4 系数（后者来自独立的运动学论证）。这为黑洞熵的普朗克元胞图像提供了理论无关的刻画。

Abstract: A black hole horizon carries exactly one nat of information per Planck cell, without naming what the black hole is made of. Two inputs suffice: entropy is irreducible uncertainty (the configurations no observation can resolve at the Planck floor, an objective bound, not ignorance), and the horizon is a complete scrambler, so by the Ehrenfest time its Wigner function has folded below the floor and no cell can be certified empty. A single cell then holds unresolved filament crossings; a cell crossed $n$ times admits $n!$ orderings that scrambling erases, so cardinality $n$ carries weight $1/n!$ and its count is $Ω_1=\sum_n 1/n!=e$, one nat. The $N=A/\ell_P^2$ independent cells give $Ω=e^N$, hence $S=N$. Multiplied by the kinematic factor $\tfrac{1}{4}$ (the causally accessible fraction of the null phase space, established separately), this returns $S_{BH}=A/4\ell_P^2$. The derivation invokes no field equations, no microscopic theory, and no posited Hilbert space.

</details>


### [270] [Gravitational-Wave Modulation of Atomic Spontaneous Emission: Electrodynamical Theory, Quadrupolar Redistribution, and Rydberg Cavity Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32905)
*Sohrab Rahvar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在量子电动力学框架下研究弱经典引力波对自发辐射的影响，发现引力波会对真空电磁模式进行动态相位调制，将光子态缀饰为频率间隔等于引力波频率的Floquet边带阶梯，导致发射图样出现自旋-2四极角向再分布及瞬态谱的相位依赖不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波为量子物质与时空动力学相互作用提供独特探针，而现有QED自发辐射理论未考虑动态引力背景的影响，作者希望发展弱经典引力波下自发辐射的量子电动力学理论，并探索其作为高频引力波探测平台的可行性。

Method: 在线性化引力背景中量子化电磁场，构建弱引力波存在下的自发辐射QED框架；揭示引力波对真空电磁模式的Floquet相位调制与边带结构；进一步建立腔QED主方程描述，并提出基于Rydberg系综或高电荷态离子的差分跨腔探测方案。

Result: 在一阶引力波应变下总自发衰变率保持不变，但发射图样呈现以引力波相位调制的特征自旋-2四极角向再分布；有限相互作用时间内载波与Floquet边带干涉导致瞬态发射谱中相位依赖的谱不对称；所提差分跨腔方案具备实验可观测性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立腔QED作为探索动态时空量子电动力学及高频引力波探测的有前景平台。

Abstract: Studying Gravitational Waves (GWs) provide a unique probe of the interplay between quantum matter and the dynamics of spacetime. We develop a QED framework for spontaneous emission in the presence of a weak classical GW by quantizing the electromagnetic field in a linearized gravitational background. We show that a passing GW dynamically phase-modulates vacuum electromagnetic modes, dressing photon states into a Floquet ladder of sidebands separated by the GW frequency. While the total spontaneous decay rate remains unchanged to first order in the GW strain, the emission pattern develops a characteristic spin-2 quadrupolar angular redistribution modulated with the GW phase. For finite interaction times, interference between the carrier and Floquet sidebands generates phase-dependent spectral asymmetries in the transient emission spectrum. To explore experimental observability, we formulate a cavity-QED master equation description and propose a differential cross-cavity detection protocol employing Rydberg ensembles or highly charged ions. These results establish cavity QED as a promising platform for exploring quantum electrodynamics in dynamical spacetime and for high-frequency gravitational-wave detection.

</details>


### [271] [Hybrid-star asteroseismology across sharp phase interfaces: Slow and rapid conversion in the $f$- and $p_1$-mode spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33002)
*Zoey Zhiyuan Dong,Hao-Jui Kuan,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在全广义相对论下计算冷、非自转混合星的四极f模与p1模复频谱，比较慢速与快速相变两种界面条件下的一阶相变特征。结果显示，p1模实部频率对界面响应敏感，快速相变相对慢速相变普遍降低p1频率，最大紧致度平均分离可达约3.5 kHz；f模对界面条件仅弱敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内部一阶相变可能在极向准正规模谱中留下特征信号，但不同相变动力学（慢速与快速相变）对非径向振荡模，尤其p1模的具体影响尚不清晰，需要系统研究界面条件对频谱的判别能力。

Method: 采用全广义相对论计算冷、非自转混合星的四极f模与p1模复频谱，构建具有尖锐强子-夸克界面的混合星模型；状态方程将强子SLy4相与常声速夸克相匹配。对比慢速相变（流体元保持相态、界面随流体运动）与快速相变（物质跨越边界时改变相态、界面遵循相平衡而非流体运动）两种物理极限，并引入二者间的代数插值。

Result: 在相同平衡背景上，两种相变极限在p1模频率实部产生显著分离，最大处对应界面附近本征函数局域切向位移分数快速变化。在所考察的状态方程参数空间内，快速相变普遍较慢速相变降低p1模频率，紧致度平均分离在非连通的孪生星分支上达到约3.5 kHz；最大分离出现在较低相变压力与大密度不连续处。f模对单个恒星模型的界面条件仅弱敏感。超过强子SLy4参考残差的偏离主要出现在低相变压力、大密度不连续或二者兼具时，且实部与虚部拟合变量的边界不同。

Conclusion: p1模主要探测界面的局域响应，而所追踪的f模反映混合星序列相对强子标定的整体偏离。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in neutron-star interiors can leave signatures in the polar quasi-normal-mode spectrum. We compute the complex quadrupolar $f$- and $p_1$-mode spectra in full general relativity for cold, nonrotating hybrid stars with a sharp hadron--quark interface. We model the equation of state by matching a hadronic SLy4 phase to a constant-sound-speed quark-phase. We compare slow phase conversion, in which fluid elements retain their phase and the interface moves with the fluid, with rapid phase conversion, in which matter changes phase as it crosses the boundary and the interface follows phase equilibrium rather than the fluid, together with an algebraic interpolation between these limits. On identical equilibrium backgrounds, the two physical limits produce a sizable separation in the real part of the $p_1$-mode frequency. This $p_1$ frequency separation is largest when the local tangential-displacement fraction of the eigenfunction changes rapidly across the interface. Across the surveyed EOS parameter space, rapid conversion generally lowers the $p_1$-mode frequency relative to slow conversion, with compactness-averaged separations reaching $\sim3.5\,{\rm kHz}$ on disconnected twin-star branches. The largest separations occur at relatively low transition pressures and large density discontinuities. By contrast, the $f$-mode is only weakly affected by the interface condition for individual stellar models. Across the sampled constant-sound-speed parameter space, departures exceeding the hadronic SLy4 reference residual occur mainly at low transition pressure, large density discontinuity, or both, with different boundaries for the real- and imaginary-part fitting variables. Thus, the $p_1$-mode probes the local interface response, whereas the tracked $f$-mode reflects the global departure of the hybrid sequence from the hadronic calibration.

</details>


### [272] [Two-horizon sector thermodynamics and NUT bi-hair in Kerr-Newman-NUT spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33032)
*Di Wu,Robert B. Mann,Shuang-Qing Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过双视界（内外视界熵与逆温度）的Σ/Δ扇区诊断，研究带电磁荷的Kerr-Newman-NUT黑洞热力学态空间结构，发现N具有电荷型扇区角色，而J_N=MN是旋转型次级毛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠第一定律自洽不足以确定NUT带电时空的黑洞态空间，因为多种不等价的宏观表述都能给出自洽热力学；需要在带电转动情形下对这一态空间的组织方式进行更严格的检验。

Method: 将内外视界熵与逆温度组织为和（Σ）与差（Δ）两个扇区，对电charged Kerr-Newman-NUT族构造双视界扇区诊断；在同一双视界热力学中同时纳入质量M、普通电荷Q、普通角动量J、NUT荷N与NUT次级毛J_N=MN，并检验物理锚定的极小齐次态空间及消除J_N或N的自然约化。

Result: 在所定义并检验的物理锚定极小齐次态空间中，出现清晰的扇区分离：Σ扇区由电荷型变量Q与N闭合，而Δ扇区还需旋转型变量J与J_N；消除J_N或N的自然约化无法再现由扇区温度固定的第一定律系数，其Bekenstein-Smarr关系也无法按指定标度权重闭合。

Conclusion: 在电charged转动Kerr-Newman-NUT族中，电荷与普通转动不能吸收NUT次级响应，支持NUT双毛组织：N承担电荷型扇区角色，J_N=MN是旋转型热力学次级毛，后者在齐次状态方程中离壳定义，而非新的度规参数或渐近守恒荷。

Abstract: First law consistency alone does not determine the black hole state space of NUT-charged spacetimes, because several inequivalent macroscopic formulations can yield consistent thermodynamics. We develop a two-horizon sector diagnostic for the electrically charged Kerr-Newman-NUT family by organizing the inner- and outer-horizon entropies and inverse temperatures into sum ($Σ$) and difference ($Δ$) sectors. The charged rotating geometry gives a more stringent test of the uncharged Taub-NUT sector result, since the mass $M$, ordinary electric charge $Q$, ordinary angular momentum $J$, NUT charge $N$, and the NUT secondary hair $J_N=MN$ all enter the same two-horizon thermodynamics. For the physically anchored minimal homogeneous state spaces defined and tested here, we find a clear sector separation: the $Σ$ sector closes with the charge-like variables $Q$ and $N$, whereas the $Δ$ sector additionally requires the rotation-like variables $J$ and $J_N$. Natural reductions that eliminate either $J_N$ or $N$ fail to reproduce the first law coefficient fixed by the sector temperature, and their associated Bekenstein-Smarr relations do not close with the assigned scaling weights. Within this class, electric charge and ordinary rotation therefore do not absorb the NUT secondary response, and the charged rotating family supports the NUT bi-hair organization: $N$ has a charge-like sector role, while $J_N=MN$ is a rotation-like thermodynamic secondary hair. The latter is defined off shell in the homogeneous equation of state, not as a new metric parameter or asymptotic conserved charge.

</details>


### [273] [A construction of charged naked singularities for the Einstein--Maxwell-charged scalar field equations in 3+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33168)
*Weihao Zheng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在(3+1)维球对称Einstein-Maxwell-带电标量场方程中构造了一类整体裸奇点解，其来自渐近平坦、单端、C^{1,α}特征初始数据，并发展了一般性的裸奇点外区构造框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸奇点的存在性与宇宙监督假设密切相关，但已有研究多集中于无电荷情形，带电且含标量场的整体裸奇点解尚缺乏系统构造。

Method: 以C^{1,α}（0<α≪1）渐近平坦单端特征初始数据出发，求解(3+1)维球对称Einstein-Maxwell-带电标量场方程；建立构造裸奇点外区（奇点发出的过去光锥的因果未来区域）的一般框架，允许特征数据奇异并容纳Maxwell场在奇点附近的奇异行为。

Result: 构造出未被事件视界包裹的曲率奇点解；所得时空是真正带电的，Maxwell场非零且在到达奇点前满足定量正下界，并具有显式渐近剖面。

Conclusion: 该工作给出带电整体裸奇点解及其构造框架，适用于广泛的特征数据，为研究宇宙监督假设在带电标量场情形下的失效提供了新工具与实例。

Abstract: We construct a class of global naked singularity solutions for the $(3+1)$-dimensional spherically symmetric Einstein--Maxwell-charged scalar field equations. The solutions arise from $C^{1,α}$ asymptotically flat one-ended characteristic initial data with $0<α\ll1$ and develop a curvature singularity that is not enclosed by an event horizon. The resulting spacetimes are genuinely charged, with a nonzero Maxwell field that satisfies a quantitative positive lower bound up to the singularity and admits an explicit asymptotic profile. As part of the construction, we develop a general framework for constructing naked singularity exteriors, namely, the causal future region of the past light cone emanating from the singularity. The framework accommodates a broad range of singular characteristic data and, in particular, allows for singular behavior of the Maxwell field near the singularity.

</details>


### [274] [Quasinormal modes of Hayward black holes from Schwarzschild to extremality: a comparative spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33267)
*Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文用多精度Chebyshev配点法研究Hayward黑洞标量与电磁准正规模及Regge-Wheeler型有效模型的轴向谱，从Schwarzschild极限到严格极端情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hayward黑洞的准正规模（QNM）在近期的研究中被广泛讨论，但自旋2（引力）方程具有模型依赖性，且离散化会产生难以物理归类的纯虚本征值序列，因此有必要系统检验低频基准、超调模式以及虚轴上"候选谱"的可靠性。

Method: 采用多精度（multiprecision）Chebyshev配点法（collocation）求解标量、电磁以及指定Regge-Wheeler型有效模型的自旋2方程，覆盖从Schwarzschild极限到精确极端（extremal）的参数范围，并与近期Hayward研究中的有效源势进行对比，量化代表性频率差异。

Result: 复现了大多数现有低阶振荡基准并给出新的超调候选；自旋2方程势函数与已有Hayward研究不同，频率存在可量化差异；离散化产生纯虚本征值序列，其有限表间隙色散与所取间隙数目有关，间距接近Mω约1/4、极端乘积接近1/3（与喉半径相关）。

Conclusion: 纯虚轴上高度可复现的阶梯并不一定是延拓格林函数的极点，故本文将其报告为未归类的谱候选而非已确立的QNM；其间距与极端乘积仅属所列候选表的性质，不足以支持普适QNM间距或喉半径控制阻尼的结论。

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic quasinormal modes of the Hayward black hole and the axial spectrum of a specified Regge--Wheeler-type effective model, from the Schwarzschild limit to exact extremality. Multiprecision Chebyshev collocation reproduces most available low-lying oscillatory benchmarks and supplies further overtone candidates. The spin-2 equation is model dependent. It differs from the effective-source potential used in recent Hayward studies, and we quantify representative frequency differences. The discretisation also produces purely imaginary eigenvalue sequences, whose physical classification requires special care because highly reproducible imaginary-axis ladders need not be poles of the continued Green function. Accordingly, the imaginary-axis entries reported here are retained as unclassified spectral candidates, not established QNMs. We quantify their finite-table gap dispersion and sensitivity to the number of included gaps. Their spacings near one quarter in $Mω$, and the extremal product near $1/3$ with the throat radius, remain properties of the tabulated candidates, and they do not establish universal QNM spacing or throat-controlled damping.

</details>


### [275] [Dimensional (In)dependence of Lorentzian Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33452)
*Masazumi Honda,Hiroki Matsui,Kota Numajiri,Kazumasa Okabayashi,Takahiro Terada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在D维时空的德西特微超空间近似下，利用洛伦兹路径积分与Picard-Lefschetz理论计算量子宇宙的跃迁振幅，并通过Borel-Padé重求和处理Stokes歧义，发现隧穿提议在各维度普遍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子宇宙学中"宇宙从无到有"的创生问题长期存在边界条件与路径积分定义上的争议，尤其是半经典近似下的振幅选取依赖于复鞍点的处理方式，缺乏跨维度的系统性检验。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦引力下的德西特微超空间近似，结合洛伦兹路径积分与Picard-Lefschetz理论识别对积分有贡献的鞍点，在Dirichlet边界条件下导出定义良好的跃迁振幅；并数值上应用基于Borel-Padé重求和的复苏分析以消解残余Stokes歧义。

Result: 对三维、四维、五维情形及大D极限的分析表明，宇宙从无到有的创生普遍实现隧穿提议，且Borel-Padé复苏分析能有效分辨特定情形下的Stokes歧义。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了基于爱因斯坦引力的量子宇宙学在各维度上具有普适性，隧穿提议不受时空维度限制。

Abstract: We study transition amplitudes of the quantum universe in $D$-dimensional spacetime within the de Sitter minisuperspace approximation in Einstein gravity. Using the Lorentzian path integral combined with Picard-Lefschetz theory, we identify the saddle points that contribute to the integral and derive a well-defined transition amplitude under Dirichlet boundary conditions. We further show numerically that a resurgence analysis based on Borel-Padé resummation is effective in resolving the residual Stokes ambiguities that arise in certain cases. Analyzing three-, four-, and five-dimensional cases, as well as the large-$D$ limit, we find that the tunneling proposal is commonly realized for the creation of the universe from nothing. This result implies a universal feature of quantum cosmology based on Einstein gravity across dimensions.

</details>


### [276] [Polar-metric/axial-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33511)
*Abhishake Sadhukhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究MMP可穿越虫洞的线性扰动，将磁荷背景上极性度规扰动与规范场轴向扰动约化为两个耦合的Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程，并证明喉部方程需包含费米子响应及诱导Hall电流才能满足Bianchi恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMP虫洞由荷电费米子最低Landau能级的Casimir能量维持喉部开放，先前处理丢弃了应力张量的迹部分，其扰动一致性与线性稳定性尚不明确，需要系统分析该扇区的耦合扰动。

Method: 在磁荷背景下处理极性度规扰动与规范场轴向扰动的耦合扇区，约化为两个Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程；利用二维共形反常精确计算费米子响应（含迹部分），引入诱导Hall电流；由费米子有效作用量导出Hall电流需带系数c/48π的Euler项，类似Wen-Zee项；在α=0时喉部为精确AdS2×S2，模式解耦为两个Pöschl-Teller问题。

Result: α=0时模式解耦为质量l(l-1)与(l+1)(l+2)的两个Pöschl-Teller问题，频率间隔为1/ℓ；给出O(α)修正的闭式表达，修正混合两模式并依赖频率，在重叠区与口部匹配；势的对称部分在整个虫洞为正，排除纯指数增长；修正降低正常频率并使两模式共享的能级分裂，一阶频移为实数；该扇区在l≥2时一阶无增长模式。

Conclusion: 包含费米子响应与诱导Hall电流后，线性化Bianchi恒等式在一阶反作用α下成立；喉部扰动在该扇区一阶稳定，无增长模式，为MMP虫洞的线性稳定性提供了自洽分析。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) traversable wormhole. On a magnetically charged background, polar metric perturbations couple to axial perturbations of the gauge field. We treat this sector and reduce it to two coupled master equations of Zerilli-Moncrief type. The wormhole has two regions. In the mouths the geometry is a nearly extremal magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole with no quantum source. In the throat the Casimir energy of the lowest Landau level of charged fermions holds the wormhole open. We show that the throat equations are consistent only if the fermions respond to the perturbation, computed exactly from the two-dimensional conformal anomaly with the full stress tensor including the trace part that MMP discard. This response must be accompanied by an induced Hall current of the fermions. With both effects included, the linearised Bianchi identities hold at first order in the fermionic backreaction $α$. Deriving the Hall current from the fermion effective action instead requires an Euler term with coefficient $c/48π$, which becomes a coupling between magnetic flux and curvature similar to a Wen-Zee term. At $α=0$ the throat is exact AdS$_2\times S^2$ and the modes decouple into two Poschl-Teller problems with masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$, with levels spaced by $1/\ell$ in frequency. We give the $O(α)$ corrections in closed form. They mix the two modes and depend on frequency, and match the mouths in the overlap region. The symmetric part of the potential is positive throughout the wormhole, excluding purely exponential growth. The corrections lower the normal frequencies and split the levels shared by the two modes, with real shifts at first order in $α$. This sector has no growing mode for $l\ge2$ at this order.

</details>


### [277] [Stochastic gravitational-wave background from self-interacting superradiant clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33596)
*Yin-Da Guo,Richard Brito,Chen Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了黑洞-玻色子云系统在包含标量自相互作用情形下产生的随机引力波背景（SGWB），并评估下一代地面引力波探测器的可探测性。自相互作用会抑制SGWB，从而放松现有LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA的限制，但中等强度自相互作用的云仍可被ET和CE探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测是探测新基本场的有力手段，黑洞-玻色子云系统（超辐射形成、辐射长寿命近单色引力波）是有充分动机的源。此前工作未包含标量自相互作用，本文旨在将其纳入以完善SGWB预言并评估可探测性。

Method: 在超辐射机制下，超轻标量场在旋转黑洞周围形成云并辐射近单色引力波；本文在原有计算基础上引入标量自相互作用，计算该系统产生的随机引力波背景（SGWB），并分析其在下—代地面探测器中的可探测性。

Result: 自相互作用可抑制SGWB，从而放松现有LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA零搜索结果给出的限制：设计灵敏度的LIGO对衰变常数f_s≲3×10^17 GeV的标量场SGWB不敏感，且与标量质量无关。对于f_s=10^17 GeV、保守假设下，ET对标量质量~[10^-13.0, 10^-11.8] eV敏感，CE对~[10^-13.2, 10^-11.7] eV敏感；ET和CE可探测SGWB所需的最小衰变常数分别为~6×10^16 GeV和~3×10^16 GeV。

Conclusion: 引入标量自相互作用显著改变黑洞-玻色子云SGWB的预言，弱化现有引力波观测约束；但中等自相互作用的云仍可由下一代探测器ET和CE探测，给出具体的敏感质量区间与最小衰变常数阈值，为未来搜寻新基本场提供理论依据。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations offer a powerful probe of new fundamental fields. One well-motivated source is black hole (BH)--boson cloud systems, in which an ultralight scalar field forms a cloud around a rotating BH via superradiance and emits long-lived nearly monochromatic GWs. In this work, we compute the stochastic GW background (SGWB) from such systems, extending previous work by including scalar self-interactions, and discuss its detectability with next-generation ground-based GW detectors. We find that self-interactions can suppress the SGWB and thereby relax existing LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA constraints inferred from null searches. Namely, the LIGO detectors at design sensitivity are insensitive to a SGWB produced by scalar fields with a decay constant $f_\mathrm{s} \lesssim 3\times 10^{17}$ GeV, independently of the scalar field mass. Looking ahead, we show that a moderately self-interacting cloud can still produce a detectable SGWB with next-generation detectors. Under conservative assumptions, for a decay constant $f_\mathrm{s} = 10^{17}$ GeV, the Einstein Telescope (ET) will be sensitive to scalar masses in the range $\sim[10^{-13.0},10^{-11.8}]$ eV, while Cosmic Explorer (CE) will be sensitive to scalar masses in the range $\sim[10^{-13.2},10^{-11.7}]$ eV. We also find that the minimum decay constants that still yield a detectable SGWB for ET and CE are $f_\mathrm{s}\sim 6\times10^{16}\,$GeV and $\sim 3\times10^{16}\,$GeV, respectively.

</details>


### [278] [Exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations in null homogeneous Petrov spaces with non-solvable motions groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33613)
*V. V. Obukhov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对零类齐性Petrov空间中，在零超曲面上单可迁作用的不可解运动群G₃下，不变电磁场的Maxwell真空方程精确解进行了完整分类，并给出了便于后续使用的分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对零类齐性Petrov空间中具有不可解运动群G₃的时空，缺乏对不变电磁场Maxwell真空方程精确解的完整分类，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用正则标架中矢量场来表示逆变度规张量的分量和不变电磁场的分量，以此为基础对Maxwell真空方程进行精确解分类。

Result: 获得了该几何设定下Maxwell真空方程精确解的完整分类，且结果以便于进一步使用的形式呈现。

Conclusion: 研究完成了特定零类齐性Petrov空间中不变电磁场Maxwell真空方程精确解的完整分类，为相关引力与电磁场精确解研究提供了可直接引用的分类结果。

Abstract: A complete classification of exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations for invariant electromagnetic fields in null homogeneous Petrov spaces with unsolvable groups of motions $G_3$, acting simply transitively on null hypersurfaces of null homogeneous Petrov spaces, is conducted. The classification of exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations is based on using the representation of components of the contravariant metric tensor and components of the invariant electromagnetic field in terms of vector fields of a canonical frame. The classification results are presented in a form convenient for further use.

</details>


### [279] [Perturbations of Kerr-Newman black holes: separation and mode stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33661)
*Peter Hintz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究亚极端Kerr-Newman黑洞线化扰动，用矩阵积分因子解耦自旋1与自旋2场耦合，并证明实轴上无实频准正规模（模式稳定性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chandrasekhar指出Kerr-Newman背景下自旋1（电磁）与自旋2（引力）扰动存在"表面不可解的耦合"，阻碍对黑洞稳定性（尤其模式稳定性）的直接分析。

Method: 对描述辐射自由度的适当变换后的一阶系统引入矩阵积分因子，克服自旋1与自旋2场的耦合；对角分离后对径向常微分方程采用Whiting型变换证明实轴上不存在实频准正规模。

Result: 成功解耦自旋1与自旋2场，并在实轴上证明模式稳定性：不存在实频准正规模。

Conclusion: 通过矩阵积分因子与Whiting型变换，克服了Kerr-Newman黑洞扰动中自旋1与自旋2耦合的困难，建立了实轴上的模式稳定性。

Abstract: We study linearized perturbations of the subextremal Kerr-Newman black hole solution of the Einstein-Maxwell equations. We show how to overcome what Chandrasekhar called the "apparent indissolubility of the coupling between the spin-1 and spin-2 fields" by using a matrix integrating factor for suitably transformed first-order systems that describe the radiative degrees of freedom. For the radial ODEs obtained after angular separation, we prove mode stability on the real axis, i.e., the absence of quasinormal modes with real frequencies, using a Whiting-type transform.

</details>


### [280] [Relic Neutrinos Probing Small Scale Primordial Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34324)
*Suraj Gavhale,Shin'ichi Nojiri,Sergei D. Odintsov,Oem Trivedi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出利用遗迹中微子记录中微子退耦后的电磁熵产生，构建探测原初折叠型非高斯性的敏感双谱窗口，将小尺度原初非高斯性与遗迹中微子俘获率直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初宇宙中微子退耦后的电磁熵产生信息在主CMB中被抹去，但可能保留在遗迹中微子中，因此需要寻找新的观测量来探测被CMB丢失的原初模式。

Method: 从声学阻尼推导立方光子数源及其主导的折叠敏感双谱窗口，将中微子退耦、热化与声学相干性合并进单一响应核；针对激发初态，该核对物理折叠谱型进行滤波，给出依赖锐度的透射条件。

Result: 该响应核能够筛选物理折叠谱型并给出锐度依赖的透射条件，从而把原初折叠型非高斯性窗口与遗迹中微子俘获率联系起来。

Conclusion: 遗迹中微子可作为探测主CMB无法触及的原初非高斯性的探针，声学阻尼诱导的光子数双谱为观测小尺度原初折叠非高斯性提供了可行通道。

Abstract: Relic neutrinos retain a record of electromagnetic entropy production after neutrino decoupling, probing primordial modes erased from the primary CMB. We derive the cubic photon number source from acoustic damping and its leading folded sensitive bispectrum window, with neutrino decoupling, thermalization, and acoustic coherence combined in a single response kernel. For excited initial states, this kernel filters the physical folded profile and yields a sharpness dependent transmission condition, directly connecting small scale primordial non-Gaussianity to relic neutrino capture rates.

</details>


### [281] [Avoided crossings and resonances in black hole ringdown with coupled fields: A case study of the Einstein-Maxwell-axion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34401)
*Takuya Takahashi,Hayato Motohashi,Kazufumi Takahashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将黑洞准正规模（QNM）谱中"避免交叉"现象的研究拓展至多场耦合微扰系统，以Einstein-Maxwell-axion带电黑洞为例，考察引力、电磁与axion模式分支间的避免交叉及其对ringdown波形的物理影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究表明，黑洞QNM谱中的避免交叉伴随共振特性（如增强的激发因子），但相关研究多局限于单场情形；多场耦合系统中避免交叉如何影响ringdown波形尚不明确，亟需推广与检验。

Method: 以Einstein-Maxwell-axion系统中的带电黑洞为具体模型，将QNM分支按解耦极限下连续连接的引力模、电磁模与axion模进行分类，重点关注引力主导与电磁主导分支之间的一个避免交叉；通过QNM重构与直接演化的时域波形对比验证，并结合QNM分解分析分支特性。

Result: QNM重构能准确再现直接演化时域波形的ringdown部分；在避免交叉附近，特定初始微扰下出现有效更慢的衰减；但两个QNM的共振增强贡献在物理波形中大幅相消；QNM分解进一步揭示共振两侧两分支的物理特性发生交换。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了多场耦合系统中共振对黑洞ringdown的印记，表明避免交叉的共振效应（增强激发因子与更慢衰减）在物理波形中因QNM贡献的相消而不直接显现，而分支特性的交换成为可辨识的特征，为多场黑洞ringdown的谱学诊断提供依据。

Abstract: Avoided crossings in black hole quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra exhibit characteristic resonant properties, including enhanced excitation factors. We extend the study of this phenomenon to perturbation systems with multiple coupled fields and investigate its consequences for ringdown waveforms. As a concrete example, we consider a charged black hole in the Einstein-Maxwell-axion system, whose QNM branches can be classified according to the gravitational, electromagnetic, and axion modes to which they continuously connect in the decoupling limit. We focus on an avoided crossing between the gravity- and electromagnetic-led branches thus defined. We verify that the QNM reconstruction accurately reproduces the ringdown portion of the directly evolved time-domain waveform. Near the avoided crossing, an effectively slower decay emerges for particular initial perturbations, while the resonantly enhanced contributions from the two QNMs largely cancel in the physical waveform. The QNM decomposition further reveals an exchange of the physical characters of the two branches across the resonance. These results characterize the imprint of resonances on black hole ringdown in systems with multiple coupled fields.

</details>


### [282] [Charged Simpson-Visser-AdS spacetime: QPOs and observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34443)
*Saeed Ullah Khan,Javlon Rayimbaev,Muhammad Zahid,Tolaniddin Nurmukhamedov,Abat Muratov,Weiwei Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究带电Simpson-Visser-AdS时空中粒子动力学与准周期振荡（QPO），揭示正则化参数与AdS半径对双峰QPO的显著影响及观测约束的简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在电荷、正则化参数与AdS半径共同作用下，修正时空几何（包括黑洞与虫洞构型）如何改变粒子轨道与QPO频率，并检验观测数据对这类时空参数的约束能力。

Method: 分析度规函数与黑洞视界随电荷Q、正则化参数b和AdS半径l的变化，按根的分类区分三类拓扑构型；计算有效势以确定ISCO及测试粒子的角动量与能量；推导Keplerian、径向和垂直频率以建模QPO，并与双黑洞双星的高频QPO数据对比。

Result: 度规根呈现非极值（内外双视界）、极值（单一简并视界）和无视界虫洞三类构型；修正几何显著移动ISCO并改变粒子角动量与能量；b与l的相互作用显著修改双峰QPO，b使基本QPO频率高于标准广义相对论背景；观测数据在约4σ置信度上排除Schwarzschild极限，但仅能约束电荷与AdS曲率的简并组合，而b完全不被数据约束。

Conclusion: 带电Simpson-Visser-AdS时空的拓扑与粒子动力学明显区别于经典黑洞，QPO频率对b与l敏感；现有高频QPO数据不足以单独约束正则化参数，仅给出电荷-曲率简并限制。

Abstract: In this article, we examine particle dynamics and Quasi-periodic oscillations within a charged Simpson-Visser-Anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime. We first analyze the behavior of the metric and black hole horizons under the competing impact of electric charge $Q$, regularization parameter $b$, and AdS radius $l$. The roots of the metric function reveal transitions between three distinct topological classes: non-extremal configurations featuring distinct inner and outer horizons, extremal limits with a single degenerate horizon, and strictly positive, horizonless wormhole geometries. By evaluating the effective potential, we determine how this modified geometry shifts the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) and alters the angular momentum and energy of test particles. Moreover, we derive the Keplerian, radial, and vertical frequencies to model quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs). We show that the interplay between the regularization parameter $b$ and the AdS radius $l$ significantly modifies twin-peak QPOs. In particular, $b$ drives fundamental QPO frequencies higher than those predicted by standard general relativistic backgrounds. Twin-peak high-frequency QPO data from two black-hole binaries disfavor the Schwarzschild limit at $\sim\!4σ$, but constrain only a degenerate combination of charge and AdS curvature, leaving the regularization parameter entirely unconstrained by the data.

</details>


### [283] [Dynamical dark energy from quantum gravity condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34917)
*Daniele Granata,Daniele Oriti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在群场论（GFT）凝聚态宇宙学框架下研究量子引力诱导的动力学暗能量，推广了此前模型，纳入额外标量物质场与多凝聚模贡献，导出有效宇宙动力学并分析晚期行为，发现系统趋于渐近de Sitter相，同时可出现偏离w=-1的动力学暗能量演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前量子引力诱导宇宙加速的模型参数空间较窄，未同时考虑相位动力学与多个凝聚模贡献，也缺乏物质内容与暗能量动力学之间的联系。作者希望扩展模型的普适性与鲁棒性，并为用量子引力动力学对接宇宙学观测奠定基础。

Method: 在GFT凝聚态宇宙学中，引入除关系时钟之外的额外标量物质场，考虑更广的参数空间；采用相干峰值态的平均场近似，仅保留Hermitian GFT相互作用；由此推导相应的有效宇宙动力学方程，并分析其晚期渐近行为。

Result: 系统趋近渐近de Sitter相，且由单一凝聚模动态主导；趋近过程中可呈现非平凡的动力学暗能量演化。凝聚相位与次主导模均可导致对渐近状态方程w=-1的偏离，包括phantom行为，并在适当条件下穿越phantom分界。额外标量物质场直接进入这些条件，建立了物质内容与量子引力诱导暗能量动力学之间的关联。

Conclusion: 结果扩展了GFT动力学暗能量机制的普适性与鲁棒性，并为将底层量子引力动力学与宇宙学观测相对照提供了基础。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical dark energy emerging from quantum gravity within the framework of group field theory (GFT) condensate cosmology. We generalise previous models of quantum gravity induced cosmic acceleration by considering a broader parameter space, including an additional scalar matter field besides the relational clock, and accounting simultaneously for the phase dynamics and the contribution of multiple condensate modes. Working in a mean-field approximation with coherent peaked states and restricting to Hermitian GFT interactions, we derive the corresponding effective cosmological dynamics and analyse its late-time behaviour. We show that the system approaches an asymptotic de Sitter regime, dynamically dominated by a single condensate mode, while the approach to this regime can exhibit a non-trivial dynamical dark-energy evolution. In particular, both the condensate phase and subdominant modes can generate deviations from the asymptotic equation of state $w=-1$, including phantom behaviour and, under suitable conditions, a crossing of the phantom divide. The additional scalar matter field enters directly into these conditions, providing a link between the matter content and the quantum gravity induced dark-energy dynamics. These results extend the robustness and generality of the GFT mechanism for dynamical dark energy and provide a basis for confronting the underlying quantum gravity dynamics with cosmological observations.

</details>


### [284] [Horizon, Null-Orbit, and Photon-Sphere Phase Structures in Logarithmic Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34957)
*Peshwaz Abdulkareem Abdoul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在LNED框架下研究静态带电渐近平坦黑洞的(Q,ε)相空间，区分视界几何、背景零测地圆轨道与有效光学几何光子圆轨道三种相结构，并揭示黑洞点、光子球及无事件视界结构的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非线性电动力学(LNED)中黑洞解的相结构，厘清视界几何与光学几何在临界参数空间中的异同，特别是零半径边界、极值曲线与合并曲线的拓扑关系，以及黑洞点与光子球的存续问题。

Method: 在(Q,ε)参数空间中对静态带电渐近平坦黑洞解进行解析与数值分析，比较视界几何、背景圆零轨道和有效光学几何圆光子轨道的临界曲线；通过有效势V_eff^bg(r)与V_eff^ph(r)分析径向/非径向测地线行为及稳定性。

Result: 三种结构共享零半径边界Q_nc(ε;M)=γM^{2/3}ε^{1/6}，但临界结构不同：极值曲线Q_ext与合并曲线Q_m均终止于分叉点(Q_b,ε_b)，其中Q_b=9M/[2Γ^2(1/4)]、ε_b=128π^2Q_b^2，而有效光子轨道合并曲线Q_m^ph不经过该点。ε≥ε_b时逼近Q_nc使视界收缩为零形成黑洞点，熵S_η(r_h→0)=η；温度在分叉点为零、ε>ε_b时发散(除ηδ=1)。背景势V_eff^bg呈无限势垒、无局域极值，为完美反射体；光子势V_eff^ph在有限半径处正则化并有极大值，为部分反射体，故黑洞点保留有限半径光子球。ε<ε_b时两几何在Q_ext之上均支持无视界结构；ε≥ε_b时仅光学几何在Q_nc<Q<Q_m^ph区间支持。

Conclusion: 黑洞的视界几何与光学几何具有共享的零半径边界但不同的临界结构，黑洞点在ε≥ε_b时仍保留光子球；临界碰撞参数同时编码引力与光学几何效应，可用于探测不同相结构。

Abstract: We investigate static, charged, asymptotically flat black holes in LNED in the $(Q,ε)$ parameter space. We distinguish three phase structures: the horizon geometry, the background circular null orbits, and the circular photon orbits of the effective optical geometry. All share a zero-radius boundary $Q_{\rm nc}(ε;M)=γM^{2/3}ε^{1/6}$, but have distinct critical structures: the extremal curve $Q_{\rm ext}(ε;M)$ and the merger curve $Q_{\rm m}(ε;M)$ both terminate at the bifurcation point $(Q_{\rm b},ε_{\rm b})$, with $Q_{\rm b}=9M/[2Γ^2(1/4)]$ and $ε_{\rm b}=128π^2Q_{\rm b}^2$, whereas the effective photon-orbit merger curve $Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}(ε;M)$ does not pass through this point.
  For $ε\geε_{\rm b}$, approaching $Q_{\rm nc}$ shrinks the horizon to zero and produces black points with quantum-corrected entropy $S_η(r_{\rm h}\to0)=η$. Their temperature vanishes at the bifurcation point and diverges for $ε>ε_{\rm b}$, except at $ηδ=1$. The background potential $V_{\rm eff}^{\rm bg}(r)$ develops an infinite barrier at $r=0$ without local extrema, acting as a perfect repeller. The photon potential $V_{\rm eff}^{\rm ph}(r)$ is regularized with a maximum at finite radius, acting as a partial reflector. Thus the black points retain a photon sphere at finite radius.
  Away from $Q_{\rm nc}$, for $ε<ε_{\rm b}$ both geometries support horizonless structures above $Q_{\rm ext}$; for $ε\geε_{\rm b}$ only the optical geometry does so in $Q_{\rm nc}<Q<Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}$. Non-radial geodesics are blocked from the singularity and can form metastable bound states, while radial geodesics either reach $r=0$ or escape to infinity. The critical impact parameter encodes both gravitational and optical-geometry effects, probing the distinct phase structures.

</details>


### [285] [A Cuscuton Representation of the Loop Quantum Cosmology Bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35222)
*Niayesh Afshordi,Kristina Giesel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文表明标准平坦FLRW的LQC反弹背景动力学（修正Friedmann方程 H²=ρ(1-ρ/ρ_c)/(3M_p²)）可由局部cuscuton有效理论实现，其标量方程为约束而非波动方程。通过将cuscuton表示为与LQC聚合角2λb对应的角度θ，其约束给出H∝sinθ，Einstein约束给出ρ=ρ_c sin²(θ/2)，精确重现LQC反弹，从而给出该背景动力学的首个闭式、局部、广义协变实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LQC用反弹取代均匀宇宙的大爆炸奇点，其修正Friedmann方程虽有明确背景行为，但缺乏闭式、局部、广义协变的场论实现；本文旨在寻找能精确重现该背景动力学的有效理论描述。

Method: 采用局部cuscuton有效理论（标量方程为约束而非波动方程），将cuscuton写成角度坐标θ并等同于LQC聚合角2λb；利用cuscuton约束得H∝sinθ、Einstein约束得ρ=ρ_c sin²(θ/2)；并通过分支Legendre变换建立clock-gauge固定均匀mimetic系统（mimetic尘埃能量密度为零）与cuscuton系统的正则等价，选出常张力cuscuton坐标Θ=sinθ−θ；同时给出角作用量的分支f(K)表示（K为空间超曲面平均外曲率）。

Result: cuscuton有效理论精确重现标准平坦FLRW LQC反弹背景动力学，适用于满足零能量条件的极小耦合物质，且不引入额外局部动力学自由度；分支Legendre变换给出mimetic系统与cuscuton系统的正则等价，选定Θ=sinθ−θ而θ保留聚合角解释；角作用量存在显式分支f(K)表示。

Conclusion: 该构造是LQC holonomy修正的有效协变表示，而非来自完整圈量子引力的推导；正则等价不保证非均匀理论或其扰动的吻合。

Abstract: Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) replaces the big bang singularity of the homogeneous universe by a bounce, usually described by the modified Friedmann equation $H^2=ρ(1-ρ/ρ_c)/(3M_p^2)$. We show that this background dynamics follows from a local cuscuton effective theory, whose scalar equation is a constraint rather than a wave equation. One way to establish this is to write the cuscuton in terms of an angular coordinate $θ$, identified with the LQC polymerization angle $2λb$. Its constraint gives $H\propto\sinθ$, while the Einstein constraint gives $ρ=ρ_c\sin^2(θ/2)$, exactly reproducing the LQC bounce. To our knowledge, this is the first closed-form, local, generally covariant realization of the exact standard flat-FLRW LQC background dynamics for minimally coupled matter satisfying null energy condition, without introducing additional local dynamical degrees of freedom. A branchwise Legendre transformation establishes a canonical equivalence between the clock-gauge-fixed homogeneous mimetic system in the case of vanishing mimetic dust energy density and the cuscuton systems. It selects the constant-tension cuscuton coordinate $Θ=\sinθ-θ$, while $θ$ retains its interpretation as the LQC polymerization angle. This equivalence does not establish agreement of the inhomogeneous theories or their perturbations. The angular action also admits an explicit branched $f(K)$ representation, where $K$ is the mean extrinsic curvature of spatial hypersurfaces. The construction is therefore an effective covariant representation of the LQC holonomy correction, not a derivation from full loop quantum gravity.

</details>


### [286] [Kasner critical scaling in Einstein--Maxwell--scalar black holes with nonlinear collapse and scalarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35500)
*Chihang He,Chao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明渐近平坦标量化黑洞 Kasner 参数的临界标度指数并非普适的 1/2，而是可通过调节高阶项实现 1/(2m)（m≥1 整数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前数值工作观察到 Kasner 参数临界指数为 1/2 并推测其具有普适性，本文旨在检验该普适性猜想。

Method: 在静态球对称 Einstein--Maxwell--scalar 框架下建立严格数学框架，在固定二次耦合参数 μ 下调节高阶项构造耦合，实现指数 1/(2m)。

Result: 非线性内部坍缩产生类空 Kasner 奇点，β_K ∝ ε^{-1}；外部支满足 q/q_*-1 ∝ ε^{2m}，从而给出 β_K ∝ |q/q_*-1|^{-1/(2m)}，并显式确定前因子。

Conclusion: 该指数不具普适性，1/2 律仅为一个特例，存在包含 1/4、1/6 等的指数层级。

Abstract: A critical scaling exponent of $1/2$ was numerically observed for the Kasner parameter of asymptotically flat scalarized black holes and conjectured to be universal [Phys.\ Rev.\ Lett.\ \textbf{136}, 251402 (2026)]. In the same static, spherically symmetric Einstein--Maxwell--scalar setting, we show that this exponent is not universal. We develop a rigorous mathematical framework and construct couplings realizing exponents $1/(2m)$ for any integer $m\ge1$ by tuning higher-order terms at fixed quadratic coupling parameter $μ$. Nonlinear interior collapse produces a spacelike Kasner singularity with $β_{\rm K}\propto\varepsilon^{-1}$, where $\varepsilon$ is the value of scalar field at the event horizon. An exterior branch satisfying $q/q_\star-1\propto\varepsilon^{2m}$, therefore, yields $β_{\rm K}\propto|q/q_\star-1|^{-1/(2m)}$, where $q$ is the charge-to-mass ratio and $q_\star$ its critical value. We also determine the prefactor explicitly. This recovers the $1/2$ law and establishes a hierarchy including $1/4$ and $1/6$.

</details>


### [287] [From Density to Mass: A New Parametrized Framework for Dark Matter Environment Around Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35771)
*Rajes Ghosh,Nicholas Speeney,Avijit Chowdhury,Chiranjeeb Singha,Miguel A. S. Pinto,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出以"包围质量函数"直接参数化暗物质包裹黑洞时空的通用框架，系统研究了环境对QNM、TLN及EMRI引力波通量的印记，发现三者对环境结构具有互补敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理黑洞并非孤立真空天体，而是处于暗物质晕与重子分布等复杂环境中，这些环境会改变黑洞周围时空几何并在引力波信号中留下可观测印记。传统基于密度的暗物质轮廓参数化方法难以直接与引力场和物理一致性要求衔接，因此需要一个更自然、物理可控的框架来连接环境模型与精密引力波观测。

Method: 以包围质量函数（enclosed mass function）而非传统密度轮廓直接参数化DM包裹黑洞的时空几何，构建通用框架。该表述与总引力场自然关联，可将多种常见晕轮廓作为极限情形纳入，并满足正则性、因果性及渐近行为等基本物理一致性要求。基于此框架，计算环境对QNM谱、潮汐Love数（TLN）以及EMRI引力波通量的影响。

Result: 发现对环境结构的互补敏感性：QNM主要探测环境的内区结构，静态TLN对环境中外层结构更敏感，而EMRI通量则通过影响轨道运动与引力波传播来探测物质分布。三种观测量从不同径向尺度反映晕结构。

Conclusion: 包围质量表述被确立为一个灵活且物理可控的框架，可将天体物理环境模型与精密引力波观测量相连接，为利用引力波探测黑洞周围环境结构提供了统一、系统的理论基础。

Abstract: Astrophysical black holes (BHs) are not isolated vacuum objects but are expected to reside within complex environments, such as dark matter (DM) halos and baryonic distributions. Such environments can modify the spacetime geometry surrounding the BH and leave observable imprints on gravitational-wave (GW) signals, including shifts in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum, tidal Love numbers (TLNs), and the inspiral dynamics of extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs). We develop a general framework for describing DM-dressed BH spacetimes by parameterizing the geometry directly in terms of the enclosed mass function, rather than the traditional density-based profile. This mass-based formulation is naturally connected to the total gravitational field, accommodates several common halo profiles as limiting cases, and incorporates basic physical consistency requirements such as regularity, causality, and appropriate asymptotic behavior. Within this framework, we investigate the environmental imprints on QNMs, TLNs, and GW fluxes from EMRIs. We find a complementary sensitivity to the halo structure: QNMs predominantly probe the inner regions of the environment, whereas static TLNs are more sensitive to its outer structure. EMRI fluxes, in contrast, probe the matter distribution through its influence on the orbital motion and GW propagation. Our results establish the enclosed-mass formulation as a flexible and physically controlled framework for connecting astrophysical environment models with precision GW observables.

</details>


### [288] [Analytic detector responses and entanglement in constant-curvature spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29992)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya,Jiayue Yang,Ming Zhang,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文解析推导了与实无质量共形耦合标量场耦合的 Unruh–DeWitt 探测器的响应函数，以区分场谱、整体几何与相互作用协议（开关）效应。针对 Minkowski、AdS 和 dS 时空中的高斯开关静态探测器对，给出了领头阶激发概率与双探测器相干性；并在三维 BTZ 黑洞中分析径向自由下落探测器的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unruh–DeWitt 探测器的响应同时受场谱、时空整体几何与探测器相互作用协议（开关方式）影响，三者效应混杂，难以厘清各因素对激发与纠缠的独立贡献。因此需要一个能将这些效应分离的解析框架。

Method: 采用解析方法推导实、无质量、共形耦合标量场下 Unruh–DeWitt 探测器的响应函数，时空维数取 D=d+1≥3。对 Minkowski、AdS、dS 中的高斯开关静态探测器对计算领头阶激发概率及双探测器相干性；利用 KMS 细致平衡分析 dS 中的间隙依赖；对三维 BTZ 黑洞采用积分 Legendre 级数处理尖锐开关的径向自由下落探测器，并用均匀镜像求和方法估计奇点附近行为。

Result: 时序相干要求两条空间世界线不同，而局域响应与单激发相干允许重合。等红移对的条件下高斯开关可分离时序相干的间隙依赖。dS 中 KMS 细致平衡确定了使非局域相干与局域激发之比最大的间隙，给出检验领头阶纠缠存在的单间隙判据，并将可能产生纠缠的间隙限制在一个有界区间内；存在纠缠时，concurrence 峰值出现在更小的正间隙处。BTZ 中镜像求和分析确立了奇点附近的对数增长并给出由覆盖 AdS 响应确定的系数；足够光滑的单调开关消除了有限时间起始的瑕疵，但保留正的对数系数，累积领头阶响应仍有限。独立数值计算验证了解析表达式及其收敛性质与上述物理区分。

Conclusion: 该工作通过解析响应函数成功分离了场谱、整体几何与开关协议对 Unruh–DeWitt 探测器的影响，为在各向异性宇宙学之外的弯曲时空中区分本征谱效应与实际相互作用效应提供了可操作判据，尤其给出了 dS 中领头阶纠缠存在的单间隙检验，并阐明了 BTZ 奇点附近响应的对数结构与开关光滑性的关系。

Abstract: We derive analytic response functions for Unruh--DeWitt detectors coupled to real, massless, conformally coupled scalar fields, to distinguish the effects of the field spectrum and global geometry from those of the interaction protocol. For Gaussian-switched static pairs in Minkowski, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter spacetimes, we obtain the leading-order excitation probabilities and two-detector coherences in spacetime dimension $\mathcal D=d+1\ge3$. Time-ordered coherence requires distinct spatial worldlines; the local response and single-excitation coherence also admit coincidence. For equal-redshift pairs, Gaussian switching separates the gap dependence of the time-ordered coherence. In de Sitter space, KMS detailed balance fixes the gap that maximizes the ratio of nonlocal coherence to local excitation, giving a single-gap test for the existence of leading-order entanglement and restricting any entangling gaps to one bounded interval. When entanglement is present, the concurrence itself peaks at a smaller positive gap. In the three-dimensional BTZ black hole, an integrated Legendre series gives the sharply switched response of a radially infalling detector. A uniform image-sum estimate establishes logarithmic growth near the singularity and determines its coefficient from the covering-AdS response. A sufficiently smooth monotone onset removes the finite-time onset glitches but leaves a positive logarithmic coefficient; the accumulated leading-order response remains finite. Independent numerical calculations test the analytic expressions, their convergence properties, and these physical distinctions.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [Inequivalent modular tensor categories with identical $S$, $T$ and $W$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33231)
*Ran Luo,Jiahua Tian*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文否定地回答了"(S,T,W)及二分支链不变量能否决定模张量范畴"的公开问题：构造出在单一单对象双射下具有相同(S,T,W)及所有至多二分量框架定向链RT不变量的两个编织不等价Dijkgraaf–Witten模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模数据(S,T)与Whitehead-link矩阵W是否在ribbon等价意义下决定模张量范畴，是长期未决的公开问题；同时需厘清"着色框架链不变量（尤其至多二分量）"作为范畴分类不变量的完备性边界。

Method: 对群(ℤ/pℤ)^3（素数p≥5），固定二次上同调类、变动脉冲交替3-余圈构造两个Dijkgraaf–Witten模范畴；利用限制到秩≤2子群来匹配链不变量，并联合二次类与交替类阻碍编织等价。

Result: 两个范畴编织不等价，却在单一单对象双射下具有相同的(S,T,W)及所有至多二分量框架定向链的Reshetikhin–Turaev不变量；并解析计算了一个闭定向三维流形上的无着色配分函数，可分离该族中全部(p−1)/2个等价类。

Conclusion: 模张量范畴的ribbon等价分类需要超出于"至多二分量着色框架链不变量"的不变量；本文的反例给出明确的完备性下界。

Abstract: It remains an open question whether the modular data $(S,T)$ together with the Whitehead-link matrix $W$ determine a modular tensor category up to ribbon equivalence. We answer negatively by constructing two braided-inequivalent Dijkgraaf--Witten modular categories for $(\mathbb Z/p\mathbb Z)^3$ for prime $p\ge5$ with identical $(S,T,W)$ and, more generally, identical Reshetikhin--Turaev invariants of all framed oriented links with at most two components under a single bijection of simple objects. The construction varies an alternating $3$-cocycle while fixing a quadratic cohomology class, using restriction to subgroups of rank at most two to match link invariants and the quadratic and alternating classes together to obstruct braided equivalence. A complete classification of modular tensor categories up to ribbon equivalence therefore requires invariants beyond those of colored framed links with at most two components. We also analytically evaluate an uncolored partition function on a closed oriented three-manifold that separates all $(p-1)/2$ equivalence classes in the family.

</details>


### [290] [Grothendieck rings of pivotal fusion categories of rank four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33272)
*Jingcheng Dong,Sebastien Palcoux,Arnaud Plessis*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对复数域上秩为四的pivotal融合范畴的Grothendieck环进行分类，共得15个，均可酉范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秩为四的pivotal融合范畴Grothendieck环此前缺乏完整分类，本文旨在补全这一小秩情形。

Method: 利用中心诱导与Frobenius-Schur指示子给出统一的Frobenius-Perron维数上界3600，并结合新的算术与twist障碍、穷举普查以及精确排除证书完成有限分类。

Result: 恰有15个Grothendieck环，每个都存在酉范畴化。

Conclusion: 秩为四的pivotal融合范畴Grothendieck环分类完成，结果有限且均可酉实现。

Abstract: We classify the Grothendieck rings of pivotal fusion categories of rank four over the complex numbers. There are exactly fifteen, each admitting a unitary categorification. Central induction and Frobenius-Schur indicators give a uniform Frobenius-Perron dimension bound of 3600. The finite classification combines new arithmetic and twist obstructions with an exhaustive census and exact exclusion certificates.

</details>


### [291] [Classical freeness of affine vertex algebras of $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ at boundary admissible levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33348)
*Tomoyuki Arakawa,Xuanzhong Dai*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 证明了简单仿射顶点代数 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 在边界可容许水平处是经典自由的，从而验证了 Andrews、van Ekeren 与 Heluani 提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射顶点代数的经典自由性是理解其结构（如模、自由场实现及与顶点泊松代数关系）的关键性质。Andrews、van Ekeren 与 Heluani 此前提出猜想，认为 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 在边界可容许水平处具有经典自由性，但该情形此前未被证明。

Method: 针对 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 在边界可容许水平（即 $k = -2 + \frac{2}{n}$ 型水平）取下简单商 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$，通过构造与分析其关联分次（associated graded）结构，证明其在相应顶点泊松代数意义下经典自由。

Result: 证明 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 在边界可容许水平处为经典自由，即 Andrews–van Ekeren–Heluani 猜想成立。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 仿射顶点代数在边界可容许水平下的经典自由性，补充了该猜想的证明，并为相关顶点代数结构研究提供支持。

Abstract: We prove that the simple affine vertex algebra $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ is classically free at the boundary admissible level, which confirms a conjecture of Andrews, van Ekeren, and Heluani.

</details>


### [292] [Idempotents in the Temperley-Lieb Monoid and Other Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34282)
*Louis H Kauffman*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究由恒等态射分解产生的代数与范畴中的幂等元，将其与广义meander（平面中横穿直线的曲线）对应，并通过平面曲线组合学刻画了Temperley-Lieb幺半群、Brauer幺半群以及Tangle幺半群与Tangle范畴中的幂等元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒等态射的分解在代数与范畴中自然产生幂等元，而meander提供了直观的组合几何模型。作者希望利用平面曲线组合学统一理解不同幺半群（Temperley-Lieb、Brauer、Tangle）中的幂等元结构。

Method: 以平面曲线组合学表述Temperley-Lieb幺半群的代数结构，将恒等态射分解对应为meander及其推广；随后将相应方法推广至Brauer幺半群、Tangle幺半群与Tangle范畴。

Result: 在Temperley-Lieb幺半群中，幂等元可由meander刻画；对Brauer幺半群、Tangle幺半群及Tangle范畴给出了相应的结果。

Conclusion: meander及其推广为研究由恒等态射分解产生的幂等元提供了有效的组合框架，并可推广到多种幺半群与范畴结构。

Abstract: This paper examines idempotents in algebras and categories that arise from factorizations of the identity morphism. In the diagrammatic and combinatorial contexts considered here, these factorizations correspond to generalizations of meanders, where a meander is understood to be a curve in the plane that wanders transversely back and forth across a given straight line. By formulating the algebra of the Temperley-Lieb Monoid in terms of planar curve combinatorics, one can understand idempotents in the Temperley-Lieb Monoid in terms of meanders. Corresponding results are shown for the Brauer Monoid and for the Tangle Monoid and Tangle Category.

</details>


### [293] [Isomorphism between the R-matrix and Drinfeld presentations of quantum affine superalgebra for type $\boldsymbol{A}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35067)
*Pengfa Xu,Hongda Lin,Honglian Zhang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子仿射超代数 $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$ 的 Drinfeld 表示与 R-矩阵表示之间的显式同构，并利用量子 Berezinian 构造了 $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}(m|n))}$ 的 R-矩阵表示及其与 Drinfeld 表示的同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frenkel-Ding 曾提出量子仿射代数 $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}(n)})$ 两种表示之间的同构；本文旨在给出该结果的超对称推广，并进一步处理 $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$ 情形。

Method: 通过显式构造建立 $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}(m|n))}$ 的 Drinfeld 表示与 R-矩阵表示之间的同构；利用量子 Berezinian、Gaussian 生成元之间的交换关系以及 $\mathbb{A}$-形式，构造 $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}(m|n))}$ 的 R-矩阵表示并证明其与 Drinfeld 表示的同构。

Result: 证明了 $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}(m|n))}$ 的 Drinfeld 表示与 R-矩阵表示同构；构造了 $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}(m|n))}$ 的 R-矩阵表示，并证明其与 Drinfeld 表示同构。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Frenkel-Ding 的同构结果推广到超对称情形，为量子仿射超代数的两种表示提供了统一的同构框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an explicit isomorphism between Drinfeld and R-matrix presentations of the quantum affine superalgebra associated with the general linear Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{gl}(m|n)$. This result can be viewed as a supersymmetric analogue of the isomorphism between two presentations of the quantum affine algebra $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}(n)})$ proposed by Frenkel-Ding. Furthermore, employing the quantum Berezinian of $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}(m|n))}$, we construct the R-matrix presentation of the quantum affine superalgebra $U_{q}(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}(m|n))}$ and prove the isomorphism between its R-matrix and Drinfeld presentations. Our proof based on the commutative relation between Gaussian generators, $\mathbb{A}$-form and quantum Berezinian.

</details>


### [294] [The dimension block of a spherical fusion category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35287)
*Andrew Schopieray*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 证明球融合范畴全局维度D的Galois共轭维数特征界定S矩阵的维数块，并给出扭曲和的上界，由此分类小于√5的全局维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球融合范畴的全局维度分类是重要问题，已有结果只覆盖到 4√3/5，需拓展到更大范围。

Method: 研究Drinfeld中心S矩阵的维数块，利用Galois共轭维数特征，对特征χ证明扭曲和∑σ χ(σ)/σ(D)的绝对值上界为 e^{-1}𝔣(χ)^{-1/2}，其中𝔣(χ)为导子，e为维度分次群阶。

Result: 得到扭曲和上界 e^{-1}𝔣(χ)^{-1/2}，并据此确定了小于√5的所有可能全局维度，将已知分类从 4√3/5 扩展至 √5。

Conclusion: 该上界为球融合范畴全局维度分类提供了有效工具，显著扩大了可分类的维度范围。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{C}$ be a spherical fusion category with global dimension $D$. The Galois conjugates of the dimension character index a block of the $S$-matrix of the Drinfeld center, which we call the dimension block. We prove that for every character $χ$ of $\mathrm{Gal}(\mathbb{Q}(D)/\mathbb{Q})$, the twisted sum $\sum_σχ(σ)/σ(D)$ has absolute value at most $e^{-1}\mathfrak{f}(χ)^{-1/2}$, where $\mathfrak{f}(χ)$ is the conductor of $χ$ and $e$ is the order of the dimensional grading group of $\mathcal{C}$. As an application, we determine the possible global dimensions of spherical fusion categories below $\sqrt{5}$, extending the known classification up to $4\sqrt{3}/5$ of V.\ Ostrik and P.\ Etingof.

</details>


<div id='math.SG'></div>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [A Smooth Hamiltonian Diffeomorphism with Two Fixed Points on $S^2\times S^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33626)
*Hao Jiao*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文在等面积辛积 $S^2\times S^2$ 上构造了一个仅有两个不动点的哈密顿微分同胚，从而给出 Arnold 关于退化 Arnold 猜想在标准 $S^2\times S^2$ 情形下的反例。工作流程为：从四不动点映射出发，转入反对角附近的缩放余切坐标，将局部不动点方程化为两个平面鞍点问题，再通过相对修改合并鞍点对，并用混合生成函数将修改转移回原映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arnold 曾提出退化版本的 Arnold 猜想，其在标准 $S^2\times S^2$ 上的正确性此前未被否证；本文旨在构造反例。作者还说明本工作与 ChatGPT 合作完成，并由其给出研究 $\\Phi_\\rho$ 极限行为这一核心思路。

Method: 1) 先构造具四个不动点的映射；2) 过渡到反对角附近的缩放余切坐标；3) 将局部不动点方程约化为两个平面鞍点问题；4) 用相对修改合并每对鞍点；5) 通过混合生成函数将修改转移至原映射。文中详述了沿水平弧的法形式、Moser 方程、混合生成函数的一致存在性、参数选择次序以及全局图像论证。

Result: 成功构造出等面积辛积 $S^2\times S^2$ 上仅有两个不动点的哈密顿微分同胚，得到退化 Arnold 猜想在标准 $S^2\times S^2$ 上的反例。作者提到 GPT 提出了采用第二生成函数构造该哈密顿系统的关键想法，且大部分计算由 GPT 完成。

Conclusion: 该构造否定了 Arnold 提出的退化 Arnold 猜想在标准 $S^2\times S^2$ 上的成立性，为辛拓扑中不动点下界问题提供了重要反例；同时也展示了人工智能辅助数学研究中思路提出与计算执行层面的实质性参与。

Abstract: We give a construction of a Hamiltonian diffeomorphism of the equal-area symplectic product $S^2\times S^2$ with two fixed points. The argument starts with a four-fixed-point map, passes to rescaled cotangent coordinates near the antidiagonal, and reduces the local fixed-point equations to two planar saddle problems. A relative modification merges each pair of saddles, and mixed generating functions are used to transfer the modification to the original map. We include detailed explanations of the normal form along a level arc, the Moser equation, uniform existence of mixed generating functions, the order of parameter choices, and the global graph argument. This construction gives a counterexample to the degenerate version of Arnold's conjecture proposed by Arnold\cite{Arnold86} when the manifold is standard $S^2\times S^2$.
  This work is done with ChatGPT. The author gave the idea to study the limit behavior of $Φ_ρ$. GPT suggested a key idea of using second generating function to construct this Hamiltonian. Also, most of the computation is done by GPT.

</details>


### [296] [Braid Stability and a Floer theory of Braid Isotopies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34961)
*Nicolas Grunder*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 该论文在闭合辛曲面上哈密顿流的周期轨道编织空间上建立Floer理论，用编织的Floer同伦（两两不交的Floer柱元组）定义微分与连续映射；由此证明定量编织稳定性，并应用于torus上给定拓扑熵上界集合在Hofer度量下无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
