Motivation: 作者希望在编织（braids）这一结构上引入Floer理论，从而以新的视角研究哈密顿系统周期轨道的编织在扰动下的持续性，并获得关于拓扑熵水平集Hofer几何的定量结论。

Method: 在闭合辛曲面上哈密顿流周期轨道的编织空间上构造Floer理论：通过计数编织的Floer同伦——即两两图不相交的Floer柱（Floer cylinders）元组——定义Floer微分与连续映射；据此建立定量编织稳定性结果，并仅利用可缩周期轨道证明torus上的应用。

Result: 证明了在可能较大的哈密顿扰动下编织的持续性（定量编织稳定性）；作为应用，对任意α≥0构造torus T²上一列哈密顿微分同胚φ_k，使得 d_H(Ent_{≤α}(T²,ω), φ_k) → ∞（k→∞），即拓扑熵不超过α的哈密顿微分同胚集合在Hofer度量下无界；该结论仅通过可缩周期轨道得到，而此前类似的高亏格情形使用的是非可缩轨道。

Conclusion: 编织上的Floer理论为哈密顿周期轨道的稳定性提供了有效工具，并给出Hofer度量下拓扑熵水平集无界性的新证明，且论证只需可缩周期轨道，区别于以往依赖非可缩轨道的高亏格结果。

Abstract: We build a Floer theory on the space of braids of periodic orbits of a Hamiltonian flow on a closed symplectic surface. The differential and continuation maps are defined by counting Floer isotopies of braids: tuples of Floer cylinders with pairwise disjoint graphs.
  With this new perspective, we prove a quantitative braid stability result that shows the persistence of braids under possibly large Hamiltonian perturbations. As an application, we show that for every $α\geq 0$ there is a sequence of Hamiltonian diffeomorphisms $φ_k$ on the two-torus $T^2$ such that $$d_H(\operatorname{Ent}_{\leq α}(T^2,ω),φ_k)\to \infty \quad (k \to \infty),$$ where $\text{Ent}_{\leq α}(T^2,ω)\subset \text{Ham}(T^2,ω)$ denotes the set of Hamiltonian diffeomorphisms with topological entropy at most $α$ and $d_H$ the Hofer metric. We prove this result by studying only contractible periodic orbits, whereas analogous higher-genus statements were previously obtained using non-contractible orbits.

</details>


### [297] [Non-decomposable cobordisms via Lagrangian moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35048)
*Roberta Guadagni*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 利用Lagrangian图变换构造Legendrian纽结的Lagrangian配边，并在添加稳定化后反转可分解配边，进而给出从稳定化的m(9_46)到稳定化unknot的显式Lagrangian concordance。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Lagrangian配边（特别是concordance）的构造与可分解性，旨在理解Legendrian纽结之间的配边关系，并刻画非可分解配边的存在性。

Method: 建立Lagrangian图变换（Lagrangian diagram moves）作为构造Lagrangian配边的工具；结合Reidemeister变换与Lagrangian投影中的把柄附着（handle attachments），在添加若干稳定化后实现可分解配边的反转。

Result: 通过上述变换实现了可分解配边在稳定化意义下的反转；作为应用，显式构造了从稳定化的m(9_46) Legendrian纽结到稳定化unknot的Lagrangian concordance。

Conclusion: 所得concordance是非空纽结之间的非可分解Lagrangian concordance，说明该方法能有效产生新的非可分解配边实例。

Abstract: We establish the use of Lagrangian diagram moves to build Lagrangian cobordisms of Legendrian knots. We then apply the moves to explore how to invert decomposable cobordisms, after adding some stabilizations. This is done by use of Reidemeister moves and handle attachments in the Lagrangian projection. As an application, we show an explicit construction of a Lagrangian concordance from a stabilized $m(9_{46})$ Legendrian knot to a stabilized unknot, i.e. a non-decomposable Lagrangian concordance of nonempty knots.

</details>


### [298] [Quantitative symplectic topology of Katok's examples and equivariant symplectic embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35209)
*Nikolas Adaloglou,Johannes Hauber*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 该文研究了由标准旋转Killing场扰动圆球面度量得到的Randers度量族$F_{\alpha,\beta}$，计算了其单位余圆盘丛$D^*(S^2,F^*_{\alpha,\beta})$可辛嵌入到圆球面余圆盘丛$D^*S^2$的参数集合，该集合呈现无限阶梯结构，并建立了余圆盘丛嵌入、奇异$A_1$-椭球嵌入与$\mathbb{Z}_2$-等变椭球嵌入之间的等价字典，同时给出$\mathbb{R}P^2$情形的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katok例子是辛几何与Finsler几何中的重要研究对象，其嵌入问题与椭球嵌入、等变嵌入等经典问题密切相关。作者旨在量化Katok型Randers度量余圆盘丛嵌入圆球面余圆盘丛的精确参数区域，并揭示不同嵌入问题之间的统一联系。

Method: 以圆球面度量经标准旋转Killing场扰动构造双参数Randers度量族$F_{\alpha,\beta}$，通过辛嵌入判据计算使$D^*(S^2,F^*_{\alpha,\beta})$辛嵌入到$D^*S^2$的参数集，并建立其与奇异$A_1$-椭球嵌入及$\mathbb{Z}_2$-等变椭球嵌入的一一对应字典。

Result: 确定了所有$(\alpha,\beta)\in(0,2+\sqrt{3})^2$中满足辛嵌入条件的参数集合，该集合具有无限阶梯（infinite staircase）结构；证明了余圆盘丛嵌入、奇异$A_1$-椭球嵌入与$\mathbb{Z}_2$-等变椭球嵌入三种问题相互等价；并得到了$\mathbb{R}P^2$情形的类似结论。

Conclusion: Katok型Randers度量的余圆盘丛嵌入问题可通过无限阶梯结构精确刻画，并与奇异$A_1$-椭球嵌入及$\mathbb{Z}_2$-等变椭球嵌入等价，该字典可推广到更广的例子族，为辛嵌入问题提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The Katok examples on $S^2$ are induced by Randers metrics obtained by perturbing the round metric by the standard rotational Killing field: allowing a scaling factor of the round metric gives a two-parameter family of Randers metrics $F_{α,β}$, parametrised by positive real numbers $(α,β)$. We compute the set of all $(α,β)\in (0,2+\sqrt{3})^2$ for which $D^*(S^2,F^*_{α,β})$, the unit codisc bundle with respect to $F^*_{α,β}$, symplectically embeds into the round codisc bundle $D^*S^2$. This set has the structure of an infinite staircase. We also establish a dictionary between embeddings of these codisc bundles, singular $A_1$-ellipsoid embeddings, and $\mathbb{Z} _2$-equivariant ellipsoid embeddings, showing that these embedding problems are equivalent. Furthermore, we prove the analogous results for the $\mathbb{R} P^2$ case and discuss a broader family of examples for which this dictionary applies.

</details>


### [299] [Orbifold Mapping Spaces via Bibundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35435)
*Yoshihiro Sugimoto*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文用双丛（Hilsum-Skandalis 态射）以更几何的方式在轨形间的映射空间上构造无穷维轨形结构，给出表示该映射空间的恰当étale（Fréchet）Lie群胚，并证明其Morita等价类与群胚表示选取无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（W. Chen 及 Chen-Du-Liao）建立了轨形间映射空间上轨形结构的存在性，但构造方式不够几何、缺少具体的群胚框架。本文希望基于 Lerman 对轨形态射的描述，用双丛给出更几何的构造，从而为映射空间与模空间研究以及辛轨形上的 Floer 理论提供具体框架。

Method: 基于 Lerman 对轨形态射的描述，使用双丛（或称 Hilsum-Skandalis 态射）来构造映射空间的无穷维轨形结构；在 $C^k$、$C^\infty$ 和 $W^{k,p}$ 三种函数空间中构造表示映射空间的恰当étale（或 Fréchet）Lie 群胚，并对其 Morita 等价类进行独立性验证。

Result: 在 $C^k$、$C^\infty$、$W^{k,p}$ 设定下成功构造出表示映射空间的恰当étale（Fréchet）Lie 群胚；证明其 Morita 等价类不依赖于群胚表示的选取；并证明局部上映射群胚同构于有限群的 action groupoid，该有限群正好对应相应双丛的自同构群。

Conclusion: 该构造为轨形上的映射空间与模空间的进一步研究提供了具体的几何框架，并可应用于辛轨形上的 Floer 理论。

Abstract: The existence of orbifold structures on mapping spaces between orbifolds was established in previous work, notably by W. Chen and B. Chen-Du-Liao. In this paper, we construct an infinite-dimensional orbifold structure on the mapping space in a more geometric way, using bibundles (or Hilsum-Skandalis morphisms), based on Lerman's description of orbifold morphisms. We construct a proper étale (or Fréchet) Lie groupoid representing the mapping space in the $C^k$, $C^\infty$, and $W^{k,p}$ settings, and show that its Morita equivalence class is independent of the choices of groupoid presentations. Locally, the mapping groupoid is isomorphic to the action groupoid of a finite group, where the finite group is identified with the automorphism group of the corresponding bibundle. This provides a concrete geometric framework for further studies of mapping spaces and moduli spaces on orbifolds, with applications to Floer theory on symplectic orbifolds.

</details>


### [300] [Fukaya category with supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35565)
*Mohammed Abouzaid,Yoel Groman,Umut Varolgunes*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文为闭辛流形的紧子集K构造了支撑于K的A∞-范畴Fuk_K，其对象为带上适当装饰的无障碍全局Lagrangian，态射复形通过加速数据的完备望远镜计算，当Lagrangian与K不相交时消失；K=M时恢复经典Fukaya范畴，并证明了弱对合覆盖上的Mayer–Vietoris下降定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Fukaya范畴是整体定义的，无法反映辛流形中特定紧子集的局部信息。作者希望构造一种"局部化"或"支撑化"的Fukaya范畴，使范畴只依赖于所选紧子集K，从而实现局部到整体的粘合（下降）理论。

Method: 对每个闭辛流形M的紧子集K，构造A∞-范畴Fuk_K：对象为带上装饰的无障碍全局Lagrangian；态射复形通过与K的加速数据相关联的完备望远镜来计算；在子集包含下定义限制函子；在Novikov环上、经典横截性方法适用时建立该构造。

Result: 态射复形可被完备望远镜计算，且当Lagrangian与K不相交时复形消失；K=M时Fuk_K恢复通常的无障碍对象Fukaya范畴；范畴对紧子集包含具有限制函子；证明了弱对合覆盖的Mayer–Vietoris下降定理。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了支撑于紧子集的Fukaya范畴及其函子性，统一了局部与整体Fukaya理论，并给出弱对合覆盖上的Mayer–Vietoris下降性质，为辛拓扑的局部化与粘合方法提供了框架。

Abstract: We associate to each compact subset $K$ of a closed symplectic manifold $M$ an $A_\infty$-category $\Fuk_K$, called the Fukaya category with support on $K$. The objects are tautologically unobstructed global Lagrangians endowed with appropriate decorations. The dependence on $K$ is detected by the morphism complexes which can be computed by completed telescopes associated with acceleration data for $K$. In particular, these vanish for Lagrangians disjoint of $K$. For $K=M$ this recovers the usual Fukaya category of tautologically unobstructed objects. These categories admit restriction functors under inclusions of compact subsets. We prove a Mayer--Vietoris descent theorem for weakly involutive covers. The construction is carried out over the Novikov ring and is established whenever classical transversality methods apply.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [Impact of Helicity-Isospin Convention Matching on the Extraction of $α$ from $B^0\to(ρπ)^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31828)
*Radek Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出 $B^0\to(\rho\pi)^0$ 时间依赖 Dalitz 图测量中使用的 $\rho\to\pi\pi$ 螺旋度约定与后续同位旋分析所假设的约定不一致；修正后，直接测定与间接测定 $\alpha$ 的张力从 $2.9\sigma$ 降至 $2.1\sigma$，并给出新的 $\alpha$ 值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用 $B^0\to(\rho\pi)^0$ 衰变的时间依赖 Dalitz 图测量直接确定幺正三角形角 $\alpha$ 时，其结果与全局拟合的间接确定存在约 $2.9\sigma$ 的张力。作者怀疑该张力部分源于 $\rho\to\pi\pi$ 螺旋度约定在测量与同位旋分析之间的不一致。

Method: 对 $B^0\to(\rho\pi)^0$ 时间依赖 Dalitz 图测量中使用的 $\rho\to\pi\pi$ 螺旋度约定进行核查，并与后续同位旋分析所假设的约定进行对比，据此对直接测定结果进行修正，再与 $B\to\pi\pi$、$B\to\rho\rho$ 模式联合分析。

Result: 修正螺旋度约定后，直接测定与间接测定 $\alpha$ 的张力从 $2.9\sigma$ 下降到 $2.1\sigma$。联合 $B\to\pi\pi$ 和 $B\to\rho\rho$ 模式后，直接测定结果为 $\alpha=(87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})^\circ$，而原世界平均值为 $(84.1^{+3.7}_{-3.0})^\circ$。

Conclusion: $\rho\to\pi\pi$ 螺旋度约定的不一致是导致 $\alpha$ 直接测定与间接测定张力偏大的部分原因；修正约定可缓解该张力，并改变直接测定的 $\alpha$ 中心值与误差，提示相关测量需统一约定。

Abstract: We show that the $ρ\toππ$ helicity convention used in the time-dependent Dalitz-plot measurements of $B^0\to(ρπ)^0$ decays does not match the convention assumed in the subsequent isospin analysis. Once corrected, the tension between the direct determination of the unitarity-triangle angle $α$ from these decays and its indirect determination from global fits decreases from $2.9σ$ to $2.1σ$. Combined with the $B\toππ$ and $B\toρρ$ modes, the direct determination becomes $α= (87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})^\circ$, compared with the original world average $(84.1^{+3.7}_{-3.0})^\circ$.

</details>


### [302] [Neutrino Direct Simulation Monte Carlo: Accurate modeling of out-of-equilibrium decaying cosmological relics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31833)
*Kensuke Akita,Miguel Escudero,Oleksii Ihnatenko,Maksym Ovchynnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了中微子直接模拟蒙特卡洛（νDSMC）方法，将衰变遗迹粒子、中微子谱、电磁等离子体、宇宙膨胀与BBN联合演化，验证其与量子动理学/玻尔兹曼方程一致，并证明其在强非热中微子情形下更快且能揭示动量平均近似的定性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeV温度下衰变遗迹注入能量可使中微子偏离热平衡，进而影响宇宙辐射密度与太初轻元素丰度。早期νDSMC仅描述中微子输运，需要更完整的耦合框架与独立验证，以支持对非标准宇宙学的广泛参数扫描。

Method: 扩展νDSMC，联合演化遗迹粒子布居、中微子谱、电磁等离子体和宇宙膨胀，并耦合BBN；纳入遗迹粒子正反衰变、中微子味转换、量子统计以及高能中微子产生π介子等过程。在多种非标准宇宙学场景下，将νDSMC与量子动理学方程和准经典玻尔兹曼方程独立解进行对比。

Result: νDSMC与独立量子动理学/准经典玻尔兹曼解吻合良好；相比后者涵盖更广泛物理过程，且在强非热中微子布居下显著更快，便于大规模参数扫描。当偏离热平衡较大时，对中微子动量取平均的近似会给出定性错误预测，包括有效中微子物种数变化符号相反以及太初核丰度严重错误。

Conclusion: 扩展的νDSMC框架通过追踪中微子热化及其可观测后果，可对新物理进行可靠宇宙学检验，并使非标准宇宙学的广泛稳健参数扫描成为可行。

Abstract: We consider nonstandard cosmologies in which decaying relics inject energy at MeV temperatures, driving neutrinos out of equilibrium. The subsequent neutrino evolution shapes predictions for the cosmic radiation density and primordial light element abundances. Earlier work introduced Neutrino Direct Simulation Monte Carlo ($ν$DSMC), which describes neutrino transport through Monte Carlo sampling of particle interactions. Here, we extend this method by jointly evolving the relic population, neutrino spectra, electromagnetic plasma, and cosmic expansion, and coupling this evolution to Big Bang nucleosynthesis. The framework includes direct and inverse decays of the relic, neutrino flavor conversion, quantum statistics, and pion production by energetic neutrinos. To reveal the physical impact of these effects and independently test $ν$DSMC, we compare several approaches to neutrino evolution across a range of nonstandard cosmological scenarios. We find excellent agreement with independent solutions of quantum kinetic or quasi-classical Boltzmann equations. Compared with these calculations, $ν$DSMC includes a broader range of physical processes and is substantially faster for strongly nonthermal neutrino populations. This makes broad, robust parameter scans for nonstandard cosmologies practical. We show that when departures from thermal equilibrium are large, approximations that average over neutrino momenta can give qualitatively incorrect predictions, including the wrong sign of the change in the effective number of neutrino species and substantially incorrect primordial nuclear abundances. We conclude that the extended $ν$DSMC framework enables reliable cosmological tests of new physics by following neutrino thermalization and its observable consequences.

</details>


### [303] [Disentangling $V_{ud}$ from New Physics in Beta Decays with the FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31834)
*Lemonia Gialidi,Elie Hammou,Kamil Laurent,Vaisakh Plakkot,Juan Rojo,Jordy de Vries*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SMEFiT框架下联合超允许β衰变似然与LEP/LHC数据及HL-LHC、FCC-ee预期，同时确定Vud与SMEFT威尔逊系数。当前对撞机数据与HL-LHC仅部分解除Vud与新物理的简并，而FCC-ee可完全解耦二者，恢复SM级精度并显著收紧对威尔逊系数的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超允许核β衰变虽能最精确测定CKM矩阵元Vud，但其仅对Vud与左手带电流新物理贡献的有效组合敏感，仅凭低能数据无法区分二者，因此需要引入高能对撞机数据来打破简并。

Method: 在SMEFiT框架内，构建包含辐射修正与核结构不确定度的超允许跃迁专用似然，并与LEP、LHC的全球数据以及HL-LHC和FCC-ee的预期数据联合拟合，同时确定Vud与相关SMEFT威尔逊系数。

Result: 当前对撞机数据与HL-LHC预期只能部分解除Vud与新物理效应之间的简并；FCC-ee则可完全解耦Vud与新物理，在恢复SM级精度的同时显著收紧对SMEFT威尔逊系数的约束。该结论对味道假设与理论不确定度的变化具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 结果凸显未来对撞机与低能实验的互补性，为第一行CKM幺正性检验与Cabibbo角反常的SMEFT一致解释开辟了道路。

Abstract: Superallowed nuclear $β$-decays enable the most precise determination of the CKM element $V_{ud}$, but they are only sensitive to an effective combination of $V_{ud}$ and New Physics contributions to left-handed charged currents, which low-energy data alone cannot disentangle. Here we determine $V_{ud}$ simultaneously with the relevant Wilson coefficients of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) by combining, within the SMEFiT framework, a dedicated likelihood for superallowed transitions, including radiative and nuclear-structure uncertainties, with LEP and LHC world data and HL-LHC and FCC-ee projections. Current collider data and HL-LHC projections only partially lift the degeneracy between $V_{ud}$ and possible New Physics effects. Instead, the FCC-ee fully decouples $V_{ud}$ from New Physics, recovering SM-level precision while significantly tightening the constraints on the SMEFT Wilson coefficients. This conclusion is robust against variations in flavour assumptions and theory uncertainties. Our results highlight the complementarity between future colliders and low-energy experiments, and pave the way towards a SMEFT-consistent interpretation of first-row CKM unitarity tests and the Cabibbo angle anomaly.

</details>


### [304] [Milky Way White Dwarfs as Inelastic Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31850)
*Javier F. Acevedo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 白矮星可作为探测银河系中心非弹性暗物质湮灭的灵敏探针：部分被引力捕获的IDM粒子未完全沉入星体内部，而在外部轨道湮灭并逃逸产生伽马射线信号，据此对TeV质量尺度散射截面给出初步限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质间接探测依赖致密天体内部湮灭被星体吸收；对于非弹性暗物质，粒子可能无法完全沉降，导致标准捕获-湮灭平衡失效，因此需要新机制与新探针。银河系中心白矮星数量丰富且暗物质密度高，适合作为此类探测目标。

Method: 考虑IDM粒子穿越白矮星时被引力捕获，但能量仅降至非弹性散射运动学阈值以下，从而停留在延展至星体体积之外的长寿命轨道上；计算这些系统外部湮灭产生的集体伽马射线通量，作为IDM参数的函数，并与H.E.S.S.对银河系中心1度范围内的观测结果进行比较。

Result: 在关于该区域IDM密度的保守假设下，通过通量对比得到TeV质量尺度上IDM散射截面的初步限制；并将其映射为暗光子媒介模型与higgsino模型在MeV量级质量劈裂下的具体参数约束。

Conclusion: 白矮星外部的非弹性暗物质湮灭可产生可探测伽马射线，为TeV尺度IDM提供了一种补充性限制手段，并可具体约束暗光子和higgsino等模型。

Abstract: We show that white dwarfs in the Galactic Center are a sensitive probe of inelastic dark matter (IDM) annihilation. IDM particles traversing a white dwarf may become gravitationally captured without fully settling in its interior if their energy drops below the kinematic threshold for inelastic scattering. In this regime, a fraction of the incoming particles remains bound on long-lived orbits that extend beyond the white dwarf's volume, enabling annihilation to partially proceed outside of the object. Some of the annihilation products subsequently escape, rather than being absorbed by the stellar material, producing a potentially detectable signal. We estimate the collective gamma-ray flux from the external annihilation sourced by these systems, as a function of the IDM parameters, and compare it to H.E.S.S. observations within the central degree of the galaxy. From this comparison, we derive tentative limits on the IDM scattering cross-section at the TeV mass scale, under largely conservative assumptions for the IDM density in this region. As a concrete application, we map these results into specific parameter constraints for dark photon-mediated and higgsino models with mass splittings at the MeV scale.

</details>


### [305] [Prompting Particle Physics: Tokenized Multi-modal Foundation Models for Combinatorially Many Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31862)
*Nilotpal Kakati,Daniel Murnane,Baran Hashemi,Samuel Klein,Jeffrey Krupa,Eilam Gross,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出统一token词汇表与单模型任意模态映射框架，用自回归/并行解码器同时完成粒子流、探测器模拟与带电能量扣除等多步重建任务，自回归模型对Geant4模拟尤为忠实并在多项喷注重建指标上超越SOTA粒子流算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机实验的重建与模拟由长链条的专用算法组成，每步单独调优，链路复杂且难以统一；作者希望用单一模型同时承担多步任务，并仍输出可解释的中间对象（径迹、量能器单元、簇、粒子、喷注）。

Method: 将喷注中所有对象（径迹、量能器单元、簇、粒子、喷注）映射到单一共享token词汇表，训练一个模型实现任意模态子集到任意模态的映射；任务被简化为"提供哪些模态、请求哪些模态"的选择，粒子流、探测器模拟与带电能量扣除均为同一权重集合下的不同方向。在所有模态组合上训练，解码器可选自回归或并行输出token。

Result: token化后两种架构均训练稳定、调参需求小；两者都能生成逼真的重建与模拟对象，自回归模型输出与Geant4尤为接近；自回归模型在多项典型喷注重建指标上优于当前最优的粒子流算法。

Conclusion: 多步重建与模拟任务可由共享token词汇表的单一模型统一建模，通过模态间的条件映射实现任务复用，自回归解码在保真度与下游重建性能上表现突出，为对撞机重建链的整合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reconstruction and simulation at a collider experiment are long chains of specialised algorithms, each tuned to a single step. We explore how one model can serve many of those steps at once, while still producing the intermediate objects (tracks, calorimeter cells, clusters, particles and jets) that make the chain interpretable. To do so, we represent every object in a jet in a single shared token vocabulary and train one model to map any subset of these modalities to any other. A task is then only a choice of which modalities to provide and which to request: particle flow, detector simulation and charged energy subtraction are all directions through the same set of weights. We train over all modality combinations, with a decoder emitting tokens either autoregressively or in parallel. With tokenisation, both architectures train stably with little tuning. In evaluations, both models produce realistic reconstruction and simulation objects, with the autoregressive model particularly faithful to output from Geant4. The autoregressive model also outperforms a state-of-the-art particle flow algorithm on many typical jet reconstruction metrics.

</details>


### [306] [The era of charmed resonance searches at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32054)
*Daniel Whiteson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究LHC双喷注共振搜索中引入粲夸克标记的可行性，预测其所需粲标记性能，并评估其相对于包容双喷注搜索的敏感度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC已广泛搜索衰变为包容喷注及底夸克喷注的共振，但尚无专门针对含粲标记喷注的双喷注共振搜索。由于背景抑制带来的敏感度增益可能超过粲标记造成的信号效率损失，值得系统评估粲标记搜索的潜力。

Method: 利用ATLAS 13 TeV、139 fb⁻¹的双喷注质量谱，结合模拟的喷注味组分以及参数化的粲、底、轻喷注标记效率，预测预期的粲标记谱，并在排他性单标记与双标记类别中估计profile似然搜索的敏感度。

Result: 在当前标记器最宽松的工作点下，粲标记搜索在所考虑的全部共振质量范围内均能改善对衰变为两个粲夸克共振的预期敏感度，但定量敏感度依赖于粲标记效率在高质量区受约束较弱的行为，以及模拟的双喷注背景重味组分。

Conclusion: 结果为粲标记器提供了性能目标，并推动在LHC发展专门的粲标记双喷注共振搜索。

Abstract: The LHC dijet program has searched extensively for resonances decaying to inclusive jets and, more recently, to bottom-quark jets, where the sensitivity enhancement from suppression of the background outweighs the loss of signal efficiency in bottom-quark tagging. However, no dedicated search for dijet resonances containing charm-tagged jets has been performed. We study the charm-tagging performance required to make such searches competitive with the inclusive dijet search. Using the ATLAS 13 TeV dijet mass spectrum with 139 fb$^{-1}$, we predict the expected charm-tagged spectra from simulated jet-flavor fractions and parameterized charm-, bottom-, and light-jet tagging efficiencies, and estimate the sensitivity of a profile-likelihood search in exclusive one- and two-tag categories. At the loosest working point of the current tagger, charm-tagged searches improve the expected sensitivity to a resonance decaying to two charm quarks across the full resonance mass range considered, although the quantitative sensitivity depends on the poorly constrained high-$\pt$ behavior of charm-tagging efficiencies and on the simulated heavy-flavor composition of the dijet background. These results provide performance targets for charm taggers and motivate the development of dedicated charm-tagged dijet resonance searches at the LHC.

</details>


### [307] [Beam-polarization-enabled witness of four-dimensional entanglement in $Ω^-\barΩ^+$ pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32419)
*Qiu-Yan Zhang,Peng-Cheng Hong,Wei-Min Song*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究e+e-→ψ(3686)中产生的Ω-Ω+对的纠缠结构，探讨是否需要自旋3/2重子的完整四维自旋空间，并分析束流极化对螺旋度干涉及纠缠见证的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高自旋重子对产生中纠缠的本质维度问题，即自旋3/2重子对的纠缠是否必须用完整四维自旋空间描述，并明确束流极化如何通过控制母态布居与相干性来调控螺旋度干涉。

Method: 基于负度判据Neg>1，结合BESIII测量的螺旋度参数，分析束流极化对母态布居和相干性的调控作用，并引入组分夸克基准进行对比研究。

Result: 等横向极化时临界极化约为每束0.65，等量反号纵向极化时约为每束0.57；全横向极化给出近最大峰值负度Neg_peak≈1.50，满足见证的产生率加权角度分数为0.60。最优产生方向上，相消干涉消除非等螺旋度通道，等螺旋度振幅近似相等使Schmidt系数近乎相等；组分夸克基准显示见证的角度范围比峰值负度对相对相位更敏感。

Conclusion: 结果将极化控制的螺旋度干涉与高自旋重子产生中的四维纠缠联系起来，表明纠缠结构确实需要完整四维自旋空间。

Abstract: We study the entanglement structure of $Ω^-\barΩ^+$ pairs produced in $e^+e^-\toψ(3686)$, focusing on whether the entanglement requires the full four-dimensional spin space of each spin-$3/2$ baryon. Beam polarization controls the parent-state populations and coherence, thereby modifying helicity interference. Using the negativity criterion $\Neg>1$ and the measured BESIII helicity parameters, we find critical polarizations of about $0.65$ per beam for equal transverse polarization and $0.57$ per beam for equal-and-opposite longitudinal polarization. Full transverse polarization gives a near-maximal peak negativity, $\Neg_{\rm peak}\simeq1.50$, with a production-rate-weighted angular fraction of $0.60$ satisfying the witness. At the optimal production directions, destructive interference removes the unequal-helicity channels, while the near equality of the relevant equal-helicity amplitudes yields nearly equal Schmidt coefficients. Constituent-quark benchmarks further show that the angular extent of the witness is more sensitive to relative phases than the peak negativity. These results link polarization-controlled helicity interference to four-dimensional entanglement in high-spin baryon production.

</details>


### [308] [Escaping Solar Capture: Majoron mediated Elastic Dark Matter for the LZ 248~keV Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32435)
*Salvador Centelles Chuliá,Ayan Chattaraj,Rahul Srivastava,Surabhi Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文用轻Majoron介导的弹性暗物质散射解释LZ实验248 keV_nr核反冲候选事件，提出Majoron作为自发轻子数破缺产生的赝Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，自然联系中微子质量生成，并在scotogenic UV完成中实现，以非相对论算符O6的动量依赖重现高反冲事件而不污染低能端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了248 keV_nr的核反冲候选事件，需要从粒子物理角度给出物理解释。同时，希望将暗物质的相互作用与中微子质量的产生机制联系起来，避免仅做无模型动机的经验性引进，并解决传统暗物质-核子散射下太阳俘获道过强的问题。

Method: 首先进行模型无关分析，考察轻Majoron介导弹性暗物质散射能否解释该反冲信号。随后给出scotogenic UV完成：最轻的单态Majorana费米子为暗物质，其质量与Majoron耦合均来自对称性破缺动力学；通过一个矢量的上型夸克和夸克部门的type-I Dirac seesaw将Majoron作用于上夸克；低能下生成的portal产生非相对论算符O6，其动量依赖和高反冲事件匹配，且自旋结构消除了主要的重元素太阳俘获通道，氢上俘获因运动学匹配差和动量转移小而被抑制。

Result: 该构造同时重现LZ反冲谱和观测到的热遗迹丰度，同时强烈抑制太阳俘获。

Conclusion: 轻Majoron介导的弹性暗物质散射可自然解释LZ 248 keV_nr核反冲候选事件，并通过scotogenic UV完成将暗物质、Majoron耦合与中微子质量生成统一起来；其O6算符的动量依赖和自旋结构既匹配高反冲数据，又避免低能端过丰和太阳俘获限制，为暗物质-中微子关联提供了一种自洽实现。

Abstract: We interpret the $248~\mathrm{keV_{nr}}$ nuclear-recoil candidate reported by LZ through elastic dark matter scattering mediated by a light Majoron. The Majoron provides a natural origin for the required pseudoscalar interaction: it is the pseudo Nambu-Goldstone boson associated with spontaneous lepton number breaking, directly connecting the recoil signal to neutrino mass generation. After a model independent analysis of this idea, we present a concrete realization in a scotogenic ultraviolet completion in which the lightest singlet Majorana fermion is dark matter and acquires both its mass and Majoron coupling from the symmetry breaking dynamics. A vector like up type quark and a type-I Dirac seesaw in quark sector communicate the Majoron interaction to the up quark. At low energies, the resulting portal generates the nonrelativistic operator $\mathcal O_6$, whose momentum dependence reproduces the high-recoil event without overpopulating the lower energy bins. Its spin structure removes the dominant heavy element solar capture channels, while capture on hydrogen is suppressed by poor kinematic matching and small momentum transfer. This construction simultaneously reproduces the LZ recoil spectrum and the observed thermal relic abundance while strongly suppressing solar capture.

</details>


### [309] [Wide-Mass-Scanning-Range Setup and Phase-Resolved Protocol for Axion Dark Matter Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32554)
*A. Cottet,T. Kontos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种不依赖功率测量的量子增强轴子暗物质搜寻范式：在非线性腔干涉协议中于量子极限直接测量轴子振幅与相位，并引入旋磁模式作为宽质量范围换能器。预期品质因数提升至少4个数量级，质量窗口提升至少2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晕镜（haloscope）依赖功率测量，灵敏度与质量覆盖受限。本文旨在突破功率测量范式，提升轴子暗物质搜寻的品质因数与可探测质量范围。

Method: 在干涉测量协议中，利用非线性腔在量子极限下直接测量轴子的振幅与相位（而非功率）；并将旋磁模式用作轴子信号的宽带换能器，且与标准晕镜设计兼容。

Result: 相对于标准晕镜，预计品质因数至少提升4个数量级，质量窗口至少扩大2个数量级。

Conclusion: 该协议具有通用性，不仅可加速轴子搜寻，也可推广至暗光子及其他宇宙学目标（如星系脉泽）的搜寻。

Abstract: We propose a paradigm for quantum enhanced axion dark matter search, which does not rely on power measurements. We propose to measure directly the axion amplitude and phase in an interferometric protocol at the quantum limit, using a non-linear cavity. In addition, we introduce gyromagnetic modes as wide mass range transducers for axion signals compatible with standard haloscope designs. We expect this scheme to offer an improvement of at least 4 orders of magnitude in figure of merit and at least 2 orders of magnitude in mass window with respect to standard haloscopes. Owing to its generality, our proposed protocol has the potential to speed up axion search but also the search for dark photons or other cosmological objects, such as galactic masers.

</details>


### [310] [Dissecting the moat regime at low energies II: Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32563)
*Fabian Rennecke,Shi Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在低能QCD的两味夸克介子模型与随机相近似框架下，研究了moat regime对介子、夸克关联函数及夸克-反夸克势的影响，发现moat regime会产生不同于Friedel振荡的独特振荡，并显著增强类空区介子谱函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究moat regime在低能QCD中的作用机制，厘清其与Friedel振荡的关系，并评估其对相图（尤其是手征相边界与临界端点）的潜在影响。

Method: 基于两味夸克介子模型，采用随机相近似（RPA），计算高密度、零温条件下介子与夸克的关联函数以及夸克-反夸克势。

Result: moat regime引起的振荡源于非物理黎曼面上的极点，与Friedel振荡本质不同；除π介子和σ介子外，矢量介子与η介子也对moat regime敏感，导致类空区介子谱函数增强；夸克则基本不敏感。

Conclusion: 即使不存在非均匀不稳定性，moat regime仍可影响手征相边界及临界端点位置，对相图具有深远意义。

Abstract: We study the effects of the moat regime on meson and quark correlation functions and the quark-antiquark potential in low-energy QCD, building on the two-flavor quark meson model in a random-phase approximation. We investigate the relation between the moat regime and Friedel oscillations in the quark-antiquark potential at large density and zero temperature. As it turns out, oscillations from the moat regime are a distinct phenomenon, arising from poles on unphysical Riemann sheets in this region. Furthermore, we find that in addition to pions and the sigma meson, also other mesons, including vector mesons and the eta, are sensitive to the moat regime. This leads in particular to an enhancement of the meson spectral function in the spacelike region. The quarks, on the other hand, appear to be essentially insensitive. This can have far-reaching consequences for the phase diagram, as for example the chiral phase boundary, including the location of the critical endpoint, can be affected in the moat regime even in absence of inhomogeneous instabilities.

</details>


### [311] [Identification of $D_{s1}(2933)$ as the $2P_{1}$ state in the quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32586)
*Jun Wang,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将新观测到的 $D_{s1}(2933)^{+}$ 视为 $c\bar{s}$ 系统的 $2P_1$ 态，通过夸克模型评估耦合道修正，计算得到截断能标、伙伴态质量、混合角及三体分支比，结果支持其 $2P_1$ 激发态归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb 在 $B^0\to D^+D^-K^+\pi^-$ 中报告了新的粲-奇异介子 $D_{s1}(2933)^+$，其内部结构（是否为 $c\bar{s}$ 的 $2P_1$ 激发态）尚未明确，需要理论检验。

Method: 在夸克模型中将其作为 $c\bar{s}$ 的 $2P_1$ 态，通过涉及 $DK^*$、$D^*K$、$D^*K^*$、$D_s\phi$、$D_s^*\phi$、$D_s^*\eta$ 中间道的两点自能圈，评估对 ${}^1P_1$–${}^3P_1$ 质量矩阵的耦合道修正。

Result: 修正后本征值重现实测质量，给出截断能标约 $0.82~\mathrm{GeV}$、$2P_1'$ 伙伴态质量约 $3012~\mathrm{MeV}$、混合角 $-12.9^{\circ}$（显著偏离重夸克理想混合角）；预测的三体分支比 $R_{\text{th}}=1.98$ 与实验值 $R_{\text{exp}}=1.27^{+1.29}_{-0.95}$ 在误差范围内一致。

Conclusion: 结果支持将 $D_{s1}(2933)$ 认定为 $c\bar{s}$ 系统的 $2P_1$ 激发态，并包含末态相互作用。

Abstract: We investigate the internal structure of the newly observed charm-strange meson $D_{s1}(2933)^{+}$, which was reported by the LHCb Collaboration in $B^{0}\to D^{+}D^{-}K^{+}π^{-}$, in the quark model by treating it as the $2P_{1}$ state of the $c\bar{s}$ system. The coupled-channel corrections to the ${}^{1}P_1$--${}^{3}P_1$ mass matrix are evaluated through the two-point self-energy loops involving the $DK^{*}$, $D^{*}K$, $D^{*}K^{*}$, $D_{s}φ$, $D_{s}^{*}φ$ and $D_{s}^{*}η$ intermediate channels. Requiring the corrected eigenvalue to reproduce the measured mass yields a cutoff scale of about $0.82~\mathrm{GeV}$, a $2P_{1}^{\prime}$ partner state at $3012~\mathrm{MeV}$, and a mixing angle of $-12.9^{\circ}$ that deviates significantly from the heavy-quark ideal-mixing angle. Moreover, the predicted three-body branching ratio fraction $R_{\text{th}}=1.98$ for $D_{s1}(2933)\to D^{+}K^{+}π^{-}$ via the $DK^{*}$ and $D_{2}^{*}K$ intermediate states is consistent with the experimental value $R_{\text{exp}}=1.27^{+1.29}_{-0.95}$ within uncertainties. These results support the assignment of $D_{s1}(2933)$ as the $2P_{1}$ excited state of the $c\bar{s}$ system with final state interactions.

</details>


### [312] [Landscape of pentaquark bound states with neural-network Variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32624)
*Jing-Zhe Song,Wei-Lin Wu,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法在组分夸克势模型下系统研究五夸克系统基态，穷举387种色-自旋-同位旋组态，找出21个束缚能2–15 MeV的候选束缚态，全部为浅束缚分子态；并复现实验观测到的隐藏粲五夸克共振对应的最低散射态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上已观测到隐藏粲五夸克共振，但五夸克是否存在真正束缚态、束缚机制及束缚深度尚不清楚。作者希望以系统、无遗漏的组态扫描给出可靠的束缚态判据与候选清单，为未来实验搜寻提供指导。

Method: 采用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NNVMC）方法，在组分夸克势模型框架下求解五夸克系统基态能量。研究对象为负宇称、显式奇异（manifestly exotic）的五夸克系统，即无法通过轻或奇异夸克对的产生与湮灭与传统重子混合的组态。共穷举387种色、自旋、同位旋组态。通过对比束缚态与未束缚系统基态能量是否收敛到重子-介子阈值之上，作为交叉验证与束缚判据。

Result: 共识别出21个五夸克束缚态候选，束缚能从2至15 MeV；所有束缚态均为浅束缚分子态，不存在深束缚五夸克。QQqqQ̄、ssqqQ̄、QsqqQ̄、QqqqQ̄类束缚态由重矢量介子与重子构成，QQqqs̄与Qqqqs̄类则由K介子与重子构成。未束缚系统基态能量自上方收敛到重子-介子阈值，与S波散射态一致，验证了束缚判据的可靠性。特别地，在qqqc̄c̄和qqsc̄c̄通道中分别复现了最低散射态η_c N与η_c Λ，与实验观测隐藏粲五夸克共振的通道一致。所有全重五夸克态均为未束缚。

Conclusion: 五夸克系统不存在深束缚态，只可能存在浅束缚分子态；本文给出的21个候选及通道信息为LHCb、Belle II等未来五夸克搜寻实验提供了有价值的理论指导。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the ground states of pentaquark systems using the neural network variational Monte Carlo method within the constituent quark potential model. We investigate all manifestly exotic pentaquark systems with negative parity, which cannot mix with the conventional baryons through the creation and annihilation of light or strange quark pair. We exhaust 387 color, spin, and isospin configurations and identify 21 pentaquark bound state candidates with binding energies ranging from $2$ to $15$ MeV. A striking observation is that all of the bound states are shallowly bound molecular states, and a deeply bound pentaquark state does not exist. The $QQqq\bar{Q}$, $ssqq\bar{Q}$, $Qsqq\bar{Q}$ and $Qqqq\bar{Q}$ bound states are composed of a heavy vector meson and a baryon, while the $QQqq\bar{s}$ and $Qqqq\bar{s}$ bound states are composed of a $K$ meson and a heavy baryon. As a cross-validation, the ground-state energies of the unbound systems converge to the baryon--meson thresholds from above, which are consistent with the S-wave scattering states and provide a reliable baseline for the bound-state criterion. Especially, we reproduce the lowest scattering states $η_c N$ and $η_c Λ$ in the $qqqc\bar{c}$ and $qqsc\bar{c}$ channels respectively, where the hidden-charm pentaquark resonances were observed experimentally. We also find that all fully heavy pentaquark states are unbound. Our results provide valuable guidance for future pentaquark searches at LHCb, Belle II, and other experiments.

</details>


### [313] [Quantum tomography at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32800)
*Michael Fucilla,Yuxun Guo,Yoshitaka Hatta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次提出在未来的电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上，通过方位角关联实现无质量和有质量夸克-反夸克对的自旋密度矩阵量子态层析，并指出重夸克扇区的最大纠缠会显著改变Collins角依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析是实验重构完整自旋密度矩阵的手段。作者希望将量子信息概念（纠缠、Bell非局域性）引入高能对撞实验，探索EIC上夸克-反夸克对的自旋量子态。

Method: 利用Collins碎裂函数和双强子碎裂函数产生的强子-双强子对之间的方位角关联，获取自旋密度矩阵的所有分量，从而进行量子态层析。

Result: 证明了自旋密度矩阵全部分量可通过方位角关联实验获取，并预测重夸克扇区新近发现的最大纠缠会表现为对通常Collins角依赖cos(φ+φ')的强烈修正。

Conclusion: 该方案使EIC成为研究量子信息性质（纠缠、Bell非局域性）的实验平台，并将最大纠缠的量子特征与可观测的角分布联系起来。

Abstract: We initiate the study of quantum state tomography---the experimental reconstruction of the complete spin density matrix---of massless and massive quark-antiquark pairs produced at the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). We show that all components of the spin density matrix can be accessed through azimuthal angular correlations between hadron and dihadron pairs generated by the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. This enables the experimental investigation of a variety of quantum information properties at the EIC, such as entanglement and Bell-nonlocality. We predict that the recently discovered maximal entanglement in the heavy-quark sector is signaled by a strong modification of the usual Collins angular dependence $\cos(φ+φ')$

</details>


### [314] [Machine learning for the LHC physics program: a 2025-2026 stocktake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32874)
*Jesse Thaler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对2025年5月至2026年5月高能物理机器学习（HEPML）领域进行年度综述：AI流水线筛选569篇新论文、精读103篇，归纳出六大论断，核心结论是HEPML已从"造工具"的研究领域转变为LHC物理项目所依赖的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC物理项目与机器学习结合日益紧密，论文数量增长迅速，传统人工综述方式难以在一年尺度内系统把握领域全貌与趋势。作者因此尝试用AI流水线方式完成年度盘点，同时借这一实践本身探讨以AI方式开展科研工作的可行性。

Method: 以HEPML Living Review为语料，以2025年5月为切分点分为1,756篇早期论文与569篇后期论文。AI流水线对569篇摘要进行调研，按引用量、时效性、主题和合作组参与度排序，精读103篇（95篇切分后论文加8篇早期基线论文），为每篇生成结构化笔记；再将笔记综合为六条论断，由独立审稿智能体核查并回溯原文复核。

Result: 核心论断：HEPML已不再仅是工具研发领域，而成为LHC物理项目依赖的基础设施——ATLAS与CMS现已发表依赖神经网络的物理结果，归档的ALEPH数据重新进入生产流程。其余五条论断为：(1) 基于模拟的推断与基础模型两场变革开始融合；(2) AI智能体是真正的新前沿，一年内出现47篇论文但尚无被采用的测量结果；(3) "我们是否信任它"是增长最快的议题，近期每五篇论文就有一篇涉及不确定性、校准或可解释性；(4) 该领域"什么在放缓"同样具有信息量：等变性已被吸收为工具，模型特异的唯象研究让位于模型无关搜寻；(5) 理论机器学习在多个方向跨过能力阈值。

Conclusion: 作者总结了已定型的方向、正在到来的方向与仍待解决的问题，并简要讨论了这种基于AI的工作方式本身所引发的顾虑。

Abstract: The first sentence of this abstract--and the introduction to these proceedings--was authored by a human, but the bulk of this document was generated by an agentic AI system. In this talk, I take stock of machine learning (ML) for the LHC physics program over the twelve months from May 2025 to May 2026. The corpus is the HEPML Living Review, split at May 2025 into 1,756 earlier papers and 569 later ones. An AI pipeline surveyed the 569 abstracts, ranked them by citations, recency, theme, and collaboration involvement, and read 103 papers in full (95 from after the split, plus 8 earlier baseline papers), producing a structured note for each. The notes were then synthesized into six claims about the state of the field, checked by independent reviewer agents, and re-verified against the source papers. The headline claim is that (1) ML for high-energy physics (HEPML) stopped being a research area that builds tools and became infrastructure that the LHC physics program depends on: ATLAS and CMS now publish physics results that depend on neural networks, and the archived ALEPH data have re-entered production. The other five claims are: (2) simulation-based inference and foundation models are two revolutions starting to merge; (3) AI agents are the genuinely new front, with 47 papers in twelve months and no adopted measurement yet; (4) "do we trust it?" is the fastest-growing agenda, with one recent paper in five about uncertainty, calibration, or interpretability; (5) what is slowing down is informative, since equivariance was absorbed into a tool and model-specific phenomenology ceded ground to model-agnostic searches; and (6) theory ML crossed a capability threshold in multiple research areas. I close with what is settled, what is incoming, and what is open, and briefly discuss the concerns raised by this way of working with AI.

</details>


### [315] [$ \bar K^0 d $ Bound State and Correlation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32986)
*M. Bayar,P. Encarnación,A. Feijoo,E. Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 采用保持弹性幺正性的固定中心近似新表述研究K̄⁰d系统，预言阈下束缚态并计算关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索K̄⁰d系统中是否存在与K⁻pp对应的束缚态，并寻找可在实验（如ALICE）中检验的无库仑观测量。

Method: 采用一种新的固定中心近似表述，保证弹性幺正性；基于两种不同的K̄N相互作用模型计算散射振幅与K̄⁰d关联函数。

Result: 散射振幅预言阈下存在束缚态，可解释为与较高Λ(1405)极点对应的Λ(1380)N组态；相互作用为吸引性，散射参数与近期K⁻d结果同量级；关联函数呈现与束缚态相关的特征结构，且两种K̄N模型结果高度一致。

Conclusion: K̄⁰d束缚态预言对底层相互作用模型具有稳健性，其关联函数可作为无库仑观测量供未来ALICE实验检验。

Abstract: We study the $\bar{K}^0d$ system using a new formulation of the fixed-center approximation that preserves elastic unitarity. The resulting scattering amplitude predicts a bound state below threshold, which can be interpreted as a $Λ(1380)N$ configuration complementary to the $K^-pp$ state associated with the higher $Λ(1405)$ pole. The interaction is attractive, with scattering parameters of the same order as recent $K^-d$ determinations. We further calculate the $\bar{K}^0d$ correlation function, which exhibits a characteristic structure associated with the bound state and provides a Coulomb-free observable that can be tested in future ALICE measurements. Results obtained with two different $\bar{K}N$ models are very similar, indicating the robustness of the predictions against the choice of the underlying interaction.

</details>


### [316] [Resonantly-Enhanced Baryogenesis through Asymmetric Capture by Primordial Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33031)
*Fayez Abu-Ajamieh,Xiaoyan Huo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用两个近简并不稳定费米子混合系统中的共振CP破坏，显著增强原初黑洞（PBH）捕获机制产生的重子不对称（BAU），并在包含热修正与连续蒸发后得到与观测一致的结果，同时给出相关引力波信号预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前通过PBH不对称捕获带电重子粒子产生BAU的机制，由于相关CP破坏散射不对称受圈图压低，所得不对称度远低于观测值，因此需要一种能大幅增强CP破坏的物理机制。

Method: 引入两个近简并的不稳定费米子Y1、Y2组成的混合系统，其质量劈裂与衰变宽度相当；通过相干Y1–Y2态的稀有产生及CP不对称的返回与损失衰变，在拉格朗日层面保持整体重子数守恒的前提下，获得约0.1量级的积分衰变概率差异。将该共振源嵌入PBH捕获输运系统，并计入热修正、连续PBH蒸发与熵产生；同时研究PBH形成所对应的原初扰动产生的标量诱导引力波谱。

Result: 零温瞬时蒸发近似下得到η_B^{T=0}≈2.37×10⁻¹⁰≈2.72 η_B^obs；计入热修正和连续蒸发与熵产生后，η_B=8.72×10⁻¹¹，与观测值一致。即使PBH吸收持续活跃而不蒸发，仍可得到η_B^{no evap}≈9.1×10⁻¹²≈0.105 η_B^obs。引力波谱峰值位于f≈0.16–0.17 MHz，h²Ω_GW^peak≈(3–7)×10⁻⁸；在理想化灵敏度假设下，U-DECIGO对基准谱可达到信噪比10以上。

Conclusion: 共振CP破坏可有效克服圈图压低，使PBH捕获机制自然产生与观测相符的重子不对称，且该机制对PBH是否蒸发具有一定稳健性；模型还预言了MHz频段的标量诱导引力波信号，可被未来空间引力波探测器检验。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) can generate the Baryon Asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) through asymmetric capture of baryon-charge-carrying particles, but previous realizations of this mechanism typically yield an asymmetry well below the observed value because the relevant CP-violating scattering asymmetry is loop suppressed. We show that this limitation can be overcome through resonant CP violation in a mixed system of two nearly degenerate unstable fermions, $Y_{1,2}$, whose mass splitting is comparable to their decay widths. Rare production of the coherent $Y_1$--$Y_2$ state, followed by CP-asymmetric return and loss decays, generates an $\mathcal{O}(0.1)$ difference in integrated decay probabilities while preserving the global baryon charge at the Lagrangian level. Embedding this resonant source in the PBH-capture transport system yields the observed baryon asymmetry. We first reproduce the mechanism in a zero-temperature, sudden-evaporation treatment, which gives $η_B^{T=0}\simeq2.37\times10^{-10}\simeq2.72 η_B^{\rm obs}$. We find that after including thermal corrections and continuous PBH evaporation and entropy production, the resulting BAU becomes $η_B = 8.72\times 10^{-11}$ in agreement with the observed value. An interesting finding of the model is that even if PBH absorption remains active, we would still have BAU $η_{B}^{\text{no evap}} \sim 9.1 \times 10^{-12} \sim 0.105 η_B^{\rm obs}$. We further investigate gravitational-wave signatures associated with the primordial fluctuations responsible for PBH formation. The corresponding scalar-induced gravitational-wave spectra peak at $f \sim 0.16\text{--}0.17~\mathrm{MHz}$, with $h^2Ω_{\rm GW}^{\rm peak} \sim (3\text{--}7)\times 10^{-8}$. Under idealized sensitivity assumptions, U-DECIGO can achieve signal-to-noise ratios above 10 for the benchmark spectra.

</details>


### [317] [Stress in static force fields and the sign of the D-term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33151)
*Adam Freese*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析静阿贝尔力场对D-term符号的贡献，指出自旋偶场贡献为负、自旋奇场（如电磁场）贡献为正，且贡献符号与相应场量子的D-term符号一致。由于不同自旋场可产生相同势和波函数，D-term符号与稳定性无关；强子由自旋1胶子束缚因而其负D-term属反常，需额外解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界长期猜测D-term为负源于某种力学稳定性条件，但氢原子正D-term等反例使该猜测存疑。作者希望厘清D-term符号究竟由何决定，并解释为何质子、π介子等强子的D-term为负。

Method: 考察静态阿贝尔力场所携带的应力对D-term符号的影响，分别计算自旋偶场与自旋奇场（如电磁场）的贡献，并比较其与相应场量子D-term符号的关系；利用不同自旋场可给出相同势与波函数这一性质来检验稳定性论证。

Result: 自旋偶场的贡献为负，自旋奇场（如电磁场）的贡献为正，且这些贡献与场量子自身的D-term符号相关联。由于不同自旋的场能产生相同势，从而得到同样稳定、波函数相同的复合体系，说明D-term符号与稳定性无关。

Conclusion: D-term符号不由力学稳定性决定。强子由自旋1胶子束缚，其负D-term因此显得反常，需要额外机制解释；作者简要推测色通量禁闭可能是产生负强子D-term的原因。

Abstract: The sign of the D-term is negative for a variety of hadrons, including the proton and pion. Speculation that this is the result of a mechanical stability condition has persisted despite counterexamples, including the positive D-term of the hydrogen atom. In this work, I explore how the sign of the D-term is influenced by the stress carried by static abelian force fields, finding the contributions from spin-even fields to be negative and the contributions from spin-odd fields (such as the electromagnetic field) to be positive. These contributions correlate with the signs of the D-terms of the fields' respective quanta. Since fields with different spin can produce identical potentials---thus resulting in equally-stable composite systems with identical wave functions---the sign of the D-term has nothing to do with stability. Since hadrons are bound by spin-one gluons, their negative D-terms appear atypical and require explanation. I briefly speculate on how color flux confinement could produce a negative hadronic D-term.

</details>


### [318] [Probing Solar Chameleons with XENONnT Ionization-Only Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33198)
*Guan-Wen Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次利用XENONnT的7.83吨·年低能S2-only（电离-only）数据搜寻太阳chameleon（变色龙）标量场，将预言电子反冲谱折叠进探测器响应并在校正S2空间做联合分箱似然分析，对共形与disformal电子耦合给出后验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏蔽标量场可联系暗能量与实验室尺度新物理，并与第五力约束相容；太阳产生为其提供直接探针，而仅电离测量能把液氙探测器灵敏度拓展到亚keV能区，故有望检验此类模型。

Method: 基于XENONnT 7.83吨·年电离-only数据集，将预测电子反冲谱经运行相关的探测器响应折叠，在cS2空间直接检验；对SR0、SR1、SR2的七个cS2分箱做联合分箱似然，采样五维参数空间{β_e,β_γ,M_e,Λ,n}，推导电子共形与disformal耦合的后验约束。反冲能量达约0.04 keV_ee，需显式建模少电子探测器响应与仪器本底。disformal主导区用β_eff≡β_γ(eV/M_e)^4表述。

Result: 给出上限 log10 β_eff < -3.64。尽管本分析能量阈值低于此前XENONnT电子反冲分析，约束却更弱，主要因为disformal吸收率随反冲能量降低而迅速下降，且低能处本底率更高。

Conclusion: 该分析确立了低能电离数据作为检验太阳chameleon与屏蔽暗能量模型的新实验通道。

Abstract: Screened scalar fields provide a possible connection between dark energy and laboratory-scale new physics while remaining compatible with existing fifth-force constraints. Solar production offers a direct probe of these models, and ionization-only measurements extend the sensitivity of liquid-xenon detectors into the sub-keV regime. We present the first search for solar chameleons using low-energy S2-only data, based on the 7.83 tonne-year XENONnT ionization-only data set. The predicted electronic-recoil spectrum is folded through the run-dependent detector response and tested directly in corrected-S2 (cS2) space. A joint binned likelihood combines the seven cS2 bins of science runs SR0, SR1, and SR2. We sample the five-dimensional parameter space $\{β_e,β_γ,M_e,Λ,n\}$ and derive posterior constraints on the conformal and disformal electron couplings. The data extend the recoil-energy reach to approximately $0.04~{\rm keV}_{ee}$, where the few-electron detector response and instrumental backgrounds must be modeled explicitly. In the disformal-dominated regime, the result is expressed in terms of $β_{\rm eff}\equivβ_γ({\rm eV}/M_e)^4$. We obtain the upper limit $\log_{10}β_{\rm eff}<-3.64$. Although the present analysis reaches a lower energy threshold than our previous XENONnT electronic-recoil analysis, the resulting constraint is weaker, primarily because the disformal absorption rate decreases rapidly toward low recoil energies, where the background rate also becomes larger. This analysis establishes low-energy ionization data as a new experimental channel for testing solar chameleons and screened dark-energy models.

</details>


### [319] [Thermal masses in the Standard Model at three loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33448)
*Mikael Chala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在维度约化框架下，将希格斯热质量以及电弱和色Debye质量参数计算至完整的 $\mathcal{O}(g^6)$ 阶，其中 $g$ 代表规范耦合或顶夸克Yukawa耦合；作者采用新方法求解了此前未知的混合签名sum-integral。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限温度场论中，热质量与Debye质量参数是描述高温等离子体集体效应的关键量，其更高阶修正对降低微扰论重整化尺度依赖、提高相变等物理过程的定量精度至关重要。此前计算多停留在较低阶（如 $\mathcal{O}(g^4)$ 或部分 $\mathcal{O}(g^5)$），而 $\mathcal{O}(g^6)$ 完整结果受限于若干尚未解析的混合签名sum-integral，因此需要发展新的积分求解技术。

Method: 采用维度约化（dimensional reduction）的框架，将高温柔性模式积分掉以获得有效三维理论的参数。为达到完整的 $\mathcal{O}(g^6)$，需要处理具有混合签名（即Matsubara频率与动量组合符号不统一）的sum-integral。作者提出/引入新的求值方法对这些此前未知的积分进行解析计算，其中 $g$ 涵盖规范耦合以及顶夸克Yukawa耦合。

Result: 给出了希格斯热质量以及电弱、色Debye质量参数至完整 $\mathcal{O}(g^6)$ 阶的解析结果；同时给出了所涉及的混合签名sum-integrals的求值方案，解决了此前无法处理的积分类型。

Conclusion: 该工作将维度约化中相关质量参数的微扰精度推进到 $\mathcal{O}(g^6)$，为高温电弱相变及等离子体输运等精密研究提供了更可靠的输入，并提供了可复用于其他高阶热场论计算的新型积分求解技术。

Abstract: We compute the Higgs thermal mass and the electroweak and colour Debye mass parameters to full $\mathcal{O}(g^6)$ in dimensional reduction, where $g$ represents a gauge coupling or the top Yukawa. We employ new methods to evaluate previously unknown mixed-signature sum-integrals.

</details>


### [320] [Mono-Z signal for dark matter searches at future lepton colliders and cosmological interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33514)
*Anupam Ghosh,Partha Kumar Paul,Abhik Sarkar,Rituparna Ghosh,Rachit Sharma,and,Subhaditya Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在暗物质有效场论框架下研究未来正负电子对撞机单Z玻色子信号的发现潜力，涵盖标量、费米子和矢量暗物质直到六维算符，并结合多种约束确定可行参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来正负电子对撞机为探测暗物质提供了清洁环境；需在有效场论框架下系统评估不同维度算符的单Z信号可发现性，并纳入现有实验约束以确定物理可行的参数空间。

Method: 在暗物质有效场论框架下，按产生机制对至六维算符分类；施加暗物质遗迹丰度、直接与间接探测、希格斯不可见衰变及LHC单Z反演分析约束；模拟初末态辐射、探测器响应及Z玻色子轻子与强子衰变道重建，利用缺失能量、缺失横向动量、反冲质量、Z玻色子赝快度及束流极化区分信号与本底。

Result: 费米子偶极、标量轻子型、矢量希格斯门户和规范门户暗物质算符表现出稳健的单Z发现潜力，可探测多TeV有效能标。

Conclusion: 未来正负电子对撞机的单Z搜索对特定暗物质算符具有强敏感性，可在多TeV尺度探测暗物质有效相互作用，束流极化与多变量分析对信号辨识至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate mono-$Z$ signature at the future electron-positron collider within the dark matter effective field theory (DMEFT) framework, considering operators up to dimension six for scalar, fermionic, and vector dark matter (DM) candidates. The operators are classified according to their underlying production mechanisms. We identify the phenomenologically viable parameter space by imposing the observed DM relic abundance together with direct and indirect detection constraints, Higgs invisible decay limits, and LHC mono-$Z$ bounds obtained through a recasting analysis. We study the discovery prospects of the surviving DM operators at the future $e^-e^+$ collider including initial and final state radiation, detector simulation, and the reconstruction of both leptonic and hadronic $Z$-boson decay modes. We exploit missing energy, missing transverse momentum, recoil mass, $Z$-boson pseudorapidity, together with beam polarization, which play a significant role to distinguish the signal from the Standard Model background. We find that fermionic dipole, scalar leptophilic, vector Higgs-portal, and gauge-portal DM operators offer robust mono-$Z$ discovery potential, probing multi-TeV effective scales.

</details>


### [321] [Scheme Transformations in Non-Linear QCD Evolution: Towards a Stable NLO BK Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33821)
*Renaud Boussarie,Paul Caucal,Piotr Korcyl,Yacine Mehtar-Tani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Doi-Peliti形式体系的系统性方案变换框架，用于关联射弹（$k^+$）与靶（$k^-$）因子化方案。通过变换高能算符和演化核，生成了可消除有问题的双共线对数的NLO项，从而得到无该不稳定性、仅保留单共线和双反共线增强项的NLO BK方程。数值解表明，对于GBW和MV初始条件，演化在$Y=10$范围内保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能QCD的NLO演化受到大共线对数困扰，导致Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）方程的微扰展开不稳定。需要一种系统性方法来消除这些有问题的双共线对数，并建立射弹与靶因子化方案之间的联系，以实现稳定的NLO演化与旋转NLO冲击因子的结合。

Method: 采用Doi-Peliti形式体系构建系统性方案变换框架，关联射弹（$k^+$）和靶（$k^-$）因子化方案。通过变换高能算符与演化核，生成能抵消问题性双共线对数的NLO项。

Result: 变换后得到的NLO BK方程消除了不稳定性，仅保留单共线和双反共线增强项。数值解显示，对于Golec-Biernat-Wusthoff和McLerran-Venugopalan两种初始条件，演化在$Y=10$范围内保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架为结合稳定的NLO演化与旋转NLO冲击因子提供了一条一致的路径，解决了高能QCD NLO演化中双共线对数导致的不稳定性问题。

Abstract: High-energy QCD evolution at NLO suffers from large collinear logarithms that make the perturbative expansion of the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) equation unstable. We develop a systematic scheme-transformation framework using the Doi-Peliti formalism to relate projectile ($k^+$) and target ($k^-$) factorization schemes. The transformation of high-energy operators and the evolution kernel generates NLO terms that cancel the problematic double collinear logarithm. The resulting NLO BK equation is free of this instability, with only single collinear and double anti-collinear enhancements remaining. Numerical solutions show stable evolution up to $Y=10$ for both Golec-Biernat-Wusthoff and McLerran-Venugopalan initial conditions, providing a consistent path toward combining stable NLO evolution with rotated NLO impact factors.

</details>


### [322] [Heavy Higgsino Dark Matter With Low Reheating in View of the LZ High-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33869)
*C. Pallis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 质量为 ~0.1 PeV 的重 higgsino 可在重加热温度 T_rh ≲ 595 GeV 条件下解释当前宇宙冷暗物质丰度，其机制依赖于模量 φ 的非平衡衰变产生的非热 higgsino。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 LUX-ZEPLIN 实验结果对暗物质直接/间接探测施加了严格限制，作者希望寻找一种既能规避这些约束、又能满足当前冷暗物质丰度的暗物质候选者与产生机制。

Method: 考虑质量为 m_h~0.1 PeV 的重 higgsino 作为暗物质候选者；引入质量 m_φ ≫ m_h、具有非零分支比衰变到 higgsino 的模量场 φ，通过其非平衡衰变进行非热 higgsino 产生，并约束重加热温度上限。φ 被识别为 goldstino 超场的标量分量，通过 Giudice-Masiero 机制参与 MSSM 中 μ 参数（μ ≃ m_h）的生成。

Result: 当重加热温度 T_rh ≲ 595 GeV 时，该重 higgsino 能够解释当前宇宙的冷暗物质丰度，且与 LUX-ZEPLIN 事件相容，避开直接和间接探测约束。

Conclusion: 重 higgsino 暗物质在模量场非热产生机制下是可行的，模量场的非平衡衰变在将重加热温度压低至上限以下起决定性作用；该模量场可与 Giudice-Masiero 机制中的 goldstino 超场标量分量一致。

Abstract: We show that a heavy higgsino $\tilde h$ with mass $m_{\tilde h}\sim0.1~{\rm PeV}$, which can be made consistent with the recent LUX-ZEPLIN event avoiding constraints from direct and indirect detection, can account for the present cold dark matter abundance of the universe, if the reheating temperature is bounded as $T_{\rm rh}\lesssim 595~{\rm GeV}$. These values can be achieved by a possible out-of-equilibrium decay of a modulus $φ$ with mass $m_φ\gg m_{\tilde h}$ and a non-vanishing branching ratio into $\tilde h$'s which plays a decisive role in reducing $T_{\rm rh}$ below its upper limit via the non-thermal $\tilde h$ production. The modulus $φ$ can be identified with the scalar component of the goldstino superfield which is involved in the generation of the $μ$ parameter of MSSM (with $μ\simeq m_{\tilde h}$) via the Giudice-Masiero mechanism.

</details>


### [323] [Constraining Ultra-Light Vector Bosons via Solar Neutrino Oscillations: The Gauged $L_μ- L_τ$ Model and Prospects for JUNO and XLZD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33877)
*Ilídio Lopes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 $U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}$ 规范对称性扩展标准模型框架下，引入超轻矢量玻色子 $Z^\prime$，通过其与光子的动能混合在太阳内部产生有效物质势，从而经由 MSW 机制修正太阳中微子振荡，并利用 Borexino、SNO、Super-Kamiokande 数据对玻色子质量与耦合给出限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该无反常构造传统上被用于解释缪子反常磁矩，本文将其作为独立的太阳中微子物理唯象框架进行检验，探索超轻玻色子对太阳中微子振荡的影响。

Method: 基于 $U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}$ 规范扩展，$Z^\prime$ 与光子动能混合 $\varepsilon$ 在太阳内部生成有效物质势，经 MSW 机制修正中微子味振荡；结合最新 Borexino、SNO、Super-Kamiokande 太阳中微子测量与最新标准太阳模型，对玻色子质量及耦合参数进行拟合限制。

Result: 将玻色子质量限制在 $5.7\times 10^{-17}\,{\rm eV}$ 以下；最优模型（$\eta_o \approx -2$）给出 $\chi_\nu^2 = 2.7$，优于标准三味情形的 $\chi_\nu^2 = 3.1$；最优拟合对应有效四费米耦合 $g_{Z^\prime}^2 \varepsilon / m_{Z^\prime}^2 \approx 6.6\times 10^{-23}\,{\rm eV}^{-2}$，约为费米常数的 5.7 倍；当 $\varepsilon\approx 1$、参考媒介子质量 $1\,{\rm eV}$ 时，规范耦合 $g_{Z^\prime}\approx 8\times 10^{-12}$。

Conclusion: 该超轻粒子可作为超轻暗物质候选，并有望缓解冷暗物质范式下星系模拟的长期偏差；JUNO（自 2025 年 8 月起运行）与 XLZD 等未来实验将显著改进这些约束，而地面长基线中微子实验可提供互补探测。

Abstract: We present an extension of the Standard Model based upon the gauged $U(1)_{L_μ- L_τ}$ symmetry, featuring an ultra-light vector boson that couples to the difference of muon and tau lepton numbers. This anomaly-free construction, historically motivated by the muon's anomalous magnetic moment, is employed here as an independent phenomenological framework for solar neutrino physics. Through kinetic mixing with the photon, the $Z^\prime$ boson generates an effective matter potential within the solar interior, thereby modifying neutrino flavour oscillations via the Mikheyev--Smirnov--Wolfenstein mechanism. Utilising recent solar neutrino measurements from Borexino, SNO, and Super-Kamiokande, together with an up-to-date standard solar model, we constrain the boson mass to be below $5.7\times 10^{-17}\,{\rm eV}$, a potential ultra-light dark matter candidate. The optimal model, with coupling parameter $η_o \approx -2$, yields $χ_ν^2 = 2.7$, compared with $χ_ν^2 = 3.1$ for the standard three-flavour scenario. This best fit corresponds to an effective four-fermion coupling $g_{Z^\prime}^2 \varepsilon / m_{Z^\prime}^2 \approx 6.6 \times 10^{-23}\,{\rm eV}^{-2}$, approximately $5.7$ times the Fermi constant, equivalent for $\varepsilon \approx 1$ and a reference mediator mass of $1\,{\rm eV}$ to a gauge coupling $g_{Z^\prime} \approx 8\times 10^{-12}$. We also present projections for forthcoming experiments: the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO), operating since August 2025 with world-leading precision on oscillation parameters, together with the XLZD consortium, should substantially refine these constraints. Such ultra-light particles may address long-standing discrepancies in galaxy simulations within the cold dark matter paradigm, whilst terrestrial long-baseline neutrino experiments provide complementary probes of this parameter space.

</details>


### [324] [Gottfried-Jackson polarization filters for holographic dilaton and transverse-traceless gluon responses in near-threshold $J/ψ$ photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33908)
*Arkadiy I. Syamtomov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在Hatta-Yang全息引力子-伸缩子振幅框架下，识别了近阈J/ψ光产生在Gottfried-Jackson基中的精确伸缩子-零自旋投影通道，并据此构造了束流极化自旋密度矩阵的可观测滤波组合，指出原始固定形状全息振幅无法以单一归一化拟合全部近阈数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈J/ψ光产生是探测质子内胶子与trace anomaly（伸缩子）机制的重要窗口；但全息振幅中伸缩子贡献与张量（TT）贡献如何被实验分离尚缺乏清晰的自旋投影分析，且模型能否描述现代高精度数据存疑。

Method: 在Gottfried-Jackson参考系中对Hatta-Yang全息引力子-伸缩子振幅进行自旋投影分析，确定m=0与m=-λ_γ等通道的伸缩子贡献结构；利用标准束流极化自旋密度矩阵元素构造仅含TT的对照量与包含线性伸缩子-TT干涉的双线性量；并将结果与GlueX、CLAS12截面数据作比较。

Result: 发现m=0与m=-λ_γ振幅无伸缩子贡献；对垂直于反应面的光子极化，m=0通道进一步孤立出A_g(t)+B_g(t)并需要核子自旋转移；束流极化自旋密度矩阵元素给出纯TT对照量和含线性伸缩子-TT干涉的双线性量。与GlueX、CLAS12数据比较表明，原固定形状全息振幅无法用单一整体归一化描述全部近阈数据。

Conclusion: 这些极化滤波更应被视作对产生张量结构的检验，而非一个已全局验证的反应模型的预言；全息振幅的固定形状假设在近阈区受到数据挑战。

Abstract: Within the Hatta-Yang holographic graviton-dilaton amplitude for near-threshold $J/ψ$ photoproduction, we identify exact dilaton-null spin-projection channels in the Gottfried-Jackson basis. The $m=0$ and $m=-λ_γ$ amplitudes receive no dilaton contribution; for photon polarization normal to the reaction plane the $m=0$ channel further isolates $A_g(t)+B_g(t)$ and requires nucleon spin transfer. Standard beam-polarization spin-density-matrix elements then furnish TT-only controls and a bilinear containing linear dilaton-TT interference. A comparison with modern GlueX and CLAS12 cross sections shows that the original fixed-shape holographic amplitude cannot describe the full near-threshold data with one overall normalization, making the polarization filters tests of the production tensor rather than predictions of a globally validated reaction model.

</details>


### [325] [Sub-MeV gamma-ray lines from an endothermic interpretation of the LZ recoil candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33952)
*Maíra Dutra,Jacinto P. Neto,Clarissa Siqueira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 重暗物质吸热上散射可解释LZ高能核反冲候选事件，反冲能正比于质量劈裂δ；若较重态辐射衰变，则同一δ对应能量约为δ的伽马射线谱线，落在COSI（2027年发射）亚MeV探测范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）近期报告了一个高能核反冲候选事件，需要寻找合理的物理解释。重暗物质吸热上散射（endothermic upscattering）提供了一个有吸引力的解释框架，其反冲能E_R ∝ δ（δ为暗物质态间的质量劈裂）。然而，宇宙学和实验约束要求原初较重态被耗竭，需要通过银河系晕中的机制重新产生。作者希望找到一种可检验的观测信号来验证这一解释。

Method: 作者提出并分析以下机制：若较重暗物质态发生辐射衰变，则相同的质量劈裂δ将决定伽马射线谱线的能量，即E_γ ≃ δ。作者以此建立了反冲信号与伽马射线谱线信号之间的直接关联，并针对基准情形评估该谱线是否落在Compton Spectrometer and Imager（COSI，计划2027年发射）的预期探测范围内。

Result: 对于所采用的基准情形，该伽马射线谱线位于亚MeV能区，处于COSI的预期探测灵敏度（projected reach）范围内。

Conclusion: 若LZ候选事件被确认为暗物质信号，则这一解释将推动对亚MeV伽马射线谱线的专门搜寻；COSI为检验该解释提供了及时的观测机会。

Abstract: Endothermic upscattering of heavy dark matter offers a compelling interpretation of the high-energy nuclear-recoil candidate recently reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ), with a characteristic recoil energy $E_R \propto δ$, where $δ$ is the mass splitting between the dark matter states. Cosmological and experimental constraints motivate the depletion of the primordial heavier state, which can be regenerated in the Galactic halo. We show that, if the heavier state decays radiatively, the same mass splitting sets the energy of a gamma-ray line, $E_γ\simeq δ$. For our benchmark, this leads to a sub-MeV line within the projected reach of the Compton Spectrometer and Imager (COSI), scheduled for launch in 2027. The confirmation of the LZ candidate event as a dark matter signal would therefore motivate dedicated searches for sub-MeV gamma-ray lines, with COSI providing a timely opportunity to test this interpretation.

</details>


### [326] [Finite-Size Effects on Symmetry Restoration in a Rotating Scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34038)
*Jorge David Castaño-Yepes,Larry A. Cerón-Suárez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究有限旋转圆柱区域中Z2对称性恢复，用背景场法推导单圈有效势并保持有限几何引起的空间结构，结合热自能与环重求和得到空间分辨有效势，其空间平均显示转变温度对横向尺寸有显式依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨旋转与有限体积几何对Z2对称性恢复的综合影响，特别是转变温度是否仅由光柱因果约束决定，还是也反映有限旋转系统的离散谱结构。

Method: 在刚性旋转的有限圆柱区域内的实λφ4理论中，采用Dirichlet边界条件得到离散Fourier-Bessel谱和显式径向依赖的涨落传播子；使用背景场法推导保留有限几何空间结构的单圈有效势；有限温度下旋转通过热模能量的角动量依赖位移进入，计算重合点传播子得到位置依赖热自能，并通过环重求和纳入有效势。

Result: 得到同时依赖背景场、温度、角速度和横向尺寸的空间分辨有效势；其空间平均给出的对称性恢复温度随旋转态和有限体积谱变化，且旋转对转变温度的修正保留对横向尺寸的显式依赖，表明其反映有限旋转系统的谱结构而非仅光柱因果约束。

Conclusion: 旋转修正的对称性恢复温度在有限旋转系统中具有横向尺寸依赖性，说明有限体积谱结构在旋转诱导的相变中起重要作用。

Abstract: We investigate $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry restoration in a real $λφ^4$ theory confined to a finite cylindrical region undergoing rigid rotation. The finite transverse extent is imposed through Dirichlet boundary conditions, resulting in a discrete Fourier-Bessel spectrum and an explicit radial dependence of the fluctuation propagator. Using the background-field method, we derive the one-loop effective potential while retaining the spatial structure induced by the finite geometry. At finite temperature, rotation enters through the angular-momentum-dependent shifts of the thermal mode energies, coupling the rotational state to the discrete transverse spectrum. The resulting coincident-point propagator defines a position-dependent thermal self-energy, which is incorporated into the effective potential through ring resummation. This yields a spatially resolved effective potential whose structure depends simultaneously on the background field, temperature, angular velocity, and transverse size. We then use its spatial average to determine the symmetry-restoration temperature and study its dependence on the rotational state and the finite-volume spectrum. The results show that the rotational modification of the transition temperature retains an explicit dependence on the transverse size, reflecting the spectral structure of the finite rotating system rather than solely the causal constraint associated with the light cylinder.

</details>


### [327] [Pseudospectra in Holographic QCD Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34084)
*Luiz F. Ferreira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用伪谱分析研究非共形全息QCD模型中准正规模的谱稳定性，发现其随温度呈现非平庸依赖，并可作为强子解离与熔化的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息QCD模型中存在内禀的有量纲标度，可能使准正规模的谱稳定性对温度产生非平庸依赖，而这一稳定性此前未被系统刻画，且需要一种补充性的谱失稳诊断手段来表征强子熔化。

Method: 将运动方程表述为线性本征值问题，通过伪谱（pseudospectrum）量化准正规模谱对微小扰动的失稳，并据此研究软墙模型（标量与矢量场）和解析改进全息QCD（IHQCD）模型（张量胶球涨落）。

Result: 准正规模的谱稳定性对温度呈非平庸依赖；温度升高时准正频率虚部增大，衰减率增加、强子激发相干性逐渐丧失；足够高温下准正频率近似线性依赖于温度，但该区间仅在相应强子态熔化后才达到；伪谱失稳的增长与谱函数行为进行了对比。

Conclusion: 伪谱失稳的增长为谱稳定性的丧失提供了补充性刻画，可作为全息QCD中强子解离与熔化的有用诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral stability of quasinormal modes in nonconformal holographic QCD models using pseudospectral analysis. We first consider the soft-wall model, focusing on scalar and vector fields, and then extend the analysis to the analytic Improved Holographic QCD (IHQCD) model, where we study tensor glueball fluctuations. In both cases, the equations of motion are formulated as linear eigenvalue problems, allowing the instability of the quasinormal spectrum to small perturbations to be quantified through the pseudospectrum. We find that the spectral stability of the quasinormal modes exhibits a nontrivial dependence on temperature, reflecting the presence of intrinsic dimensionful scales in holographic QCD models. As the temperature increases, the magnitude of the imaginary part of the quasinormal frequencies grows, signaling an increasing decay rate and the progressive loss of coherence of the corresponding hadronic excitations. At sufficiently high temperatures, the quasinormal frequencies approach an approximately linear dependence on temperature, although this regime is reached only after the associated hadronic states have melted. We further compare the pseudospectral instability with the behavior of the corresponding spectral functions. Our results indicate that the growth of pseudospectral instability provides a complementary characterization of the loss of spectral stability and can serve as a useful diagnostic of hadronic dissociation and melting in holographic QCD.

</details>


### [328] [Freeze-in of anapole and charge-radius inelastic dark matter: X-ray probes of a decoupled vector portal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34230)
*Krzysztof Jodłowski,Chih-Ting Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究通过维度6跃迁反偶极子和电荷半径算符与标准模型耦合的非弹性暗物质(iDM)的冻结产生及实验探测，重点关注质量劈裂低于双电子阈值的区域，结合X射线限制、直接探测和加速器约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过解耦矢量入口产生非弹性暗物质的冻结机制，并利用X射线观测作为探测手段，同时研究重加热温度对iDM产生的影响。

Method: 在欧拉-海森堡极限下解析计算衰变率和光子谱，使用完整单圈振幅评估有限电子质量修正，并结合X射线限制、冻结产生遗迹密度目标以及直接探测和加速器约束。

Result: 在Δ=900 keV时，INTEGRAL/SPI可探测冻结产生目标的玻尔兹曼压制高质分支；而在Δ=100 keV时，NuSTAR限制显著较弱，原因是衰变宽度对劈裂的陡峭依赖。存活的χ₁粒子导致直接探测实验中的放热下散射特征，可能解释LZ信号。

Conclusion: X射线观测可作为通过解耦矢量入口探测冻结产生以及控制iDM产生的重加热温度的有效手段。

Abstract: We study freeze-in production and experimental probes of inelastic dark matter (iDM) coupled to the Standard Model through dimension-6 transition anapole and charge-radius operators. These interactions arise upon integrating out the heavy dark photon of a broken $U(1)_D$ vector portal. We focus on mass splittings below the dielectron threshold, where the excited-state decay is cosmologically long-lived and decays predominantly through $χ_1\toχ_0+3γ$, producing a distinctive keV--MeV photon spectrum. We calculate the decay rate and photon spectrum analytically in the Euler--Heisenberg limit and evaluate finite-electron-mass corrections using the full one-loop amplitude. We then combine X-ray limits with freeze-in relic density targets and complementary direct detection (DD) and accelerator constraints. The surviving $χ_1$ population also leads to exothermic downscattering signatures at DD experiments, including the possibility of explaining the LZ signal, while the three-photon decay is constrained by X-ray data. At $Δ=900\,\mathrm{keV}$, INTEGRAL/SPI probes the Boltzmann-suppressed high-mass branches of the freeze-in targets, whereas the NuSTAR limits at $Δ=100\,\mathrm{keV}$ are substantially weaker because of the steep splitting dependence of the decay width. Our results demonstrate the potential of X-ray observations as a probe of freeze-in through a decoupled vector portal and of the reheating temperature governing the iDM production.

</details>


### [329] [Next-to-Next-to-Leading Order QCD Corrections to $η_t \to HZ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34364)
*Cai-Ping Jia,Yan-Qing Ma,Huai-Min Yu,Yu-Jie Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NRQCD因子化框架下计算了η_t→HZ硬散射短距离系数的NNLO QCD修正，结果显著压低领先阶宽度，为未来阈值研究提供必要输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期LHC在t\bar t系统中观测到与toponium类似动力学一致的阈值增强现象启发，需要研究η_t→HZ这一通道，并评估其对top-Higgs相互作用的敏感性，因此需精确计算其硬系数的高阶QCD修正。

Method: 在NRQCD因子化框架下计算η_t→HZ硬短距离系数的NNLO QCD修正，保留对Higgs和Z玻色子质量的完整依赖，包含双圈虚修正与实双胶子发射通道，并检验γ_5方案依赖性及重整化群结构一致性。

Result: NLO有限质量结果接近大质量极限；NNLO系数出现μ_R与μ_Λ的对数。取μ_R=μ_Λ=m_t时，NLO和NNLO项分别将领先阶宽度压低约29%和19%，两圈贡献约为单圈的三分之二且同号，可能达到领先阶宽度的约52%。

Conclusion: 微扰级数收敛缓慢，NNLO预测约为领先阶宽度的52%；该系数为未来η_t→HZ阈值研究及top-Higgs相互作用敏感性评估提供了必要输入，并通过大质量极限复现、γ_5方案无关性和重整化群结构一致性三项检验。

Abstract: Motivated by recent LHC observations of a threshold enhancement in the $t\bar t$ system consistent with toponium-like dynamics, we compute the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) QCD correction to the hard short-distance coefficient for $η_t\to HZ$, where $η_t$ denotes the pseudoscalar color-singlet configuration $t\bar t({}^1S_0^{[1]})$. Within the NRQCD factorization framework, we retain the full dependence on the Higgs- and $Z$-boson masses and include both the two-loop virtual corrections and the real double-gluon-emission channel. At next-to-leading order (NLO), the finite-mass result remains close to its large-mass limit. The NNLO coefficient develops logarithms of both the renormalization scale $μ_R$ and the NRQCD factorization scale $μ_Λ$. For $μ_R=μ_Λ=m_t$, the NLO and NNLO terms suppress the leading-order width by about $29\%$ and $19\%$, respectively; the two-loop contribution is about two thirds of the one-loop effect and of the same sign, so the perturbative series converges slowly and the NNLO prediction amounts to about $52\%$ of the leading-order width. The resulting coefficient provides a necessary ingredient for future threshold studies of $η_t\to HZ$ and for assessing the sensitivity of this channel to the top-Higgs interaction. The accuracy of the result is further supported by three checks: reproduction of the known large-mass limit at one loop, independence of the extracted coefficient on the $γ_5$ prescription, and consistency of its scale logarithms with the renormalization-group structure.

</details>


### [330] [Radial Excitation Spectra of Light Pseudoscalar and Vector Mesons in Light-Front Holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34644)
*Abhisth Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在光前全息QCD（LFHQCD）框架下，将横向禁闭势从标准软墙模型扩展为加入库仑项与对数项，数值求解光前薛定谔方程并结合BdT纵向处方，计算π、ρ、K*介子家族的径向激发谱与Regge轨迹，将平均偏差从约7.6%降至5.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD非微扰区难以解析处理，LFHQCD提供了研究强子结构的半经典有效框架。但标准软墙模型配合BdT纵向处方虽能较好描述介子基态，对π家族径向激发态却存在约17—18%的偏差，需改进横向禁闭势以提高径向激发谱的精度。

Method: 在标准软墙势基础上引入短程库仑贡献和中程对数贡献来扩展横向禁闭势；数值求解相应的光前薛定谔方程得到横向质量谱，再与BdT纵向处方结合，得到完整的介子质量谱，并计算π、ρ、K*家族的径向激发谱及Regge轨迹。

Result: 相对于标准软墙模型，修改后的框架将实验数据的平均偏差由约7.6%降低到5.2%；对用于拟合的九态数据集，在描述径向激发态方面约提升32%。

Conclusion: 短程库仑贡献与中程对数贡献对在光前全息方法中改进介子径向谱具有重要作用，说明扩展横向禁闭势是提升LFHQCD对径向激发态描述能力的有效途径。

Abstract: The non-perturbative regime of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) poses significant challenges for analytical treatment. Light-Front Holographic QCD (LFHQCD) offers an effective semiclassical framework for exploring hadronic structure through a holographic dual description of confinement. While the standard soft-wall model, along with the Brodsky-de Téramond (BdT) longitudinal prescription, provides satisfactory results for meson ground states, it shows deviations of around 17 to $18\%$ for radial excitations of the pion family. In this work, we extend the transverse confining potential by adding Coulombic and logarithmic contributions beyond the standard soft-wall framework. We solve the resulting light-front Schrödinger equation numerically, and we combine the transverse mass spectrum with the BdT longitudinal prescription to obtain the complete meson mass spectrum. We compute the radial excitation spectra of the $π$, $ρ$, and $K^{*}$ meson families and analyze their Regge trajectories. The modified framework reduces the overall average deviation from experimental data from about $7.6\%$ to $5.2\%$ compared to the standard soft-wall model, representing a $\sim 32\%$ improvement for the fitted nine-state dataset in describing radially excited states. The results show that short-range Coulombic and intermediate-range logarithmic contributions are important for refining the radial meson spectrum within the light-front holographic approach.

</details>


### [331] [Soft-Collinear Chiral Perturbation Theory for $B \to π$ form factors at large recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34696)
*Thorsten Feldmann,Jack Jenkins,Jaime del Palacio Lirola*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个由重夸克与手征对称性组织、描述重介子衰变为高能π介子的有效强子理论，并在SCET框架下通过局域化软、共线手征变换，将非局域四夸克算符匹配到强子算符，计算了B→π形状因子中手征对数修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大反冲能量下的排他B→π跃迁涉及软与共线自由度的相互作用，现有描述（如Hard-Pion Chiral Perturbation Theory）给出的手征对数修正模式可能不完整或不自洽，需要一个系统匹配软-共线手征对称性的有效强子理论来刻画重到轻形状因子的软交叠贡献及其π质量依赖。

Method: 基于重夸克与手征对称性构造有效强子理论：动力学自由度为准静态重介子场与软、共线π介子耦合，适用于E_π≈M_B/2的大反冲排他过程。利用SCET中软夸克与共线夸克的因子化，导致两个运动学扇区出现手征对称性的重复；将软、共线手征变换提升为局域对称性，从而系统地将支配重到轻形状因子软交叠贡献的非局域四夸克算符匹配到手征对称性破缺尺度以下的强子有效算符。作为首次应用，计算领头强子算符的单圈矩阵元，得到大反冲下B→π形状因子中对数增强的π质量依赖。

Result: 通过计算领头强子算符的单圈矩阵元，得到了B→π形状因子在大反冲下对数增强的π质量依赖；发现有效理论中的非局域跃迁流所导致的手征对数修正模式与Hard-Pion Chiral Perturbation Theory现有表述不相容。

Conclusion: 该有效强子理论为研究重介子衰变中软-共线手征动力学提供了系统框架，其结果表明现有基于Hard-Pion Chiral Perturbation Theory的手征对数修正描述需要修正，非局域跃迁流的贡献不可忽略。

Abstract: We develop an effective hadronic theory for heavy-meson decays into energetic pions, organized by the approximate heavy-quark and chiral symmetries. The dynamical degrees of freedom of the theory consist of quasi-static heavy-meson fields coupled to soft and collinear pions, relevant for exclusive $B \to π$ transitions at large recoil energy ${E_π\simeq M_B/2}$. Our approach exploits the factorization of soft and collinear quarks in Soft-Collinear Effective Theory (SCET), leading to an emergent duplication of chiral symmetry in the two kinematic sectors. Promoting the soft and collinear chiral transformations to local symmetries enables us to systematically match non-local four-quark operators in SCET -- governing the soft-overlap contribution to heavy-to-light form factors -- onto effective operators constructed from hadronic fields below the scale of chiral symmetry breaking. As a first application, we compute the logarithmically enhanced pion-mass dependence of the $B \to π$ form factors at large recoil by calculating the one-loop matrix elements of the leading hadronic operators. We find that the non-local transition currents in our effective theory lead to a pattern of chiral-logarithmic corrections that is not compatible with the existing formulation provided by Hard-Pion Chiral Perturbation Theory.

</details>


### [332] [Termination of the Radial Ladder in Mixed-Flavor Heavy Mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34734)
*Wen-Xuan Zhang,Wen-Nian Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过求解含屏蔽效应的 Godfrey-Isgur 哈密顿量并结合高斯展开方法，研究了混合味介子 $D_s$、$B_s$、$B_c$ 的径向激发谱是否因轻夸克对产生导致的禁闭屏蔽而终止。结果显示谱在有限质量处饱和，径向梯子在 $D_s$ 中最先终止，其次 $B_s$，最后 $B_c$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子谱学的核心开放问题之一是：当禁闭势被轻夸克对产生所屏蔽时，重介子的径向激发阶梯是否会在有限能级处终止。论文针对混合味介子 $D_s$、$B_s$、$B_c$ 系统性地回答这一问题。

Method: 采用高斯展开方法求解带有屏蔽效应的 Godfrey-Isgur 哈密顿量。屏蔽长度由依赖于阈值的弦断裂分析确定，并与粲偶素和底偶素锚点进行匹配。

Result: 计算谱在 $D_s$、$B_s$、$B_c$ 家族中分别饱和于 $3.36$、$6.78$、$8.19~{\rm GeV}$；同时 RMS 半径增长数 fm，质量间隙降至 $10~{\rm MeV}$ 以下。质量-半径解耦给出安全径向梯子终点约为 $n_{\mathrm{safe}}\approx 4$--$6$（$D_s$）、$5$--$7$（$B_s$）、$9$--$11$（$B_c$）。在极限质量窗口之上（$3.49$、$6.86$、$8.24~{\rm GeV}$），径向阶梯的态无法被观测到。

Conclusion: 径向梯子的终止顺序为 $D_s$ 最先、$B_s$ 其次、$B_c$ 最后，该终止现象可由 BESIII、Belle II 和 LHCb 即将获得的高统计量数据直接检验。

Abstract: A fundamental open question in hadron spectroscopy is whether the radial excitation ladder of heavy mesons terminates at a finite level once confinement is screened by light-quark pair creation. We address this question for the mixed-flavor mesons $D_s$, $B_s$, and $B_c$ by solving a screened Godfrey--Isgur Hamiltonian with the Gaussian expansion method. The screening length is fixed by threshold-dependent string breaking analysis, and is matched to the charmonium and bottomonium anchors. The calculated spectra saturate at $3.36$, $6.78$, and $8.19~{\rm GeV}$ in $D_s$, $B_s$, and $B_c$ families, while the RMS radii grow by several fm and mass gaps fall below $10~{\rm MeV}$. Mass-radius decoupling places the end of the safe radial ladder at $n_{\mathrm{safe}}\approx 4$--$6$ for $D_s$, $5$--$7$ for $B_s$, and $9$--$11$ for $B_c$, so the ladder terminates first in $D_s$, then in $B_s$, and last in $B_c$. Above the limiting-mass windows, at $3.49$, $6.86$, and $8.24~{\rm GeV}$, states of the radial ladder cannot be discovered. This termination is directly testable with forthcoming high-statistics data from BESIII, Belle II, and LHCb.

</details>


### [333] [On the status of the 4-loop splitting functions in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34837)
*S. Moch,A. Vogt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了强子部分子分布标度依赖的四圈分裂函数的最新结果，非单态情形已完全确定，单态情形展现出新的解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的演化核是精确确定部分子分布及其不确定度的关键，四圈分裂函数是当前高阶微扰计算的前沿目标。

Method: 计算四圈分裂函数，并分析其解析结构与数值影响。

Result: 三个非单态情形已完全已知，结果符合绝大多数预期，但出现低阶未见的某个小x双对数；单态情形从现有部分结果看已包含以往分裂函数中未见过的解析结构。四圈贡献对标度微分的数值效应（及单态情形的不确定度）在α_s=0.2基准点、x低至约10^-4时约为1%或更小，总价夸克分布除外。

Conclusion: 四圈分裂函数已取得重要进展，非单态完全确定，单态显示新结构，整体数值效应较小。

Abstract: We briefly discuss recent results on the four-loop splitting functions for the scale dependence of the parton distributions of hadrons. The three non-singlet cases are now completely known. The results agree with almost all expectations, but exhibit some small-$x$ double logarithms not encountered at lower orders. The flavour-singlet cases include, as already obvious from the present partial results, analytical structures not seen in the splitting functions up to now. The numerical effects (and their uncertainties, in the singlet cases) of the four-loop contributions to the scale derivatives amount to about $1\%$ or less down to momentum fractions $x$ as low as about $10^{-4}$ at a standard benchmark point with $α_{\rm s} = 0.2$, with the exception of the total valence quark distribution.

</details>


### [334] [Twist-4 GTMDs of sea quarks at zero skewness in the light-cone quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34937)
*Xiaoyan Luan,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 采用光锥形式论中的重叠表示，在零偏度下研究了质子内反上夸克和反下夸克的twist-4广义横动量依赖部分子分布（GTMDs），基于重子-介子涨落模型导出的光锥波函数计算了相关分布，并探讨了GTMDs与广义部分子分布（GPDs）之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质子内部反夸克的高扭度（twist-4）三维结构，特别是GTMDs所蕴含的横动量与纵向动量关联信息，对于揭示质子的非微扰内部结构和轨道角动量等问题具有重要意义。

Method: 在光锥形式论框架下采用重叠表示，使用由重子-介子涨落模型基于|q q̄ B⟩福克态导出的质子光锥波函数，计算质子的反上夸克与反下夸克的twist-4 GTMDs，并推导其与GPDs的关系。

Result: 给出了零偏度下反上夸克和反下夸克的twist-4 GTMDs及twist-4 GPDs的数值结果，并建立了GTMDs与GPDs之间的复杂关联关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为质子内反夸克的高扭度广义横动量依赖结构提供了理论预测，并有助于进一步理解GTMDs与GPDs之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We investigate the twist-4 generalized transverse momentum dependent parton distributions (GTMDs) of $\bar{u}$ and $\bar{d}$ quarks inside the proton at zero skewness by adopting the overlap representation within the light-cone formalism. Using the light-cone wave functions of the proton derived from the baryon-meson fluctuation model in terms of the $|q\bar{q}B\rangle$ Fock states, we calculate the twist-4 GTMDs of $\bar{u}$ and $\bar{d}$ quarks. Additionally, the intricate relations between the GTMDs and the generalized parton distributions are also explored in our research. Numerical results for these twist-4 GTMDs and twist-4 GPDs at zero skewness are analyzed and presented.

</details>


### [335] [A First Glimpse at Super-Leading Logarithms in Monte Carlo Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34984)
*Nicolas Schalch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 将基于有效场论的蒙特卡洛非全局重求框架 Marzili 扩展至可重求涉及两次 Glauber 交换的超领头对数，首次实现超领头对数的蒙特卡洛重求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强子对撞机上，Glauber 交换产生的超领头对数使非全局可观测量的重求变得复杂，而此前缺乏对这类对数的蒙特卡洛重求手段。

Method: 在基于有效场论的 Marzili 蒙特卡洛非全局重求框架基础上进行扩展，纳入两次 Glauber 交换所对应的最低非零色贡献。

Result: 实现了首个超领头对数的蒙特卡洛重求，并与 Marzili 原有的非全局对数处理相结合。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究超领头对数与非全局对数之间的相互作用，以及将 Glauber 交换纳入部分子簇射开辟了路径。

Abstract: Super-leading logarithms from Glauber exchange complicate the resummation of non-global observables at hadron colliders. In this Letter, we extend Marzili, a Monte Carlo framework for non-global resummation based on effective field theory, to resum the leading nonzero colour contribution involving two Glauber exchanges. This is the first Monte Carlo resummation of super-leading logarithms. Together with Marzili's treatment of non-global logarithms, this opens a path towards studying their interplay and incorporating Glauber exchange into parton showers.

</details>


### [336] [Meson photoproduction at Jefferson Lab: from two-meson final states to meson-baryon spin-density matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35045)
*Vincent Mathieu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了JPAC与Jefferson Lab的GlueX、CLAS实验合作在介子光生研究方面的进展，重点讨论了ηπ系统光生过程的成功理解，以及为寻找最轻混杂介子π₁(1600)而开展的ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺光生完整角分布推导工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最轻混杂介子π₁(1600)预期主要衰变为b₁π，因此含反冲Δ⁺⁺的反应（如γp→b₁πΔ⁺⁺）是寻找奇异态的有前景通道。作为迈向该目标的第一步，需要先理解类似的γp→ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺过程。

Method: 推导了线偏振光子束下γp→ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺反应的完整角分布，涉及56个双指标自旋密度矩阵元以描述ρ与Δ衰变之间的关联；并分析当产生振幅因子化（如单Regge极点交换情形）时的简化形式。

Result: γp→ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺的完整角分布被成功导出，包含56个双指标自旋密度矩阵元。当振幅因子化时，强度简化为标准ρ和Δ衰变分布的乘积，仅依赖14个参数。

Conclusion: ηπ系统光生过程已从a₂(1320)共振区到双Regge交换主导的高质量区得到良好理解；ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺反应角分布的推导为搜寻π₁(1600)等奇异混杂介子奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent progress in the understanding of meson photoproduction, obtained from the joint effort of the Joint Physics Analysis Center (JPAC) and the GlueX and CLAS experiments at Jefferson Lab, is reviewed. The photoproduction of the $ηπ$ system is now well understood, from the $a_2(1320)$ resonance region to the high-mass region dominated by double Regge exchanges. Since the lightest hybrid meson, the $π_1(1600)$, is expected to decay predominantly into $b_1π$, reactions with a recoiling $Δ^{++}$ are promising channels for exotic searches. As a first step towards $γp \to b_1 πΔ^{++}$, the complete angular distribution of $γp \to ρ^-Δ^{++}$ with a linearly polarized photon beam is derived. It involves 56 double-index spin-density matrix elements, which describe the correlations between the $ρ$ and the $Δ$ decays.
