When the production amplitudes factorize, as is the case for the exchange of a single Regge pole, the intensity reduces to the product of the standard $ρ$ and $Δ$ decay distributions and depends on only 14 parameters.

</details>


### [337] [Parameter degeneracy and information loss in inverse flavor mapping for high-energy astrophysical neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35132)
*Zi-Qiang Chen,Zhi-zhong Xing,Ye-Ling Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高能天体物理中微子源味比 η 与探测器测得味比 f 之间通过 f = Pη 的映射，重点分析逆映射 η = P⁻¹f 在 det P = 0 附近存在的参数简并和信息损失问题，并利用 IceCube 全天空中微子通量数据探讨源味分布的还原程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实逆味映射 η = P⁻¹f 存在不可避免的参数简并和信息损失，尤其是在当前中微子振荡数据允许的 det P = 0 极限附近，这限制了从探测器测量反推源味分布的能力。

Method: 探索 det P = 0 的参数关联条件（包括 μ-τ 反射对称情形），推导对 f_e、f_μ 与 η_e、η_μ 的相应约束，并在此基础上利用 IceCube 从 5 TeV 到 10 PeV 的全天空中微子通量数据进行分析，假设相关源具有共同的味组成。

Result: 给出了 det P = 0 的参数关联条件及 μ-τ 反射对称情形下的约束关系，并对 f_e、f_μ 与 η_e、η_μ 建立了约束公式，说明了利用 IceCube 全天空数据可在何种程度上还原源味分布。

Conclusion: 在 det P = 0 极限附近，逆味映射存在不可避免的简并与信息损失，源味分布只能被有限程度地还原；借助 IceCube 数据可在共同味组成假设下评估该还原能力。

Abstract: Given a high-energy astrophysical neutrino flux, its source flavor ratios $η= \{η^{}_e, η^{}_μ, η^{}_τ\}$ are correlated with the ones $f = \{f^{}_e, f^{}_μ, f^{}_τ\}$ measured at a neutrino telescope via $f = P η$, where the elements of $P$ consist of four lepton flavor mixing parameters. But realistic {\it inverse} flavor mapping $η= P^{-1} f$ encounters unavoidable parameter degeneracy and information loss, especially in or near the $\det P = 0$ limit allowed by current neutrino oscillation data. In this work we explore the parameter correlation condition for $\det P = 0$ including the $μ$-$τ$ reflection symmetry case, formulate the corresponding constraints on $f^{}_e$ and $f^{}_μ$ versus $η^{}_e$ and $η^{}_μ$, and illustrate to what extent the source flavor distribution can be mapped by using the recent IceCube all-sky neutrino flux data ranging from 5 TeV to 10 PeV under the assumption that the relevant sources have a common flavor composition.

</details>


### [338] [The $Dπ$ and $D^*π$ femtoscopy puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35133)
*Pablo Encarnación,Miguel Albaladejo,Albert Feijoo,Juan Nieves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出包含强相互作用与库仑相互作用的耦合道相对论动量空间 femtoscopic 关联函数框架，应用于 $D\pi$、$D^*\pi$ 体系，发现与 ALICE 实验提取结果存在显著张力，指出实验拟合窗口假设导致提取的散射参数不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: femtoscopy 有望成为低能强子相互作用精密研究的定量工具，但理论与 ALICE 实验在 $K\pi$、$D\pi$ 等体系的关联函数提取结果存在未解张力，需要厘清其起源。

Method: 将 Vincent–Phatak 方案推广到三维 Bethe–Salpeter 方程，结合完全相对论处理与通过波函数匹配实现的正确库仑渐近行为；采用重现 $D_0^*(2300)$ 双极点结构的耦合道重介子手征相互作用，计算 $D\pi$ 与 $D^*\pi$ 体系的关联函数。

Result: 计算结果与已有理论一致，但与 ALICE 协作组提取的关联函数显著不同；张力源于实验分析假设关联函数在相对动量 100 MeV 以上趋于 1，而该区域仍受较低 $D_0^*(2300)$ 极点影响；在不同拟合窗口重复提取显示信号强烈依赖所选区间。$D^*\pi$ 体系存在与重夸克自旋对称性密切相关的同样问题。

Conclusion: 现有 $D\pi$ 散射参数不稳健，需要对实验数据进行专门重新分析；结合 ALICE $K\pi$ 测量的张力，亟需澄清该谜题起源，以确立 femtoscopy 作为低能强子相互作用精密研究的可靠定量工具。

Abstract: We present a relativistic momentum-space framework for femtoscopic correlation functions including strong and Coulomb interactions in coupled channels. The approach extends the Vincent--Phatak prescription to the three-dimensional Bethe--Salpeter equation, combining a fully relativistic treatment with the correct Coulomb asymptotics through wave-function matching. We apply it to the $Dπ$ system using a coupled-channel heavy-meson chiral interaction that reproduces the established two-pole structure of the $D_0^*(2300)$. Our correlation functions agree with previous theoretical calculations but differ significantly from those extracted by the ALICE Collaboration. We trace this tension to the assumption made in the experimental analysis that the genuine correlation function reaches unity above a relative momentum of $100$ MeV, whereas this region remains affected by the lower $D_0^*(2300)$ pole. Repeating the extraction over different fitting windows reveals a strong dependence of the extracted signal on the chosen range, demonstrating that the resulting $Dπ$ scattering parameters are not robust and motivating a dedicated reanalysis of the experimental data. We extend the analysis to the $D^*π$ system, where the same issue arises in close connection with heavy-quark spin symmetry. Together with the unresolved tension between theoretical calculations and the ALICE $Kπ$ femtoscopic measurements, this highlights the need to clarify the origin of this puzzle, with the aim of establishing femtoscopy as a reliable quantitative tool for precision studies of low-energy hadron interactions.

</details>


### [339] [Perturbative QCD correction to the nonleptonic weak rate in dense quark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35165)
*Matti Heikinheimo,Hanna Lempiäinen,Risto Paatelainen,Tomi Ruosteoja,Kimmo Tuominen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了稠密夸克物质中非轻子弱味平衡速率的领头微扰QCD修正，基于Kadanoff-Baym动力学方程和实时热场论，修正为规范不变且完全有限，在低温致密星相关区域使树图速率增强30-40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密夸克物质中非轻子弱味平衡过程是致密星体动力学（如体粘滞性）的关键微观输入，但过去三十余年唯象计算一直沿用树图级速率，缺乏微扰QCD修正，精度不足。

Method: 基于Kadanoff-Baym动力学方程，采用实时热场论计算领头微扰QCD修正，并检验规范不变性与发散抵消情况。

Result: 所得QCD修正规范不变且完全有限；软发散与共线发散在联合实修正和虚修正后依Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg定理完全抵消；在致密星相关的低温区、微扰QCD适用高密度范围内，修正使树图速率增强30-40%。

Conclusion: 该修正速率改进了沿用三十余年的树图结果，为稠密夸克物质体粘滞性计算提供了更准确的微观输入。

Abstract: We present the first calculation of the leading perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD) correction to the nonleptonic weak flavor-equilibration rate in dense quark matter. The computation is based on the Kadanoff-Baym kinetic equation and performed using real-time thermal field theory. The resulting QCD correction is both gauge invariant and fully finite. In particular, all soft and collinear divergences cancel after combining real and virtual corrections, in accordance with the Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg theorem. In the low-temperature regime relevant to compact-star dynamics, the correction enhances the tree-level rate by $30 - 40\%$ over the high-density range where perturbative QCD is applicable. The corrected rate improves on the tree-level result used in phenomenological calculations for more than three decades, providing a more accurate microscopic input for bulk-viscosity calculations in dense quark matter.

</details>


### [340] [Purely flavon driven leptogenesis for exactly degenerate Dirac or Majorana neutrino mass matrix in the type-I seesaw model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35299)
*Yan Shao,Zhen-hua Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用味对称模型中固有的flavon场来克服传统leptogenesis在高度约束的中微子质量结构下失效的问题，为中微子味结构与leptogenesis之间建立自然联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I型跷跷板模型可同时解释中微子质量起源和宇宙重子不对称，味对称性又能自然地解释观测到的中微子混合模式。然而，味对称性预言的高度约束的中微子质量结构可能使传统leptogenesis失效：当Dirac中微子质量矩阵或Majorana右手中微子质量矩阵严格简并（正比于单位矩阵）时，右手中微子衰变的CP不对称会因Yukawa耦合列的正交性或质量严格简并而消失。本文旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 作者提出味对称中微子质量模型中天然存在的flavon场可以克服上述障碍。针对M_D正比于I的情形，flavon-右手中微子相互作用诱导额外衰变通道N_I→N_J φ，并产生新的CP破坏贡献；针对M_R正比于I的情形，由重矢量费米子媒介的三体衰变N_I→L_α H φ即使在右手中微子质量严格简并时也能提供CP不对称来源。

Result: flavon-RHN相互作用在M_D ∝ I时通过额外衰变通道N_I→N_J φ产生新的CP破坏贡献；三体衰变N_I→L_α H φ在M_R ∝ I时提供CP不对称来源。两种情形下均成功恢复了leptogenesis所需的CP不对称。

Conclusion: flavon部门为中微子味结构与leptogenesis之间提供了自然联系，为基于味对称性的中微子质量模型提出了一种新的leptogenesis机制。

Abstract: While the type-I seesaw model provides an attractive framework for simultaneously explaining the origin of neutrino masses and the baryon asymmetry of the Universe, flavor symmetries offer a natural approach to understanding the observed neutrino mixing pattern. However, the highly constrained neutrino mass structures predicted by flavor symmetries may also prevent conventional leptogenesis to work. In particular, when the Dirac neutrino mass matrix $M^{}_{\rm D}$ or the Majorana right-handed-neutrino (RHN) mass matrix $M^{}_{\rm R}$ is exactly degenerate (namely, they are proportional to the identity matrix $I$), the CP asymmetry of RHN decays will vanish either due to the orthogonality of different neutrino Yukawa coupling columns or the exact degeneracy of RHN masses. In this work, we propose that the flavon fields (which are inherently present in flavor-symmetry neutrino mass models to be responsible for generating the nontrivial neutrino flavor structure) can naturally overcome these obstacles. For the case of $M^{}_{\rm D} \propto I$, flavon--RHN interactions induce additional decay channels $N_I \to N_J φ$ and generate new CP-violating contributions to RHN decays. For the case of $M^{}_{\rm R} \propto I$, three-body decays $N_I\rightarrow L^{}_αHφ$ mediated by heavy vectorlike fermions provide a CP asymmetry source even when the RHN masses are exactly degenerate. Our results demonstrate that the flavon sector provides a natural connection between neutrino flavor structure and leptogenesis, offering a new mechanism for leptogenesis in flavor-symmetry-based neutrino mass models.

</details>


### [341] [System-size dependence of jet quenching from light to heavy-ion collisions at LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35321)
*Man Xie,Xiang-Yu Wu,Han-Zhong Zhang,Xin-Nian Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于次领头阶pQCD模型，系统研究了LHC能量下O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe和Pb+Pb碰撞中单举强子抑制，模型可同时描述各系统与中心度下的带电强子R_AA，并揭示喷注淬火的系统尺寸依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单举强子抑制是探测部分子能量损失与QGP中喷注输运系数的敏感探针，而喷注淬火的系统尺寸依赖可进一步约束其动力学机制。鉴于LHC轻离子碰撞的新实验数据，有必要系统检验模型在不同碰撞系统下的普适性。

Method: 采用次领头阶微扰QCD模型，纳入核修正的初始态部分子分布、基于higher-twist方法的末态部分子能量损失，以及来自RHIC和LHC数据贝叶斯分析得到的喷注输运系数温度依赖关系 q̂/T³。计算O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe和Pb+Pb在LHC能量下的单举带电强子R_AA。

Result: 模型计算可同时描述O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe和Pb+Pb碰撞在广泛事件中心度及最小偏置事件下测得的单举带电强子R_AA。通过R_NeNe/R_OO比值以及R_AA随⟨N_coll⟩的变化展示喷注淬火的系统尺寸依赖，在固定p_T下呈现共同行为。

Conclusion: 不同碰撞系统中R_AA随⟨N_coll⟩遵循共同行为，表明即使在最小碰撞系统中也已形成夸克-胶子等离子体。

Abstract: Single inclusive hadron suppression in high-energy heavy-ion collisions provides a sensitive probe of parton energy loss and jet transport coefficient in quark-gluon plasma (QGP). System size dependence of jet quenching can provide further constraints on the dynamics of jet quenching and parton energy loss. Motivated by recent experiments on light-ion collisions at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), we perform a systematic study of single-inclusive hadron suppression in O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe and Pb+Pb collisions at the LHC energies. The calculations are carried out within the next-to-leading-order perturbative QCD model, incorporating the nuclear modified initial-state parton distributions, final-state parton energy loss in the higher-twist approach and the temperature dependence of the jet transport coefficient $\hat q/T^3$ from the Bayesian analyses of previous experimental data at the Relativistic Heavy-ion Collider (RHIC) and LHC. Our model calculations can simultaneously describe the measured single-inclusive charged hadron $R_{AA}$ in O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe and Pb+Pb collisions over a broad range of event centralities and in the minimum-bias events. We further illustrate the system size dependence of jet quenching through the ratio $R_{\mathrm{NeNe}}/R_{\mathrm{OO}}$ and the modification factors $R_{AA}$ as a function of $\langle N_{\mathrm{coll}} \rangle$ which follow a common behavior at fixed $p_{\rm T}$, indicating the formation of QGP even in the smallest collision systems.

</details>


### [342] [Resummation-scale uncertainties in PDF determinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35344)
*Hamed Abdolmaleki,Valerio Bertone,Giuseppe Bozzi,Francesco Giuli,Alexander Glazov,Valentina Guglielmi,Francesco Hautmann,Fred Olness,Pavel Starovoitov,Oleksandr Zenaiev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重求和尺度变化对PDF拟合的影响，通过xFitter量化该理论不确定性并评估其对LHC顶夸克对产生预言的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDF演化与强耦合α_s相关的重求和尺度变化会引入额外理论不确定性，但此前PDF拟合从未考虑这一来源，需加以量化。

Method: 基于开源xFitter框架，在HERA深度非弹性散射数据拟合中引入重求和尺度变化，量化其不确定性，并检验对LHC顶夸克对产生预言的影响。

Result: 重求和尺度不确定性对LHC观测量的影响相当可观。

Conclusion: 在精确QCD分析中纳入重求和尺度这一不确定性来源十分重要。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of resummation-scale variations on parton distribution function (PDF) determinations. These variations introduce an additional source of theoretical uncertainty related to the evolution of PDFs and strong coupling $α_s$ that has never previously been accounted for in PDF fits. Using the open-source xFitter framework, we quantify this uncertainty in fits to HERA deep-inelastic scattering data and examine its implications for predictions of top-quark pair production at the LHC. We show that the resulting resummation-scale uncertainties have a sizable impact on LHC observables, highlighting the importance of including this source of uncertainty in precision QCD analyses.

</details>


### [343] [Differential Higgs production at small-$x$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35458)
*Benedetta Bernardini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在高能精度物理背景下，将HELL框架中的微分重求和技术扩展到胶子聚变希格斯玻色子产生过程，报告了初步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精度物理中，变量 $x=Q^2/s$ 的大对数重求在高能区日益重要，需将现有微分重求形式推广至胶子聚变希格斯产生过程。

Method: 在HELL框架内扩展微分重求（differential resummation）形式，应用于胶子聚变（gluon fusion）希格斯玻色子产生。

Result: 给出了该扩展的初步（preliminary）结果。

Conclusion: HELL框架的微分重求形式可初步推广至胶子聚变希格斯产生，为高能精度计算提供基础。

Abstract: In the context of precision physics, the resummation of large logarithms of the variable $x = Q^2/s$ is increasingly relevant at high energies. We report here preliminary results on the extension of the differential resummation formalism within the HELL framework to Higgs boson production via gluon fusion.

</details>


### [344] [Exploring $U_A(1)$ symmetry restoration in a magnetic field by meson screening masses in the NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35468)
*Bing-Kai Sheng,Zigeng Ding,Danning Li,Xinyang Wang,Lang Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在(2+1)味NJL模型中研究有限温度外磁场下U_A(1)有效对称性恢复，发现纵向屏蔽质量差与磁化率显示轴向磁催化/逆磁催化，横向质量差随eB增大，但三者给出的轴向赝临界温度均随eB降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨有限温度与强磁场环境中U_A(1)轴向对称性的有效恢复行为，尤其是逆磁催化效应下轴向赝临界温度随磁场的变化规律。

Method: 采用(2+1)味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL)模型，引入依赖磁场的四夸克耦合以纳入逆磁催化，并由格点QCD手征赝临界温度约束，同时固定't Hooft耦合；以介子屏蔽质量与介子磁化率作为序参量进行分析。

Result: U_A(1)伙伴粒子δ^0与π^0的纵向屏蔽质量差及其归一化介子磁化率差在低温呈轴向磁催化、在手征跨接附近呈轴向逆磁催化；横向屏蔽质量差在所有温度下随eB增大。然而三种序参量的热拐点所提取的轴向赝临界温度均随eB下降。

Conclusion: 在固定温度下，横向屏蔽质量差增大可与轴向赝临界温度下降并存，表明不同序参量对磁场的响应存在差异，赝临界温度随磁场的降低不依赖于横向质量差的行为。

Abstract: We investigate the effective axial $U_A(1)$ symmetry restoration at finite temperature in an external magnetic field using meson screening masses, as well as meson susceptibilities, in a $(2+1)$-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model. To incorporate inverse magnetic catalysis, we employ a magnetic-field-dependent four-quark coupling constrained by the lattice QCD chiral pseudo-critical temperature, with the 't Hooft coupling fixed. In the lattice-improved NJL model, the longitudinal screening mass difference between the $U_A(1)$ partners $δ^0$ and $π^0$ and their normalized meson susceptibility difference exhibit axial magnetic catalysis at low temperatures and axial inverse magnetic catalysis near the chiral crossover, whereas the transverse screening mass difference increases with $eB$ at all temperatures. Despite these different responses, the axial pseudo-critical temperatures extracted from the thermal inflection points of these three alternative order parameters decrease with $eB$, implying that a decreasing pseudo-critical temperature can coexist with an increasing transverse screening mass difference at fixed temperature.

</details>


### [345] [Magnetic thresholds and spin-resolved spectral moments of $φ$ and $K^{*0}$ in a hadronic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35503)
*Lang Yu,Tao-Tao Sui,Xinyang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在恒定磁场热强子模型中研究φ与K*0的spin-resolved谱函数，结合真空匹配、朗道能级与热散射项构造静止系推迟传播子，发现闭合横向阈值产生eV尺度窄峰，并分析谱展宽对阈值结构与积分矩的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化热强子介质中磁场诱导的阈值效应与谱投影对自旋谱矩的敏感性，检验阈值峰面积估算并考察有限谱展宽如何改变局域阈值结构。

Method: 构造静止系推迟传播子：实真空匹配、精确朗道能级、连接接触项与热散射项；用独立碰撞核与文献极限检验稳定子计算；用完整核独立积分检验领头阈值面积估计；引入正紧致子谱研究有限谱展宽；通过带界Taylor余项的加权恒等式关联重分布与光滑矩；评估完整窗口对三种端点替换及补丁补集的响应。

Result: 闭合横向阈值给出eV尺度失谐的窄峰；阈值峰面积需在自旋谱矩计算中分辨；零点消失由展宽相对失谐控制，而有限区域权重可近似不变；稳定子φ谱在最小两个采样场下响应为负且与b^2标度兼容；硬窗口结果符号振荡，赝标量混合显著改变光滑响应系数。

Conclusion: 结果量化了对阈值与谱投影的敏感性；微观子宽度与源匹配的实验自旋取向仍需额外输入。

Abstract: We study the spin-resolved spectral functions of $φ$ and $K^{*0}$ in a thermal hadronic model with a constant magnetic field. We construct the rest-frame retarded propagators by combining real vacuum matching with exact Landau levels, connection contacts, and thermal scattering terms. The resulting stable-daughter calculation is checked against independent collision kernels and published expressions in common limits. We find that closed transverse thresholds give rise to narrow peaks with eV-scale detunings, whose areas must be resolved in the calculation of small spin spectral moments. We test the leading threshold estimate for the peak area by an independent integration of the full kernel. Using positive compact daughter spectra, we then investigate how a finite spectral spread changes the local threshold structure. The disappearance of zeros of the real part of the inverse propagator is controlled by the spread relative to the detuning, whereas the weight integrated over a finite patch can remain nearly unchanged. We relate this redistribution to smooth accepted moments through a weighted identity with a bounded Taylor remainder, and evaluate the complete-window response to three endpoint replacements, including the patch complement. For the stable-daughter $φ$ spectra with a fixed smooth weight, the response at the two smallest sampled fields is negative and compatible with $b^2$ scaling. Hard-window results instead oscillate in sign, and pseudoscalar mixing substantially changes the smooth response coefficient. These results quantify the sensitivity to thresholds and spectral projection; microscopic daughter widths and source-matched experimental spin alignment require additional input.

</details>


### [346] [Heavy Quark Recombination Across Collision Systems: Meson and Baryon production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35546)
*Vincenzo Minissale,Salvatore Plumari,Maria Lucia Sambataro,Gloria Calà,Vincenzo Greco*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述了重味重子产生中基于"复合+碎裂"的重夸克强子化框架的最新进展，重点预测多粲重子（Ξcc、Ωccc）与底味重子（Λb、Ξb、Ωb）在多种碰撞系统中的产额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核-核与质子-质子碰撞中重味重子测量表明，重夸克强子化无法仅用真空碎裂描述。Λc/D0、Ξc/D0、Ωc/D0 比值在 RHIC 与 LHC 能区的显著增强已被"复合+碎裂"方法成功解释，说明复合（重组）机制在大、小碰撞系统中均具重要性，由此推动对复合强子化框架的进一步发展。

Method: 在"复合+碎裂"（coalescence plus fragmentation）框架下，讨论该强子化模型的最新发展：将其推广至多粲重子（Ξcc、Ωccc）产生的产额预测，并扩展至底味（beauty）部分，给出 Λb/B0、Ξb/B0、Ωb/B0 的产生预测；同时尝试以同一框架统一描述小碰撞系统中的夸克偶素（hidden heavy flavor）产生。

Result: 预测了 Ξcc、Ωccc 在从 PbPb 到 KrKr、ArAr 与 OO 等广泛碰撞系统中的产额；这些态（尤其 Ωccc）对底层粲夸克分布高度敏感，可作为 QGP 中粲夸克热化与非平衡效应的探针。底味部分结果显示存在可观的复合效应，且重子增强强于粲味部分，表明复合机制对更重夸克作用更显著。

Conclusion: 复合（重组）机制是重夸克强子化的关键组成部分，其对重夸克的贡献随夸克质量增大而增强；多粲与底味重子可作为探测粲夸克热化及非平衡效应的有效探针。未来目标是建立统一的框架，同时描述开味与闭味（夸克偶素）重味强子产生。

Abstract: Measurements of heavy-flavor baryons in nucleus--nucleus and proton--proton collisions have provided strong evidence that heavy-quark hadronization cannot be described solely in terms of vacuum fragmentation. In particular, the large enhancement of the $Λ_c/D^0$, $Ξ_c/D^0$ and $Ω_c/D^0$ ratios observed at RHIC and LHC energies has been successfully described within a coalescence plus fragmentation approach, supporting the relevance of recombination mechanisms in both large and small collision systems. Building on these results, we discuss recent developments in the coalescence hadronization framework. Particular emphasis is given to the production of multi-charmed baryons, namely $Ξ_{cc}$, and $Ω_{ccc}$, whose yields are predicted in a wide range of collision systems from PbPb to KrKr, ArAr and OO collisions. The strong sensitivity of these states, especially $Ω_{ccc}$, to the underlying charm-quark distribution makes them valuable probes of charm thermalization and non-equilibrium effects in the QGP. We also present recent extensions of the model to the beauty sector, including predictions for $Λ_b/B^0$, $Ξ_b/B^0$ and $Ω_b/B^0$ production. The results indicate sizeable recombination effects and a stronger baryon enhancement than in the charm sector, highlighting the increasing role of coalescence for heavier quarks. Finally, we briefly comment on ongoing developments toward a unified description of open and hidden heavy flavor through the application of the same framework to quarkonium production in small collision systems.

</details>


### [347] [The LZ event with a two-state dark matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35650)
*Vinicius Oliveira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 重新构建LZ实验统计分析，研究伪狄拉克暗物质中激发态$χ_2$存在时的放热散射贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个核反冲候选事件，并以非弹性暗物质吸热散射解释。但伪狄拉克模型中激发态$χ_2$可存活至今，放热散射同样开放，而已发表的LZ区间未涵盖此情形。

Method: 重建LZ统计推断并与发表区间对比验证；将此方法应用于含有$χ_2$比例$f$的暗物质晕。

Result: 当$f \gtrsim 10^{-5}$–$10^{-2}$（依质量劈裂而定）时放热贡献主导。$f=1/2$时所需截面在$δ=250$–$350$ keV下降$80$至$1.6\times10^4$倍，吸热信号的动力学截断消失。

Conclusion: 仅用$χ_1$解释事件的基准点在半个晕为$χ_2$时超出上限约$30$倍。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a single nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R \simeq 248$keV. LZ interprets it in terms of inelastic dark matter that scatters endothermically from its ground state, $χ_1 N \to χ_2 N$. In pseudo-Dirac models the excited state $χ_2$ can survive to the present day, and exothermic scattering, $χ_2 N \to χ_1 N$, is then also open. The published LZ intervals do not describe this case. We rebuild the LZ statistical inference and validate it against the published intervals. We then apply it to a halo that contains a fraction $f$ of $χ_2$. For $f \gtrsim 10^{-5}$--$10^{-2}$, depending on the mass splitting, the exothermic contribution dominates. For $f=1/2$ the cross section required by the candidate drops by factors of $80$ to $1.6\times10^4$ for $δ= 250$--$350$~keV, and the kinematic cutoff of the endothermic signal disappears. A benchmark that explains the event with $χ_1$ alone lies a factor of $30$ above the upper limit when half of the halo is $χ_2$.

</details>


### [348] [Factorization and virtuality evolution of jet functions in heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35653)
*Carlos Lamas,Carlos A. Salgado,Bin Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新的理论框架，利用微扰方法对重离子碰撞中喷注传播进行"虚度分辨"（virtuality-resolved）的描述，将BDMPS-Z形式推广到包含虚度微分的横动量展宽分布与介质诱导软胶子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BDMPS-Z形式描述介质中喷注传播时未显式区分喷注虚度，因此无法刻画真空类演化与介质诱导动力学之间的相互竞争。作者希望引入虚度这一自由度，以更精细地理解喷注在介质中的演化过程。

Method: 首先推导出一个因子化的喷注截面，从而定义"介质内喷注函数"（in-medium jet function），由此严格识别虚度的作用。该定义允许把标准BDMPS-Z形式扩展为包含虚度微分的横动量展宽分布及介质诱导软胶子谱。研究两类情形：介质在硬碰撞后延迟时间τ₀产生，以及τ₀→0的极限情形。

Result: 对整个虚度范围积分可还原标准BDMPS-Z结果；有限虚度则揭示真空类演化与介质诱导动力学之间的相互影响。作者识别出介质形成时间与喷注形成时间之比是控制哪些初始虚度对喷注函数有显著贡献的关键变量。

Conclusion: 该框架为喷注在重离子碰撞介质中的传播提供了虚度分辨的描述，将BDMPS-Z结果作为虚度积分后的特例纳入，并指出形成时间比是调节初始虚度贡献的控制参数，为更精细地研究喷注淬火提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a new framework to perform a virtuality-resolved description of jet propagation in heavy-ion collisions using perturbative techniques. The role of virtuality is rigorously identified by first deriving a factorized jet cross section that defines an in-medium jet function. This new definition allows to extend the usual BDMPS-Z formalism to include the virtuality-differential transverse momentum broadening distribution and medium-induced soft gluon spectrum. We study both the cases of a medium which is created with some delay time $τ_0$ after the hard collision as well as the case in which $τ_0\to 0$. Integration over the complete virtuality range recovers the standard BDMPS-Z results, while finite virtuality reveals the interplay between vacuum-like evolution and medium-induced dynamics. We identify the ratio between the medium formation time and the jet formation time as the variable controlling which initial virtualities contribute appreciably to the jet function.

</details>


### [349] [GAIA meets LZ: a high velocity tail-tale sign for dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35737)
*Anirban Das,Subhabrata Majumdar,Manibrata Sen,Amogh Srivastav*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 结合LZ实验的核反冲事件与Gaia银河动力学模型，研究非弹性暗物质解释，发现速度分布高速度尾部显著影响内热/外热解释的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能核反冲事件的解释敏感依赖于银河系暗物质分布的高速度尾部。LZ实验报告了248 keV的核反冲事件LZ230616，而Gaia任务提供了前所未有的银河系运动学与质量分布信息，将两者结合可更可靠地检验非弹性暗物质解释。

Method: 采用基于Gaia信息的银河模型推断的速度分布，替代标准晕模型；对LZ230616进行非弹性暗物质（外热与内热）解释分析；并通过模拟大麦哲伦云引力效应研究高速度尾部的影响。

Result: Gaia推断的速度分布相比标准晕模型具有更低的逃逸速度和显著压低的高速度尾部，但本地密度更高。外热解释对此变化基本不敏感；内热解释则需要更大的散射截面和更小的质量劈裂。大麦哲伦云引力效应的模拟表明，高速度尾部可将内热允许参数空间扩展至更低的暗物质质量和更大的劈裂。

Conclusion: 将暗物质直接探测与银河动力学精密测量相结合，能够定性地影响高能核反冲的粒子物理诠释。

Abstract: The interpretation of high-energy nuclear recoils in direct-detection experiments can depend sensitively on the high-velocity tail of the galactic dark matter (DM) distribution. The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported an event, LZ230616, with nuclear recoil energy 248 $\pm$ 23 (stat) $\pm$ 23 (sys) keV, while the Gaia mission has provided an unprecedented view of the kinematics and mass distribution of the Milky Way. We bring these two probes together to investigate the inelastic DM interpretation of LZ230616, using a velocity distribution inferred from Gaia-informed galactic model. Compared to the Standard Halo Model, the resulting distribution has a lower escape speed and a significantly suppressed high-velocity tail, with a higher local density. We find that while the exothermic interpretation is largely insensitive to this change, the endothermic interpretation requires substantially larger scattering cross sections and smaller mass splittings. Furthermore, using simulation-based study of the gravitational effects of the Large Magellanic Cloud, we show that the high-velocity tail can extend the allowed endothermic parameter space towards lower DM masses and larger splittings. Our results demonstrate how combining direct searches for DM detection with precision measurements of galactic dynamics can qualitatively affect the particle-physics interpretation of high-energy nuclear recoils.

</details>


### [350] [Covariant Contrastive Learning for Uncertainty-Aware Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35755)
*Shelley Tong,Philip Harris,Gaia Grosso*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于似然正则化的表示学习策略，通过与含连续不确定性（作为冗余参数）的参数化分类任务联合训练，使神经嵌入空间随系统偏移可预测地协变，从而在LHC异常检测中有效建模系统不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的异常检测（AD）为传统LHC分析提供了模型无关的替代方案，可同时搜索多种信号；表示学习的进展推动用神经嵌入将高维物理观测量映射到低维潜空间以便统计推断。然而，系统不确定性在嵌入空间中的传播机制尚不清楚，严重限制了这类方法在真实LHC分析中的应用。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在先前将监督对比学习用于物理感知嵌入的工作基础上，提出一种机器学习策略，采用基于似然的 regularization 改善嵌入空间结构：将嵌入与下游参数化分类任务联合训练，该分类任务把连续不确定性作为冗余参数（nuisance parameters）纳入。由此获得的潜空间随系统偏移可预测地协变，并得到可被下游统计检验利用的畸变模型。实验使用模拟的CMS Level-1触发数据。

Result: 在4D潜空间中，该方法成功建模了连续不确定性，并配合线性参数化下游分类器，提升了统计异常检测任务的可解释性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种研究与控制潜空间中系统不确定性的通用途径，为LHC及其他高能物理实验中的异常检测开辟了新的可扩展统计分析工作流。

Abstract: Machine-learning-based anomaly detection (AD) offers a promising, model-agnostic alternative to traditional LHC analyses, allowing to search for many signals at once. Recent advances in representation learning motivate the use of neural embeddings to map high-dimensional physics observables into low-dimensional latent spaces better suited to statistical inference. However, the propagation of systematic uncertainty in embedded spaces remains poorly understood, severely limiting the application of these methods to real LHC analyses. We address this gap with a machine learning strategy that uses a likelihood-based regularization to improve the structure of embedded spaces. Building on previous work using supervised contrastive learning for physics-aware embeddings, our approach trains them jointly with a downstream parameterized classification task that incorporates continuous uncertainties as nuisance parameters. This method returns a latent space that covaries predictably with systematic shifts, and a model of such distortions that can be exploited in the downstream statistical test for anomaly detection. Using simulated CMS Level-1 trigger data, we show that our method successfully models continuous uncertainties in a 4D latent space with a linear parametric downstream classifier, improving both the interpretability and robustness of the statistical anomaly detection task. This framework offers a general way to study and control systematic uncertainties in latent spaces, opening the way to a new scalable statistical analysis workflow for anomaly detection at the LHC, and other high energy physics experiments.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [351] [Cardy random matrix theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31825)
*Marco Ambrosini,Alexandre Belin,Jan de Boer,Julian Sonner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造并研究了"Cardy随机矩阵模型"：以S-模不变性为唯一输入的随机矩阵理论，通过对固定宽度能量窗口做粗粒化正则化该约束，得到单随机矩阵势，其本征值扮演2d CFT原初场标度维度，势驱使粗粒化态密度在黑洞阈值能隙之上趋于Cardy分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从最大化无知（maximal-ignorance）的角度出发，仅以2d CFT的S-模不变性（即bootstrap约束）作为输入来构造随机矩阵理论，从而理解态密度为何呈现Cardy行为，并为在计算机上求解与3D量子引力对偶的矩阵/张量模型迈出第一步。

Method: 通过对能量窗口（固定宽度）进行粗粒化来正则化S-模不变性约束，得到一个单随机矩阵的有效势，其本征值对应2d CFT原初场的标度维度。随后采用Monte Carlo模拟验证该势的行为，并确定相关现象涌现的标度极限。

Result: Monte Carlo模拟证实了粗粒化态密度趋于Cardy分布的行为，两者吻合良好；同时发现邻近本征值呈现GUE统计。作者识别出这些现象涌现的标度极限，在该极限下模型呈现出尺度层级结构。

Conclusion: 在标度极限下，模型展现出多层尺度：宏观尺度（窗口大小）上占据数被冻结，bootstrap约束刚性固定谱为Cardy分布；介观尺度（窗口内）Vandermonde排斥如随机矩阵理论般重排本征值；仅在本征值平均间距的微观尺度上单个能级才被分辨。模型因此具有由粗粒化窗口能量宽度倒数设定的有效Thouless时间。

Abstract: We construct and study a ``Cardy random matrix model'': a random matrix theory whose only input, in a maximal-ignorance prescription, is the constraint of $\mathbb{S}$-modular invariance. We regularize this constraint by coarse-graining over energy windows of fixed width, and obtain a potential for a single random matrix whose eigenvalues play the role of the scaling dimensions of primaries in a 2d CFT. The potential drives the coarse-grained density of states to the Cardy profile above a gap at the black hole threshold. We perform Monte Carlo simulations to confirm this behavior and find good agreement for the coarse-grained density of states, while at the same time revealing GUE statistics for nearby eigenvalues. We identify the scaling limit in which these phenomena emerge. In this limit, the model realizes a hierarchy of scales: at the macroscopic scale given by the window size, the occupations are frozen and the bootstrap constraint rigidly fixes the spectrum to the Cardy profile, while at the mesoscopic scale (inside a window) the Vandermonde repulsion rearranges the eigenvalues as in a random matrix theory. It is only at the microscopic scale of the mean level spacing that the individual levels are resolved. Accordingly, our random matrix model displays an effective Thouless time, set by the inverse energy width of the coarse-graining window. Our model provides a first step towards solving the matrix/tensor model dual to 3D quantum gravity on a computer.

</details>


### [352] [Localizing the M-Theory Path Integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31829)
*Pietro Benetti Genolini,Florian Gaar,Jerome P. Gauntlett,Jaeha Park,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出M理论路径积分局部微扰贡献的超对称局域化公式，将其约化为等变通量数据的积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为M理论路径积分的局部微扰部分寻找可计算的闭式表达，并给出ABJM等理论在一般参数下的解析结果。

Method: 利用十一维Chern-Simons耦合的等变延拓确定局域化作用量；对M2膜背景用切丛-余切丛特征标计算单圈行列式。

Result: 首次给出一般squashing和化学势下完整微扰ABJM配分函数的闭式；得到描述黑洞的ABJM拓扑扭曲指标的解析结果；对非环面V^{5,2}理论给出闭式Airy常数；导出Type IIA展开的全亏格结果及积分关联函数。

Conclusion: 公式复现所有已知独立场论结果，并在尚无场论答案的一般参数处给出解析预言。

Abstract: We propose a supersymmetric localization formula for the local perturbative contribution to the M-theory path integral, reducing it to an integral over equivariant flux data. Relative equivariant extensions of the eleven-dimensional Chern--Simons couplings determine the localized action, while for M2-brane backgrounds a tangent-cotangent character computes the one-loop determinant. This gives the first closed form expression for the complete perturbative ABJM partition function at general squashing and chemical potentials, and new analytic results for the ABJM topologically twisted index describing black holes. Applied to the non-toric $V^{5,2}$ theory, the same prescription yields a closed analytic Airy constant previously known only numerically. Our formulas reproduce all available independent field theory results, and provide analytic predictions at general parameters where no field theory answer is currently known. We derive an all-genus result for the Type IIA expansion which is an equivariant form of the usual topological string constant map result, and also extract integrated correlators.

</details>


### [353] [Spread Complexity for Local Operator Quenches with Conserved Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31867)
*Ali Fatemiabhari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维共形场论中带净动量的局域激发态，计算其Lanczos系数与扩展复杂度，并与全息对偶量比较，发现两者增长率不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对局域激发态的量子复杂度研究多限于特定设定，且提出的全息对偶量（与体内部落粒子固有动量相关）是否准确描述扩展复杂度增长率尚待检验。本文旨在推广到平面、柱面和有限温度等多种二维共形场论设定，并验证该对偶关系。

Method: 通过在局域原初算符上分别施加独立的手征与反手征调节子来构造态，得到携带净动量的激发态。解析计算前几个Lanczos系数及扩展复杂度的早期行为；再用数值方法求得更大Krylov指标下的系数，并延长时间演化范围计算扩展复杂度。

Result: 解析得到前几阶Lanczos系数与早期扩展复杂度；数值上获得更大Krylov指标范围的系数及更长时间演化下的扩展复杂度。将扩展复杂度变化率与所提出的全息对偶量（对偶体内部落粒子的固有动量相关量）比较，观察到二者不一致。

Conclusion: 局域激发态的扩展复杂度增长率与所提出的全息对偶量存在偏差，表明该全息对应关系并不成立或需要修正。

Abstract: We study a family of locally excited states in two-dimensional conformal field theories in different setups, defined on the plane, on the cylinder, or at finite temperature. These states are constructed by assigning independent chiral and anti-chiral regulators to a local primary operator, leading to excitations carrying net momentum. We analytically calculate the first few Lanczos coefficients and the early time behaviour of the spread complexity associated with these states. Then, using numerical methods, we obtain the coefficients reaching larger Krylov indices and find the associated spread complexity for a longer range of time evolution. We also compare the rate of change of the resulting spread complexity with the proposed holographic dual quantity, related to the proper momentum of an infalling particle in the dual bulk, but we observe a mismatch.

</details>


### [354] [Nonlinear electroweak sphaleron clouds around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31845)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Jutta Kunz,Hendrik Mennenga,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在探测极限下构造了Schwarzschild与Kerr黑洞周围的非线性电弱sphaleron云，揭示其存在域与丰富分支结构，发现sphaleron云保持有限质量间隙且不趋近线性云，并给出Schwarzschild背景下精确Yang-Mills组态上的闭式领头阶Higgs解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr黑洞与电弱sphaleron分别是广义相对论和粒子物理标准模型的重要解，作者希望研究二者的相互作用，即黑洞环境中sphaleron组态的存在性与性质。

Method: 在探测极限（probe limit）下，围绕Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞构造非线性sphaleron云；先重访平直时空中的静态与旋转（多）sphaleron解，采用实测电弱参数；随后探索对应黑洞组态的存在域与物理性质，并在Schwarzschild背景下求取精确Yang-Mills组态上的闭式领头阶Higgs解。

Result: 得到具有丰富分支结构的黑洞-sphaleron云组态；sphaleron云保持有限质量间隙（mass gap），在所研究族内不趋近线性云，这与超辐射不稳定性起始所对应的常见云不同；同时获得Schwarzschild背景下精确Yang-Mills组态上的闭式领头阶Higgs解。

Conclusion: 黑洞周围的非线性sphaleron云在物理性质上区别于超辐射云，具有有限质量间隙和独特分支结构，表明黑洞与电弱sphaleron的耦合可产生新型组态，为跨广义相对论与标准模型的非微扰研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Kerr black holes and electroweak sphalerons are remarkable solutions of two pillars of modern physics: General Relativity and the Standard Model of particle physics, respectively. In this work, we investigate their interplay by constructing nonlinear sphaleron clouds around Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes in the probe limit. We first revisit static and spinning (multi-)sphalerons in flat spacetime, using the measured electroweak parameters. We then explore the domain of existence and physical properties of the corresponding black-hole configurations, revealing a rich branch structure. Remarkably, the sphaleron clouds retain a finite mass gap and do not approach linear clouds within the families studied, distinguishing them from the familiar clouds associated with the onset of superradiant instabilities. We also obtain a closed-form leading-order Higgs solution on an exact Yang-Mills configuration in a Schwarzschild background.

</details>


### [355] [Quantum vacuum interaction between semitransparent plates in a Pöschl-Teller background: an exact relative TGTG formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31866)
*Jose M. Muñoz-Castañeda,Inés Cavero-Peláez,Gonzalo Sancho-Garrido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 推导了单孤子Pöschl-Teller背景下两块半透明狄拉克-δ板之间单圈量子真空相互作用的精确背景修饰TGTG公式，并扩展至平面板情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究背景场对量子真空相互作用的影响，特别是将TGTG公式推广到具有非平凡背景（单孤子Pöschl-Teller）的情形，以刻画板间相互作用能量对板位置的依赖。

Method: 基于相对行列式定义相互作用，在相同背景下减去两块孤立单板的贡献，从而导出精确的背景修饰TGTG公式；并将该构造推广到平面板并分析对应的随位置变化的相互作用力。

Result: 所得能量不仅依赖于板间距，还依赖于各板的个体位置；对于排斥板，可归一化的零模不会在相对相互作用能中引起红外发散。

Conclusion: 建立了单孤子Pöschl-Teller背景下半透明狄拉克-δ板的精确真空相互作用公式，并推广至平面板情形，给出了相互作用力的位置依赖分析。

Abstract: We derive an exact background-dressed TGTG formula for the one-loop quantum vacuum inter- action between two semitransparent Dirac-delta plates in the one-soliton Pöchl-Teller background. The interaction is defined through a relative determinant in which the two isolated one-plate contributions are subtracted in the same background. As a result, the energy depends on the individual plate positions as well as on their separation. For repulsive plates, the normalizable zero mode does not induce an infrared divergence in the relative interaction energy. We also extend the construction to planar plates and analyze the corresponding position-dependent interaction forces.

</details>


### [356] [Transient Coherence and Multiscale Spectral Structure after a Mass Quench in a 1+1-Dimensional Scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31884)
*Gabriel Abellán,Nelson Bolívar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了有限时间窗内Morlet核平滑的Keldysh关联双局域观测量，定义相对相干权重，并证明其在固定动量下与初始温度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子淬火会生成末态粒子占据数中不存在的反常配对关联，需要一种能刻画这种非对角相干性的观测量，并厘清其与温度、观测窗口及既有谱量（如Wigner谱）之间的关系。

Method: 在1+1维自由实标量场中，用Morlet核在有限淬火后相对时间区间上平滑统计Keldysh关联子，构造双局域观测量；以退相干关联子为基线提取无量纲相对相干权重，推导有限窗口响应，并与带号有限时间Wigner谱对比，进而导出Gaussian-extension正定性界；最后给出重整化Hartree扩展。

Result: 固定动量下相对相干权重对Gaussian热态的初始温度完全无关（分子分母共同热振幅相消）；该相消非纯度判据，也非小波专属；反常信号本质依赖观测窗口， admissible零均值小波在全直线上湮灭相对时间常数反常项；得到与Wigner谱不同的Gaussian-extension正定性界，并以精确光滑淬火占据数作为独立动力学参照。

Conclusion: 相对相干权重提供了一个窗口敏感、温度鲁棒的非平衡配对关联探针，并可推广至重整化Hartree近似，用于刻画固定二维de Sitter背景中标量模的振荡与非振荡区域。

Abstract: A mass quench generates anomalous pair correlations that are absent from the final particle occupations. For a free real scalar field in $1+1$ dimensions, we define a bilocal observable by smearing the statistical Keldysh correlator with a Morlet kernel on the finite post-quench relative-time interval. Its excess over the dephased correlator gives a dimensionless relative coherence weight. At fixed momentum, this weight is exactly independent of the initial temperature for Gaussian thermal states evolved with the same free Hamiltonian: the common thermal amplitude cancels between numerator and denominator. The cancellation is not a purity criterion and is not exclusive to wavelets. We derive the finite-window response and show that the anomalous signal depends essentially on the observation window; an admissible zero-mean wavelet annihilates the relative-time-constant anomalous term on the full line. We compare this response with the signed finite-time Wigner spectrum and derive a distinct Gaussian-extension positivity bound. Exact smooth-quench occupations provide an independent dynamical reference. Finally, we formulate a renormalized Hartree extension and delimit the oscillatory and nonoscillatory regimes of a scalar mode on a fixed two-dimensional de Sitter background.

</details>


### [357] [General Relativity with Finite Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31928)
*Chawakorn Maneerat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限尺寸时间型边界流形上、覆盖所有宇宙学常数符号的广义相对论，施加共形边界条件，并在洛伦兹与欧几里得两种签名下分别分析线性化爱因斯坦方程与引力路径积分热力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限尺寸时间型边界上研究广义相对论及其边界条件，有助于统一理解不同宇宙学常数符号下的引力理论，并为引力的共形边界条件及其对应的热力学描述提供框架。

Method: 在洛伦兹签名下，将Kodama-Ishibashi形式体系推广到带边界流形，围绕Minkowski、dS和AdS背景对多种边界分析线性化爱因斯坦方程；在欧几里得签名下，计算被边界包围的黑洞与宇宙学视界的引力路径积分半经典近似。

Result: 给出了不同宇宙学常数符号、不同边界（含Minkowski、dS、AdS）下线性化爱因斯坦方程的分析，并得到由边界数据定义的固定共形温度与固定K的共形正则系综所对应的引力路径积分半经典近似。

Conclusion: 共形边界条件在洛伦兹与欧几里得两种框架下为研究有限尺寸时间型边界上的广义相对论、黑洞及宇宙学视界热力学提供了一个统一描述。

Abstract: In this thesis, we consider general relativity on a manifold with a timelike boundary of finite size, across all signs of the cosmological constant $Λ$. We impose conformal boundary conditions, whereby the conformal class of the induced metric and the trace of the extrinsic curvature $K$ are fixed at the boundary. In Lorentzian signature, we analyse the linearised Einstein equations about Minkowski, de Sitter (dS), and anti-de Sitter (AdS) space for a variety of boundaries, employing the Kodama-Ishibashi formalism adapted to manifolds with a boundary. In Euclidean signature, we study the thermodynamics of black hole and cosmological horizons enclosed by the boundary, computing the leading semiclassical approximation of the gravitational path integral, for which the boundary data define a conformal canonical ensemble of fixed conformal temperature and $K$.

</details>


### [358] [Wormhole fragmentation and the axion Weak Gravity Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32063)
*Puxin Lin,Gary Shiu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从欧几里得虫洞的非微扰碎裂为带电缺陷出发，推导出轴子弱引力猜想（aWGC），并确定其作用量-电荷界的量级一系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子弱引力猜想（aWGC）此前缺乏从虫洞物理出发的第一性原理推导，其系数与相关猜想（如虚距离界 IDB）的关系也不清晰。

Method: 要求欧几里得虫洞碎裂为带电缺陷能降低欧几里得作用量，由此确定轴子作用量-电荷界的系数；并讨论含额外标量场及多轴子-伸缩子对的情形，给出几何表示。

Result: 在无额外标量场时，所得系数与虚距离界（IDB）一致；加入额外标量场会加强该界。多轴子-伸缩子对情形可用几何方式表示。

Conclusion: 满足 aWGC 的轴子会使对应虫洞不稳定，可能抑制虫洞增殖，从而缓解相关的因子化问题。

Abstract: We derive the axion Weak Gravity Conjecture (aWGC) from the nonperturbative fragmentation of Euclidean wormholes into charged defects. Requiring fragmentation to lower the Euclidean action fixes the axion action-to-charge bound and its order-one coefficient. Without additional scalars, this coefficient coincides with that of the Imaginary Distance Bound (IDB), while additional scalars strengthen the bound. The aWGC in the presence of multiple axio-dilaton pairs is discussed, leading to geometric representations. Axions satisfying the aWGC render the corresponding wormholes unstable, potentially suppressing their proliferation and resolving associated factorization problems.

</details>


### [359] [A backreacted bouncing geodesic in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32071)
*Gauri Batra,Adam Levine,Stephen H. Shenker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究2D膨胀引力中有限温度关联函数$G(t)$的解析延拓,发现黑洞奇点通过世界线反弹的零测地线在体(体)内留下奇点信号;引力的回反应修正会抹平这些反弹奇点,并将黑洞奇点物理编码为$t=0$处重合点奇点附近的Stokes现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS/CFT全息框架下,黑洞奇点如何体现在边界有限温度关联函数的解析延拓中是一个基本问题。已知的反弹测地线奇点图像需要在量子修正(引力回反应)下进一步检验其命运。

Method: 在2D膨胀引力模型中,计算反弹测地线对黑洞几何的引力回反应修正,并沿回反应后的测地线鞍点做最陡下降分析,追踪$G(t)$在复$t$平面上的Stokes线结构。

Result: 回反应修正使反弹测地线奇点被平滑掉;黑洞奇点信息转而通过$t=0$(及其热像)处重合点奇点附近的Stokes现象体现。该Stokes线结构与大$c$ 2D共形块中禁戒奇点的分析结构存在平行性。

Conclusion: 量子修正并不消除黑洞奇点的物理,而是改变其编码方式——从显式反弹奇点转变为Stokes现象;这一结构与大$c$共形块禁戒奇点类似,并提示需进一步研究回反应之外的量子修正意义。

Abstract: In holography, analytically continued finite temperature correlation functions $G(t)$ exhibit signatures of the black hole singularity. These take the form of singularities at certain values of $t$ whose bulk origin is a null geodesic worldline "bouncing off" the black hole singularity. We study certain quantum corrections to this effect in 2D dilaton gravity that are captured by gravitational backreaction of the worldline onto the black hole geometry. These corrections smooth out the bouncing geodesic singularities. By following the backreacted geodesic saddle in a steepest descent analysis we find instead that the black hole singularity physics gets encoded in a subtle way involving a Stokes phenomenon near the coincident point singularity of $G(t)$ at $t=0$ (and its thermal image). We point out a parallel between the structure of Stokes lines uncovered in this analysis and the structure present in the analysis of forbidden singularities in large $c$ 2D conformal blocks. Finally we make some preliminary remarks about the significance of quantum corrections in these models beyond backreaction.

</details>


### [360] [Learning Scattering Amplitudes with Transformer Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32096)
*Philip Velie,Tianji Cai,Piyush Jha,Vijay Ganesh,Aishik Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种 transformer 强化学习算法，通过将已知对称性与线性关系嵌入学习过程，求解平面 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论中的高圈散射振幅，显著减少需先验已知的解比例并保证输出满足已知关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高圈散射振幅的状态空间随圈数阶乘式增长，此前纯 transformer 方法需要大量先验已知解且无法有效泛化到更高圈数，难以与其他方法的结果比较。

Method: 采用 symbology 表示形式因子，将问题建模为离散序列到整数系数的映射；模型不独立预测所有系数，而是提出赋值并通过精确线性关系传播，传播不足时使用 MCTS 在赋值空间搜索；训练采用 transformer 强化学习并结合已知对称性。

Result: 所需先验已知解的比例大幅降低，且每个输出都自动满足已知关系集合，克服了状态规模的阶乘式增长问题，从而实现向更高圈的泛化。

Conclusion: 将已知对称性与精确线性关系整合进学习算法，是机器学习方法推广到高圈振幅计算的关键，使结果可与其他方法推导的结果进行比较。

Abstract: We introduce a transformer reinforcement learning algorithm that learns to solve high loop-level scattering amplitudes in planar N = 4 Super Yang-Mills theory. Our algorithm improves on previous transformer-only results by incorporating previously derived symmetries and relationships into the learning algorithm. This results in a greatly decreased fraction of the solution that needs to be known a priori to solve the entire problem. An additional benefit is that our algorithm also ensures that every output obeys the set of known relationships. Rather than predicting all coefficients independently, the model proposes assignments that are propagated through exact linear relations, while MCTS searches over assignments when propagation alone is insufficient. This is crucial to the generalization of machine learning approaches to higher loops, as without this, there is no way to overcome the factorially scaling of state sizes and compare to results derived via other methods. Using the symbology representation of the form factor, we frame the problem as learning a mapping between discrete sequences and integer coefficients.

</details>


### [361] [Hairy Taub-NUT solution in gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32139)
*Masato Nozawa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维 N=2 规范超引力中构造并研究了一类带标量毛的精确 Taub-NUT-AdS 解，分析了其视界、超对称性、因果结构、守恒质量与欧氏扇区性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Taub-NUT-AdS 背景下引入标量毛并探讨其几何与热力学性质，有助于理解 NUT 参数变形对视界存在性的影响以及超引力解的结构。

Method: 通过求解仅由非平凡标量场及其自相互作用势支撑、关闭所有规范场的 N=2 规范超引力方程，分析 Killing 视界条件与 Killing 旋量；采用协变相空间方法计算守恒质量，并研究欧氏扇区的 nuts 与 bolts 正则性。

Result: 解至多存在一个 Killing 视界且必为非退化视界；NUT 变形可使静态极限无视界的支出现视界；带标量毛的解不存在 Killing 旋量；整体结构存在闭合类时曲线；在固定 NUT 参数下给出受限第一定律；欧氏扇区包含特殊解族并给出 nuts 与 bolts 的正则性条件。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一类新的带标量毛 Taub-NUT-AdS 精确解，揭示了 NUT 参数对视界和欧氏几何的显著影响，并建立了相应的质量与第一定律框架。

Abstract: We present and study a family of exact Taub-NUT-AdS solutions with scalar hair in four-dimensional ${\cal N}=2$ gauged supergravity. The solutions are supported solely by a nontrivial scalar field and its self-interaction potential, with all gauge fields switched off. We determine the conditions under which Killing horizons exist and demonstrate that the spacetime admits at most one Killing horizon, and whenever it exists it is necessarily nondegenerate. In particular, the NUT deformation can give rise to a horizon in a branch whose static limit is horizonless. We also establish that the solutions with nontrivial scalar hair admit no Killing spinors. As in ordinary Taub-NUT-AdS spacetime, the global structure is afflicted by closed timelike curves. We compute the conserved mass using the covariant phase space method and formulate a restricted first law with the NUT parameter held fixed. Finally, we study the Euclidean sector, including an exceptional family specific to Euclidean signature, and determine the regularity conditions for bolts and nuts.

</details>


### [362] [Higher-Form Description of Chiral Soliton Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32212)
*Yutaka Ookouchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将手性孤子晶格（CSL）重新表述为基于二形式规范场 $C_2$ 与三形式规范场 $A_3$ 之间 Stückelberg 耦合的高形式框架，其中弦与墙作为延展源直接处理；该框架复现了精确的 Sine--Gordon 结果，包括晶格间距、墙间相互作用系数、能量最小化条件及无隙声子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上 CSL 被描述为紧致标量场的周期性绕卷构型，这种表述不便直接处理弦、墙等延展对象。作者希望建立一个以延展源为基本变量的高形式框架，使弦和墙的物理直观与规范不变性直接体现在理论结构中。

Method: 引入二形式规范场 $C_2$ 与三形式规范场 $A_3$ 之间的 Stückelberg 耦合，高形式规范不变性要求弦的世界面为墙的世界体的边界。在二次对偶有效理论中，重高形式模式交换产生平行墙之间的排斥 Yukawa 相互作用，其系数由对偶性确定。随后将描述推广到非线性、多支的高形式理论。

Result: 二次对偶理论给出的 CSL 晶格间距与精确 Sine--Gordon 结果吻合良好，且无需参数拟合。非线性多支高形式理论复现了精确的稀薄墙--墙相互作用系数、周期性 CSL 及其能量最小化条件，以及无隙的 CSL 声子。

Conclusion: CSL 声子被识别为周期性高形式背景的集体位移，标量绕卷则被重新解释为非线性高形式理论的分支流动。该工作表明高形式框架可为 CSL 及其激发提供等价且更几何化的描述。

Abstract: The chiral soliton lattice (CSL) is conventionally described as a periodic winding configuration of a compact scalar field. In this paper, we formulate the CSL in a higher-form framework based on a Stückelberg coupling between a two-form gauge field $C_2$ and a three-form gauge field $A_3$, in which strings and walls can be treated directly as extended sources. Higher-form gauge invariance requires the string worldsheet to be the boundary of the wall worldvolume. In the quadratic dual effective theory, exchange of the massive higher-form mode generates a repulsive Yukawa interaction between parallel walls. With its coefficient fixed by duality, the resulting CSL lattice spacing agrees well with the exact Sine--Gordon result without parameter fitting. We then extend the description to a nonlinear, multi-branch higher-form theory. It reproduces the exact dilute wall--wall interaction coefficient, the periodic CSL and its energy-minimization condition, as well as the gapless CSL phonon. The phonon is identified as a collective displacement of the periodic higher-form background, while scalar winding is reinterpreted as branch flow of the nonlinear higher-form theory.

</details>


### [363] [Rational Probes of Spectral Geometry in Hermitian Matrix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32497)
*Ali Nassar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文引入基于Cauchy核的Pick矩阵作为有限平面谱探针，用以刻画Hermitian单矩阵模型中固定多割拓扑在矩空间中的嵌入结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面圈方程留下有限个低阶矩未定，Hankel正性只能约束这些矩，却无法揭示固定多割族在矩空间中如何嵌入。作者希望找到一个有限、可操作的诊断工具来显式描述这一嵌入结构。

Method: 以Cauchy核f_n(x)=(z_n-x)^{-1}作谱探针，构造半正定Pick矩阵P_{mn}，形成有限节点bootstrap；结合谱判别式的非分支双零点（dressed saddles）对矩空间轨迹施加横向约束，余下方向为填充分数；将P(z,z)视为有限平面谱响应，∇_m P(z,z)视为矩空间磁化率，分析其在η^{-2}增强与共法向对齐的性质，并用多个独立鞍点的响应重构共法向空间与切空间。

Result: 在规则实dressed saddle附近，磁化率以η^{-2}增强且沿填充分数流形共法向对齐，切向形变时主增强项抵消；多个独立鞍点的响应可重构共法向空间并经共同核得到切空间。结合Pick正性与预解式的实性和解析性，重新解析推导了四次和六次模型的已知平面结果：非对称四次模型显式给出填充分数的一维几何，六次模型展现独立共法向方向。

Conclusion: Pick正性与有限平面响应提供了局部一致性条件，可约束低阶矩，先于全局周期匹配与平衡变分不等式对平衡测度的选择，从而明确固定多割族在矩空间中的几何嵌入。

Abstract: The planar loop equations of a Hermitian one-matrix model leave finitely many low moments undetermined. Hankel positivity constrains these moments but does not reveal how a fixed multicut family is embedded in moment space. We introduce a finite-plane diagnostic using the Cauchy kernels $f_n(x)=(z_n-x)^{-1}$ which we use as spectral probes. Their Gram matrix is the positive-semidefinite Pick matrix $P_{mn}$, whose positivity defines a finite-node bootstrap analogous to the Hankel bootstrap.
  For a fixed regular multicut topology, the nonbranching double zeros of the spectral discriminant, which we call \emph{dressed saddles}, impose transverse constraints on the corresponding moment-space locus, while its remaining directions are filling fractions. Writing $z=E+iη$, $E$ selects a spectral region and $η>0$ sets a continuous resolution scale. We use $P(z,z)$ as a finite-plane spectral response and interpret $\nabla_{\mathbf m}P(z,z)$ as a moment-space susceptibility. Near a regular real dressed saddle, this susceptibility is enhanced as $η^{-2}$ and aligns with a conormal to the filling-fraction manifold, whereas the leading enhancement cancels along tangent deformations. Responses near several independent saddles can therefore reconstruct the conormal space and, through their common kernel, its tangent space.
  Combining Pick positivity with reality and analyticity of the resolvent, we obtain alternative analytic derivations of known planar results in quartic and sextic models. The asymmetric quartic makes the one-dimensional filling-fraction geometry explicit, while sextic models exhibit independent conormal directions. These results clarify how local consistency conditions constrain the low moments before global period matching and the equilibrium variational inequality select the equilibrium measure.

</details>


### [364] [Greybody Factors and Hawking Spectra of Quantum-Corrected Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32702)
*Moslem Shafiee,Ahmad Sheykhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究含真空极化与共形反常修正的量子修正史瓦西黑洞的霍金辐射，计算了无质量玻色场的灰体因子、能谱与发射率，发现量子修正使黑洞寿命大幅延长并渐近趋向稳定极端残余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典史瓦西黑洞霍金辐射图像存在量子效应缺失问题，量子修正对蒸发末期的辐射谱与寿命有决定性影响，而低能灰体因子、高自旋模贡献及残余稳定性尚需系统研究。

Method: 采用含真空极化与共形反常修正的量子修正史瓦西度规，解析其双视界结构、非单调霍金温度与对数熵修正，并计算不同自旋与角动量无质量玻色场的灰体因子、辐射谱及发射率。

Result: 修正几何具有两个视界，霍金温度在某临界质量处为零且呈非单调行为，熵含对数修正。量子修正压低低能灰体因子、增强高能灰体因子；蒸发后期温度下降显著抑制发射，高自旋模可忽略，标量 s=l=0 模占主导，黑洞寿命大幅延长并渐近趋向稳定极端残余。

Conclusion: 量子修正显著改变霍金辐射的晚期演化，使蒸发过程趋于停滞，最终形成稳定极端残余，而非经典图像中的完全蒸发。

Abstract: We investigate Hawking radiation of a quantum-corrected Schwarzschild black hole with vacuum polarization and conformal anomaly effects. The corrected geometry has two horizons, a non-monotonic Hawking temperature that vanishes at a critical mass, and a logarithmic entropy correction. We compute greybody factors, spectra, and emission rates for massless bosonic fields of various spins and angular momenta. Quantum corrections suppress low-energy greybody factors and enhance high-energy ones. During late evaporation, where quantum influences become significant, the decreasing temperature strongly suppresses emission. In this case, the higher-spin modes become negligible, and the scalar $s=l=0$ mode dominates. The black hole lifetime is greatly extended as it asymptotically approaches a stable extremal remnant.

</details>


### [365] [Nonlinear pole topology of slow currents in holographic probe-brane metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32748)
*Yan Han,Xiang-Qian Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文通过极点拓扑分类孤立反演对称慢矢量的三阶推迟响应，并在Lifshitz黑膜背景中计算DBI探针膜的全复数三次谐波响应，发现z=2时红外响应接近弛豫拓扑、z=1与z=3/2则为混合权重，表明拓扑由动力学而非DBI平方根决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低频Drude响应本身无法确定哪一个近守恒部门承载电流，需要一个能区分不同近守恒机制（本构非线性、非线性弛豫、与第二慢模耦合）的判据，并检验其是否在强耦合全息模型中由动力学而非唯象假设决定。

Method: 按极点拓扑对孤立、反演对称慢矢量的领头立方推迟响应进行分类：本构非线性只产生入射频率极点，非线性弛豫在发射频率处增加传播子，与第二标量慢模耦合产生对频极点；随后在Lifshitz黑膜背景中计算有限密度Dirac–Born–Infeld探针膜的全复数三次谐波响应，并求精确非线性直流解。

Result: 边际z=2理论中稠密红外响应趋近弛豫拓扑，带符号红外权重p_IR=0.994±0.002，且在ω_×/Γ_J=0.2918处存在与归一化无关的零点；非边际z=1与z=3/2背景实现混合本构—弛豫权重，说明拓扑是动力学选择的而非DBI平方根强加。精确非线性直流解给出无参数稠密极限标度函数与z=2时E_nl∝T^{3/2}的交叉场。

Conclusion: 极点拓扑可作为区分近守恒电流部门中本构与弛豫贡献的判据，且在全息DBI体系中由动力学决定；该框架连接了涌现高阶形式慢电流、非线性响应理论与全息输运，双模推广进一步指出当能量或形变模仍为慢模时应出现的额外极点结构。

Abstract: Low-frequency Drude response does not by itself determine which nearly conserved sector carries an electric current. We classify the leading cubic retarded response of an isolated, inversion-symmetric slow vector by its pole topology. Constitutive nonlinearity produces only incoming-frequency poles, whereas nonlinear relaxation adds a propagator at the emitted frequency; coupling to a second scalar slow mode generates pair-frequency poles. We then compute the full complex third-harmonic response of finite-density Dirac--Born--Infeld probe branes in Lifshitz black-brane backgrounds. In the marginal $z=2$ theory, the dense infrared response approaches the relaxation topology, with a signed infrared weight $p_{\rm IR}=0.994\pm0.002$ and a normalization-independent zero at $ω_\times/Γ_J=0.2918$. Nonmarginal $z=1$ and $z=3/2$ backgrounds instead realize mixed constitutive--relaxation weights, demonstrating that the topology is selected dynamically rather than imposed by the DBI square root. The exact nonlinear dc solution further yields a parameter-free dense-limit scaling function and a crossover field $E_{\rm nl}\propto T^{3/2}$ at $z=2$. These results connect emergent higher-form slow currents, nonlinear response theory, and holographic transport, while the two-mode extension identifies the additional pole structures expected when energy or deformation modes remain slow.

</details>


### [366] [3d Lovelock gravity and the holographic c-theorem: Proof at all orders and resummation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32866)
*Gokhan Alkac,Luis Guajardo,Hikmet Ozsahin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 3维Lovelock引力（由正则化Lovelock不变量得到的Horndeski理论）在任意耦合下、所有阶都满足全息c定理；通过将Horndeski标量方程化为全微分，可将零能量条件积分为单调c函数，并精确/渐近计算中心荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验3维Lovelock引力这类高阶Horndeski理论在任意耦合下是否仍存在全息c定理，并澄清其真空的对称性（标度不变但非共形）及相应中心荷的定义与耦合依赖性。

Method: 在畴壁解的minisuperspace中，证明Horndeski标量方程在每一阶都是全微分，从而将标量的导数锁定为翘曲因子的导数；利用零能量条件积分得到单调c函数。真空的线性标量剖面破坏特殊共形变换，中心荷取球面上on-shell作用量对数发散项的系数。对Gauss-Bonnet情形精确计算，对完整tower作渐近计算。

Result: c定理对所有阶和任意耦合成立；中心荷在Gauss-Bonnet情形可精确求出，对完整tower渐近求出，且与耦合线性相关并与c函数的UV值一致。所有结果仅通过两个生成函数依赖耦合，进而给出导致规则黑洞的耦合选择的闭合形式重求和Lagrangian、c函数与中心荷。

Conclusion: 3维Lovelock引力在任意耦合下具备单调c函数与全息c定理，但其真空是标度不变而非共形的。可按真空中有效牛顿常数的正性以及非平凡标量真空的唯一性对重求和理论进行分类，并给出相应闭合表达式。

Abstract: We prove that 3d Lovelock gravity, the Horndeski theory obtained from the regularized Lovelock invariants in three dimensions, admits a holographic c-theorem at all orders and for arbitrary values of the couplings. In the minisuperspace of domain wall solutions, the equation of the Horndeski scalar is a total derivative at every order, which locks the derivative of the scalar to that of the warp factor along the flows we consider and allows the null energy condition to be integrated into a monotonic c-function. The linear scalar profile of the resulting vacuum breaks the special conformal transformations, so the dual theory is scale-invariant but not conformal, and the central charge is defined as the coefficient of the logarithmic divergence of the on-shell action on the sphere. We compute it exactly for the Gauss-Bonnet case and asymptotically for the full tower, and find that it is linear in the couplings and matches the UV value of the c-function. Since all results depend on the couplings only through two generating functions, we give the resummed Lagrangians, c-functions and central charges in closed form for the choices of couplings that lead to regular black holes, and classify the resummed theories by the positivity of the effective Newton constant on the vacuum and the uniqueness of the vacuum with a non-trivial scalar.

</details>


### [367] [RG Limit Cycles in BKT Flows $\equiv$ Periodic Real-Time Dynamics in the Current-Current Perturbed $SU(2)_1$ WZW Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34248)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在可积费米子表示下，利用广义Bethe拟设构造了各向异性current-current微扰SU(2)_1 WZW模型的精确含时多体波函数，证明周期边界条件导致与XXZ三角R-矩阵相关的量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程，并将可积性条件与BKT重整化群流方程对应，从而把RG极限环实现为相互作用强度的周期性实时演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立重整化群演化与实时量子动力学之间的精确对应，并给出RG极限环的精确动力学实现，连接循环RG流、量子可积性与周期驱动量子场论。

Method: 采用各向异性current-current微扰的SU(2)_1 Wess-Zumino-Witten模型，借助其可积费米子表示与广义Bethe拟设构造精确含时多体波函数；分析周期边界条件导出的量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程，其与XXZ三角R-矩阵及量子仿射代数U_q(sl_2^)的spin-1/2赋值表示相关；通过方程相容性约束纵/横向相互作用强度的时间演化，并在普适区将物理时间等同于对数RG尺度以导出可积性条件与BKT RG流方程的等价性。

Result: 周期边界条件导致与XXZ三角R-矩阵相关的量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程；方程相容性约束纵/横向相互作用强度的含时演化；在普适区，可积性条件在物理时间等同对数RG尺度时精确等价于BKT RG流方程；各向异性current-current微扰SU(2)_1 WZW模型的RG极限环被实现为相互作用强度的周期性实时演化。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了RG极限环的精确动力学实现，并建立了循环RG流、量子可积性与周期驱动相互作用量子场论之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We establish an exact correspondence between renormalization-group (RG) evolution and real-time quantum dynamics in the anisotropic current-current perturbed $SU(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) model. Using its integrable fermionic representation, we construct the exact time-dependent many-body wavefunction by means of the generalized Bethe ansatz and show that periodic boundary conditions lead to quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov equations associated with the XXZ trigonometric $R$-matrix, which corresponds to the quantum affine algebra $\mathcal{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}_2})$ evaluated in its spin-$1/2$ evaluation representation. Consistency of these equations constrains the temporal evolution of the longitudinal and transverse interaction strengths. In the universal regime, these integrability conditions are exactly equivalent to the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless RG flow equations upon identifying physical time with the logarithmic RG scale. Consequently, the RG limit cycles of the anisotropic current-current perturbed $SU(2)_1$ WZW model are realized as periodic real-time evolution of its interaction strengths. Our results provide an exact dynamical realization of RG limit cycles and establish a direct connection between cyclic RG flows, quantum integrability, and periodically driven interacting quantum field theories.

</details>


### [368] [Charged regular AdS$_3$ black holes in a scalar-vector-tensor theory coupled to Born-Infeld electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32878)
*Gokhan Alkac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在三维标量-矢量-张量理论中构建了精确、静态、圆对称的带电正则AdS₃黑洞解，通过耦合非线性Born-Infeld电动力学消除Maxwell型解的曲率奇点，并分析了其视界结构与微观熵计算意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该理论的正则化Gauss-Bonnet耦合中，Maxwell带电对应解在原点存在对数曲率奇点，需要寻找消除该病理的正则时空解。

Method: 将作用量与非线性Born-Infeld电动力学耦合，在三维标量-矢量-张量理论中求解精确静态圆对称带电AdS₃黑洞解，得到度规函数并显式验证核心正则性。

Result: Born-Infeld耦合成功治愈对数曲率奇点；根据参数选择，解呈现出单视界、双视界、极端黑洞以及无视界正则几何等多种视界结构。

Conclusion: 该正则时空可作为无奇点框架下微观熵计算的重要测试平台。

Abstract: We construct exact, static, circularly symmetric charged regular AdS$_3$ black hole solutions in a three-dimensional scalar-vector-tensor theory obtained from regularized Gauss-Bonnet couplings. While the Maxwell-charged counterpart of this theory suffers from a logarithmic curvature singularity at the origin, we demonstrate that coupling the action to non-linear Born-Infeld electrodynamics cures this pathology. We obtain the metric function and show explicitly the regularity of the core. Depending on the choice of parameters, the solution exhibits various horizon structures, including single-horizon, double-horizon, extremal black holes, and horizonless regular geometries. Finally, we discuss the significance of this regular spacetime as a testbed for microscopic entropy calculations in singularity-free setups.

</details>


### [369] [Cylinder Defect Casimir Energy and Weyl Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34529)
*Yang Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文区分了共形面缺陷（conformal surface defect）的两种Casimir可观测量：嵌入平坦空间的圆筒具有由位移反常（displacement anomaly）固定的对数贡献；而通过环境理论径向量子化得到的"大圆柱"则在共形缺陷真空中给出与局部反常约定相关的Casimir能量，并用自由场与全息计算加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形缺陷的Casimir能量在几何嵌入方式不同时其形式与来源可能不同，尤其对数项与反常系数的关系尚需厘清。作者希望明确区分两种嵌入情形下Casimir可观测量的性质，并将其与反常系数（位移反常、局部反常）建立定量联系。

Method: 通过在平坦空间中嵌入圆筒（circular cylinder）以及通过对环境理论做径向量子化获得大圆柱（great cylinder）两种方式，分别构造共形面缺陷的Casimir可观测量，并利用自由场计算与全息计算进行检验。

Result: 嵌入平坦空间的圆筒其Casimir能量含对数项 $-d_1\log(R/\varepsilon)/(24R)$，由位移反常固定；径向量子化得到的大圆柱在共形缺陷真空中Casimir能量为 $-b/(12R)$（在固定局部反常约定下）。自由场与全息计算对两种嵌入下的关系均给出验证。

Conclusion: 共形面缺陷的Casimir能量依赖嵌入方式：圆筒情形体现位移反常的普适对数贡献，大圆柱情形则由局部反常系数决定。所提出的关系经自由场与全息检验成立，为通过Casimir效应探测共形缺陷反常提供了途径。

Abstract: We distinguish two Casimir observables associated with a conformal surface defect. A circular cylinder embedded in flat space has a logarithmic contribution to its Casimir energy, $-d_1\log(R/ε)/(24R)$, fixed by the displacement anomaly. A great cylinder obtained by radial quantization of the ambient theory instead has Casimir energy $-b/(12R)$ in the conformal defect vacuum with a fixed local anomaly convention. Free-field and holographic calculations provide checks of the proposed relations in both embeddings.

</details>


### [370] [Accelerating systems as probes of Lorentz violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33029)
*Quentin G. Bailey,Nils A. Nilsson,Sawyer J. Star*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究局域洛伦兹破坏有效场论中的Fulling-Davies-Unruh效应，通过精确量子化实标量场并构造均匀加速探测器的有限时间Unruh-DeWitt响应，发现一般洛伦兹破坏系数下响应是非稳态的，其能谱在约10^{-7}的无量纲系数下与常规热谱存在可辨别差异；作为替代视角，还在点粒子扇区中由恒定电场生成轨迹，研究标量波沿修正轨迹的固有时傅里叶响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验洛伦兹对称性破坏对加速探测器量子场响应（即Unruh效应）的影响，判断常规热谱结论在洛伦兹破坏情形下是否依然成立，并寻找可通过实验探测的偏离信号。

Method: 在局域洛伦兹破坏有效场论中，对实标量场按洛伦兹破坏系数进行精确正则量子化，导出色散关系、哈密顿量以及无质量和有质量的Wightman函数；区分保持boost对称性的条件与仅要求沿单一加速世界线稳态的较弱条件；用Wightman函数构造均匀加速探测器的有限时间Unruh-DeWitt响应，并数值计算其能谱。另外在点粒子扇区引入洛伦兹破坏，求解恒定电场产生的精确轨迹，研究标量波沿修正轨迹的固有时傅里叶响应。

Result: 一般洛伦兹破坏系数下，探测器响应为非稳态，依赖于测量持续时间和时间位置；数值得到激发率随探测器能级差的谱，在约10^{-7}的无量纲系数下与常规有质量热谱存在可辨别差异；文中还讨论了实验意义。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破坏会导致加速探测器响应偏离标准Unruh热谱，其可观测偏离可能出现在约10^{-7}量级的无量纲系数下，为实验检验洛伦兹对称性提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: We investigate the Fulling-Davies-Unruh effect in a local Lorentz-violating effective field theory, focusing on the quantum-field response of accelerated detectors. We canonically quantize a real scalar field exactly in the coefficients for Lorentz violation. The dispersion relation, Hamiltonian, and massless and massive Wightman functions are obtained. We determine the conditions under which the scalar dynamics preserves boost symmetry and distinguish these from the weaker conditions required for stationarity along a single accelerated worldline. The Wightman functions are used to construct the finite-time Unruh-DeWitt response for a uniformly accelerated detector. For generic coefficients the response is nonstationary and depends on both the duration and temporal location of the measurement. The spectrum, which is the resulting excitation rate as a function of the energy level difference in the detector, is obtained numerically. It shows discernable differences with the usual massive thermal spectrum for dimensionless coefficients on the order of $10^{-7}$. Experimental implications of the result are discussed. As an alternative viewpoint, we place Lorentz violation in the point-particle sector, derive the exact trajectory generated by a constant electric field, and study the proper-time Fourier response of a conventional scalar wave along the modified trajectory.

</details>


### [371] [Extrinsic Renormalization of New Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33690)
*Cristóbal Corral,Rodrigo Olea,Leonardo Sanhueza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用外曲率构造边界项，系统研究了New Massive Gravity中渐近AdS时空的重整化，涵盖一般耦合、简并点及Noether-Wald荷计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在New Massive Gravity中建立一致的全息描述需要对渐近AdS时空进行重整化，而此前缺乏基于外曲率边界项的通用、可直接用全息源表达有限作用量的重整化方案，尤其是简并点处出现额外模式与发散的问题。

Method: 采用外曲率（extrinsic curvature）构造边界项与二次反项（counterterm）；对一般耦合值计算总作用量变分的有限性；通过渐近展开与辅助场表述对比全息应力张量；在简并点分析Fefferman-Graham展开的额外模式与放宽的AdS边界条件；并运用Noether-Wald形式计算守恒荷。

Result: 单一外曲率边界项在一般耦合下给出有限且可由全息源表达的总作用量变分，适用于常曲率几何及满足Brown-Henneaux渐近条件的非爱因斯坦解，其全息应力张量与辅助场表述一致；简并点处二次外曲率反项消除新发散，得到含两个独立源的全息图像，响应满足修改的Ward恒等式并给出有限守恒荷；Noether-Wald形式正确重现BTZ黑洞、非爱因斯坦AdS波与旋转毛发黑洞的荷。

Conclusion: 外曲率边界项为重力和New Massive Gravity的渐近AdS重整化提供了简洁而一致的全息框架，可推广至简并点及非爱因斯坦解，并配合Noether-Wald形式获得正确守恒荷。

Abstract: The renormalization of asymptotically AdS spacetimes in New Massive Gravity is studied using boundary terms constructed with the extrinsic curvature. For generic values of the theory couplings, a single extrinsic boundary term produces a consistent holographic description, as the variation of the total action is finite and expressible in terms of the holographic source. This prescription applies to constant-curvature geometries and non-Einstein solutions with Brown-Henneaux asymptotic conditions. A suitable asymptotic expansion shows the agreement of the resulting holographic stress tensor with the one obtained from the auxiliary-field formulation.
  At the degenerate point, where the two maximally symmetric vacua coalesce, the Fefferman-Graham expansion admits an additional mode associated with relaxed AdS boundary conditions. A quadratic extrinsic counterterm removes the new divergences and leads naturally to a holographic picture with two independent sources. The corresponding responses obey a modified holographic Ward identity and determine finite conserved charges.
  Finally, the proper use of the Noether-Wald formalism reproduces the correct charges of the BTZ black hole, non-Einstein AdS waves, and the rotating hairy black hole.

</details>


### [372] [Quantization of the electroweak Volkov-Proca theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33825)
*Tudor Pătuleanu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将电弱理论带电部分在自发对称破缺后于外电磁平面波背景中量子化，称为电弱Volkov-Proca理论，系统分析其对称性、守恒量、完备性关系、传播子及谱表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强电磁平面波背景（如激光场）下，电弱带电扇区的量子化与散射理论需要超越传统微扰框架。现有Volkov方法主要针对标量与旋量QED，缺乏对自发对称破缺后Proca（有质量矢量）扇区在平面波背景中的完整量子场论处理。

Method: 1. 在自发对称破缺后对外电磁平面波背景中的电弱带电扇区进行量子化，构建电弱Volkov-Proca理论；2. 分析全局U(1)对称性与连续残余平移子群，导出相应守恒荷与标记态的动量分量；3. 推导Ritus形式下Volkov-Proca态的完备性与正交归一关系；4. 构造离壳与在壳传播子，并给出其用标量Volkov传播子的表示；5. 推导Källén-Lehmann谱表示与LSZ约化公式。

Result: 建立了电弱Volkov-Proca理论的形式框架，给出了守恒荷与动量分量、Ritus形式态的完备性与正交归一关系、离壳与在壳传播子及其标量Volkov传播子表示，以及Källén-Lehmann谱表示和LSZ约化公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为平面波背景下电弱带电扇区的量子场论提供了完整的形式工具，可用于后续散射过程与谱性质的研究，是Volkov方法向自发破缺规范理论中矢量扇区的推广。

Abstract: We quantize the charged sector of the electroweak theory in an external electromagnetic plane-wave background after spontaneous symmetry breaking, which we refer to as the electroweak Volkov-Proca theory, and analyze its global $U(1)$ symmetry and continuous residual translation subgroup together with the associated conserved charges and momentum components that label the states. We further derive the completeness and orthonormality relations for the Volkov-Proca states in Ritus form, and construct the off-shell and on-shell propagators, including their representation in terms of the scalar Volkov propagator. Finally, we derive the Källén-Lehmann spectral representation and the LSZ reduction formula for the Volkov-Proca states.

</details>


### [373] [Vacuum polarization by charged matter fields in AdS spacetime in the presence of a cosmic string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33942)
*E. R. Bezerra de Mello*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在高维反德西特时空背景下,重新分析了带有磁通量宇宙弦周围带电物质场(玻色子与费米子)的真空极化效应,计算了能量-动量张量的真空期望值,发现其为磁通量的偶函数、周期为量子磁通,并以图像展示其随距离与参数的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维AdS时空与带磁通宇宙弦共存时,带电物质场的真空极化效应及相应的能量-动量张量真空期望值,以理解该弯曲流形中量子真空的性质。

Method: 分别考虑带电玻色场与费米场在高维AdS时空中的传播,该时空包含一条沿弦芯携带磁通量的理想化宇宙弦;通过计算这些场对应的能量-动量张量真空期望值(⟨T^μ_ν⟩)进行分析,并将结果用特殊函数表示。

Result: 所得到的能量-动量张量真空期望值是沿弦芯磁通量的偶函数,周期等于量子磁通 Φ_0=2π/e;由于结果以特殊函数表达、其行为不够直观,作者给出了随到弦距离以及不同模型参数变化的图像。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了高维AdS时空中带磁通宇宙弦附近玻色场与费米场真空极化的能量-动量张量真空期望值,揭示了其对磁通的偶周期性,并通过数值图像展示了其空间与参数依赖行为。

Abstract: In this paper we revisited the analyse the vacuum polarizations effect associated wit charged matter fields in a higher-dimensional anti de-Sitter (AdS) spacetime in the presence of an idealized cosmic string carrying magnetic flux running along its core. Specifically we will consider, separately, charged bosonic and fermionic fields propagating in this manifold. Our main objective is to calculate the vacuum expectation values (VEV) of the energy-momentum tensor, $\langle T^μ_ν\rangle$, associated with both matters fields. As will see, these VEVs are even function of the magnetic flux running along the string's core, with period equal to the quantum flux $Φ_0=\frac{2π}{e}$. Because the results obtained are expressed in terms of special functions, and a clear behavior for them are not very enlightening, we provide some graphs exhibiting their behavior as function of the distance to the string considering different values for the parameters presented in the model considered.

</details>


### [374] [dS/CFT Holography including Fermionic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34073)
*Akashdeep Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究de Sitter全息对偶，分析Dirac场在晚期边界的波函数，发现边界模式具有等幅振荡衰减，构造Bunch-Davies真空波函数并验证两点函数，指出bulk单个Dirac场对应边界一对初级旋量场，满足共形Ward恒等式，并在Yukawa相互作用下检验映射稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于将de Sitter对偶理论置于晚期边界的提议（arXiv:astro-ph/0210603v5），此前工作主要针对标量场，本文旨在纳入费米子场，系统研究Dirac场在全息框架下的边界行为与对偶映射。

Method: 求解dS时空中Dirac方程，分析边界附近的模式衰减特性；通过适当地在Dirac作用量中加入边界项，用路径积分方法计算Bunch-Davies真空波函数，以时间-like γ矩阵本征空间投影表示；用正则形式的两点函数验证路径积分构造；通过识别边界源与bulk场投影分量，建立边界理论映射并检验共形Ward恒等式；最后加入Yukawa相互作用（标量场分别属于complementary series和principal series）检验映射稳健性。

Result: Dirac场边界模式具有等幅振荡衰减，与属于principal series的标量场类似；路径积分波函数与正则两点函数一致；bulk单个Dirac场对应边界一对初级旋量场，波函数系数满足共形Ward恒等式；在Yukawa相互作用下（两种标量场表示情形）映射保持稳健。

Conclusion: de Sitter晚期边界全息对偶可自然扩展至费米子场，单个bulk Dirac场映射为边界双初级旋量场，且该对偶在引入Yukawa相互作用后仍然成立，支持晚期边界对偶 proposal。

Abstract: We investigate some aspects of holography in de Sitter space, pertaining to the proposal for the dual theory residing on the late-time boundary [arXiv:astro-ph/0210603v5], including a Fermionic field that satisfies the Dirac equation. The modes of the solutions near the boundary are found to possess equal and oscillatory fall-off, similar to the scalar field belonging to the principal series representation of the isometry group of de Sitter space. We evaluate, by suitably adding a boundary term to the Dirac action, the wavefunctional of the Bunch Davies vacuum in path integral formalism in terms of the projections of the field on the eigenspaces of the time-like $γ$-matrix. We validate our path-integral construction of the wavefunctional by reproducing the two-point function between the field operators in the vacuum state as computed in the canonical formalism. By appropriately identifying the boundary sources in terms of the projected components of the bulk field, we find that a single Dirac field in the bulk corresponds to a pair of primary spinor fields in the boundary theory and show that the resulting wavefunction coefficients satisfy the conformal Ward identities. We check the robustness of our holographic map under the addition of a Yukawa interaction considering the two cases where the scalar field in the interaction term belongs to the complementary series and the principal series representations of the isometry group of de Sitter space respectively.

</details>


### [375] [Three-loop anomalous dimensions of leading-twist operators in the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34323)
*Mrigankamauli Chakraborty,Leonid A. Shumilov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型的$\overline{\text{MS}}$方案下计算了领头扭度算符的三圈反常量纲，利用广义理论的涌现超对称优化计算，并通过临界点分析提取Regge截距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型中领头扭度算符的高阶（三圈）反常量纲，并借助临界点性质深入理解该模型的标度行为，包括与$1/N$展开、广义Gribov-Lipatov互易性及Regge理论的联系。

Method: 采用计算机代数系统计算若干低自旋算符的三圈反常量纲，通过求解一组Diophantine方程重构解析形式；利用对应广义理论的涌现超对称性优化计算。

Result: 获得了领头扭度算符的三圈反常量纲解析结果，并完成临界点处的综合分析：与$1/N$展开进行比较、检验广义Gribov-Lipatov互易性、分析最右奇点附近的行为，从而以$ε$展开形式提取所有领头扭度轨迹的Regge截距值。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型中领头扭度算符三圈反常量纲及其对应的Regge截距，验证了多种理论自洽性关系。

Abstract: We compute the anomalous dimensions of the leading-twist operators at three-loop level in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ scheme in the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model. Calculations are done for a number of low-spin operators using computer algebra systems, and the analytical form is reconstructed by solving a set of Diophantine equations. Calculations are optimized using the emergent supersymmetry of the corresponding generalized theory. For the obtained results, we perform a comprehensive analysis at the critical point, including a comparison with the $1/N$ expansion, a check of the generalized Gribov-Lipatov reciprocity, and an analysis of the behavior next to the rightmost singularity. The latter allows us to extract the values of Regge intercepts for all the leading-twist trajectories in the form of an $ε$ expansion.

</details>


### [376] [M2 brane $S^3/\mathbb Z_k$ instanton partition function in ${\rm AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$: 2-loop correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34777)
*Arkady A. Tseytlin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究M2膜瞬子 $S^3/\mathbb{Z}_k\subset S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$ 的双圈修正，该瞬子对偶于ABJM自由能的大$N$非微扰贡献。计算需处理玻色与费米零模，采用投影格林函数并考虑集体坐标雅可比，发现雅可比与4-顶点贡献的非平庸$k$依赖相互抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作(arXiv:2609.14497)已证明包裹 $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^1$ 的M2膜双圈修正为零，与ABJM中 $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson loop的局域化预言一致；而 $S^3/\mathbb Z_k$ 瞬子的单圈贡献已与局域化匹配(arXiv:2307.14112)。本文旨在将双圈计算推广至该瞬子情形，检验对偶预言的更高阶修正。

Method: 计算M2膜瞬子 $S^3/\mathbb Z_k\subset S^7/\mathbb Z_k$ 的双圈修正。因存在玻色和费米零模，需使用投影格林函数并考虑集体坐标雅可比。总双圈贡献由4-顶点项与雅可比项组成，分析其对零模分离选择的依赖性。

Result: 对 $k>2$，4-顶点贡献的超越$k$依赖被雅可比项对任意费米零模分离选择所抵消；残余有理项在超对称选择的分离下消失，此时集体坐标与量子场构成独立超多重态。由此推测集体坐标积分仅贡献整体归一化，故为单圈精确。对 $k=1$，四次顶点贡献约化为运动方程项，与 ${\rm AdS}_3\subset{\rm AdS}_7\times S^4$ 情形(arXiv:2511.22306)通过形式解析延拓相联系。

Conclusion: M2膜瞬子双圈修正中，雅可比项对抵消非平庸$k$依赖起关键作用。在超对称分离下总贡献消失，暗示集体坐标积分至多为单圈精确，支持瞬子对偶预言；$k=1$ 情形与 ${\rm AdS}_4\times S^7$ 中 $S^3$ 情形经形式解析延拓关联。

Abstract: As was shown in arXiv:2609.14497, the 2-loop correction to the partition function of M2 brane wrapping $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^1$ inside $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$ vanishes, in agreement with the localization prediction for $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson loop in ABJM theory in the grand-canonical ensemble according to the conjecture of arXiv:2505.21633. Here we study the 2-loop correction in the case of the M2 brane instanton $S^3/\mathbb Z_k\subset S^7/\mathbb Z_k$ dual to non-perturbative large $N$ contribution to ABJM free energy on 3-sphere. The 1-loop instanton contribution was shown in arXiv:2307.14112 to match the localization prediction. The 2-loop computation is complicated by the presence of bosonic and fermionic zero modes, which require using projected Green's functions and accounting for the collective-coordinate Jacobian. The Jacobian provides a non-trivial 2-loop contribution in addition to the one of the 4-vertex in the M2 brane action. We find that for $k>2$ the transcendental $k$-dependence of the 4-vertex contribution is cancelled by the Jacobian for any choice of the separation of the fermionic zero modes from the quantum fields. The residual rational term vanishes for the separation selected by the supersymmetry, under which the collective coordinates and the quantum fields form separate supermultiplets. We conjecture that the remaining integral over the collective coordinates contributes only to an overall normalization of the partition function, so it is 1-loop exact. For $k=1$ the quartic vertex contribution reduces to equation-of-motion terms as in the case of ${\rm AdS}_3 \subset { \rm AdS}_7 \times S^4$ in arXiv:2511.22306 related to $S^3 \subset {\rm AdS}_4 \times S^7$ case by a formal analytic continuation.

</details>


### [377] [Semiclassical Liouville Theory on the Real Projective Plane: A Complex Interpretation of the Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34811)
*Yu Nakayama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过半经典路径积分计算验证了实射影平面上Liouville理论的一点函数，发现贡献来自无穷多个复鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liouville理论在实射影平面上的一点函数早已由bootstrap方法导出，但从未与半经典路径积分计算进行过对比验证。这一验证比预期更困难，因为实射影平面上所有常曲率度量都是正曲率的，而经典Liouville理论产生常负曲率的度量，因此不存在实的经典解。

Method: 作者展示半经典路径积分需要从无穷多个具有负定度量的复鞍点获得贡献，并选择使路径积分收敛的积分围道。

Result: 在选定使路径积分收敛的积分围道后，这些复鞍点成功重现了精确的一点函数。

Conclusion: 复鞍点在类时Liouville理论和二维de Sitter引力中以相同方式出现，本案例为检验复鞍点方法的有效性提供了难得的与已知精确结果对比的机会。

Abstract: The exact one-point function of Liouville theory on the real projective plane was derived long ago from the bootstrap, yet it has never been verified against a semiclassical path-integral computation. The check is less straightforward than one might expect. On the real projective plane every constant-curvature metric is positively curved, whereas classical Liouville theory produces metrics of constant negative curvature, so no real classical solution exists. Instead, as we show, the semiclassical path integral receives contributions from infinitely many complex saddles with a negative-definite metric. Once the integration contour is chosen so that the path integral converges, these saddles reproduce the exact one-point function.
